# jfguiLib API reference

Generated by `python tools/gendoc.py` from the doc comments of `lib` -- do not edit by hand; `python tools/gendoc.py --check` tells whether this file is up to date.

Every crate is `#![no_std]`. An app depends on `ui` (which brings `gfx`, `text`, `plat`, `base`, `img_core` and the codecs), links one style crate as `style` and calls `ui::run` (docs/GUIDE.md). Colours are `0xAARRGGBB`, straight alpha in the API, premultiplied (`FMT_PARGB`) in images. UI sizes are DIP (96 dpi = 100 %), drawing works in pixels. A name in a description such as `ui_box_begin` or `[img_save]` is the assembly routine the function was ported from; the algorithm specs in `legacy/ASMLIB/docs/modules/*.md` describe both versions.

Not listed here: `simd` (the SSE2 / AVX2 kernel layer, see `README.md`, "SIMD kernels"), the style crates (docs/STYLES.md), plugins (`plugins/README.md`).

## ui

*the immediate-mode UI: runtime, layout, widgets, windows, style contract* -- crate root [`lib/ui/lib.rs`](../lib/ui/lib.rs)

```text
ui -- the immediate-mode runtime: one Ui context instead of ASMLIB's g_* globals  [src/ui]

  run(desc, app, style) -> exit code   command line, headless (--shot / --script) or window
  App: init(ui) / frame(ui) builds the UI every pass / command(ui, id, arg) -> handled
  Style: how widgets look (style.rs, the contract of ASMLIB/inc/style.inc); the app
  passes the style crate it was linked with (build_rs.py --style)
  main!(expr): the app's entry point (jfgui_main, called by plat's process entry)

Modules (one per ASMLIB file): ctx (ui_ctx.s: input, hit testing, behaviour, slots,
timers, commands, semantics, clip, layers, passes), layout (ui_layout.s), cmd (ui_cmd.s:
draw command lists and their replay), dirty (ui_dirty.s), theme (ui_theme.s), basic
(ui_basic.s), auto (ui_auto.s: scripts and the widget dump), runtime (ui_main.s), cursor
(ui_cursor.s), drop (ui_drop.s), clipimg (ui_clipimg.s), style (style.inc); the widget
families popup, edit, list, value, color, win, chrome, filedlg (R5 agents).

State: ASMLIB keeps every window's UI state in per-module blocks (`*_wb0` .. `*_wb1`)
that ui_win.s swaps when it builds another window.  Here that state is `WinParts` (the
live window's part of every module: `ui.wp.ctx`, `ui.wp.lay`, `ui.wp.cmd`, `ui.wp.dirty`,
`ui.wp.rt`, `ui.wp.popup`, ...); win.rs swaps it with a window's saved WinParts
(core::mem::swap).  State shared by all windows lives in `Ui` itself (core) and in
`ui.m` (the widget families' shared state).

For the widget families (R5): the asm globals map to fields -- g_mx / g_my / g_focus /
g_hot / g_active / g_last_rect / g_keyq ... are `self.wp.ctx.*`, g_boxes / g_next_* are
`self.wp.lay.*`, g_dcol / the command lists `self.wp.cmd.*`, g_fb / g_ui_dpi
`self.wp.rt.fb` / `.dpi`; g_frame / g_now_ms / g_repass / g_mods / g_sems / slots / timers /
g_uiw_cur are fields of Ui.  A family's per-window state goes into its `W` (in WinParts),
its shared state into its `S` (in Mods).  Style calls: `let st = self.style; let mut sp =
Sp { .. }; let (w, h) = st.xxx_size(self, &mut sp); self.place(w, h); let ev =
self.behave(id, hb); self.sp_placed(&mut sp, ev); st.xxx(self, &sp);
self.focus_ring_draw(ev); self.sem(K_*, id, label, value)` (basic.rs shows every pattern).
The core calls into the families through these functions (all ported, R5): win.rs uiw_*,
chrome.rs titlebar_begin / titlebar_end / edge_grips (ui-win), popup.rs accel_dispatch /
tooltip / popup_count (ui-popup), edit.rs text_key_filter, clip_set / clip_get /
hclip_text_* / open_folder / open_url (ui-edit).
```

- [Drawing commands](#drawing-commands): `cmd_layer`, `draw_color`, `draw_color_get`, `draw_rect`, `draw_rrect`, `draw_frame`, `cmd_rrect`, `cmd_panel_fx`, `draw_text`, `cmd_text`, `emit_text`, `draw_icon`, `draw_circle`, `draw_line`, `draw_image`, `draw_clip`, `draw_unclip`, `icon_hook`, `cmd_custom`, `cmd_shadow`
- [Scripts and the widget dump](#scripts-and-the-widget-dump): `dump`
- [Theme](#theme): `theme_set`, `theme_dark`, `accent_set`, `color`, `ts_font`, `ts_line`, `ts_base`
- [Cursors, drops, clipboard images, dirty regions](#cursors-drops-clipboard-images-dirty-regions): `cursor_new`, `cursor_custom`, `cursor_current`, `cursor_render`, `drop_count`, `drop_path`, `drop_list`, `drop_pos`, `drop_window`, `clip_has`, `clip_set_image`, `clip_get_image`, `dirty_force_full`, `canvas_damage`

### App, AppDesc, Ui, run

#### `ui/lib.rs`

Source: [`lib/ui/lib.rs`](../lib/ui/lib.rs)

- **`main!(...)`**
  the app's entry point: `ui::main!(ui::run(&DESC, &mut MyApp::default(), &style::STYLE));`
  (expands to the `jfgui_main` symbol the platform entry calls; the app crate denies
  unsafe code, this one item is the exception).  It first installs the app's permissions
  (apps/NAME/PERMISSIONS, which build_rs.py passes as JFGUI_PERMISSIONS; perm.rs).

- **`struct AppDesc`**
  the app descriptor (APP_DESC): title, AF_\* flags, sizes in DIP, accent 0xRRGGBB.  Write
  `AppDesc { title: "..", w: .., ..AppDesc::NONE }` so later fields keep their defaults.
  - `title: &'static str`
  - `flags: u32`
  - `w: u16`
  - `h: u16`
  - `min_w: u16`
  - `min_h: u16`
  - `accent: u32`
  - `cmds: &'static [Command]` -- (ui-popup) the command table (AD_CMDS: ids, labels, icons, accelerators, CF_\* flags)
  - `menubar: &'static [Mi]` -- (ui-popup) the menu bar of menubar(None) (AD_MENUBAR: Mi::Menu records)

**`impl AppDesc`**

- **`const NONE: AppDesc = AppDesc { title: "", flags: 0, w: 0, h: 0, min_w: 0, min_ ...`**
  every field empty / zero

- **`trait App`**
  the app's callbacks (APP_DESC on_init, on_frame, on_command)
  - `fn actions(&self) -> &'static [agent_core::ActionSpec]`
    Optional domain actions, in addition to the universal UI action catalogue.
  - `fn action_revision(&self) -> Option<u64>`
    Domain revision; None uses the UI runtime's monotonically increasing frame revision.
  - `fn action_available(&self, _spec: &agent_core::ActionSpec) -> Result<(), agent_core::ActionError>`
    Dynamic domain constraints such as read-only files or an absent selection.
  - `fn action(&mut self, _ui: &mut Ui, _spec: &agent_core::ActionSpec, _input: &agent_core::Value, _context: &agent_core::ActionContext) -> Result<agent_core::ActionResult, agent_core::ActionError>`
    Domain action handler; adapters should call the same editing functions as the GUI.
  - `fn action_inspect(&self, _query: &agent_core::Value) -> Result<agent_core::Value, agent_core::ActionError>`
    Filtered domain state; the default universal inspection includes the widget tree.
  - `fn action_resource_info(&self, _uri: &str) -> Result<agent_core::Resource, agent_core::ActionError>`
    Describe an application-owned binary resource returned by one of its actions.
  - `fn action_resource_read(&self, _uri: &str, _offset: u64, _limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>, agent_core::ActionError>`
    Read a bounded byte range of an application-owned resource, never an arbitrary path.
  - `fn init(&mut self, _ui: &mut Ui) -> bool`
    called before the first frame; false = initialisation failed (exit code 2)
  - `fn frame(&mut self, ui: &mut Ui)`
    builds the UI (every pass; no side effects except through widgets)
  - `fn command(&mut self, _ui: &mut Ui, _id: u32, _arg: u32) -> bool`
    commands queued with ui.cmd_run and the built-in UIC_\* ids; true = handled

- **`struct WinParts`**
  the per-window state of the live window: the core's per-window blocks (ui_ctx_wb0,
  ui_layout_wb0, ui_cmd_wb0, ui_dirty_wb0, ui_main_wb0) and the families' (ui_popup,
  ui_list, ui_chrome).  win.rs keeps one of these per window and swaps it with `Ui::wp`
  when another window is built (core::mem::swap); a new window starts from `WinParts::new`.

- **`struct Ui`**
  the UI context: everything ASMLIB keeps in g_\* variables
  - `desc: AppDesc`
  - `style: &'static dyn Style`
  - `text: Text` -- fonts, glyph cache, text runs (shared: the asm's font module)
  - `dc: Dc` -- the gfx draw context (the asm's g_dc; render time only)

#### `ui/runtime.rs`

Source: [`lib/ui/runtime.rs`](../lib/ui/runtime.rs)

```text
runtime -- the command line, window or headless run, event loop, passes, rendering
                                                                    [src/ui/ui_main.s]

  run(desc, app, style) -> exit code     jfgui_main of an app
  fb() -> &Surface   dpi() -> i32   dp(dip)   px2dip(px)   headless() -> bool
  invalidate()   quit()   client_size() -> (w, h) DIP   set_title(s)   title() -> &str
Runtime internals used by the script runner (auto.rs): run_passes, render,
process_events, resize(w_px, h_px).

Command line (every app):
  --shot FILE.png     headless: render the first frame, write a PNG, exit
  --script FILE       headless: run a UI test script (auto.rs)
  --dump              headless: print the widget tree (JSON lines) after --shot
  --size WxH          client size in DIP (default: the AppDesc)
  --scale PERCENT     headless scale (100 = 96 dpi)
  --theme light|dark  force a theme (default: AppDesc flags, else the system)
  --accent RRGGBB     accent colour
  --anim off          no animations (values jump to their targets)
  --log-events        print every platform event
  --auto-close MS     close the window after MS milliseconds (smoke tests)
  --verify-dirty      after every partial redraw compare with a full redraw

The frame loop: platform events are applied to the input state one "edge" (button,
key, wheel) at a time; each edge runs App::frame (plus up to three settle passes when
the layout moved); commands queued by widgets are delivered to App::command in between;
then the recorded draw commands are rendered and presented.  Nothing runs while the app
is idle.  Several windows (win.rs): one App::frame builds all of them; every event
carries its window (Event::win).
```

- **`fn launch(desc: &AppDesc, app: impl App + 'static, style: &'static dyn Style) -> i32`**
  Launch the same owned app on a desktop event loop, browser worker or Android host.
  Host-driven applications remain alive after their entrypoint returns.

- **`struct Runtime`**
  An owned application driven one event/tick at a time. Browser hosts and automation
  use the same passes, input handling and software renderer as native windows.

**`impl Runtime`**

- **`fn new(desc: &AppDesc, app: impl App + 'static, style: &'static dyn Style) -> Result<Self, i32>`**
  Initialize a headless surface with the normal command-line/theme configuration.
  `Err(2)` means application initialization or framebuffer allocation failed.
- **`fn step(&mut self, now_ms: u32, events: &[Event]) -> bool`**
  Apply input edges and due timers, then render if required. False means closed.
  Time is milliseconds since initialization; it must advance monotonically.
- **`fn frame(&self) -> &Surface`**
  Current framebuffer for presentation or screenshot encoding.
- **`fn ui(&self) -> &Ui`**
  Read the runtime context, including dimensions and semantic widgets.
- **`fn command(&mut self, id: u32, arg: u32) -> bool`**
  Deliver a registered numeric command through the normal application callback.
- **`fn finish(&mut self)`**
  Close exactly once and deliver the existing application exit callback.

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn fb(&self) -> &Surface`**
  ui_fb: the live window's framebuffer
- **`fn client_size(&self) -> (i32, i32)`**
  ui_client_size: client area in DIP
- **`fn anim_off(&self) -> bool`**
  g_anim_off: animations off (--anim off, the settings)  // pn-core
- **`fn set_anim_off(&mut self, off: bool)`**
- **`fn headless(&self) -> bool`**
  ui_headless: running without a window (--shot / --script)
- **`fn invalidate(&mut self)`**
  ui_invalidate: request a new frame (state changed outside the callbacks)
- **`fn quit(&mut self)`**
  ui_quit: leave the main loop after this frame
- **`fn set_title(&mut self, s: &str)`**
  ui_set_title(s): change the window title (kept for title() / title bars; 255 bytes)
- **`fn title(&self) -> &str`**
  ui_title: the window title (the AppDesc title until set_title)

- **`fn run(desc: &AppDesc, app: &mut dyn App, style: &'static dyn Style) -> i32`**

### Layout

Source: [`lib/ui/layout.rs`](../lib/ui/layout.rs)

```text
layout -- box layout (flex-like, measured over passes)              [src/ui/ui_layout.s]

  box_begin(id, flags, gap)          open a container (id 0 = automatic)
  box_end()                          close it
  next_size(w, h)                    size of the next widget / box in DIP: > 0 fixed,
                                     0 natural, < 0 fill weight
  next_rect(x, y, w, h)              absolute placement of the next widget in DIP,
                                     relative to the current box's content origin
  next_rect_px(x, y, w, h)           absolute placement in window px (w / h 0 = natural)
  next_flags(ST_*)   next_icon(cp)   style flags / icon for the next widget
  spacer(dip)                        empty space along the main axis (< 0 = fill)
  last_rect() -> [x, y, w, h]        the last placed widget (px)
  box_rect() -> [x, y, w, h]         content rectangle of the current box (px)
  box_id() -> id                     id of the current box
  box_offset(dx, dy)                 scroll the current box's content
  avail_width(iw) -> (px, fixed)     width the next widget will get (TX_WRAP)
Internal: place(iw, ih), take_flags(), take_icon(), layout_root(), layout_end_root()

A box lays its children out along its main axis (BOX_H: x, BOX_V: y).  Fill children
share the space left by the others; because that is only known after all children were
placed, the previous pass's totals are used and a pass is repeated when they change (the
runtime caps this at 4 passes).
```

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn next_size(&mut self, w: i32, h: i32)`**
  ui_next_size(w, h): DIP; > 0 fixed, 0 natural, &lt; 0 fill weight
- **`fn next_rect(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32)`**
  ui_next_rect(x, y, w, h): DIP relative to the current box's content origin
- **`fn next_rect_px(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32)`**
  ui_next_rect_px(x, y, w, h): absolute placement of the next widget / box in window
  px (w or h 0 = natural size); nothing is accounted in the parent box
- **`fn box_offset(&mut self, dx: i32, dy: i32)`**
  ui_box_offset(dx, dy): shift the content of the current box by (-dx, -dy) px
- **`fn next_flags(&mut self, f: u32)`**
  ui_next_flags(ST_\*): style flags for the next widget (OR-ed)
- **`fn take_flags(&mut self) -> u32`**
  ui_take_flags: the pending next_flags value (cleared; for widgets)
- **`fn next_icon(&mut self, cp: u32)`**
  ui_next_icon(cp): icon code point (IC_\*) for the next widget
- **`fn take_icon(&mut self) -> u32`**
  ui_take_icon: the pending icon (cleared; for widgets)
- **`fn last_rect(&self) -> [i32; 4]`**
  ui_last_rect: [x, y, w, h] of the last placed widget (px)
- **`fn box_id(&self) -> u32`**
  ui_box_id: id of the current box (the root box's id at the top level; 0 outside a pass)
- **`fn box_rect(&self) -> [i32; 4]`**
  ui_box_rect: [x, y, w, h] content rectangle of the current box (px)
- **`fn box_flags(&self) -> u32`**
  flags of the current box (ui_box_rect - 8 + 4 in the asm)
- **`fn place(&mut self, iw: i32, ih: i32) -> [i32; 4]`**
  ui_place(iw, ih) -> last_rect: place the next widget (natural size in px)
- **`fn avail_width(&self, iw: i32) -> (i32, i32)`**
  ui_avail_width(iw) -> (width px a wrapping widget of natural (unwrapped) width iw
  gets from the next place, 1 when that width does not depend on iw) -- no side
  effects.  0 = unknown (no wrapping).
- **`fn box_begin(&mut self, id: u32, flags: u32, gap: i32)`**
  ui_box_begin(id, flags, gap): open a container (id 0 = automatic)
- **`fn box_end(&mut self)`**
  ui_box_end: store the measured natural size, correct the parent's totals
- **`fn spacer(&mut self, dip: i32)`**
  ui_spacer(dip): empty space along the main axis (&lt; 0 = fill weight)
- **`fn dp(&self, dip: i32) -> i32`**
  ui_dp(dip): DIP -> physical pixels, round(dip \* dpi / 96) (signed)
- **`fn px2dip(&self, px: i32) -> i32`**
  ui_px2dip(px): physical pixels -> DIP, (px \* 96 + dpi / 2) / dpi
- **`fn dpi(&self) -> i32`**
  ui_dpi: current dpi of the live window (96 = 100 %)
- **`fn set_dpi(&mut self, dpi: i32)`**
  ui_set_dpi(dpi): override the dpi (tests)
- **`fn dp_fx(&self, dip_fx: i32) -> i32`**
  dp_fx(dip_fx) -> px in 24.8 (dip_fx \* dpi / 96, truncated)

- **`fn dp_at(dip: i32, dpi: i32) -> i32`**
  dip2px_at / dp_at: round(dip \* dpi / 96) (signed)

- **`fn px2dip_at(px: i32, dpi: i32) -> i32`**
  px2dip_at: round(px \* 96 / dpi), dpi 0 = 96 (ui_win.s)

### Context: input, focus, timers, commands, semantics

Source: [`lib/ui/ctx.rs`](../lib/ui/ctx.rs)

```text
ctx -- UI context: input state, passes, hit testing, hot / active / focus, state slots and
animations, deferred commands and timers, semantic records, clip, layers, modal,
disabled regions, warnings                                            [src/ui/ui_ctx.s]

App-facing:
  now() -> ms                     milliseconds (virtual clock in headless runs)
  key(vk) -> bool                 vk pressed this frame and not yet consumed (consumes it)
  key_peek(vk) / key_down(vk)     the same without consuming / while the key is held
  mods() -> MOD_*   mouse() -> (x, y) px (-1 outside)   pen() -> (pressure, PTR_*)
  focus(id) / focused() -> id     give / ask the keyboard focus
  cmd_run(id, arg)                queue a command for App::command (runs after the frame)
  timer(id, ms)                   one-shot timer -> App::command(UIC_TIMER, id)
  anim(id, target, ms) -> value   animated value 0..256 towards target
  warn(msg)                       "UIWARN f<frame>: ..." on stderr; scripts and jfgui_DEBUG
                                  also warn once about an id two widgets of one pass share
                                  (compound widgets take id + 1 .. or (id << 3 | k) parts)
Widget building blocks:
  behave(id, hbflags) -> EV_*     hit box on last_rect + pointer / keyboard behaviour
  hit(id, hbflags)                hit box only
  hit_rect(id, x, y, w, h, hb)    behave on an explicit rectangle (px)
  slot(id) -> &mut Slot           persistent per-id state record
  sem(kind, id, label, value)     semantic record (state from the last behave)
  clip_push(x, y, w, h) / clip_pop()   clip drawing and hit boxes (px)
  layer_set(l) -> previous        modal(layer)   disabled_push() / disabled_pop()
Runtime (runtime.rs, win.rs): frame_begin / frame_end (a pass over all windows),
uiw_pass_begin / uiw_pass_end (one window), input_event, input_clear_edges, hot_update.
```

- **`struct Sem`**
  semantic record (dump / expect)  [SR_\*]
  - `id: u32`
  - `kind: u16`
  - `state: u16` -- SS_\*
  - `rect: [i32; 4]` -- x, y, w, h physical px
  - `label: Option<String>`
  - `value: i32`
  - `max: i32`
  - `layer: u8`
  - `depth: u8`
  - `win: u8` -- window index (0 = the main window)

- **`struct Slot`**
  persistent per-id state (slot)  [SL_\*]
  - `seen: u32`
  - `afrom: u16`
  - `ato: u16`
  - `at0: u32`
  - `adur: u32`
  - `measw: i32`
  - `meash: i32`
  - `fixed: i32`
  - `wsum: i32`
  - `scrollx: i32`
  - `scrolly: i32`
  - `aux: i32`

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn now(&self) -> u32`**
  ui_now: milliseconds since start (virtual clock in headless runs)
- **`fn mods(&self) -> u32`**
  ui_mods: MOD_\* bits of the current input
- **`fn mouse(&self) -> (i32, i32)`**
  ui_mouse: pointer position in px (-1 when outside)
- **`fn pen(&self) -> (u32, u32)`**
  ui_pen: (pressure 0 = none / 1..UI_PRESSURE_ONE, PTR_\*) of the last pointer event
- **`fn focus(&mut self, id: u32)`**
  ui_focus(id): give the keyboard focus to a widget (focus ring shown).  The id is
  looked up in whichever window emits it in the next pass (that window is activated).
- **`fn focused(&self) -> u32`**
  ui_focused: id of the focused widget (0 = none)
- **`fn key(&mut self, vk: u32) -> bool`**
  ui_key(vk): pressed this frame and not yet consumed (consumes it)
- **`fn capture_shortcut(&mut self, vk: u32, mods: u32) -> bool`**
  Capture an exact application shortcut before text widgets handle it. Unlike key(),
  this bypasses the focused textbox's key reservation. Use only for an editing
  surface whose history/commands the application owns, and not while a modal draft
  dialog owns input. Other shortcuts and the normal key() behavior are unchanged.
- **`fn key_peek(&mut self, vk: u32) -> bool`**
  ui_key_peek(vk): pressed this frame and unconsumed (not consumed)
- **`fn key_down(&self, vk: u32) -> bool`**
  ui_key_down(vk): held (mods: VK_SHIFT / VK_CONTROL / VK_MENU)
- **`fn key_first(&self) -> u32`**
  ui_key_first: the first unconsumed key this frame (VK | mods &lt;&lt; 16) or 0
- **`fn text_take(&mut self, cap: usize) -> Vec<u32>`**
  ui_text_take(cap): the typed codepoints (at most cap), consumed
- **`fn hit_flags_at(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32) -> u32`**
  ui_hit_flags_at(x, y): HB_\* flags of the topmost hit box at a point of the main
  window (previous pass) -- non-client hit testing of the platform
- **`fn hit(&mut self, id: u32, hbflags: u32)`**
  ui_hit(id, hbflags): register last_rect (clipped) as a hit box of the current layer
- **`fn behave(&mut self, id: u32, hbflags: u32) -> u32`**
  ui_behave(id, hbflags) -> EV_\*: the standard pointer / keyboard behaviour of a
  widget occupying last_rect
- **`fn hit_rect(&mut self, id: u32, x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32, hbflags: u32) -> u32`**
  ui_hit_rect(id, x, y, w, h, hbflags) -> EV_\*: behave on an explicit rectangle (px)
- **`fn focus_ring(&self, ev: u32) -> bool`**
  ui_focus_ring(ev): the focus visual should be drawn for the widget that just
  called behave (its events)
- **`fn slot(&mut self, id: u32) -> &mut Slot`**
  ui_slot(id): persistent per-id state (created on first use)
- **`fn anim(&mut self, id: u32, target: u32, ms: u32) -> u32`**
  ui_anim(id, target, ms) -> current value (0..256) moving towards target
- **`fn cmd_run(&mut self, id: u32, arg: u32)`**
  ui_cmd_run(id, arg): queue a command for App::command after this frame
- **`fn timer(&mut self, id: u32, ms: u32)`**
  ui_timer(id, ms): App::command(UIC_TIMER, id) after ms (replaces a timer with the
  same id)
- **`fn last_reset(&mut self)`**
  g_last_rect = 0, g_last_state = 0: the apps' hidden probe records (PaintCraft's \*\_sem
  helpers zero both before ui_sem; later records of the pass see the zeroes too)  // pn-core
- **`fn last_set(&mut self, rect: [i32; 4], state: u32)`**
  g_last_rect = rect, g_last_state = state (apps that build a widget from drawing calls
  set the record's rectangle and state before ui_sem)  // pn-core
- **`fn last_state(&self) -> u32`**
  g_last_state: the SS_\* state of the last behave  // pn-core
- **`fn mbtn(&self) -> u32`**
  g_mbtn / g_pressed / g_released: mouse buttons held / pressed / released this frame
  (bit 1 &lt;&lt; BTN_\*)  // pn-core
- **`fn pressed(&self) -> u32`**
- **`fn released(&self) -> u32`**
- **`fn sem(&mut self, kind: u16, id: u32, label: Option<&str>, value: i32) -> &mut Sem`**
  ui_sem(kind, id, label, value) -> the record (rect = last_rect, state from the last
  behave); the label is copied
- **`fn clip_push(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32)`**
  ui_clip_push(x, y, w, h): px; intersects drawing and hit clipping
- **`fn clip_pop(&mut self)`**
  ui_clip_pop
- **`fn layer_set(&mut self, layer: u32) -> u32`**
  ui_layer_set(layer) -> previous: switch the command layer and hit layer together
- **`fn begin_pass(&mut self, w: i32, h: i32)`**
  ui_begin_pass(w, h): start recording a frame for a w x h px window (one window)
- **`fn end_pass(&mut self) -> bool`**
  ui_end_pass -> another pass is needed (one window)
- **`fn repass(&mut self)`**
  ui_repass: request another pass before this frame is shown
- **`fn modal(&mut self, layer: u32)`**
  ui_modal(layer): a modal element (dialog) is open on `layer` this pass
- **`fn modal_active(&self) -> u32`**
  ui_modal_active -> layer of the modal element of the last pass (0 = none)
- **`fn disabled_push(&mut self)`**
  ui_disabled_push / ui_disabled_pop: widgets in between are disabled
- **`fn disabled_pop(&mut self)`**
- **`fn warn(&mut self, msg: &str)`**
  ui_warn: "UIWARN f&lt;frame>: &lt;msg>" on stderr

### Widgets

Source: [`lib/ui/basic.rs`](../lib/ui/basic.rs)

```text
basic -- the basic widgets                                           [src/ui/ui_basic.s]

  text(style, s)                         TS_* | TX_* flags (TX_ELLIPSIS: cut with "…" at
                                         the placed width, record value 1 when cut;
                                         TX_WRAP: lines at the layout width, value = lines)
  button(id, label) -> EV                standard button
  accent_button(id, label) -> EV         accent (primary) button
  icon_button(id, icon, label) -> EV     subtle toolbar button (label = tooltip / name;
                                         ST_LARGE shows it under a big icon)
  toggle_button(id, label, &mut v) -> EV checked look while v != 0; toggles v
  tool_button(id, icon, label, &mut sel, mine) -> EV   selects sel = mine (tool palettes)
  checkbox(id, label, &mut v) -> EV      v: 0, 1, 2 = indeterminate
  radio(id, label, &mut v, mine) -> EV   v = mine when clicked
  switch(id, label, &mut v) -> EV        toggle switch
  slider(id, &mut v, &range) -> EV       EV_ACT while the value changes, EV_COMMIT on release
  progress(id, v, max)                   progress bar
  separator()                            divider line across the current box
  icon(cp, size_dip)                     an icon in the text colour
  image(id, &sf, ver) -> EV              a surface at its natural size (or next_size)
  canvas(id, draw, user, version) -> &UiInput   custom area: draw.draw(g, rect) at render
                                         time, clipped; bump `version` when its content
                                         changes
  hair() -> px                           border width for the current dpi
Label strings are copied, so they may live in scratch buffers.
```

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn hair(&self) -> i32`**
  ui_hair: max(1, (dpi + 24) / 96)
- **`fn text(&mut self, style: u32, s: &str)`**
  ui_text(style, s): a text in a text style (TS_\* | TX_\*)
- **`fn button(&mut self, id: u32, label: &str) -> u32`**
  ui_button(id, label) -> EV_\* (EV_ACT on click)
- **`fn label_button(&mut self, id: u32, label: &str, typography: u32, inset: i32) -> u32`**
  A focusable text action with explicit TS_\*/TX_\* typography and horizontal inset
  in DIP. Uses the style's button surface and ordinary button semantics. Long labels
  are ellipsized visually; accessibility and agent inspection retain the full label.
- **`fn link(&mut self, id: u32, label: &str, typography: u32) -> u32`**
  A hyperlink-style text action: accent-coloured text (T_ACCENT_TEXT), underlined while
  the pointer is over it, hand cursor, focus ring, no button surface.  TS_\*/TX_\*
  typography as in text(); a narrower slot than the text ellipsizes it.
- **`fn accent_button(&mut self, id: u32, label: &str) -> u32`**
  ui_accent_button(id, label) -> EV_\*: the accent (primary) button
- **`fn icon_button(&mut self, id: u32, icon: u32, label: &str) -> u32`**
  ui_icon_button(id, icon, label) -> EV_\*: subtle toolbar button
- **`fn toggle_button(&mut self, id: u32, label: &str, v: &mut i32) -> u32`**
  ui_toggle_button(id, label, &mut v) -> EV_\*: checked look while v != 0; toggles v
- **`fn tool_button(&mut self, id: u32, icon: u32, label: &str, sel: &mut i32, mine: i32) -> u32`**
  ui_tool_button(id, icon, label, &mut sel, mine) -> EV_\*: subtle icon button, checked
  when sel == mine
- **`fn checkbox(&mut self, id: u32, label: &str, v: &mut i32) -> u32`**
  ui_checkbox(id, label, &mut v) -> EV_\*: v 0, 1, 2 = indeterminate
- **`fn radio(&mut self, id: u32, label: &str, v: &mut i32, mine: i32) -> u32`**
  ui_radio(id, label, &mut v, mine) -> EV_\*: v = mine when clicked
- **`fn switch(&mut self, id: u32, label: &str, v: &mut i32) -> u32`**
  ui_switch(id, label, &mut v) -> EV_\*: toggle switch
- **`fn slider(&mut self, id: u32, v: &mut i32, rg: &Range) -> u32`**
  ui_slider(id, &mut v, &range) -> EV_\*: EV_ACT while the value changes, EV_COMMIT on
  release
- **`fn progress(&mut self, id: u32, v: i32, max: i32)`**
  ui_progress(id, v, max): progress bar
- **`fn separator(&mut self)`**
  ui_separator: divider across the current box (the style draws it)
- **`fn icon(&mut self, cp: u32, size: i32)`**
  ui_icon(cp, size_dip): an icon in the text colour
- **`fn image(&mut self, id: u32, sf: &Rc<Surface>, ver: u32) -> u32`**
  ui_image(id, sf, ver) -> EV_\* (clickable when id != 0): a surface at its natural size
  (or next_size); ver stands for its SF_AUX version
- **`fn canvas(&mut self, id: u32, draw: Option<DrawRef>, user: usize, version: u32) -> &UiInput`**
  ui_canvas(id, draw, user, version) -> &UiInput: a custom area.  The hit box wants
  every pointer move; the cursor comes from next_flags bits 12..15 (CUR_\* &lt;&lt;
  HB_CURSOR_SHIFT), default crosshair.

### Popups, menus, command table, tooltips

Source: [`lib/ui/popup.rs`](../lib/ui/popup.rs)

```text
popup -- popups, flyouts, menus, menu bar, command table and accelerators, drop-down / split buttons, combo boxes, tooltips
                                                                 [src/ui/ui_popup.s]

Popups (a stack of up to 6 levels per window, drawn on L_POPUP):
  popup_open(id)                      open popup `id` (inside a flyout: as its child)
  popup_close()                       close every popup; the focus returns to the opener
  popup_is_open(id) -> bool   popup_count() -> n
  flyout_begin(id, FL_*) -> bool      draw popup `id` when it is open: a flyout surface
                                      anchored to the last widget, content box BOX_V|PAD_4
  flyout_end()                        always paired with flyout_begin
  A press outside every popup closes them all and is consumed; Esc closes the top one;
  Up / Down move the focus inside the top one.
  popup_ext(i) / popup_rect(i) -> rect (U3: popups that leave a secondary window)
Commands (the table AppDesc::cmds, Command records):
  cmd_find(id) -> &Command   cmd_state(id, CF_*)   cmd_flags(id) -> CF_*
  cmd_label(id) -> label without '&'   accel_text(id) -> "Strg+Umschalt+S"
  accel_order_set(1): "Strg+Alt+Umschalt+S" (ui_accel_order)
  cmd_button(id, ST_*) -> EV          button from the table (icon, label, tooltip with
                                      the accelerator, disabled / checked); queues it
Menus (Mi records; Mi::List rows come from menu_list(base, rows), row k = id base + k; a
checked row with an icon gives the flyout a check column and an icon column):
  menubar(None | Some(bar)) -> EV_OPEN   WinUI MenuBar (None = AppDesc::menubar): items,
                                      flyouts, submenus, keys (Alt / F10, Alt+mnemonic,
                                      arrows, Enter, Esc)
  menu_popup(menu)                    open a menu at the pointer (drawn by the runtime)
  context_menu(menu) -> EV_OPEN       post-modifier: right click / menu key / Shift+F10 on
                                      the last widget opens `menu`
  context_target() -> id              the widget whose context menu is open
  menu_item(id, label) -> EV          row inside a flyout (next_icon, next_flags
                                      ST_DISABLED / ST_CHECKED); activation closes popups
  menu_cmd(cmd) -> EV   menu_sep()    a row for a command (queues it), a separator
  menu_list(base, rows)               the rows of the Mi::List with that base (the asm's
                                      app-owned STRLIST buffer)
Drop-down controls:
  dropdown_button(id, label) -> EV    button with a chevron; opens popup `id` (EV_OPEN)
  split_button(id, label) -> EV       EV_ACT from the main part; the chevron part
                                      (id | 0x80000000) opens popup `id`
  combobox(id, &mut sel, list) -> EV  EV_ACT when sel changed, EV_OPEN while open
  (drop-downs open with Alt+Down, and with F4 unless the command table has an F4 accelerator)
Tooltips:
  tooltip(s)                          post-modifier: tooltip for the last widget after
                                      500 ms of hover (timer UIT_TOOLTIP wakes the loop)
Runtime: accel_dispatch() at the start of uiw_pass_end: runtime-drawn menus, stale
popups, command accelerators.

Ids: a menu's popup id is a hash of the menu's address (menu_pid), the menu bar's item
ids a hash of the bar's address -- like the asm, whose dumps therefore show other
numbers for them.
```

- **`struct Command`**
  CMD record: one row of the app's command table (AppDesc::cmds)
  - `id: u32` -- command id (!= 0); also the widget id of cmd_button
  - `icon: u32` -- IC_\* code point or 0
  - `label: &'static str` -- UTF-8 label, '&' marks the mnemonic ("&&" = '&')
  - `key: u16` -- accelerator key VK_\* (0 = none)
  - `mods: u8` -- MOD_SHIFT | MOD_CTRL | MOD_ALT
  - `flags: u8` -- initial CF_\* state

**`impl Command`**

- **`const fn new(id: u32, label: &'static str, icon: u32, mods: u32, key: u32, flags: u32) -> Command`**
  CMD id, "&Label", icon, mods, key, flags (the order of the asm macro)

- **`enum Mi`**
  menu record (MENUBAR / MENU / MI_\* of ui.inc); menus are `static` slices of them
  - `Cmd(u32)` -- MI_CMD cmd: a row from the command table
  - `Sep` -- MI_SEP
  - `Sub(&'static str, &'static [Mi])` -- MI_SUB "Label", submenu
  - `Menu(&'static str, &'static [Mi])` -- MENU "Label", menu: a top-level menu of a menu bar
  - `Item(u32, &'static str, u32)` -- MI_ITEM id, "Label", icon: a plain row, runs App::command(id, 0)
  - `List(u32, u16, &'static str)` -- MI_LIST base, max, "placeholder": one row per string of the rows set with
    Ui::menu_list(base, ..) (at most max); row k runs App::command(base + k, 0) and has
    the widget id base + k; no rows: the placeholder as a disabled row ("" = none)  (U1)

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn popup_is_open(&self, id: u32) -> bool`**
  ui_popup_is_open(id): popup `id` is open
- **`fn popup_count(&self) -> u32`**
  ui_popup_count -> open popups of the live window (U3)
- **`fn popup_open(&mut self, id: u32)`**
  ui_popup_open(id): open popup `id` (inside a flyout: as a child of that flyout,
  otherwise it replaces every open popup); an open popup only loses its children
- **`fn popup_close(&mut self)`**
  ui_popup_close: close every popup (the focus returns to where it was)
- **`fn flyout_begin(&mut self, id: u32, flags: u32) -> bool`**
  ui_flyout_begin(id, FL_\*) -> popup `id` is open: then its content follows (a vertical
  box with 4 DIP padding) until flyout_end, which is called in any case.  The flyout is
  anchored to the last placed widget (FL_POINT: to the pointer position where it
  opened) and kept inside the window.
- **`fn popup_ext(&self, i: u32) -> Option<[i32; 4]>`**
  ui_popup_ext(i) -> rect x, y, w, h (px, window coordinates) of item i of the live
  window when it was drawn in the last pass partly outside its framebuffer.  Items
  0 .. 5 are the open popups, 6 the tooltip.  (U3, OS popup windows)
- **`fn popup_rect(&self, i: u32) -> Option<[i32; 4]>`**
  ui_popup_rect(i) -> rect (px) of item i of the live window when it was drawn in the
  last pass (anywhere).  The main window gives its popups own OS windows with it while
  tool windows are shown, so menus stay above them.
- **`fn flyout_end(&mut self)`**
  ui_flyout_end: close the content of flyout_begin (also when it returned false)
- **`fn cmd_find(&self, id: u32) -> Option<&'static Command>`**
  ui_cmd_find(id) -> the command record of the app's command table
- **`fn cmd_state(&mut self, id: u32, flags: u32)`**
  ui_cmd_state(id, flags): set the CF_\* state of a command; it stays until changed
  (calling it every frame with the same flags is fine) and overrides the table flags.
  Up to 1536 commands have a state; more print one UIWARN and are ignored.
- **`fn cmd_flags(&self, id: u32) -> u32`**
  ui_cmd_flags(id) -> CF_\* state (cmd_state, else the table flags)
- **`fn cmd_label(&self, id: u32) -> String`**
  ui_cmd_label(id) -> label of a command without '&' ("" if unknown)
- **`fn accel_text(&self, id: u32) -> String`**
  ui_accel_text(id) -> accelerator of a command as text, e.g. "Strg+Umschalt+S"
- **`fn accel_order_set(&mut self, order: u32)`**
  ui_accel_order: 0 = "Strg+Umschalt+Alt+X" (default), 1 = "Strg+Alt+Umschalt+X"  (U1)
- **`fn cmd_button(&mut self, id: u32, flags: u32) -> u32`**
  ui_cmd_button(id, ST_\*) -> EV_\*: button for a command: icon and label from the table
  (ST_ICON_ONLY: icon only), tooltip "Label (Strg+S)", ST_DISABLED / ST_CHECKED from the
  command state; a click queues the command.  Hidden commands draw nothing.
- **`fn menu_item(&mut self, id: u32, label: &str) -> u32`**
  ui_menu_item(id, label) -> EV_\*: menu row inside a flyout (next_icon; next_flags
  ST_DISABLED / ST_CHECKED); activation closes every popup
- **`fn menu_cmd(&mut self, cmd: u32) -> u32`**
  ui_menu_cmd(cmd) -> EV_\*: menu row for a command (widget id = command id)
- **`fn menu_sep(&mut self) -> u32`**
  ui_menu_sep -> 0: separator line across the flyout
- **`fn menu_list<T: AsRef<str>>(&mut self, base: u32, rows: &[T])`**
  the rows of the Mi::List menus with this base id (row k: id base + k; the asm app's
  STRLIST buffer); the list ends at the first empty string
- **`fn menubar(&mut self, menubar: Option<&'static [Mi]>) -> u32`**
  ui_menubar(menubar) -> EV_OPEN while one of its menus is open (None = the one of
  AppDesc::menubar).  WinUI MenuBar: subtle 32 DIP items, a flyout per menu.
  Keyboard: Alt (tap) or F10 enter / leave menu bar mode, Alt+mnemonic opens a menu,
  Left / Right move between the menus, Down / Enter open one, Esc steps back.
- **`fn menu_popup(&mut self, menu: &'static [Mi])`**
  ui_menu_popup(menu): open a menu at the pointer (the runtime draws it)
- **`fn context_menu(&mut self, menu: &'static [Mi]) -> u32`**
  ui_context_menu(menu) -> EV_OPEN while `menu` is open for the last widget.  Opens on a
  right click (released over the widget), or with the menu key / Shift+F10 while the
  widget has the focus (then at its lower left corner, first row focused).
- **`fn context_target(&self) -> u32`**
  ui_context_target -> id of the widget whose context menu is (or was last) open
- **`fn dropdown_button(&mut self, id: u32, label: &str) -> u32`**
  ui_dropdown_button(id, label) -> EV_\* | EV_OPEN while popup `id` is open (a click,
  Enter / Space, Alt+Down or F4 opens it; the app draws the flyout with
  flyout_begin(id, ..) right after).  next_flags ST_SUBTLE: flat tool bar look,
  ST_ICON_ONLY: icon + chevron, the label as tooltip (U1)
- **`fn split_button(&mut self, id: u32, label: &str) -> u32`**
  ui_split_button(id, label) -> EV_\* of the main part | EV_OPEN while popup `id` is
  open.  The chevron part (widget id | 0x80000000; Alt+Down / F4 on the main part)
  opens it.
- **`fn combobox(&mut self, id: u32, sel: &mut i32, list: &[&str]) -> u32`**
  ui_combobox(id, &mut sel, list) -> EV_\*: EV_ACT when sel changed, EV_OPEN while the
  list is open.  WinUI ComboBox: 32 DIP, control fill, chevron; the list opens over the
  box with the selected row (accent pill) on it.  Keyboard: Up / Down change the
  selection, Enter / Space / Alt+Down / F4 open the list.  `list` is a STRLIST (it ends
  at the first empty string).
- **`fn tooltip(&mut self, s: &str)`**
  ui_tooltip(s): post-modifier: show s as the tooltip of the last widget after 500 ms of
  hover (at once when another tooltip hid less than 300 ms ago); hidden on press, key or
  leave.  Icon-only buttons call it with their label.

### Text boxes and the clipboard

Source: [`lib/ui/edit.rs`](../lib/ui/edit.rs)

```text
edit -- text input: single-line and multi-line text boxes, number boxes, the clipboard for
widgets                                                             [src/ui/ui_edit.s]

  textbox(id, placeholder, &mut buf, cap_flags) -> EV
       WinUI TextBox (32 DIP high).  buf: the app's UTF-8 text; cap_flags = the size of
       the asm's buffer in bytes incl. the NUL (bits 0..23: the text holds at most cap - 1
       bytes) | TB_PASSWORD | TB_READONLY | TB_DIGITS | TB_SIGNED.  placeholder (or None)
       shows while the text is empty.  ST_DISABLED via next_flags.
       EV_ACT: the text changed this frame; EV_COMMIT: Enter, Esc or focus lost; EV_FOCUS
       while it has the keyboard focus.
       Semantic record K_TEXTBOX: label = the text (one dot per character for passwords),
       value = caret, max = selection anchor (byte indices; 0 while not focused).
       TB_MULTI (U1): several lines (natural height 5 lines): Enter inserts a line break,
       Ctrl+Enter commits, Up / Down keep the preferred x, PgUp / PgDn move a view, Home /
       End work on the line (Ctrl: the text), the wheel and the caret scroll it vertically,
       a thin bar shows the position; TB_WRAP wraps at the width (else it scrolls
       horizontally).  Pasted text keeps its lines.
  number_box(id, &mut v, &range) -> EV
       an integer in a text box: digits only ('-' too when the range minimum is negative);
       Up / Down add -/+ step; typed text is taken on Enter or focus loss; the value is
       clamped to the range.  EV_ACT when v changed, EV_COMMIT on Enter / focus loss.
       Semantic value = v, max = range maximum.  PgUp / PgDn: 10 steps.
  number_box_f(id, &mut v, &range_f) -> EV   (U1)
       an f64 in a text box: shown with the range's decimals and decimal_sep; ',' and '.'
       are both accepted; Up / Down = one step (step 0: 1 % of the range), PgUp / PgDn =
       10 steps; clamped, snapped and rounded like the slider.  Semantic value =
       v * 10^decimals.
  (U2) both number boxes follow v while they have the focus as long as the user has not
       edited their text; Enter / focus loss only store the text when it was edited.
  nb_live(id, &mut v) / nb_live_f(id, &mut v) -> bool   the number typed so far into box
       id (being edited) is stored when it differs from v (clamped / fixed to the range),
       without reformatting the text -- property rows preview while the user types
  open_folder(path) / open_url(url) -> 0 / -1   (U2) headless runs only print them
  clip_set(fmt, data) -> 0 / -1, clip_get(fmt) -> data   the clipboard (headless runs:
       an in-process clipboard, so scripts are deterministic)
  text_key_filter(k) (crate)  the keys the focused text box consumes are hidden from key()

Keys: Left/Right (+Ctrl words, +Shift select), Home/End, Backspace/Delete (+Ctrl words),
Ctrl+A/C/X/V, Ctrl+Z undo, Ctrl+Y / Ctrl+Shift+Z redo, Shift+Delete cut, Ctrl+Insert copy,
Shift+Insert paste, Enter commits, Esc drops the focus.  Plain character keys are consumed
(their text arrives as typed text).  Tab, Up/Down/PgUp/PgDn, F-keys, Alt chords and other
Ctrl chords stay for the app.
Mouse: click places the caret (Shift+click extends), drag selects, double click selects a
word.  The caret blinks every 530 ms (timer UIT_CARET; always visible in headless runs).

Only one text box has the keyboard focus, so the edit state (caret, anchor, undo history)
is one block that belongs to the focused box (ed_*); the horizontal / vertical scroll
offsets are kept per box in its state slot.  The asm edits the app's NUL-terminated buffer
in place; here the box's text is moved into S::buf for the call (byte for byte the asm's
buffer up to its NUL) and handed back as a String.

Deviation (Rust ownership): when another number box takes the edit buffer, the asm stores
the previous owner's edited text into that owner's value right away (through the `&v` it
kept); here that store waits for the owner's next number_box call (S::pend_text), which
runs before the owner reads its value.  The events it reports and the settled frame are the
same; an owner that is never drawn again keeps its old value.
```

- **`struct TextboxPresentation`**
  Opt-in presentation for a multiline editor embedded in document content. Geometry is in
  physical pixels; callers scale it with the document zoom and DPI. Editing, clipboard,
  selection, accessibility and IME behavior remain the ordinary textbox implementation.
  - `font: u32` -- Font identifier registered in the UI text engine.
  - `size: i32` -- Font size in 24.8 physical pixels, clamped to 1..512 pixels.
  - `line_height: i32` -- Line advance in physical pixels, clamped to 1..2048.
  - `baseline: i32` -- Baseline offset from a line's top, clamped to the line height.
  - `color: u32` -- Text and caret color, straight ARGB.
  - `background: u32` -- Solid editor background, straight ARGB.
  - `underline: bool` -- Draw an underline beneath every text run.

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn textbox_selection(&self, id: u32) -> Option<(usize, usize)>`**
  Read the focused textbox's UTF-8 byte selection (anchor, caret), after drawing it.
  Opt-in API for document editors; ordinary textbox behavior is unchanged.
- **`fn textbox_select(&mut self, id: u32, text: &str, anchor: usize, caret: usize) -> bool`**
  Select valid UTF-8 byte boundaries in an already focused/drawn textbox. Pass the
  same current text used by textbox(); returns false without changes for invalid ranges.
- **`fn textbox(&mut self, id: u32, placeholder: Option<&str>, buf: &mut String, cap_flags: u32) -> u32`**
  ui_textbox(id, placeholder, buf, cap_flags) -> EV_\* (see the module header)
- **`fn textbox_icon(&mut self, id: u32, icon: u32, placeholder: Option<&str>, buf: &mut String, cap_flags: u32) -> u32`**
  Single-line text input with a leading semantic icon. The icon reserves its own
  inset, so pointer selection, caret scrolling and IME share the visible text origin.
  Multiline inputs ignore the icon and retain their ordinary presentation.
- **`fn textbox_presented(&mut self, id: u32, placeholder: Option<&str>, buf: &mut String, cap_flags: u32, mut presentation: TextboxPresentation) -> u32`**
  Edit multiline text on a caller-provided surface without the style's textbox frame.
  Uses zero text insets and the supplied font, line metrics and colors. The supplied
  presentation applies only to this call; ordinary textboxes keep their default style.
  `TB_MULTI` is implicit; other capacity and textbox flags retain their usual meaning.
- **`fn ime_pos(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32, h: i32)`**
- **`fn clip_set_many(&mut self, entries: &[(u32, &[u8])]) -> i32`**
  Publish distinct TEXT/HTML/RTF/DOCX representations of one selection (64 MiB total).
  Validation precedes replacement; 0 means every representation was published.
- **`fn clip_set(&mut self, fmt: u32, data: &[u8]) -> i32`**
  Replace the clipboard with one text, image or rich representation.
- **`fn clip_get(&mut self, fmt: u32) -> Option<Vec<u8>>`**
  ui_clip_get(fmt) -> the clipboard data (text: UTF-8) or None
- **`fn open_folder(&mut self, path: &str) -> i32`**
  ui_open_folder(path) -> 0 / -1: (U2) show a folder (or a file selected in its folder)
  in the system file manager; headless runs start nothing: they print
  "ui_open_folder: PATH" and return 0
- **`fn open_url(&mut self, url: &str) -> i32`**
  ui_open_url(url) -> 0 / -1: a web page / mailto link in the default application;
  headless runs print "ui_open_url: URL" and return 0
- **`fn number_box(&mut self, id: u32, v: &mut i32, rg: &Range) -> u32`**
  ui_number_box(id, &mut v, &range) -> EV_\* (see the module header)
- **`fn number_box_f(&mut self, id: u32, v: &mut f64, rf: &RangeF) -> u32`**
  ui_number_box_f(id, &mut v, &range_f) -> EV_\* (U1; see the module header)
- **`fn nb_live(&mut self, id: u32, v: &mut i32) -> bool`**
  nb_live(id, &mut v) -> true when number box `id` is being edited and the text typed
  so far is a number different from v: the value (clamped to the range) is stored
  without reformatting the text -- property rows preview while the user types
- **`fn nb_live_f(&mut self, id: u32, v: &mut f64) -> bool`**
  nb_live_f(id, &mut v) -> nb_live of an f64 number box (fixed to its RangeF)

### Lists, scrolling, tabs, expanders

Source: [`lib/ui/list.rs`](../lib/ui/list.rs)

```text
list -- scroll views, scroll bars, lists, tabs and expanders (WinUI 3 style)
                                                                 [src/ui/ui_list.s]

  scroll_begin(id, flags)                clipped viewport that takes its size like a box
       (next_size / parent) and lays its children out in a content area that may be
       larger; flags = SC_V (default) | SC_H | SC_NOBAR | SC_NOWHEEL | BOX_BG | BOX_CARD |
       PAD_n ...  Mouse wheel (48 DIP per notch, smooth), overlay scroll bars (2 DIP, 6 DIP
       while pointed at, draggable thumb, click on the track pages), Tab focus is scrolled
       into view.
  scroll_end()
  scroll_to(id, x, y)                    jump to a position in DIP (-1 keeps that axis)
  scroll_pos(id) -> (x, y)               current position in DIP
  scroll_extent(id) -> (w, h)            content size in DIP (last measure; 0 before) (U3)
  scrollbar(id, &mut pos, content, view, vertical) -> EV
       standalone scroll bar (natural thickness 12 DIP) for custom canvases; pos runs from
       0 to content - view in the caller's unit; EV_ACT when pos changed
  list_begin(id, &mut sel, count, item_h | LF_*) -> (first, end)
       virtualized list (own scroll view, fixed row height in DIP, 0 = 40); emit the rows
       first .. end-1; row i has the widget id `id + 1 + i`.  Up/Down/Home/End/PgUp/PgDn
       move the selection (sel, -1 = none) and scroll it into view.
       LF_REORDER (U1): a row dragged more than 4 DIP is moved: an accent insertion line
       follows the pointer, the view scrolls near its edges, Esc cancels; on release
       list_end returns EV_DRAG with from | to << 16 (the app moves item `from` so that it
       ends at index `to`) and sel = to.  Widgets inside custom rows keep their clicks.
  list_item(i, label) -> EV              text row (EV_ACT clicked, EV_DOUBLE, EV_RCLICK)
  list_item_begin(i) -> EV               custom row: opens a BOX_H inside the row
  list_item_end()
  list_end(&mut sel) -> (EV, sel)        EV_ACT if sel changed this pass, EV_DOUBLE;
                                         EV_DRAG: a row was dropped (from | to << 16)
       (Rust: list_begin reads the selection into the list, list_end writes it back --
       the asm keeps a pointer to the app's variable in between; list_sel() reads it in
       the rows)
  tabs(id, &mut sel, names) -> EV        text tab header row, tab i has the id
                                         `id + 1 + i`; EV_ACT when sel changed
  expander_begin(id, header, &mut open) -> open   (emit the content when 1)
  expander_end()

Widget ids used internally: (id << 3 | k) | 0x80000000 for sub parts (k = 1..7),
(id + 1 + i) | 0xC0000000 for the content box of custom list rows.
```

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn scroll_begin(&mut self, id: u32, flags: u32)`**
  ui_scroll_begin(id, flags)
- **`fn scroll_end(&mut self)`**
  ui_scroll_end
- **`fn scroll_to(&mut self, id: u32, x: i32, y: i32)`**
  ui_scroll_to(id, x, y): DIP, -1 keeps that axis; jumps without animation
- **`fn scroll_pos(&mut self, id: u32) -> (i32, i32)`**
  ui_scroll_pos(id) -> (x, y) DIP
- **`fn scroll_extent(&mut self, id: u32) -> (i32, i32)`**
  ui_scroll_extent(id) -> (w, h): measured content size of scroll view `id` (DIP; 0
  before its first pass).  With the view size (last_rect after scroll_end) the app can
  tell whether the content overflows.  (U3)
- **`fn scrollbar(&mut self, id: u32, pos: &mut i32, content: i32, view: i32, vertical: bool) -> u32`**
  ui_scrollbar(id, &mut pos, content, view, vertical) -> EV_\*
- **`fn list_begin(&mut self, id: u32, sel: &mut i32, count: i32, item_h: u32) -> (i32, i32)`**
  ui_list_begin(id, &mut sel, count, item_h | LF_\*) -> (first, end): the rows to emit
- **`fn list_item(&mut self, i: i32, label: &str) -> u32`**
  ui_list_item(i, label) -> EV_\*
- **`fn list_item_begin(&mut self, i: i32) -> u32`**
  ui_list_item_begin(i) -> EV_\*: row i with a BOX_H (gap 8 DIP) for custom content
- **`fn list_sel(&self) -> i32`**
  the innermost list's selection while its rows are emitted (the asm's rows see the
  app's variable change at once; here the list holds it until list_end), -1 outside a
  list
- **`fn list_item_end(&mut self)`**
  ui_list_item_end
- **`fn list_end(&mut self, sel: &mut i32) -> (u32, i32)`**
  ui_list_end(&mut sel) -> (EV_\* (EV_ACT if sel changed, EV_DOUBLE), sel); EV_DRAG: a
  row was dropped, the second value is from | to &lt;&lt; 16
- **`fn tabs_scrolling(&mut self, id: u32, sel: &mut i32, names: &[&str]) -> u32`**
  Tab strip with horizontal overflow arrows. Mouse scrolling and Left/Right,
  Home/End on the strip retain ordinary tab semantics; changed selections
  and resized viewports reveal the selected tab. Internal IDs follow the
  usual `(id * 8 + part) | 0x80000000` convention (parts 1, 2, 3 and 7).
- **`fn tabs(&mut self, id: u32, sel: &mut i32, names: &[&str]) -> u32`**
  ui_tabs(id, &mut sel, names) -> EV_\* (EV_ACT when sel changed)
- **`fn expander_begin(&mut self, id: u32, header: &str, open: &mut i32) -> i32`**
  ui_expander_begin(id, header, &mut open) -> open: a card-like header (48 DIP) with a
  chevron; when open the content follows in a padded card below it (BOX_V, PAD_16, gap
  8 DIP).  Always call expander_end.
- **`fn expander_end(&mut self)`**
  ui_expander_end

### Data views: grid, tree, badges, meters

Source: [`lib/ui/data.rs`](../lib/ui/data.rs)

```text
data -- data views: grid (table with columns), tree, badges, status dots, meters

  grid_begin(id, &mut st, cols, keys) -> (first, end)
       a virtualized table (fills its parent unless next_size says otherwise): a fixed
       header (click: sort by the column, again: descending;
       drag a column's right edge: its width, double click there: the default width) and
       the rows below it in a vertical scroll view (row height and header height from
       StyleData::grid_metrics).  keys: one stable key per row in display order (the app
       sorts and filters; the selection is kept by key, so it survives refreshes).
       Emit rows first .. end-1: grid_row(&mut st, i), then per column grid_cell(&st, c)
       and any widget (it is placed into the cell), or grid_text(&st, c, s).
       Mouse: click selects, Ctrl+click toggles, Shift+click selects a range, double click
       activates, right click selects the row and reports it.  Keys (grid focused): Up /
       Down / PgUp / PgDn / Home / End move the current row (Shift: extend), Ctrl+A all,
       Space toggles the current row, Enter activates.
  grid_row(&mut st, i) -> EV        row i (widget id id + 1 + i)
  grid_cell(&st, c)                 the next widget goes into column c of the current row
  grid_text(&st, c, s)              text in column c (ellipsis, the column's alignment)
  grid_end(&mut st) -> GridEv       ev: EV_ACT selection changed, EV_DOUBLE a row was
                                    activated, EV_RCLICK context menu requested; row: that
                                    row's index; sort_changed: re-sort the keys
  tree(id, &mut st, rows, row_h) -> TreeEv
       a virtualized tree from the app's visible rows (TreeRow: key, depth, children,
       check state, icon, label, detail); the chevron / Right / Left expand and collapse
       (st.expanded; TreeEv::expanded names a newly opened node for lazy loading), the
       check box / Space report TreeEv::check (the app applies it, e.g. with cascade),
       double click / Enter TreeEv::activated
  badge(kind, label)                status pill (BADGE_NEUTRAL .. BADGE_ACCENT; next_icon)
  status_dot(kind)                  a small status dot (10 DIP)
  meter(id, v, max, kind)           a capacity bar (next_size sets its size)

Ids: the header box (id << 3) | 0x80000000, the header cells ((id ^ 0xA5A5) << 3 | k) |
0x80000000 with k = 1 + column (sort), the grips ((id ^ 0x5A5A) << 3 | k) | 0x80000000; the
rows' scroll view id (its bars (id << 3 | 1 / 2) | 0x80000000); rows id + 1 + i; tree chevrons / check boxes
(id + 1 + i) | 0x40000000 / | 0x20000000.
```

- **`struct GridCol<'a>`**
  a grid column: title, width and minimum width in DIP, GC_\* flags
  - `title: &'a str`
  - `width: i32`
  - `min: i32`
  - `flags: u32`

**`impl<'a> GridCol<'a>`**

- **`const fn new(title: &'a str, width: i32) -> GridCol<'a>`**
  a column `width` DIP wide (at least 48)
- **`const fn flags(mut self, flags: u32) -> GridCol<'a>`**
  the same column with GC_\* flags
- **`const fn min(mut self, min: i32) -> GridCol<'a>`**
  the same column with a minimum width (DIP)

- **`struct GridState`**
  the app-owned state of a grid: selection (by key), current row, sort, column widths
  - `sel: BTreeSet<u64>` -- the selected rows' keys
  - `cur: Option<u64>` -- the current row (keyboard focus / last clicked)
  - `sort: Option<(usize, bool)>` -- the sort column and direction (true = descending); None = the app's order
  - `widths: Vec<i32>` -- column widths set by the user (DIP, 0 = the column's own)
  - `striped: bool` -- alternate row shading

**`impl GridState`**

- **`fn selected_in_order(&self) -> Vec<u64>`**
  the keys selected, in display order of the last pass
- **`fn select_only(&mut self, key: Option<u64>)`**
  select exactly this key (None: nothing) and make it current
- **`fn retain(&mut self, keys: &[u64])`**
  drop selected keys that are no longer shown (after a refresh)

- **`struct GridEv`**
  what happened in a grid during the pass
  - `ev: u32` -- EV_ACT selection changed, EV_DOUBLE a row was activated, EV_RCLICK context requested
  - `row: i32` -- the activated / context row (-1 = none)
  - `sort_changed: bool` -- the sort column or direction changed

- **`struct TreeRow<'a>`**
  one visible row of a tree (the app flattens its model through TreeState::expanded)
  - `key: u64`
  - `depth: u16`
  - `has_children: bool`
  - `check: Option<u8>` -- check box state 0 / 1 / 2 (indeterminate); None = no check box
  - `icon: u32`
  - `label: &'a str`
  - `detail: Option<&'a str>` -- secondary text after the label (size, date)

- **`struct TreeState`**
  the app-owned state of a tree: expanded nodes, current node
  - `expanded: BTreeSet<u64>`
  - `cur: Option<u64>`
  - `marks: BTreeMap<u64, u32>` -- app data per node (free use, e.g. a loading flag)

- **`struct TreeEv`**
  what happened in a tree during the pass
  - `expanded: Option<u64>` -- a node opened in this pass (load its children)
  - `collapsed: Option<u64>` -- a node closed in this pass
  - `check: Option<(u64, u8)>` -- a check box was toggled: (key, new state 0 / 1)
  - `activated: Option<u64>` -- double click / Enter on a node
  - `sel_changed: bool` -- the current node changed

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn grid_begin(&mut self, id: u32, st: &mut GridState, cols: &[GridCol], keys: &[u64]) -> (i32, i32)`**
  ui_grid_begin(id, &mut st, cols, keys) -> (first, end): the rows to emit
- **`fn grid_row(&mut self, st: &mut GridState, i: i32) -> u32`**
  ui_grid_row(&mut st, i) -> EV_\* of row i (EV_ACT clicked)
- **`fn grid_cell(&mut self, st: &GridState, c: usize)`**
  ui_grid_cell(&st, c): the next widget is placed into column c of the current row
  (inset by the cell padding, full row height)
- **`fn grid_text(&mut self, st: &GridState, c: usize, s: &str)`**
  ui_grid_text(&st, c, s): body text in column c (cut with "…", the column's alignment)
- **`fn grid_text_ts(&mut self, st: &GridState, c: usize, ts: u32, s: &str)`**
  ui_grid_text_ts(&st, c, ts, s): text in column c with a text style (TS_\* | TX_\*)
- **`fn grid_end(&mut self, st: &mut GridState) -> GridEv`**
  ui_grid_end(&mut st) -> GridEv
- **`fn tree(&mut self, id: u32, st: &mut TreeState, rows: &[TreeRow], row_h: i32) -> TreeEv`**
  ui_tree(id, &mut st, rows, row_h) -> TreeEv: the visible rows of a tree (row_h DIP,
  0 = the grid row height)
- **`fn badge(&mut self, kind: u32, label: &str)`**
  ui_badge(kind, label): a status pill (BADGE_\*; next_icon puts an icon before the text)
- **`fn status_dot(&mut self, kind: u32)`**
  ui_status_dot(kind): a 10 DIP status dot (BADGE_\*)
- **`fn meter(&mut self, id: u32, v: i64, max: i64, kind: u32)`**
  ui_meter(id, v, max, kind): a capacity bar (natural 120 x 8 DIP; next_size)

### Shell parts: navigation, command bar, splitter, breadcrumb, wizard steps

Source: [`lib/ui/shell.rs`](../lib/ui/shell.rs)

```text
shell -- the parts of an application shell: navigation pane, command bar, splitter,
breadcrumb, segmented control, wizard step list

  nav_begin(id, &mut NavState)        vertical navigation pane (next_size sets its width;
                                      the style's surface), items scroll when they do not fit
  nav_toggle(id, label)               the pane's menu button: toggles NavState::compact
  nav_section(label)                  a group caption (compact: a divider)
  nav_item(id, icon, label, selected, badge) -> EV   EV_ACT when clicked; badge: a count
  nav_footer()                        the items after it stay at the bottom of the pane
  nav_end()
  command_bar(id, items) -> Option<id>   a row of subtle icon + label buttons; what does not
                                      fit goes into a "…" menu; CBI_END starts the group
                                      aligned to the end, CBI_SEP a separator; ST_ACCENT /
                                      ST_DISABLED / ST_CHECKED per item
  split_begin(id, pos, vertical)      two panes: the first `pos` DIP wide (vertical: high),
  split_next()                        a draggable grip, the second fills
  split_end(&mut pos) -> changed      the dragged position (DIP)
  breadcrumb(id, parts) -> Option<i>  path of links, the last part is the current page
  segmented(id, &mut sel, items) -> EV   a segmented control (EV_ACT when sel changed)
  wizard_steps(id, steps, current, states) -> Option<i>   vertical step list; a click on
                                      a done step (or one before the current) returns it

Ids: parts (id << 3 | k) | 0x80000000; items of segmented / wizard / breadcrumb id + 1 + i.
```

- **`struct NavState`**
  the app-owned state of a navigation pane
  - `compact: bool` -- icons only

- **`struct CmdItem<'a>`**
  an item of a command bar
  - `id: u32`
  - `icon: u32`
  - `label: &'a str`
  - `flags: u32` -- ST_ACCENT, ST_DISABLED, ST_CHECKED, CBI_SEP, CBI_END

**`impl<'a> CmdItem<'a>`**

- **`const fn new(id: u32, icon: u32, label: &'a str) -> CmdItem<'a>`**
- **`const fn flags(mut self, f: u32) -> CmdItem<'a>`**
  add flags (ST_ACCENT, CBI_SEP, ...; in any order with enabled())
- **`const fn enabled(mut self, on: bool) -> CmdItem<'a>`**
  disabled unless `on`

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn nav_begin(&mut self, id: u32, nav: &mut NavState)`**
  ui_nav_begin(id, &mut nav): open the pane (always call nav_end)
- **`fn nav_toggle(&mut self, id: u32, label: &str, nav: &mut NavState) -> u32`**
  ui_nav_toggle(id, label): the menu button of the pane (toggles compact)
- **`fn nav_section(&mut self, label: &str)`**
  ui_nav_section(label): a group caption (a divider when the pane is compact)
- **`fn nav_item(&mut self, id: u32, icon: u32, label: &str, selected: bool, badge: Option<&str>) -> u32`**
  ui_nav_item(id, icon, label, selected, badge) -> EV_\* (EV_ACT clicked)
- **`fn nav_footer(&mut self)`**
  ui_nav_footer: end the scrolling item list; the items after it stay at the bottom
- **`fn nav_end(&mut self)`**
  ui_nav_end
- **`fn command_bar(&mut self, id: u32, items: &[CmdItem]) -> Option<u32>`**
  ui_command_bar(id, items) -> the id of the item clicked in this pass
- **`fn split_begin(&mut self, id: u32, pos: i32, vertical: bool)`**
  ui_split_begin(id, pos, vertical): two panes, the first `pos` DIP wide (vertical:
  high); emit the first pane, split_next, the second pane, split_end
- **`fn split_next(&mut self)`**
  ui_split_next: the grip, then the second pane
- **`fn split_end(&mut self, pos: &mut i32) -> bool`**
  ui_split_end(&mut pos) -> the grip moved (pos updated, DIP)
- **`fn breadcrumb(&mut self, id: u32, parts: &[&str]) -> Option<usize>`**
  ui_breadcrumb(id, parts) -> the index of a clicked part (the last one is the current
  page and not a link)
- **`fn segmented(&mut self, id: u32, sel: &mut i32, items: &[&str]) -> u32`**
  ui_segmented(id, &mut sel, items) -> EV_\* (EV_ACT when sel changed)
- **`fn wizard_steps(&mut self, id: u32, steps: &[&str], current: usize, states: &[i32]) -> Option<usize>`**
  ui_wizard_steps(id, steps, current, states) -> a step to go back to (clicked);
  states: WS_\* per step (missing: done before the current, todo after it)
- **`fn page_header(&mut self, title: &str, subtitle: &str)`**
  ui_page_header(title, subtitle): a page title (TS_TITLE) with an optional secondary
  line under it
- **`fn key_value(&mut self, key: &str, value: &str, key_width: i32)`**
  ui_key_value(key, value, key_width): a "label: value" row (TS_BODY, the key in the
  secondary colour, key_width DIP)
- **`fn empty_state(&mut self, icon: u32, title: &str, text: &str)`**
  ui_empty_state(icon, title, text): a centred hint for an empty view (icon 0 = none)

### Charts

Source: [`lib/ui/chart.rs`](../lib/ui/chart.rs)

```text
chart -- small charts for dashboards: sparkline, bar chart (grouped or stacked), donut,
legend.  Colours: 0 = the style's chart palette (StyleData::chart_palette) by index.

  sparkline(id, values, color)          area + line, natural 120 x 32 DIP (next_size)
  bar_chart(id, labels, series, stacked) -> hovered column or -1
                                        value grid with labels, one column per label,
                                        natural 360 x 180 DIP; hovering a column shows a
                                        tooltip with its values
  donut(id, slices, center, sub)        ring of slices with a centre text, natural 160 DIP
  legend(items)                         colour dot + label per item, wrapping row

Area, line and ring are vector paths filled at render time (cmd_custom); their drawing
object is kept per id and replaced only when the data or the size change, so an unchanged
chart does not redraw (dirty.rs hashes the object's identity and version).
```

- **`struct BarSeries<'a>`**
  one series of a bar chart
  - `label: &'a str`
  - `values: &'a [f32]`
  - `color: u32` -- 0xAARRGGBB, 0 = palette colour of the series index

- **`struct DonutSlice<'a>`**
  one slice of a donut
  - `label: &'a str`
  - `value: f32`
  - `color: u32` -- 0xAARRGGBB, 0 = palette colour of the slice index

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn sparkline(&mut self, id: u32, values: &[f32], color: u32)`**
  ui_sparkline(id, values, color): area + line over the values (min .. max scaled)
- **`fn bar_chart(&mut self, id: u32, labels: &[&str], series: &[BarSeries], stacked: bool) -> i32`**
  ui_bar_chart(id, labels, series, stacked) -> the hovered column (-1 = none)
- **`fn donut(&mut self, id: u32, slices: &[DonutSlice], center: &str, sub: &str)`**
  ui_donut(id, slices, center, sub): ring of slices (fractions of their sum) with a
  centre text (TS_SUBTITLE) and a caption below it
- **`fn legend(&mut self, items: &[(&str, u32)])`**
  ui_legend(items): a colour dot and a label per item (colour 0 = palette by index),
  laid out in a wrapping row

### Toasts, spinners, progress

Source: [`lib/ui/notify.rs`](../lib/ui/notify.rs)

```text
notify -- transient feedback: toasts, spinner, indeterminate progress

  toast(kind, title, msg, ms)         queue a notification card (IB_INFO .. IB_ERROR; ms 0
                                      = 5 s, u32::MAX = until closed); returns its id
  toast_host()                        draw the queued toasts (bottom right, newest at the
                                      bottom, above dialogs); call it once per frame after
                                      the content.  Hovering keeps a toast; its close button
                                      removes it; expiry wakes the loop (timer UIT_TOAST).
  toast_dismiss(id) / toast_clear()
  toasts() -> [(id, kind, title, msg)]   the queued toasts (a notification history)
  spinner(id, size_dip)               a turning ring while something loads
  progress_indeterminate(id)          a bar with a running segment (natural 200 x 4 DIP)
The animations keep the window repainting while they are shown.
```

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn toast(&mut self, kind: u32, title: &str, msg: &str, ms: u32) -> u32`**
  ui_toast(kind, title, msg, ms) -> toast id
- **`fn toast_dismiss(&mut self, id: u32)`**
  ui_toast_dismiss(id)
- **`fn toast_clear(&mut self)`**
  ui_toast_clear
- **`fn toasts(&self) -> Vec<(u32, u32, String, String)>`**
  the queued toasts: (id, kind, title, message)
- **`fn toast_host(&mut self)`**
  ui_toast_host: draw the toasts (once per frame, after the content)
- **`fn spinner(&mut self, id: u32, size: i32)`**
  ui_spinner(id, size_dip): a turning ring
- **`fn progress_indeterminate(&mut self, id: u32)`**
  ui_progress_indeterminate(id): a bar with a running segment

### Date and time input

Source: [`lib/ui/datepick.rs`](../lib/ui/datepick.rs)

```text
datepick -- date and time input

  time_box(id, &mut hour, &mut minute) -> EV    two number boxes "HH : MM" (EV_ACT changed)
  weekday_toggles(id, &mut mask) -> EV          seven toggle buttons, bit 0 = Monday ..
                                                bit 6 = Sunday (EV_ACT changed)
  date_picker(id, &mut y, &mut m, &mut d) -> EV a button with the date that opens a month
                                                calendar (EV_ACT when a day was picked)
  civil helpers: days_in_month(y, m), weekday(y, m, d) (0 = Monday)

Ids: parts (id << 3 | k) | 0x80000000; calendar days ((id ^ 0x3C3C) << 6 | cell) | 0x80000000.
```

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn time_box(&mut self, id: u32, hour: &mut i32, minute: &mut i32) -> u32`**
  ui_time_box(id, &mut hour, &mut minute) -> EV_\* (EV_ACT changed)
- **`fn weekday_toggles(&mut self, id: u32, mask: &mut u32) -> u32`**
  ui_weekday_toggles(id, &mut mask) -> EV_\* (EV_ACT changed); bit 0 = Monday
- **`fn date_picker(&mut self, id: u32, y: &mut i32, m: &mut u32, d: &mut u32) -> u32`**
  ui_date_picker(id, &mut y, &mut m, &mut d) -> EV_\* (EV_ACT when a day was picked)

### Languages

Source: [`lib/ui/i18n.rs`](../lib/ui/i18n.rs)

```text
i18n -- the language of the library's own texts and translation catalogues for apps

  set_language(LANG_*) / language()   the UI language (process wide; default German)
  tr(de) -> text                      a text of the library itself (file dialog, command
                                      bar, tree, toasts, shortcuts) in the current language;
                                      the German source text is the key
  Catalog::from_tsv(text)             an app's catalogue: a TSV file whose first line names
                                      the languages ("de\ten\tfr"), then one row per text,
                                      the source text first; catalog.tr(s) -> the text in
                                      the current language (the source when missing)
  month_name(m) / month_short(m) / weekday_short(d)   calendar names (m 1..12, d 0 = Mon)
  set_catalog(catalog) / t(s)         the app's catalogue for the whole process: t(s) is the
                                      text in the current language (German source when missing)
  fill(text, args)                    "{}" placeholders replaced in order

The language index is the catalogue column: LANG_DE = 0 (the source column), LANG_EN = 1.
```

```rust
LANG_DE: u32 = 0
LANG_EN: u32 = 1
```

- **`fn set_language(lang: u32)`**
  set the UI language of the library texts (LANG_DE, LANG_EN)

- **`fn language() -> u32`**
  the current UI language (LANG_\*)

- **`fn tr(de: &'static str) -> &'static str`**
  a library text in the current language (the German source when there is no translation)

- **`fn month_name(m: u32) -> &'static str`**
  the name of month m (1..12) in the current language

- **`fn month_short(m: u32) -> &'static str`**
  the short name of month m (1..12)

- **`fn weekday_short(d: u32) -> &'static str`**
  the two-letter name of weekday d (0 = Monday .. 6 = Sunday)

- **`fn weekday_abbr(d: u32) -> &'static str`**
  the usual abbreviation of weekday d (0 = Monday) in running text: "Sa" / "Sat"

- **`struct Catalog`**
  a translation catalogue of an app (see the module comment)

**`impl Catalog`**

- **`fn from_tsv(text: &'static str) -> Catalog`**
  parse a TSV catalogue (first line: the language codes); "\\n" and "\\t" escapes in the
  cells are not interpreted
- **`fn languages(&self) -> &[String]`**
  the language codes of the columns ("de", "en", ...)
- **`fn tr<'a>(&'a self, s: &'a str) -> &'a str`**
  the text in the current language (the source text when the cell is missing or empty)
- **`fn has(&self, s: &str) -> bool`**
  whether the catalogue has a row for this source text

- **`fn set_catalog(c: Catalog)`**
  install the app's catalogue for the whole process (t() reads it)

- **`fn t(s: &'static str) -> &'static str`**
  the app's text in the current language: the installed catalogue, then the library's own
  texts, then the German source

- **`fn fill(text: &str, args: &[&str]) -> String`**
  replace the "{}" placeholders of a text in order

**`impl Catalog`**

- **`fn get_static(&self, s: &str) -> Option<&'static str>`**
  the translation of a source text in the current language, when the catalogue has one

### Vector icons

Source: [`lib/ui/vicon.rs`](../lib/ui/vicon.rs)

```text
vicon -- vector icons from SVG path data, for styles (Style::paint_icon) and app icon hooks

  paint_svg_icon(g, art, x, y, size_fx, color, stroke) -> drawn
       art: SVG path data on a 24 x 24 grid; several layers are separated by '|', a layer
       that starts with 'F' is filled (non-zero rule), the others are stroked with round
       caps and joins `stroke` grid units wide (1.75 is a good outline weight); 'E' fills
       with the even-odd rule (cut-outs: a mark inside a filled disc).  The icon's
       em box has its top-left corner at (x, y) target pixels and is size_fx (24.8) pixels
       square.  A layer that starts with 'A' is drawn in the accent colour (second colour).
  paint_svg_icon2(.., color, accent, stroke)   the same with an accent colour for 'A' layers

Icons are geometry, not font glyphs: crisp at every size and dpi, and free of the icon
font's licence.  Parsing is cheap (a few dozen commands per icon).
```

- **`fn paint_svg_icon(g: &mut Gfx, art: &str, x: i32, y: i32, size_fx: i32, color: u32, stroke: f32) -> bool`**
  paint an icon from 24-grid SVG path data (see the module comment)

- **`fn paint_svg_icon2(g: &mut Gfx, art: &str, x: i32, y: i32, size_fx: i32, color: u32, accent: u32, stroke: f32) -> bool`**
  paint_svg_icon with a second (accent) colour for the layers that start with 'A'

### Values and colours

#### `ui/value.rs`

Source: [`lib/ui/value.rs`](../lib/ui/value.rs)

```text
value -- decimal values, gradient tracks, angle dial and pan control (U1)
                                                                 [src/ui/ui_value.s]

  slider_f(id, &mut v, &rf) -> EV           slider for an f64 (RangeF): EV_ACT while the
       value changes, EV_COMMIT on release and on key steps.  Keys (focused): arrows = one
       step, PgUp / PgDn = 10 steps, Home / End = minimum / maximum.
  track_slider(id, &mut v, &rg, track) -> EV   slider for an i32 (Range) whose track shows
       the colour gradient of a Track (TK_CHECKER: over a checkerboard, for alpha);
       horizontal or RG_VERT; same keys as slider_f
  prop_slider(id, label, &mut v, &rg, default) -> EV   paint.net property row: label over
       slider + number box + reset button (i32; default NO_DEFAULT = no reset button);
       EV_ACT while the slider is dragged or a number is typed (live preview), EV_COMMIT
       on release, Enter / focus loss, key steps and reset
  prop_slider_f(id, label, &mut v, &rf, default) -> EV   the same for an f64 (None = no
       reset button).  Parts: slider = id, number box = part_id(id, 1), reset =
       part_id(id, 2); the label selects the slider in scripts.
  angle(id, &mut deg, flags) -> EV          angle dial (f64 degrees, -180 .. 180 or ANG_360
       0 .. 360, counter-clockwise, 0 = right): drag sets the angle, Shift snaps to 15
       degrees; keys: arrows 1 (Shift 15), PgUp / PgDn 15, Home 0.  Natural 64 DIP.
       Semantic value = degrees * 100.
  pan(id, &mut x, &mut y, thumb, ver, flags) -> EV   pan control: a thumbnail (surface or
       None, ver = its version) with a crosshair; drag sets the point (f64, -1 .. 1,
       PAN_UNIT 0 .. 1; y down); arrows move 1 % (Shift 10 %), Home centres.  Semantic
       value = x * 1000, max = y * 1000.
  part_id(id, k) -> id                      id of part k (0..15) of a composite widget
  fmt_f64(&v, decimals) -> String           "-12,50" with the decimal separator
  parse_f64(s) -> Some(v) / None            accepts ',' and '.' as decimal separator
  decimal_sep() / set_decimal_sep(c)        separator shown (default ',')
Shared with the styles and ui_color / ui_wheel: grad_at, px_mix, deg_atan2, deg_sincos,
Ui::cmd_track (the cmd_custom of a gradient track: track_cb / track_hash of the asm),
Ui::slider_track_draw; internal: rf_fix, rf_scaled, rf_t16, sld_*, vl_state, f_round.

No per-window state: everything lives in the app's variables and in state slots.  The
decimal separator (ui_decimal_sep) and the cache of gradient track drawings are shared.
```

```rust
/// prop_slider: no reset button
NO_DEFAULT: i32 = i32::MIN
```

- **`struct RangeF`**
  RANGE_F min, max, step, decimals, flags -- descriptor of an f64 value
  - `min: f64`
  - `max: f64`
  - `step: f64`
  - `dec: u32`
  - `flags: u32`

**`impl RangeF`**

- **`const fn new(min: f64, max: f64, step: f64, dec: u32, flags: u32) -> RangeF`**

- **`struct Track`**
  TRACK -- the colour gradient of track_slider: TK_\* flags and 1..TK_MAXN straight-ARGB
  stops; with TK_CUSTOM the track is drawn by `custom` (draw object, user value, version:
  bump it when the drawing changes) instead
  - `flags: u32`
  - `n: u32`
  - `stops: [u32; TK_MAXN]`
  - `custom: Option<(DrawRef, usize, u32)>`

**`impl Track`**

- **`fn new(flags: u32, stops: &[u32]) -> Track`**
  TRACK flags, argb0, argb1, ... (at most TK_MAXN stops)

- **`fn part_id(id: u32, k: u32) -> u32`**
  ui_part_id(id, k) -> PART_BASE | (id &lt;&lt; 4 | k) & 0x0FFFFFFF

- **`fn deg_atan2(y: f64, x: f64) -> f64`**
  deg_atan2(y, x) -> degrees in (-180, 180] (x87 fpatan)

- **`fn deg_sincos(d: f64) -> (f64, f64)`**
  deg_sincos(degrees) -> (cos, sin) (x87 fsincos)

- **`fn grad_at(tk: &Track, t: i32) -> u32`**
  grad_at(track, t) -> colour (straight ARGB) of the gradient at t (0..65536)

- **`fn px_mix(dst: u32, src: u32, k: u32) -> u32`**
  px_mix(dst, src, k) -> per channel (dst \* (255 - k) + src \* k) / 255 of 0x..RRGGBB
  (alpha byte 0)

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn cmd_track(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32, tk: &Track)`**
  cmd_custom of a value slider's gradient track over (x, y, w, h): the track's own
  drawing (TK_CUSTOM) or the gradient (track_cb, version = track_hash)  [styles' value.s]
- **`fn decimal_sep(&self) -> u32`**
  ui_decimal_sep: the separator fmt_f64 shows (and parse_f64 accepts besides ',' / '.')
- **`fn set_decimal_sep(&mut self, c: u32)`**
  set ui_decimal_sep (a code point)
- **`fn fmt_f64(&self, v: f64, decimals: u32) -> String`**
  ui_fmt_f64(&v, decimals) -> fixed decimals with the decimal separator; -0 is shown as 0
- **`fn parse_f64(&self, s: &str) -> Option<f64>`**
  ui_parse_f64(s) -> the value, or None when s has no digits.  Blanks, a sign, digits,
  one ',' or '.' (or the decimal separator when ASCII), digits.
- **`fn slider_f(&mut self, id: u32, v: &mut f64, rf: &RangeF) -> u32`**
  ui_slider_f(id, &mut v, &rf) -> EV_\*
- **`fn track_slider(&mut self, id: u32, v: &mut i32, rg: &Range, track: Option<&Track>) -> u32`**
