  (None = the standard look)
- **`fn prop_slider(&mut self, id: u32, label: &str, v: &mut i32, rg: &Range, default: i32) -> u32`**
  ui_prop_slider(id, label, &mut v, &rg, default) -> EV_\* (default NO_DEFAULT: no reset
  button)
- **`fn prop_slider_f(&mut self, id: u32, label: &str, v: &mut f64, rf: &RangeF, default: Option<f64>) -> u32`**
  ui_prop_slider_f(id, label, &mut v, &rf, default) -> EV_\* (default None: no reset
  button)

#### `ui/color.rs`

Source: [`lib/ui/color.rs`](../lib/ui/color.rs)

```text
color -- colour swatches, the colour picker and the HSV colour wheel
                                                    [src/ui/ui_color.s, src/ui/ui_wheel.s]

  swatch(id, argb, selected) -> EV        round colour well (22 DIP or next_size); alpha 0
       draws an empty slot; EV_ACT on click, EV_RCLICK on right click
  color_picker(id, &mut argb) -> EV       spectrum (saturation x value), hue slider, preview
       and hex text; EV_ACT while the colour changes, EV_COMMIT on release.  Uses the ids
       id + 0x20000000 (spectrum) and id + 0x10000000 (hue) for its parts.
  color_picker_ex(id, &mut argb, flags) -> EV   (U1) the same with CP_ALPHA: an alpha bar
       (id + 0x30000000, checkerboard) under the hue bar; CP_FIELDS: number boxes R G B (A)
       and H S V (part_id(id, 3..9)) and a hex text box (part_id(id, 10); RRGGBB,
       AARRGGBB with CP_ALPHA, taken on Enter / focus loss) instead of the hex text.
  color_wheel(id, &mut color, flags) -> EV  (U1) hue around the circle (0 = red at the
       right, counter-clockwise), saturation from the centre to the rim; the value is not
       part of the wheel (a separate track slider) but the wheel is drawn at that value.
       color: [0xAARRGGBB] (alpha kept; the HSV state lives in a state slot so greys keep
       their hue), or with CW_HSV an HSV record [h 0..3599, s 0..1000, v 0..1000].  Drag
       sets hue and saturation; keys: Left / Right hue -/+ 1 degree, Up / Down saturation
       +/- 1 % (Shift: x10).  EV_ACT while it changes, EV_COMMIT on release / keys.
       Natural size 160 DIP.  Semantic record: K_SLIDER, value = hue, max = saturation.
  hsv_to_rgb(h, s, v) -> 0xFFRRGGBB       h 0..3599 (tenths of a degree), s / v 0..1000
  rgb_to_hsv(rgb) -> (h, s, v)            inverse (same ranges)

The wheel image is cached per widget (WC_MAX entries, shared by all windows): a
premultiplied image at full value for the current size (antialiased rim), and the image
scaled to the current value; only a change of size or value re-renders it.
```

- **`fn hsv_to_rgb(h: u32, s: u32, v: u32) -> u32`**
  hsv_to_rgb(h, s, v) -> 0xFFRRGGBB   (h 0..3599, s / v 0..1000)

- **`fn rgb_to_hsv(rgb: u32) -> (u32, u32, u32)`**
  rgb_to_hsv(rgb) -> (h 0..3599, s 0..1000, v 0..1000)

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn swatch(&mut self, id: u32, argb: u32, selected: bool) -> u32`**
  ui_swatch(id, argb, selected) -> EV_\*
- **`fn color_picker(&mut self, id: u32, argb: &mut u32) -> u32`**
  ui_color_picker(id, &mut argb) -> EV_\*
- **`fn color_picker_ex(&mut self, id: u32, argb: &mut u32, flags: u32) -> u32`**
  ui_color_picker_ex(id, &mut argb, flags) -> EV_\* (CP_ALPHA, CP_FIELDS)
- **`fn color_wheel(&mut self, id: u32, color: &mut [u32], flags: u32) -> u32`**
  ui_color_wheel(id, &mut color, flags) -> EV_\*: color = [argb], or [h, s, v] with
  CW_HSV

### Windows

Source: [`lib/ui/win.rs`](../lib/ui/win.rs)

```text
win -- multi-window runtime: tool windows and dialog windows built by the same App::frame
as the main window, each with its own UI state                       [src/ui/ui_win.s]

App-facing:
  window_begin(id, title, open, flags, rect) -> true: emit the content now (always pair
       with window_end; see WF_* / WinRect; open / rect: shared with the library)
  window_end()
  window_place(id, &rect)              move / resize (copied into the window's WINRECT)
  window_rect(id) -> Option<WinRect>   current placement (None = unknown window)
  window_state(id) -> UWS_*            UWS_OPEN | UWS_VISIBLE | UWS_ACTIVE | UWS_MINIMIZED
  window_activate(id)                  give it the keyboard (raises it)
  window_destroy(id)                   free its state and buffers (created again when shown)
  window_current() -> id               id of the window being built (0 = main)
  window_modal() -> id                 id of the topmost modal window or 0
  sync_hook_set(f)                     f() -> true while background jobs run (`sync`)
Platform: hit_flags_win(win, x, y), win_hooks_set(on) (OS windows through the plat_win_*
API, the asm's uiw_plat_hooks table), uiw_hit_pw (the platform's hit hook), uiw_pw /
uiw_from_pw (UI index <-> platform window).
Runtime (runtime.rs, ctx.rs, chrome.rs, auto.rs, drop.rs): the uiw_* functions below.

How it works: the per-window state of every module lives in WinParts (lib.rs); `Ui::wp`
is the live window's.  uiw_use(i) swaps the live parts into the record of the window
that was live and window i's parts out of its record (core::mem::swap of a Box), so every
widget, popup, dialog and the dirty-region code work unchanged in whichever window is
live.  Outside of the passes and of the event dispatch the main window (index 0) is live.
Platform operations are never done inside window_begin: they are recorded in Rec::want
and done by uiw_settle after the frame settled (headless runs: none -- the windows only
exist as framebuffers).

The asm keeps the app's `&open` and `&rect` pointers in the window record and writes
through them later (close button, Esc, placement by the user); here the app passes a
Shared<T> (Rc<Cell<T>>) and the record keeps a clone of it.
```

- **`struct Shared<T: Copy>(Rc<Cell<T>>)`**
  a value the app shares with the library: the asm passes its address and the library
  keeps it (a window's `open` flag, its WINRECT, a content dialog's `open` flag)

**`impl<T: Copy> Shared<T>`**

- **`fn new(v: T) -> Shared<T>`**
- **`fn get(&self) -> T`**
- **`fn set(&self, v: T)`**

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn window_begin(&mut self, id: u32, title: &str, open: Option<&Shared<i32>>, flags: u32, rect: Option<&Shared<WinRect>>) -> bool`**
  ui_window_begin(id, title, open, flags, rect) -> true: the window is shown, emit its
  content now (window_end always follows).  open None = always open; rect None: the
  library keeps the placement.  The owner is the window being built.
- **`fn window_end(&mut self)`**
  ui_window_end: close window_begin (also when it returned false)
- **`fn sync_hook_set(&mut self, f: Option<fn() -> bool>)`**
  ui_sync_hook_set(f): f() -> true while background jobs are pending (the script
  command `sync` waits for false)
- **`fn window_place(&mut self, id: u32, rect: &WinRect)`**
  ui_window_place(id, &rect): set the window's placement (copied into its WINRECT;
  applied when it is built next).  id 0: the main window (headless: its size)
- **`fn window_rect(&self, id: u32) -> Option<WinRect>`**
  ui_window_rect(id) -> the window's placement (None = unknown window)
- **`fn window_state(&mut self, id: u32) -> u32`**
  ui_window_state(id) -> UWS_OPEN | UWS_VISIBLE | UWS_ACTIVE | UWS_MINIMIZED (0 unknown)
- **`fn window_activate(&mut self, id: u32)`**
  ui_window_activate(id): give the window the keyboard and put it on top
- **`fn window_destroy(&mut self, id: u32)`**
  ui_window_destroy(id): hide the window and free its state and buffers (not while it
  is being built)
- **`fn window_current(&self) -> u32`**
  ui_window_current -> id of the window being built (0 = main window)
- **`fn window_modal(&self) -> u32`**
  ui_window_modal -> id of the topmost modal window, 0 = none
- **`fn window_pw(&self, id: u32) -> (i32, i32)`**
  uiw_index + uiw_pw: (UI index of app window id (-1 none, 0 the main window), its
  platform window (-1 none))  // pn-core

### Chrome: title bar, dialogs, info and status bars

Source: [`lib/ui/chrome.rs`](../lib/ui/chrome.rs)

```text
chrome -- window chrome and overlays: content dialog, info bar, status bar, title bar,
caption buttons, the chrome of secondary windows, edge grips       [src/ui/ui_chrome.s]

  dialog_begin(id, title, open) -> true while open (emit the content, then
       dialog_buttons and dialog_end; always call dialog_end)
       WinUI ContentDialog on L_DIALOG: smoke over the window, centred 320..548 DIP
       wide, title, content; everything below is inert while it is open
  dialog_buttons(primary, secondary, close) -> DLG_NONE / DLG_PRIMARY / DLG_SECONDARY /
       DLG_CLOSE -- footer buttons (labels, None = none; primary is the accent button);
       Enter = primary, Esc = close.  Any result clears the open flag.
  dialog_end()
  infobar(id, severity, title, msg) -> EV (EV_ACT: the close button was clicked)
       IB_INFO / IB_SUCCESS / IB_WARNING / IB_ERROR; the close button has the id id + 1
  statusbar_begin() / statusbar_end()
       32 DIP bar (layer fill, top divider) laying out its children left to right,
       vertically centred, 16 DIP apart (captions: text(TS_CAPTION, ...))
  edge_grips()   resize handles for frameless windows on Linux (the runtime calls it;
       also for resizable secondary windows with the library frame)
  titlebar_begin(title, icon) / titlebar_end()
       custom title bar row (style: SM_TB_H, SM_TB_H_LARGE with next_flags(ST_LARGE)):
       icon + title, then the caller's widgets; empty parts are an HB_DRAG region, the end
       adds the caption buttons (HB_MIN / HB_MAX / HB_CLOSE; with SM_CAP_LEFT the begin puts
       them first, close / min / max)
Windows (win.rs):
  win_chrome_begin(i) / win_chrome_end(i) -> natural content height (DIP)
       border, title bar (28 DIP tool / 32 DIP dialog: title, close button) and the
       content box of a secondary window; inside a WF_DIALOG window dialog_buttons puts
       its footer below the content box (pinned to the bottom)

Ids: dialog parts (id << 3 | k) | 0x80000000; title bar 0xFFFF7400.. (min +1, max +2,
close +3); status bar 0xFFFF7410, 0xFFFF7411; edge grips 0xFFFF7430..
```

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn dialog_begin(&mut self, id: u32, title: &str, open: &Shared<i32>) -> bool`**
  ui_dialog_begin(id, title, open) -> true if the dialog is open
- **`fn dialog_buttons(&mut self, primary: Option<&str>, secondary: Option<&str>, close: Option<&str>) -> u32`**
  ui_dialog_buttons(primary, secondary, close) -> DLG_\* (any result clears the open
  flag).  Inside a content dialog: its footer.  Otherwise, inside a WF_DIALOG window:
  a footer pinned to the window's bottom; a result clears the window's open flag.
- **`fn dialog_end(&mut self)`**
  ui_dialog_end
- **`fn infobar(&mut self, id: u32, severity: u32, title: &str, msg: &str) -> u32`**
  ui_infobar(id, severity, title, msg) -> EV (EV_ACT when the close button was clicked)
- **`fn statusbar_begin(&mut self)`**
  ui_statusbar_begin: a 32 DIP bar; call it last in a vertical layout (after a fill)
- **`fn statusbar_end(&mut self)`**
  ui_statusbar_end
- **`fn titlebar_begin(&mut self, title: &str, icon: u32)`**
  ui_titlebar_begin(title, icon): a title bar row (icon 0 = none)
- **`fn titlebar_begin_opt(&mut self, title: Option<&str>, icon: u32)`**
  ui_titlebar_begin with title 0: no title text (the record has no label)  // pn-core
- **`fn titlebar_begin_overlay(&mut self, title: Option<&str>, icon: u32)`**
  Overlay native window controls and a drag region without painting a titlebar surface.
  Use `next_rect_px` to place it over the empty top edge of a file/start screen.
  The agent indicator remains visible beside the caption buttons when control is enabled.
- **`fn titlebar_end(&mut self)`**
  ui_titlebar_end: caption buttons at the right end (unless the style puts them at the
  left: SM_CAP_LEFT)

### File dialog

Source: [`lib/ui/filedlg.rs`](../lib/ui/filedlg.rs)

```text
filedlg -- Open / Save file dialog (ContentDialog with a folder list), both OS
                                                                 [src/ui/ui_filedlg.s]

  filedlg(id, flags, open, path, cap, exts) -> EV_ACT when a file was chosen
       flags FD_OPEN / FD_SAVE | index << FD_EXT_SHIFT (initially selected extension).
       path (at most cap - 1 bytes, UTF-8) gives the initial file or folder (empty = the
       folder used last, else the home folder) and receives the chosen path ('/'
       separators on every OS; Windows accepts them).  exts = the extension list, one
       entry per type: "png=PNG-Bild", "jpg;jpeg;jfif=JPEG-Bild" (lower case; several
       extensions of one type separated by ';', the first is the one appended; optional
       "=description"; empty = every file); FD_SAVE appends the selected type's extension
       when the name has none.  Call it every frame like any dialog; open = 1 shows it.
       Where the system has a file dialog (Windows) that one is shown (modal); otherwise,
       headless or with jfgui_FILEDLG=builtin, the library's own dialog.
       Widget ids used: id .. id + 0x3FFF (list rows id + 17 + i).

One file dialog can be open at a time (the state below is shared).
```

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn filedlg(&mut self, id: u32, flags: u32, open: &Shared<i32>, path: &mut String, cap: u32, exts: &[&str]) -> u32`**
  ui_filedlg(id, flags, open, path, cap, exts) -> EV_ACT when confirmed

### Drawing commands

Source: [`lib/ui/cmd.rs`](../lib/ui/cmd.rs)

```text
cmd -- per-layer draw command lists, recorded during a UI pass and replayed into the
framebuffer afterwards (popups drawn late still end up on top, passes that are thrown
away cost no pixels)                                                 [src/ui/ui_cmd.s]

Drawing calls for apps (current layer, physical pixels):
  draw_color(argb)                     colour for the next draw_* calls
  draw_rect(x, y, w, h)                filled rectangle
  draw_rrect(x, y, w, h, r)            filled rounded rectangle
  draw_frame(x, y, w, h, r, t)         rounded border of thickness t
  draw_text(x, y, s, font_size)        text with baseline y; font_size = FONT_* | size_fx << 8
  draw_icon(cp, x, y, size_fx)         icon, em box top-left at x, y
  icon_hook(f)                         an app draws chosen icons itself (render time)
  draw_circle(cx, cy, r, t)            24.8 fixed; t = 0 fills
  draw_line(x0, y0, x1, y1, width)     24.8 fixed, round caps
  draw_image(sf, x, y, w, h)           surface scaled to w x h (nearest) or 1:1
  draw_clip(x, y, w, h) / draw_unclip()
Internal: cmd_reset, cmd_layer(l) -> previous, cmd_rrect, cmd_text, cmd_custom,
cmd_shadow, render_all, render_popup(dx, dy).

Every record keeps the byte size the asm gives it (cmd_alloc rounds to 8): a layer holds
at most its capacity (CAPS_MAIN / CAPS_SMALL), further commands are dropped as in the asm.
The dirty regions (dirty.rs) hash every record's content (cmd_hash) and its box (cmd_bbox).
```

- **`struct Gfx<'a>`**
  the gfx target of a custom draw call (render time): the draw context (clip, origin,
  colour), the surface and the text engine (its path and rasterizer serve Shapes)
  - `dc: &'a mut Dc`
  - `sf: &'a mut Surface`
  - `text: &'a mut Text`

**`impl Gfx<'_>`**

- **`fn shapes(&mut self) -> Shapes<'_>`**
  the anti-aliased shape calls (fill_rrect, circles, lines) on this target

- **`trait Draw`**
  a custom draw function (cmd_custom: canvases, custom tracks): draw(g, rect) at render
  time with the clip set to rect (absolute px).  Its identity (the Rc) and the version of
  the command decide whether it is redrawn.
  - `fn draw(&self, g: &mut Gfx, r: [i32; 4])`

- **`type DrawRef = Rc<dyn Draw>`**

- **`type IconHook = Rc<dyn Fn(&mut Gfx, u32, i32, i32, i32, u32) -> bool>`**
  icon_hook: f(g, cp, x, y, size_fx, argb) -> true when it drew icon cp itself (x, y =
  target pixels with the origin applied), false = the icon font draws it

- **`enum Cmd`**
  one recorded draw command  [C_\*]
  - `Rect { x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32, c: u32 }`
  - `RRect { x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32, r: u16, t: u16, fill: u32, stroke: u32, stroke2: u32 }` -- fill (alpha 0 = none), border t px in `stroke`, its bottom band in `stroke2`; r and t
    are kept as u16
  - `PanelFx { x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32, r: i32, t: i32, fill: u32, stroke: u32 }` -- Fractional rounded panel; geometry is 24.8 pixels and composited once.
  - `Text { x: i32, y: i32, font: u16, size: i32, c: u32, maxw: i32, s: Vec<u8> }` -- text with baseline y; maxw != 0 clips it to x .. x + maxw
  - `Icon { x: i32, y: i32, cp: u32, size: i32, c: u32 }` -- icon, em box top-left at x, y
  - `Image { x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32, sf: Rc<Surface>, ver: u32 }`
  - `Clip { x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32 }`
  - `Unclip`
  - `Circle { x: i32, y: i32, r: i32, t: i32, c: u32 }` -- 24.8; t = 0 fills
  - `Line { x0: i32, y0: i32, x1: i32, y1: i32, w: i32, c: u32 }` -- 24.8, round caps
  - `Custom { x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32, f: Option<DrawRef>, user: usize, ver: u32 }`
  - `Shadow { x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32, r: i32, depth: i32, c: u32 }`

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn cmd_layer(&mut self, l: u32) -> u32`**
  cmd_layer(l) -> previous layer
- **`fn draw_color(&mut self, argb: u32)`**
  ui_draw_color(argb): colour for the following draw_\* calls
- **`fn draw_color_get(&self) -> u32`**
  the colour of the following draw_\* calls (g_dcol)
- **`fn draw_rect(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32)`**
  ui_draw_rect(x, y, w, h)
- **`fn draw_rrect(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32, r: i32)`**
  ui_draw_rrect(x, y, w, h, r): filled rounded rectangle in the draw colour
- **`fn draw_frame(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32, r: i32, t: i32)`**
  ui_draw_frame(x, y, w, h, r, t): border only, in the draw colour
- **`fn cmd_rrect(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32, r: i32, t: i32, fill: u32, stroke: u32, stroke2: u32)`**
  cmd_rrect(x, y, w, h, r, t, fill, stroke, stroke2): rounded rectangle with an
  optional fill and border (stroke2: the bottom edge)
- **`fn cmd_panel_fx(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32, r: i32, t: i32, fill: u32, stroke: u32)`**
  Rounded fill/border with 24.8 pixel geometry and a single coverage composite.
  Fractional bounds participate in dirty-region hashing and clipping.
- **`fn draw_text(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32, s: &str, font_size: u32)`**
  ui_draw_text(x, y, s, font_size): font_size = FONT_\* | size_fx &lt;&lt; 8
- **`fn cmd_text(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32, s: &[u8], font: u32, size: i32, c: u32, maxw: i32)`**
  cmd_text(x, y, s, font, size_fx, colour, maxw): at most 4000 bytes, never cut inside
  a UTF-8 sequence
- **`fn emit_text(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32, s: &str, c: u32)`**
  emit_text(x, baseline, s, colour): text in the current text font (styles)
- **`fn draw_icon(&mut self, cp: u32, x: i32, y: i32, size: i32)`**
  ui_draw_icon(cp, x, y, size_fx)
- **`fn draw_circle(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32, r: i32, t: i32)`**
  ui_draw_circle(cx, cy, r, t): 24.8 fixed, t = 0 fills
- **`fn draw_line(&mut self, x0: i32, y0: i32, x1: i32, y1: i32, w: i32)`**
  ui_draw_line(x0, y0, x1, y1, width): 24.8 fixed
- **`fn draw_image(&mut self, sf: &Rc<Surface>, ver: u32, x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32)`**
  ui_draw_image(sf, x, y, w, h): w / h 0 = natural size; `ver` stands for the
  surface's SF_AUX version (bump it when the pixels change)
- **`fn draw_clip(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32)`**
  ui_draw_clip(x, y, w, h)
- **`fn draw_unclip(&mut self)`**
  ui_draw_unclip
- **`fn icon_hook(&mut self, f: Option<IconHook>)`**
  ui_icon_hook(f): an app draws chosen icons itself (None removes the hook)
- **`fn cmd_custom(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32, f: Option<DrawRef>, user: usize, ver: u32)`**
  cmd_custom(x, y, w, h, f, user, version): f.draw(g, rect) at render time, clipped
- **`fn cmd_shadow(&mut self, x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32, r: i32, depth: i32)`**
  cmd_shadow(x, y, w, h, r, depth): soft shadow in the draw colour

### Scripts and the widget dump

Source: [`lib/ui/auto.rs`](../lib/ui/auto.rs)

```text
auto -- UI automation for headless tests: scripts and the widget dump  [src/ui/ui_auto.s]

  script_run(app, path) -> exit code   0 all expectations met, 1 failures, 2 parse error
  dump(path)                           widget tree as JSON lines (None = stdout)

Script syntax (one command per line, '#' starts a comment; coordinates in DIP):
  size W H                      resize the headless window
  theme dark|light
  move S          down S [l|r|m]      up [S] [l|r|m]      click S [l|r]
  rclick S        dblclick S          drag S1 S2 [steps] [shift|ctrl|alt|right|middle]...
                  drag ... pen|eraser P0 P1   (PN1) a pen stroke, pressure P0 -> P1 (%)
  pointer mouse | pen [P [TX TY]] | eraser [P [TX TY]] | touch   (PN1) pen data of the
                  following pointer events
  wheel S DY
  key CHORD...    e.g. key ctrl+s   key tab   key shift+tab enter esc left f4
                  (also [ ] , . - + / and num0..num9 numadd numsub nummul numdiv numdec)
  focus S         give the widget the keyboard focus (focus ring shown)
  type "text"     ("\n" presses Enter)
  cmd ID [ARG]    run a command as if a menu item was chosen
  wait MS         advance the virtual clock (16 ms frames)
  shot FILE.png   render and save a screenshot
  dump [FILE]     print / save the widget tree
  expect S exists | missing | hover | focused | checked | unchecked | disabled |
           enabled | selected | open | pressed | value N | max N | text "s" | rect X Y W H
  print TEXT      echo
  bench N         time N frames (build, full render, unchanged frame) with the real clock
  quit
Windows (ID = the app's window id, 0 / main = the main window):
  window ID|main|any            selectors only look into that window (default any)
  expect window ID open|closed|visible|active|modal     expect window ID rect X Y W H
  close window ID   movewin ID X Y   resizewin ID W H   activate ID
  scale P [window ID]           dpi of the main window (default) or of one window
  shot F.png [window ID|desktop] [X Y W H]   one window, or all visible windows on the
                                virtual desktop; a crop rectangle in DIP
  sync [MS]                     wait until no background job is pending
  expect_warn / expect_nowarn   a UIWARN was / was not printed since the last check (a
                                script also warns about a widget id used twice in a pass)
(K1) Files and clipboard:
  drop X Y FILE...              files dropped at (X, Y) DIP of the scoped window
  clip text "s" | clip file F | clip dib F | clip clear | clip save F [bmp] |
  clip expect none|text|image|png
Selectors S:  #123  (widget id)   "Label"   kind:"Label"   X,Y  (a point in DIP)
A label must match exactly one widget (plain text is ignored when a control with the
same label exists; matches in several windows: the topmost window, then the topmost
layer).  #id selectors must be unique across windows (else a UIWARN, the topmost window
wins).  Points are in the scoped window (main for any).  Failed expectations print
  FAIL line N: <command> -> <detail>
and the script continues.

Scripts are bytes (as the asm reads them): lines of at most 1023 bytes, CR dropped; a NUL
ends the script.
```

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn dump(&mut self, path: Option<&[u8]>)`**
  ui_dump(path): JSON lines -- a header, one line per shown secondary window, then one
  line per semantic record (None = stdout)

### Theme

Source: [`lib/ui/theme.rs`](../lib/ui/theme.rs)

```text
theme -- colour tokens, accent colour and type ramp of the linked style  [src/ui/ui_theme.s]

  theme_set(mode)            0 light, 1 dark, 2 follow the system (plat::theme)
  theme_dark() -> bool       the dark theme is active
  accent_set(rgb)            0xRRGGBB accent, 0 = the style's default
  color(tok) -> argb         0xAARRGGBB of a T_* token (byte offset)
  ts_font(ts)                select font and pixel size of a TS_* text style
  ts_line(ts) -> px          line height of a text style
  ts_base(ts) -> px          baseline offset from the line top
  color_mix(a, b, t)         per-channel a + (b - a) * t / 256 (t 0..256)

The values come from the style (Style::tokens, accent, ts_size / ts_line / ts_font).
```

- **`fn color_mix(a: u32, b: u32, t: i32) -> u32`**
  color_mix(a, b, t): per-channel a + (b - a) \* t / 256 (t 0..256), every channel
  including alpha

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn theme_set(&mut self, mode: u32)`**
  ui_theme_set(mode): 0 light, 1 dark, 2 the system setting (unknown: dark)
- **`fn theme_dark(&self) -> bool`**
  ui_theme_dark: the dark theme is active
- **`fn accent_set(&mut self, rgb: u32)`**
  ui_accent_set(0xRRGGBB), 0 = the style's default accent
- **`fn color(&self, tok: u32) -> u32`**
  ui_color(tok): 0xAARRGGBB of a token (byte offset; any value reads inside the table)
- **`fn ts_font(&mut self, ts: u32)`**
  ui_ts_font(ts): select the font and pixel size (size \* dpi / 96, 24.8) of a style
- **`fn ts_line(&self, ts: u32) -> i32`**
  ui_ts_line(ts): line height of a text style in px
- **`fn ts_base(&mut self, ts: u32) -> i32`**
  ui_ts_base(ts): baseline offset from the top of the line box (px; selects the font)

### Cursors, drops, clipboard images, dirty regions

#### `ui/cursor.rs`

Source: [`lib/ui/cursor.rs`](../lib/ui/cursor.rs)

```text
cursor -- custom pointer cursors drawn by the app, at the dpi of the window
                                                                    [src/ui/ui_cursor.s]

  cursor_new(f, w, h, hot) -> handle 1..CUR_USER_MAX (0 = no slot left)
       a cursor of w x h DIP with the hot spot hot = x | y << 16 (DIP); f(g, dpi) draws it
       at `dpi` into g (FMT_PARGB, transparent; physical px: ceil(w * dpi / 96) x
       ceil(h * dpi / 96)) -- called again for every new dpi
  cursor_custom(handle)      the cursor that hit boxes with CUR_CUSTOM show (their
                             next_flags(CUR_CUSTOM << HB_CURSOR_SHIFT)); set it each
                             frame before the widget; 0 = the crosshair
  cursor_render(handle, dpi) -> Some(surface)   the cursor drawn into a new surface
                             (FMT_PARGB; tests, previews)
  cursor_current() -> handle the handle CUR_CUSTOM stands for (0 = none)
Internal: cursor_resolve(shape) -> the plat shape for a hit box cursor (CUR_CUSTOM: the
custom cursor made for the current window's dpi with plat::cursor_create, rebuilt when
that dpi changes; the crosshair when there is none or the platform has no custom
cursors, e.g. headless runs).
```

- **`type CursorDraw = Rc<dyn Fn(&mut Gfx, i32)>`**
  a cursor's draw function: f(g, dpi) draws into the cursor surface

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn cursor_new(&mut self, f: CursorDraw, w: u32, h: u32, hot: u32) -> u32`**
  ui_cursor_new(f, w, h, hot) -> handle (0 = none)
- **`fn cursor_custom(&mut self, h: u32)`**
  ui_cursor_custom(handle): the cursor of CUR_CUSTOM hit boxes
- **`fn cursor_current(&self) -> u32`**
  ui_cursor_current -> handle
- **`fn cursor_render(&mut self, h: u32, dpi: i32) -> Option<Surface>`**
  ui_cursor_render(handle, dpi) -> the cursor drawn at dpi into a new surface

#### `ui/drop.rs`

Source: [`lib/ui/drop.rs`](../lib/ui/drop.rs)

```text
drop -- (K1) dropped files for apps, the clipboard format probe, the headless image
clipboard                                                           [src/ui/ui_drop.s]

  drop_count() -> n               files of the last drop (UIC_DROP, arg = window id)
  drop_path(i) -> Option<&str>    path i; valid until the next drop arrives
  drop_list() -> &[String]        every path of the last drop
  drop_pos() -> (x, y)            drop position in DIP of the window it happened in
  drop_window() -> id             that window's app id (0 = main window)
  clip_has(fmt) -> bool           the clipboard offers CLIP_TEXT / CLIP_PNG / CLIP_IMAGE
                                  (headless runs: the in-process clipboard)
Internal:
  drop_accept(ev)                 E_DROP: take the platform's pending list (runtime.rs)
  drop_inject(list)               script "drop": the list of the next E_DROP
  hclip_img_set(fmt, data)        headless CLIP_PNG (bytes) / CLIP_IMAGE (a CLIPIMG
                                  record: png then bmp, see clipimg.rs): PNG + BMP copies
  hclip_img_get(fmt)              CLIP_PNG: the PNG; CLIP_IMAGE: the PNG, else the BMP
  hclip_img_clear()   hclip_img_has(fmt)
```

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn drop_count(&self) -> u32`**
  ui_drop_count: files of the last drop
- **`fn drop_path(&self, i: u32) -> Option<&str>`**
  ui_drop_path(i): path i of the last drop (None when out of range)
- **`fn drop_list(&self) -> &[String]`**
  ui_drop_list: every path of the last drop
- **`fn drop_pos(&self) -> (i32, i32)`**
  ui_drop_pos: drop position in DIP of the window it happened in
- **`fn drop_window(&self) -> u32`**
  ui_drop_window: the app id of the window of the last drop (0 = main window)
- **`fn clip_has(&self, fmt: u32) -> bool`**
  ui_clip_has(fmt): the clipboard offers fmt (cheap: no data is transferred)

#### `ui/clipimg.rs`

Source: [`lib/ui/clipimg.rs`](../lib/ui/clipimg.rs)

```text
clipimg -- (K1) images on the clipboard                            [src/ui/ui_clipimg.s]

  clip_set_image(sf) -> 0 / -1        sf (FMT_ARGB straight, FMT_PARGB or FMT_XRGB) as
                                      CLIP_IMAGE: PNG (deflate level 1) + a 32-bit BMP
                                      (BITMAPV5HEADER, BI_BITFIELDS, straight alpha)
  clip_get_image() -> Ok(sf) / Err    the clipboard image (CLIP_IMAGE: PNG, else the DIB /
                                      BMP) decoded into a new FMT_ARGB surface (straight,
                                      like paint.net layers); ERR_FAIL (-1) = none
  clip_bmp_from_sf(sf) -> BMP file    the BMP of clip_set_image

A CLIP_IMAGE clipboard record (the asm's CLIPIMG: PNG and BMP pointers) travels as bytes:
u64 PNG length (LE), the PNG, u64 BMP length, the BMP (clipimg_record / clipimg_parse);
plat::clip_set(CLIP_IMAGE, ..) takes this record.
```

- **`fn clipimg_record(png: Option<&[u8]>, bmp: Option<&[u8]>) -> Vec<u8>`**
  the CLIP_IMAGE record: u64 PNG length, PNG, u64 BMP length, BMP

- **`fn clipimg_parse(r: &[u8]) -> Option<(Option<&[u8]>, Option<&[u8]>)>`**
  the parts of a CLIP_IMAGE record (an empty part = none); None when it is malformed

- **`fn clip_bmp_from_sf(sf: &Surface) -> Option<Vec<u8>>`**
  clip_bmp_from_sf(sf) -> the BMP file of clip_set_image (None: an empty / huge surface or
  another format)

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn clip_set_image(&mut self, sf: &Surface) -> i32`**
  ui_clip_set_image(sf) -> 0 / -1: sf as CLIP_IMAGE (PNG level 1 + BMP)
- **`fn clip_get_image(&mut self) -> Result<Surface, i32>`**
  ui_clip_get_image -> the clipboard image in a new FMT_ARGB surface, or ERR_\* (-1 =
  no image)

#### `ui/dirty.rs`

Source: [`lib/ui/dirty.rs`](../lib/ui/dirty.rs)

```text
dirty -- dirty regions: only redraw and present what changed        [src/ui/ui_dirty.s]

  dirty_compute(w, h) -> n         number of dirty rectangles (Ui::drects: x, y, w, h,
                                   clipped to w x h), -1 = everything.  Hashes the
                                   recorded commands of every layer into 32 x 32 px cells
                                   and compares with the previous frame.
  dirty_force_full()               the next dirty_compute reports "everything"
  dirty_free()                     forget the live window's cells (a closed window)
  canvas_damage(id, x, y, w, h)    (U1) explicit damage: the part x, y, w, h (px,
                                   relative to the canvas) of canvas `id` is redrawn with
                                   the next render even when its command did not change
  dmg_canvas_seen(id)              canvas() remembers its rectangle and window

A command's hash covers all of its content (colours, coordinates, text, a custom draw
function's identity and version) and the clip it is drawn in, so any visible change
marks its cells.
```

**`impl Ui`**

- **`fn dirty_force_full(&mut self)`**
  dirty_force_full: the next frame redraws and presents everything
- **`fn canvas_damage(&mut self, id: u32, x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32)`**
  ui_canvas_damage(id, x, y, w, h): redraw part of a canvas with the next render (px
  relative to the canvas, cut to it)

### Style contract

Source: [`lib/ui/style.rs`](../lib/ui/style.rs)

```text
style -- the contract between the core and a style                (ASMLIB/inc/style.inc)

The core holds every widget's logic (measuring, placing, input, state, semantics); how
things look comes from exactly one style crate, chosen when the app is linked
(build_rs.py --style S, the app passes `&style::STYLE` to run).  Size functions return
the widget's size and may keep what they computed in Sp::s for the paint call; paint
functions see the placed rectangle and the state and emit draw commands -- no layout,
no input, no semantic records.

The contract is split like the files of a style (ASMLIB/styles/<name>/*.s): Style (theme,
type ramp, fonts) and one sub-trait per widget family -- StyleBasic (basic.s), StyleValue
(value.s), StyleEdit (edit.s), StyleList (list.s), StylePopup (popup.s), StyleChrome
(chrome.s), StyleMisc (misc.s).  R5: the default bodies (empty / zero) exist only while
the widget families are being ported; a finished style overrides every function.
```

- **`struct Sp<'a>`**
  the SP record: inputs of a widget's size / paint call (SP_\* of style.inc)
  - `x: i32` -- placed rectangle (px; filled before the paint call)
  - `y: i32`
  - `w: i32`
  - `h: i32`
  - `state: u32` -- 0 rest, 1 hover, 2 pressed, 3 disabled
  - `ev: u32` -- EV_\* of behave()
  - `flags: u32` -- ST_\* / BOX_\* / MIF_\* / RG_\* flags of the widget
  - `icon: u32` -- icon code point (0 = none)
  - `label: Option<&'a str>`
  - `value: i32`
  - `value2: i32`
  - `s: [i32; 4]` -- kept by the style from its size call to its paint call (SP_S0 .. SP_S3)
  - `label2: Option<&'a str>` -- the 8-byte SP_VALUE variants: a second text (menu accelerator, info bar message)
  - `f: f64` -- ... an f64 (angle degrees)
  - `img: Option<&'a Rc<gfx::Surface>>` -- SP_LABEL variants: a thumbnail (pan), a TRACK record (value sliders: flags, n, stops)
  - `img_ver: u32`
  - `track: Option<&'a Track>`

**`impl Sp<'_>`**

- **`fn rect(&self) -> [i32; 4]`**
- **`fn place(&mut self, r: [i32; 4])`**

- **`trait Style: StyleBasic + StyleValue + StyleEdit + StyleList + StylePopup + StyleChrome + StyleMisc + Sync`**
  a style: tokens, type ramp, fonts and every widget family's paint functions
  - `fn ribbon_height(&self) -> i32`
    Ribbon height in DIP. Existing styles retain the original 110-DIP layout.
  - `fn ribbon_padding(&self) -> u32`
    Outer ribbon padding in DIP, independently styled from control content heights.
  - `fn ribbon_preview(&self, _typography: u32) -> Option<(u32, i32, u32)>`
    Optional document-style gallery face, size in 24.8 DIP and ink color.
    None preserves the ordinary UI type ramp in existing style previews.
  - `fn ribbon_metrics(&self) -> crate::RibbonMetrics`
    Group sizing and overflow policy. Existing styles preserve compact flyouts.
  - `fn preview_card_offset(&self) -> Option<[i32; 2]>`
    Optional optical offset of a preview card in 24.8 DIP. Opts into the
    fractional panel painter; None keeps the original integer painter.
  - `fn tokens(&self, dark: bool) -> [u32; T_COUNT]`
    the T_\* tokens (light or dark; accent entries may be 0: accent() fills them)
  - `fn accent(&self, theme: &mut [u32; T_COUNT], dark: bool, rgb: u32)`
    fill T_ACCENT, \_HOVER, \_PRESSED and T_ACCENT_TEXT from 0xRRGGBB (0 = the default)
  - `fn ts_size(&self) -> [u8; 8]`
    font px / line px / FONT_\* of TS_CAPTION .. TS_DISPLAY (+ one spare) at 100 %
  - `fn ts_size_fx(&self) -> [u16; 8]`
    Font sizes in 24.8 DIP. The default keeps every existing integer type ramp exact.
  - `fn ts_line(&self) -> [u8; 8]`
  - `fn ts_font(&self) -> [u8; 8]`
  - `fn fonts(&self) -> [&'static [u8]; 4]`
    Embedded SGF1 or TrueType/OpenType assets of FONT_REG, FONT_SEMI, FONT_BOLD, FONT_ICON.
  - `fn font_raster(&self) -> [text::FontRaster; 4]`
    Raster policy for the four embedded UI faces. Runtime/document fonts keep
    their own defaults. Existing styles preserve their pixel output.
  - `fn font_names(&self) -> [[&'static str; 5]; 4]`
    per embedded font: family, style, full, typographic family, typographic style
    (sty_font_names)
  - `fn paint_icon(&self, _g: &mut crate::Gfx, _cp: u32, _x: i32, _y: i32, _size_fx: i32, _color: u32, _accent: u32, _dark: bool, _disabled: bool, _dpi: u32) -> bool`
    Paint a semantic icon with the shared rasterizer; false uses the icon font.
    Coordinates are target pixels, size is 24.8 pixels; dpi permits fixed DIP details.
    Application icon hooks
    run first. Existing styles retain their original monochrome font rendering.
  - `fn data(&self) -> &'static dyn StyleData`
    The painters of the data views (grids, trees, badges, meters, charts).  The default
    paints them from the theme tokens (stock.rs), so every style shows them; a style
    returns its own StyleData to give them its look.
  - `fn shell(&self) -> &'static dyn StyleShell`
    The painters of the app shell parts (navigation pane, command bar, splitter,
    breadcrumb, segmented control, wizard steps, toasts, spinner, calendar).  Default:
    token-based (stock.rs).

- **`trait StyleData`**
  data views: grid header / rows, tree rows, badges, status dots, meters, charts  (data.rs,
  chart.rs).  Every method has a token-based default (stock.rs); a style overrides what it
  wants to look different.
  - `fn grid_metrics(&self, _ui: &Ui) -> (i32, i32, i32)`
    (header strip height, row height, cell padding) in DIP
  - `fn grid_header_bg(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
    the header strip behind the header cells (rect = the strip)
  - `fn grid_header(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
    header cell: label, ev, flags GH_\*; s[0] = the label inset (px)
  - `fn grid_row(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
    row background: ev, value 1 = selected, value2 = row index, flags GR_\*
  - `fn tree_twisty(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
    tree expander glyph: value 1 = expanded, ev
  - `fn badge_size(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    badge: flags BADGE_\*, label; size -> (w, h)
  - `fn badge(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn status_dot(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
    status dot: flags BADGE_\*
  - `fn meter(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
    meter (capacity bar): value, value2 maximum, flags BADGE_\* of the fill
  - `fn chart_palette(&self, ui: &Ui) -> [u32; 8]`
    the categorical colours of charts (series 0 .. 7, 0xAARRGGBB)

- **`trait StyleShell`**
  app shell parts: navigation pane and items, command bar, splitter grip, breadcrumb,
  segmented control, wizard steps, toasts, spinner, indeterminate progress, calendar cells
  (shell.rs, notify.rs, datepick.rs).  Token-based defaults in stock.rs.
  - `fn nav_pane(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
    the navigation pane's surface (rect = the pane), flags 1 = compact
  - `fn nav_item_size(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    navigation item: ev, value 1 = selected, icon, label, label2 = badge text, flags
    NAV_\*; size -> (w, h)
  - `fn nav_item(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn nav_section_size(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    section header of the navigation pane: label, flags NAV_COMPACT (then a divider)
  - `fn nav_section(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn command_bar(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
    the bar behind a command bar (rect = the bar)
  - `fn split_grip(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
    splitter grip: ev, flags 1 = vertical split (a horizontal grip)
  - `fn breadcrumb_sep(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
    breadcrumb separator (rect = its place)
  - `fn segment(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
    segmented control item: ev, value 1 = selected, label, flags SEG_FIRST / SEG_LAST
  - `fn wizard_step(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
    wizard step row: value step number (from 1), value2 WS_\* state, label, ev
  - `fn toast(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &mut Sp)`
    toast card: flags IB_\* severity, label title, label2 message; s[0] = close button
    x, s[1] its size (set by the painter, hence &mut)
  - `fn spinner(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
    spinner: value phase 0 .. 1023 (one turn), rect = its square
  - `fn progress_indeterminate(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
    indeterminate progress bar: value phase 0 .. 1023
  - `fn calendar_day(&self, ui: &mut Ui, sp: &Sp)`
    day cell of a calendar: label, value 1 = selected, value2 1 = today, flags 1 = other
    month, ev

- **`trait StyleBasic`**
  buttons, check boxes, radios, switches, sliders, progress, separators  (basic.s)
  - `fn focus_ring(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _r: [i32; 4], _radius_dip: i32)`
    focus visual around rectangle r (px) of a control with that corner radius (DIP)
  - `fn button_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    buttons (ST_ACCENT, ST_SUBTLE, ST_CHECKED, ST_LARGE, ST_COMPACT, ST_ICON_ONLY)
  - `fn button(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn checkbox_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    value 0 / 1 / 2 (indeterminate)
  - `fn checkbox(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn radio_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    value 1 = selected
  - `fn radio(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn switch_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    value 0 / 1, value2 knob position 0 .. 256 (animated)
  - `fn switch(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn slider_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    size: value 1 = vertical; must set s[0] = thumb px along the axis; paint: value thumb
    offset, value2 track length (px)
  - `fn slider(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn progress_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    value, value2 maximum
  - `fn progress(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn separator_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> i32`
    thickness incl. margins; paint: value 1 = horizontal line (in a vertical box)
  - `fn separator(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`

- **`trait StyleValue`**
  value sliders (gradient tracks), angle, pan  (value.s)
  - `fn value_slider_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    flags RG_\*, value t 0..65536 along the travel, track (gradient) or None; state held = 2
  - `fn value_slider(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn value_slider_thumb(&self, _ui: &mut Ui) -> i32`
    thumb length px (travel = length - thumb)
  - `fn angle_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    f degrees, s[0] 1 = focus visual
  - `fn angle(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn pan_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    img thumbnail or None; paint: value, value2 = the point (px)
  - `fn pan(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`

- **`trait StyleEdit`**
  text boxes  (edit.s)
  - `fn textbox_font(&self, ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    Select the single-line input face and return its line height and baseline in px.
    Default preserves the body font; icon inputs may use a compact search-field face.
  - `fn textbox_metrics(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    value natural width (DIP), flags 1 = multi-line (value2 line height px); must set
    s[0], s[1], s[2] = left, right, top text inset (px)
  - `fn textbox_frame(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
    ev (hover), value 1 = editing, value2 1 = disabled; the core draws text, selection, caret
  - `fn textbox_vbar(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
    value view h, value2 content h, s[0] scroll y, s[1] text top (px)

- **`trait StyleList`**
  focus rectangles, scroll bars, list rows, tabs, expanders  (list.s)
  - `fn focus_rect(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _x: i32, _y: i32, _w: i32, _h: i32)`
    focus visual drawn ON a rectangle (rows, tabs, headers)
  - `fn scrollbar_thickness(&self, _ui: &mut Ui) -> i32`
    hover area, track margin at both ends, shortest thumb (px)
  - `fn scrollbar_margin(&self, _ui: &mut Ui) -> i32`
  - `fn scrollbar_min_thumb(&self, _ui: &mut Ui) -> i32`
  - `fn scrollbar(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
    flags 1 = vertical, value expansion 0..256, s[0] track start, s[1] thumb length,
    value2 thumb offset (px)
  - `fn list_drop_line(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
    reorder marker centred on y across x .. + w
  - `fn list_row(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
    ev, value 1 = selected, value2 1 = disabled, flags 1 = dragged row, 2 = focus visual
  - `fn tab_metrics(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp)`
    sets s[0] label padding, s[1] height, s[2] pill width
  - `fn tab_font(&self, ui: &mut Ui, _selected: bool)`
    Select the font used to measure or paint a tab; default preserves the body face.
  - `fn tab(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
    tab: ev, value selected, value2 disabled, label; pill: strip rect, value pill x
  - `fn tab_pill(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn expander_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    header: ev, value open, value2 disabled, label; body: the content card's rectangle
  - `fn expander_header(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn expander_body(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`

- **`trait StylePopup`**
  flyouts, menus, menu bars, drop-downs, split buttons, combo boxes, tooltips  (popup.s)
  - `fn flyout_pad(&self, _ui: &mut Ui) -> i32`
    px
  - `fn flyout_box(&self, _ui: &mut Ui) -> u32`
    BOX_\* | PAD_\* flags of the content
  - `fn flyout_frame(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
    shadow + surface of a flyout / menu (rect = the flyout)
  - `fn menu_item_h(&self, _ui: &mut Ui) -> i32`
    row height px
  - `fn menu_item_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    flags MIF_\*, label, label2 accelerator text, icon, ev; size must set s[1] =
    accelerator width
  - `fn menu_item(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn menu_sep_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui) -> i32`
  - `fn menu_sep(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn menubar_item_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    flags 1 open, 2 current item, value mnemonic byte index or -1, label, ev
  - `fn menubar_item(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn dropdown_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    flags ST_\*, icon, label, value open, value2 disabled
  - `fn dropdown(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn split_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    size sets s[0] / s[1] = main / chevron part width; paint: ev main, s[2] chevron
    events, value open, value2 1 main / 2 chevron disabled
  - `fn split(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn combo_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    size: value widest text px; paint: label the selected text, ev, value2 disabled
  - `fn combo(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn tooltip_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    size sets s[0] text width, s[1] / s[2] gap below / above the pointer; paint: label
  - `fn tooltip(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`

- **`trait StyleChrome`**
  layout metrics, dialogs, info bars, status / title bars, caption buttons, window frames
  (chrome.s)
  - `fn navigation_surface(&self, ui: &Ui) -> u32`
    Background for navigation placed on a branded accent surface.
  - `fn metric(&self, _id: u32) -> i32`
    layout value SM_\* (sizes, gaps, box flags, text styles)
  - `fn dialog_smoke(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn dialog_frame(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
    dialog surface (value footer h)
  - `fn infobar_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    size: flags severity, label title, label2 message, sets s[0] / s[1] title / message
    width; paint sets s[2] / s[3] = close button x / size (hence &mut); close: ev
  - `fn infobar(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp)`
  - `fn infobar_close(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn statusbar(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn caption_button(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
    ev, flags HB_\* (HB_CLOSE), icon, value 1 = window active
  - `fn window_titlebar(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
    background of a library title bar: rect = the bar, value 1 = active
  - `fn window_border(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
    value thickness px
  - `fn window_dialog_fill(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`

- **`trait StyleMisc`**
  swatches, colour picker parts, box decorations, window background  (misc.s)
  - `fn swatch_size(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &mut Sp) -> (i32, i32)`
    value colour, value2 1 = selected, ev
  - `fn swatch(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
  - `fn color_preview(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _argb: u32, _r: [i32; 4])`
  - `fn color_marker(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _x_fx: i32, _y_fx: i32)`
    marker at 24.8 px
  - `fn color_wheel(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
    rect = the wheel's square, s[0] radius px, value hue (tenths of a degree), value2
    saturation 0..1000, s[1] 1 = focus visual
  - `fn box_deco(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _sp: &Sp)`
    BOX_BG / BOX_CARD / BOX_LAYER decoration (sp: rectangle, flags)
  - `fn window_bg(&self, _ui: &mut Ui, _w: i32, _h: i32)`
    the background of a window's whole surface (px)

### Stock painters of the data and shell parts

Source: [`lib/ui/stock.rs`](../lib/ui/stock.rs)

```text
stock -- the default painters of the data views and the app shell parts: drawn from the
theme tokens only, so every style shows grids, trees, badges, meters, charts, navigation
panes, command bars, splitters, breadcrumbs, segmented controls, wizard steps, toasts,
spinners and calendars.  A style that wants its own look returns its StyleData /
StyleShell from Style::data / Style::shell and overrides the methods it likes; the others
keep these painters (call them from the override to extend them).

  kind_colors(ui, BADGE_*) -> (text, fill, solid)   the colours of a status kind
  Stock                                             the stock StyleData + StyleShell
```

- **`struct Stock`**
  the stock data-view and shell painters (Style::data / Style::shell default)

- **`fn card_opaque(ui: &Ui) -> u32`**
  the card colour as it shows on the window: a translucent card token (some styles layer
  their cards) composited over the window background

- **`fn kind_colors(ui: &Ui, kind: u32) -> (u32, u32, u32)`**
  the (text, fill, solid) colours of a BADGE_\* kind in the current theme

### Constants

Source: [`lib/ui/consts.rs`](../lib/ui/consts.rs)

```text
consts -- public constants of the immediate-mode UI                (ASMLIB/inc/ui.inc)

Widget calls return event flags.  Bit 0 (EV_ACT) always means "the primary action
happened" (clicked, changed, selected, closed).  Sizes and positions given to the UI are
DIP (device independent pixels, 96 dpi = 100 %); drawing calls and last_rect use
physical pixels.
```

*widget events*

```rust
EV_ACT: u32 = 1  // primary action (click / change / select / close)
EV_COMMIT: u32 = 2  // value committed (drag released, Enter, focus lost)
EV_HOVER: u32 = 4  // pointer is over the widget
EV_HELD: u32 = 8  // pressed and still held
EV_FOCUS: u32 = 16  // widget has keyboard focus
EV_DOUBLE: u32 = 32  // double click
EV_RCLICK: u32 = 64  // right click (released over the widget)
EV_OPEN: u32 = 128  // a popup / dropdown opened
EV_DRAG: u32 = 256  // being dragged (moved while held)
```

*hit box flags (behave)*

```rust
HB_FOCUS: u32 = 1  // reachable with Tab, takes focus on click
HB_BLOCK: u32 = 2  // swallows the pointer (panels, popups)
HB_DRAG: u32 = 4  // window drag region (title bar)
HB_TEXT: u32 = 8  // text input (gets all keys while focused)
HB_MOVES: u32 = 16  // wants a frame on every pointer move while hovered
HB_DISABLED: u32 = 32  // disabled: visible to the dump, no input
HB_MIN: u32 = 64  // caption buttons (for the OS: snap layouts, etc.)
HB_MAX: u32 = 128
HB_CLOSE: u32 = 256
HB_CURSOR_SHIFT: u32 = 12  // cursor shape: flags | CUR_* << HB_CURSOR_SHIFT
```

*semantic kinds and states*

```rust
K_BOX: u16 = 0
K_TEXT: u16 = 1
K_BUTTON: u16 = 2
K_CHECKBOX: u16 = 3
K_RADIO: u16 = 4
K_SWITCH: u16 = 5
K_SLIDER: u16 = 6
K_PROGRESS: u16 = 7
K_TEXTBOX: u16 = 8
K_MENU: u16 = 9
K_MENUITEM: u16 = 10
K_LIST: u16 = 11
K_LISTITEM: u16 = 12
K_TAB: u16 = 13
K_CANVAS: u16 = 14
K_ICON: u16 = 15
K_IMAGE: u16 = 16
K_COMBO: u16 = 17
K_DIALOG: u16 = 18
K_TITLEBAR: u16 = 19
K_SWATCH: u16 = 20
K_LINK: u16 = 21
K_KIND_COUNT: u16 = 22
SS_HOVER: u16 = 1
SS_PRESSED: u16 = 2
SS_FOCUSED: u16 = 4
SS_CHECKED: u16 = 8
SS_DISABLED: u16 = 16
SS_SELECTED: u16 = 32
SS_OPEN: u16 = 64
/// names of the K_* kinds (dump, selectors kind:"Label")
KIND_NAMES: [&str; 22] = [ "box", "text", "button", "checkbox", "radio", "switch", ...
/// names of the SS_* state bits (dump)
STATE_NAMES: [&str; 7] = ["hover", "pressed", "focused", "checked", "disabled", "s ...
```

*layers*

```rust
L_BASE: u32 = 0
L_DIALOG: u32 = 1
L_POPUP: u32 = 2
L_TIP: u32 = 3
L_COUNT: usize = 4
```

*box flags (box_begin)*

```rust
BOX_H: u32 = 0  // children left to right
BOX_V: u32 = 1  // children top to bottom
BOX_CENTER: u32 = 2  // centre children along the main axis
BOX_END: u32 = 4  // align children to the end of the main axis
BOX_XCENTER: u32 = 8  // cross axis: centre children (default: stretch)
BOX_XSTART: u32 = 16  // cross axis: natural size at the start
BOX_XEND: u32 = 32  // cross axis: natural size at the end
BOX_CARD: u32 = 64  // draw a card (fill + stroke, 8 DIP corners)
BOX_BG: u32 = 128  // fill with the window background colour
BOX_CLIP: u32 = 256  // clip children to the box
BOX_BLOCK: u32 = 512  // swallow pointer input over the box
BOX_DISABLED: u32 = 1024  // disable every widget inside
BOX_LAYER: u32 = 2048  // fill with the layer colour (command bars)
BOX_WRAP: u32 = 4096  // (horizontal) start a new row when full
BOX_FIXED: u32 = 8192  // the box's size never depends on its content
PAD_SHIFT: u32 = 16  // padding in DIP: flags | N << PAD_SHIFT
PAD_2: u32 = 2 << 16
PAD_4: u32 = 4 << 16
PAD_8: u32 = 8 << 16
PAD_12: u32 = 12 << 16
PAD_16: u32 = 16 << 16
PAD_20: u32 = 20 << 16
PAD_24: u32 = 24 << 16
PAD_32: u32 = 32 << 16
```

- **`const fn pad(dip: u32) -> u32`**
  padding in DIP for box_begin flags: BOX_V | pad(24)

```rust
// next_size values: > 0 fixed DIP, 0 = natural size, < 0 = fill weight
SIZE_AUTO: i32 = 0
SIZE_FILL: i32 = -1
```

*text styles (text)*

```rust
TS_CAPTION: u32 = 0  // 12 / 16
TS_BODY: u32 = 1  // 14 / 20
TS_BODY_STRONG: u32 = 2  // 14 / 20 semibold
TS_SUBTITLE: u32 = 3  // 20 / 28 semibold
TS_TITLE: u32 = 4  // 28 / 36 semibold
TS_TITLE_LARGE: u32 = 5  // 40 / 52 semibold
TS_DISPLAY: u32 = 6  // 68 / 92 semibold
TS_COUNT: u32 = 7
TX_SECONDARY: u32 = 0x100  // secondary text colour
TX_TERTIARY: u32 = 0x200
TX_ACCENT: u32 = 0x400  // accent text colour
TX_WRAP: u32 = 0x800  // wrap at the box width
TX_ELLIPSIS: u32 = 0x1000  // cut with "…" when too long
TX_CENTER: u32 = 0x2000  // horizontal alignment
TX_RIGHT: u32 = 0x4000
TX_DISABLED: u32 = 0x8000
TX_ON_ACCENT: u32 = 0x10000  // text on an accent fill (T_ON_ACCENT)
```

*widget style flags (next_flags)*

```rust
ST_DISABLED: u32 = 1
ST_LARGE: u32 = 2  // icon button: big icon above the label
ST_SUBTLE: u32 = 4  // transparent button (toolbar style)
ST_ACCENT: u32 = 8  // accent button
ST_CHECKED: u32 = 16  // toggle / tool buttons: checked look
ST_COMPACT: u32 = 32  // smaller height
ST_NOFOCUS: u32 = 64  // not reachable with Tab
ST_ICON_ONLY: u32 = 128  // hide the label (tooltip only)
ST_ALIGN_START: u32 = 256  // start-aligned button content; styles may ellipsize long labels
/// Button displayed on an accent-colored surface, using its on-accent foreground.
ST_ON_ACCENT: u32 = 512
/// Button with a menu indicator beside its label; use the normal popup API for behavior.
ST_MENU: u32 = 1024
/// Rounded identity/action button; supporting styles use a circular or pill surface.
ST_ROUND: u32 = 2048
```

*built-in commands (App::command ids)*

```rust
UIC_CLOSE: u32 = 0x7FFF_0001  // window close requested: return true to keep the window
UIC_THEME: u32 = 0x7FFF_0002  // theme changed
UIC_DPI: u32 = 0x7FFF_0003  // dpi changed
UIC_ESCAPE: u32 = 0x7FFF_0004  // Esc not consumed by any widget
UIC_TIMER: u32 = 0x7FFF_0005  // arg = timer id
UIC_WINCLOSE: u32 = 0x7FFF_0006  // arg = window id: close requested; true keeps it
UIC_DROP: u32 = 0x7FFF_0007  // arg = window id: files dropped (drop_*)
UIC_JOB: u32 = 0x7FFF_0008  // arg = job id finished
UIC_WAKE: u32 = 0x7FFF_0009  // plat::wake() from another thread
UIC_EXIT: u32 = 0x7FFF_000A  // the window loop ends (only after a real window ran)
```

*app flags (AppDesc)*

```rust
AF_CUSTOM_TITLEBAR: u32 = 1
AF_RESIZABLE: u32 = 2
AF_DARK: u32 = 4  // force dark theme
AF_LIGHT: u32 = 8  // force light theme
```

*theme tokens (color)*

```rust
// byte offsets as in ASMLIB; index = token / 4
T_BG: u32 = 0  // window background (Mica fallback)
T_BG_ALT: u32 = 4
T_LAYER: u32 = 8  // layer fill (command bars, panels)
T_LAYER_STROKE: u32 = 12
T_CARD: u32 = 16
T_CARD_STROKE: u32 = 20
T_CTRL: u32 = 24  // control fill: rest, hover, pressed, disabled (24..36)
T_CTRL_HOVER: u32 = 28
T_CTRL_PRESSED: u32 = 32
T_CTRL_DISABLED: u32 = 36
T_CTRL_STROKE: u32 = 40  // control border
T_CTRL_STROKE2: u32 = 44  // control border, bottom edge
T_STRONG_STROKE: u32 = 48  // checkbox / radio border
T_SUBTLE: u32 = 52  // transparent button: rest, hover, pressed, disabled (52..64)
T_SUBTLE_HOVER: u32 = 56
T_SUBTLE_PRESSED: u32 = 60
T_SUBTLE_DISABLED: u32 = 64
T_TEXT: u32 = 68  // text: primary, secondary, tertiary, disabled (68..80)
T_TEXT2: u32 = 72
T_TEXT3: u32 = 76
T_TEXT_DISABLED: u32 = 80
T_ACCENT: u32 = 84  // accent fill: rest, hover, pressed, disabled (84..96)
T_ACCENT_HOVER: u32 = 88
T_ACCENT_PRESSED: u32 = 92
T_ACCENT_DISABLED: u32 = 96
T_ON_ACCENT: u32 = 100  // text on accent
T_ON_ACCENT_DIS: u32 = 104
T_ACCENT_TEXT: u32 = 108  // accent-coloured text / links
T_FOCUS_OUT: u32 = 112
T_FOCUS_IN: u32 = 116
T_FLYOUT: u32 = 120  // menus, popups
T_FLYOUT_STROKE: u32 = 124
T_SMOKE: u32 = 128  // dim behind dialogs
T_DIVIDER: u32 = 132
T_INPUT_ACTIVE: u32 = 136  // focused text box fill
T_CRIT: u32 = 140
T_OK: u32 = 144
T_WARN: u32 = 148
T_INFO: u32 = 152
T_CRIT_BG: u32 = 156
T_OK_BG: u32 = 160
T_WARN_BG: u32 = 164
T_INFO_BG: u32 = 168
T_CLOSE_HOVER: u32 = 172
T_SHADOW: u32 = 176
T_SCROLL: u32 = 180  // scroll bar thumb
T_TRACK: u32 = 184  // slider / progress track
T_KNOB: u32 = 188  // solid control fill (slider thumb, toggle knob)
T_ALT_FILL: u32 = 192  // toggle off background
T_STRONG_FILL: u32 = 196  // toggle off knob / strong fill
T_COUNT: usize = 50
```

- **`struct Range`**
  RANGE min, max, step, flags -- slider / number box descriptor
  - `min: i32`
  - `max: i32`
  - `step: i32`
  - `flags: u32`

**`impl Range`**

- **`const fn new(min: i32, max: i32, step: i32, flags: u32) -> Range`**

```rust
RG_VERT: u32 = 1  // vertical slider
RG_TICKS: u32 = 2
RG_TIP: u32 = 4  // value tooltip while dragging
/// Logarithmic pointer/paint mapping for integer sliders with positive bounds.
/// Keyboard increments, semantic values and range endpoints retain their units.
RG_LOG: u32 = 8
LF_REORDER: u32 = 0x10000  // list_begin: rows can be dragged to a new place
// decimal values, colour tracks, angle, pan, colour wheel (ui_value.s, ui_wheel.s)
TK_CHECKER: u32 = 1  // draw the gradient over a checkerboard (alpha tracks)
TK_CUSTOM: u32 = 2  // the track is drawn by a custom function (no stops)
TK_RECT: u32 = 4  // square ends, the gradient spans the whole rectangle
TK_MAXN: usize = 16
ANG_360: u32 = 1  // angle: 0 .. 360 instead of -180 .. 180
PAN_UNIT: u32 = 1  // pan: coordinates 0 .. 1 instead of -1 .. 1
CW_HSV: u32 = 1  // color_wheel: an HSV record instead of 0xAARRGGBB
CP_ALPHA: u32 = 1  // color_picker_ex: alpha (transparency) track
CP_FIELDS: u32 = 2  // ... number boxes R G B (A) H S V and a hex text box
PART_BASE: u32 = 0x6000_0000  // part_id(id, k): ids of the parts of composite widgets
```

- **`struct UiInput`**
  UIINPUT -- pointer state handed to custom widgets (canvas)
  - `ev: u32` -- EV_\* flags
  - `btn: u32` -- buttons held (bit 1 &lt;&lt; BTN_\*)
  - `pressed: u32` -- buttons pressed this frame
  - `released: u32` -- buttons released this frame
  - `x: i32` -- pointer x relative to the widget (px)
  - `y: i32`
  - `press_x: i32` -- position of the last press (px, relative)
  - `press_y: i32`
  - `wheel_x: i32` -- wheel delta (120 per notch)
  - `wheel_y: i32`
  - `mods: u32`
  - `pts: alloc::vec::Vec<[i32; 2]>` -- move points since the last frame (relative px)
  - `rect: [i32; 4]` -- widget rectangle (absolute px)
  - `dpi: i32`
  - `key: u32` -- first unconsumed key this frame (VK | mods &lt;&lt; 16) or 0
  - `pressure: u32` -- pen pressure now: 0 = none (mouse), 1..UI_PRESSURE_ONE
  - `ptype: u32` -- PTR_MOUSE / PTR_PEN / PTR_ERASER / PTR_TOUCH
  - `ppress: alloc::vec::Vec<u32>` -- pressure per move point
  - `tilt_x: i32` -- pen tilt in degrees (-63..63), 0 without tilt
  - `tilt_y: i32`

```rust
UI_PRESSURE_ONE: u32 = 65535
```
