- **`struct Hdr`**
  the SGF1-compatible header every font kind has (font_data): what gc_scale and the
  text metrics read.  i16 fields sign-extended, upem / nglyphs zero-extended.
  - `flags: u16`
  - `upem: i32`
  - `asc: i32`
  - `desc: i32`
  - `gap: i32`
  - `caph: i32`
  - `xh: i32`
  - `ulpos: i32`
  - `ulthick: i32`
  - `nglyphs: u32`
  - `baseid: u32` -- derived fonts: base font id + 1 (0 = none)

- **`struct Metrics`**
  FONTMETRICS (font.inc): all values in font units (y up)
  - `upem: i32`
  - `asc: i32`
  - `desc: i32`
  - `gap: i32`
  - `typo_asc: i32`
  - `typo_desc: i32`
  - `typo_gap: i32`
  - `win_asc: i32`
  - `win_desc: i32`
  - `xh: i32`
  - `caph: i32`
  - `ul_pos: i32`
  - `ul_thick: i32`
  - `st_pos: i32`
  - `st_thick: i32`
  - `weight: i32`
  - `flags: i32`
  - `nglyphs: i32`
  - `advmax: i32`
  - `width: i32`

**`impl Metrics`**

- **`fn field(&self, off: u32) -> i32`**
  the field at byte offset FM_\* (0 for an invalid field)  [font_metric]
- **`fn as_array(&self) -> [i32; 20]`**

- **`struct Gp`**
  the glyph transform (font.s g_gp): origin in float pixels, pixels per font unit,
  composite offset in font units
  - `x: f32`
  - `y: f32`
  - `sx: f32`
  - `sy: f32`
  - `dx: i32`
  - `dy: i32`

- **`struct Font`**
  - `flags: u16`
  - `upem: i32`
  - `asc: i32`
  - `desc: i32`
  - `gap: i32`
  - `caph: i32`
  - `xh: i32`
  - `ulpos: i32`
  - `ulthick: i32`
  - `nglyphs: u32`
  - `fallback: u32`

**`impl Font`**

- **`fn parse(data: &'static [u8]) -> Option<Font>`**
  font_register's parse: None when the data is not SGF1
- **`fn new(data: &'static [u8]) -> Font`**
- **`fn hdr(&self) -> Hdr`**
- **`fn lookup(&self, cp: u32) -> Option<u32>`**
  binary search over the ranges {u32 firstCp, u16 count, u16 firstGlyph}  [font_lookup]
- **`fn glyph(&self, cp: u32) -> u32`**
- **`fn adv(&self, g: u32) -> i32`**
- **`fn kern(&self, g1: u32, g2: u32) -> i32`**
  kern(l, r) = matrix[lclass[l] \* kernR + rclass[r]] \* 4 font units
- **`fn glyph_box(&self, g: u32) -> Option<[i32; 4]>`**
- **`fn path(&self, g: u32, gp: &mut Gp, path: &mut Path, depth: u32)`**
  glyph_path: append the outline transformed by gp (implied on-curve midpoints,
  composites recursively)
- **`fn outline(&self, g: u32, dx: i32, dy: i32, depth: u32, ol: &mut Outline)`**
  append glyph g (composites resolved) to the outline buffer: points in font units +
  (dx, dy), one contour per SGF1 contour  [sgf_outline]

### `text/fontdb.rs`

Source: [`lib/text/fontdb.rs`](../lib/text/fontdb.rs)

```text
fontdb -- system font database: enumerate the installed fonts, group them into families,
match styles, cache the result                                      [src/text/fontdb.s]

  scan(flags) -> faces found      walk the font folders (recursively, depth <= 8) and read
       only the table directory, name, OS/2 and head of every .ttf / .otf / .ttc / .otc;
       files already in a loaded cache with the same size are not opened again (flags
       FDB_RESCAN: ignore the cache).  Folders: jfgui_FONTDIRS (';' separated, on Linux
       also ':'), else Windows %WINDIR%/Fonts and %LOCALAPPDATA%/Microsoft/Windows/Fonts;
       Linux /usr/share/fonts, /usr/local/share/fonts, $XDG_DATA_HOME/fonts (else
       ~/.local/share/fonts), ~/.fonts (directories are visited once: symlink loops)
  clear()                         forget the scan result (the cache stays)
  family_count(), family_name(i), family_faces(i) -> &[FdFace]   families sorted
       case-insensitively
  face_count(), face(i)           every face, family order
  find(family, weight, italic) -> (face index, FS_SYN_* flags to add)   family = legacy
       family name (FN_FAMILY, like GDI), else a typographic family; closest weight,
       italic before upright; synthetic bold / italic asked when the family lacks them
  load(text, family, weight, italic) -> font id / ERR_*   find + load_file (+ style);
       each (face, style) is loaded once until the next scan
  save_cache(path) / load_cache(path) -> 0 / -1

Families group faces by the legacy family name (name ID 1); names prefer German, then
English.  Cache file (little endian): "FDB1", u32 version 2, u32 files, u32 faces, u32
pool bytes, u64 FNV-1a hash of everything after the 28-byte header; files {u32 path, u32
size, u32 first face, u32 faces}; faces {u32 file, u32 face index, u16 weight, u16 flags,
u32 family, u32 style, u32 full, u32 typo family, u32 0}; pool of NUL-terminated UTF-8
strings (offsets above point into it).  Entries are validated by path and file size.
```

```rust
FDB_RESCAN: u32 = 1
FDFF_ITALIC: u16 = 1
FDFF_BOLD: u16 = 2
FDFF_OBLIQUE: u16 = 4
FDFF_CFF: u16 = 8
FDFF_COLOR: u16 = 16
FDFF_VAR: u16 = 32
FDFF_SYMBOL: u16 = 64
```

- **`struct FdFace`**
  a face of the database (FDFACE)
  - `path: String`
  - `style: String` -- FN_STYLE
  - `full: String` -- FN_FULL
  - `family: String` -- FN_FAMILY, the grouping key
  - `tfamily: String` -- FN_TYPO_FAMILY
  - `face: u32` -- face index inside the file (TTC / OTC)
  - `weight: u16` -- usWeightClass
  - `flags: u16` -- FDFF_\*

- **`struct FontDb`**

- **`fn fnv64(d: &[u8]) -> u64`**
  FNV-1a 64 (hash_fnv64, seed 0 = the offset basis)

**`impl FontDb`**

- **`fn new() -> FontDb`**
- **`fn clear(&mut self)`**
  fontdb_clear(): forget the scan result (the cache stays)
- **`fn family_count(&self) -> usize`**
- **`fn family_name(&self, i: usize) -> &str`**
  the family's name ("" when i is out of range)
- **`fn family_faces(&self, i: usize) -> &[FdFace]`**
  the faces of family i (empty when out of range)
- **`fn face_count(&self) -> usize`**
- **`fn face(&self, i: usize) -> Option<&FdFace>`**
- **`fn scan(&mut self, flags: u32) -> usize`**
  fontdb_scan(flags) -> number of faces
- **`fn find(&self, family: &str, weight: u32, italic: bool) -> Option<(usize, u32)>`**
  fontdb_find(family, weight, italic) -> (face index, FS_SYN_\* flags to add)
- **`fn load(&mut self, text: &mut Text, family: &str, weight: u32, italic: bool) -> i32`**
  fontdb_load(text, family, weight, italic) -> font id / ERR_\*
- **`fn save_cache(&self, path: &str) -> i32`**
  fontdb_save_cache(path) -> 0 / -1
- **`fn load_cache(&mut self, path: &str) -> i32`**
  fontdb_load_cache(path) -> 0 / -1 (a malformed file is rejected as a whole)

### `text/glyphcache.rs`

Source: [`lib/text/glyphcache.rs`](../lib/text/glyphcache.rs)

```text
glyphcache -- rasterized glyph masks, cached by (font, glyph, size, x phase)
                                                                 [src/text/glyphcache.s]

  gc_get(font, glyph, size_fx, phase) -> Entry / None (nothing to draw) / Direct (too
       large to cache: draw it with glyph_path + fill).  size_fx: pixel size (em height)
       in 24.8; phase: 0..3 quarter pixels.  Entry: left, top (relative to pen x /
       baseline y), w, h, w*h coverage bytes.  An empty glyph is an entry of 0 x 0.
  gc_get_bitmap(font, glyph, size_fx) -> colour bitmap entry (CBDT fonts): w*h PARGB
       pixels; bitmaps larger than GC_BIGBMP bytes are not cached (valid until the next one)
  gc_box(font, glyph, size_fx, phase) -> {left, top, w, h} as gc_get computes it
  gc_flush()   gc_scale(font, size_fx) -> (sx, sy) pixels per font unit (sy snapped)

Hinting-free: for sizes up to 24 px the vertical scale is adjusted so the x-height lands
on a whole pixel; horizontally only quarter-pixel phases.  Key: 1<<63 | colour<<62 |
font<<40 (22 bits) | phase<<36 | size<<16 (20 bits) | glyph (16 bits).  Like the
assembly the cache starts over when it holds GC_SLOTS / 2 entries or its 2 MB arena is
full.
```

- **`struct GcEntry`**
  a cached glyph: placement relative to the pen x / baseline, w x h coverage bytes (masks)
  or PARGB pixels (bitmaps)
  - `left: i32`
  - `top: i32`
  - `w: i32`
  - `h: i32`
  - `px: Vec<u8>`

- **`enum GcRef<'a>`**
  - `None`
  - `Direct`
  - `Entry(&'a GcEntry)`

**`impl Text`**

- **`fn gc_flush(&mut self)`**
  gc_flush(): forget every cached glyph
- **`fn gc_scale(&self, font: u32, size_fx: i32) -> (f32, f32)`**
  gc_scale(font, size_fx) -> (sx, sy): pixels per font unit, sy snapped so the
  x-height is whole up to 24 px
- **`fn gc_box(&mut self, font: u32, g: u32, size: i32, phase: i32) -> Option<[i32; 4]>`**
  gc_box(font, glyph, size_fx, phase) -> {left, top, w, h} (not cached)
- **`fn gc_get(&mut self, font: u32, g: u32, size: i32, phase: i32) -> GcRef<'_>`**
  gc_get(font, glyph, size_fx, phase): the cached mask, rendered on first use
- **`fn gc_get_bitmap(&mut self, font: u32, g: u32, size: i32) -> Option<&GcEntry>`**
  gc_get_bitmap(font, glyph, size_fx): the PARGB colour bitmap entry or None

### `text/outline.rs`

Source: [`lib/text/outline.rs`](../lib/text/outline.rs)

```text
outline -- glyph outline buffer, path emitter and the operations of synthetic styles
                                                                      [src/text/outline.s]

  Outline::reset / point / reserve / close / contour / npts / bbox
  emit(path, gp, zones)       append the buffer to the path with the gp transform (TrueType
                              contours exactly like glyph_path; cubic contours flattened like
                              path_cubic); zones = FS_SHARP grid fitting (fit_zones)
  embolden(strength)          FreeType FT_Outline_EmboldenXY(strength, strength)
  shear(k), transform(from, a, b, c, d), translate(from, dx, dy)
  owner_set / owner_is        "prepared" tracking: a box query leaves the untransformed
                              outline of (font context, glyph) in the buffer

Points f32 x, y (font units, y up) + u8 tags; contour ends u32 (last point index).
TrueType contours (OLT_ON / OLT_OFF) follow the implied-midpoint rules; cubic contours
(CFF) start with an on-curve point, two OLT_CUBIC points between on-curve points.
```

```rust
OLT_OFF: u8 = 0  // quadratic control point
OLT_ON: u8 = 1  // on-curve point
OLT_CUBIC: u8 = 2  // cubic control point
OL_MAX_PTS: usize = 1 << 20
OL_MAX_CONT: usize = 1 << 18
F_TAN12: f32 = 0.21255656
F_INV30: f32 = 0.033333335
```

- **`struct Zones`**
  FS_SHARP zones: {z (font units), p (px)} ascending
  - `n: usize`
  - `z: [(f32, f32); ZONE_MAX]`

**`impl Zones`**

- **`fn fit(desc: i32, xh: i32, caph: i32, asc: i32, sy: f32) -> Zones`**
  descender, 0, x-height, cap height, ascender snapped with sy  [ol_fit_zones]

- **`struct Outline`**
  - `pts: Vec<(f32, f32)>`
  - `tags: Vec<u8>`
  - `ends: Vec<u32>`
  - `err: bool`

**`impl Outline`**

- **`fn reset(&mut self)`**
  empty the buffer (owner cleared)  [ol_reset]
- **`fn npts(&self) -> usize`**
- **`fn point(&mut self, x: f32, y: f32, tag: u8) -> bool`**
  append a point (font units, OLT_\*)  [ol_point]
- **`fn reserve(&mut self, n: usize) -> Option<usize>`**
  append n points (0, 0, OLT_OFF) to be filled in; the index of the first  [ol_reserve]
- **`fn close(&mut self)`**
  end the current contour  [ol_close]
- **`fn contour(&mut self, last: u32) -> bool`**
  end a contour at point index last (last &lt; cstart: empty, ignored)  [ol_contour]
- **`fn owner_set(&mut self, serial: u64, glyph: u32)`**
- **`fn owner_is(&self, serial: u64, glyph: u32) -> bool`**
- **`fn bbox(&self) -> Option<[i32; 4]>`**
  control box {xmin, ymin, xmax, ymax} (floor / ceil, clamped to +-1e6)  [ol_box]
- **`fn shear(&mut self, k: f32)`**
  x += y \* k  [ol_shear]
- **`fn transform(&mut self, from: usize, a: f32, b: f32, c: f32, d: f32)`**
  points from..n: (x, y) -> (a x + c y, b x + d y)  [ol_transform]
- **`fn translate(&mut self, from: usize, dx: f32, dy: f32)`**
  points from..n += (dx, dy)  [ol_translate]
- **`fn embolden(&mut self, strength: f32)`**
  FreeType FT_Outline_EmboldenXY(strength, strength): every edge moves outwards by
  strength / 2, the glyph grows up / right by strength  [ol_embolden]
- **`fn emit(&self, path: &mut Path, gp: &Gp, zones: Option<&Zones>)`**
  append every contour to the path (gp transform, composite offset dx / dy honoured;
  zones: FS_SHARP vertical fitting)  [ol_emit]

- **`fn cubic(path: &mut Path, p: [f32; 8])`**
  flatten the cubic {p0, c1, c2, p3} (device px; p0 is the current point) into the path,
  the subdivision of path_cubic (tolerance 0.2 px)  [ol_cubic]

### `text/rendering.rs`

Source: [`lib/text/rendering.rs`](../lib/text/rendering.rs)

```text
Per-face raster policy. Layout advances, document fonts and legacy renderers
keep their original contracts; styles opt in only for their own UI faces.
```

- **`enum FontRaster`**
  Glyph rasterization independent of font metrics and text editing coordinates.
  - `Legacy` -- Original x-height fitting and downward quarter-pixel positioning.
  - `Natural` -- Preserve the outline scale and round to the nearest quarter pixel.

**`impl Text`**

- **`fn font_raster(&self, font: u32) -> FontRaster`**
  Raster policy of a registered face. Unknown ids follow the fallback face.
- **`fn set_font_raster(&mut self, font: u32, raster: FontRaster) -> bool`**
  Change only this face's rasterization; invalidate cached masks on change.
  Returns false for an unregistered id. Measuring and caret advances are unchanged.

### `text/registry.rs`

Source: [`lib/text/registry.rs`](../lib/text/registry.rs)

```text
registry -- the font registry: embedded SGF1 fonts, runtime fonts (ttf.rs) and the derived
fonts of font_style (synthetic bold / italic / sharp)       [src/text/font.s, outline.s]

  register(data) -> id / -1            add an SGF1 font
  load_mem(data, face) / load_file(path, face) -> id / ERR_*   runtime fonts (ttf.rs)
  style(font, flags) -> id / ERR_*     derived font with FS_SYN_BOLD (outline dilated by
       upem/30, advance + upem/30), FS_SYN_ITALIC (x += y * tan 12 deg) and/or FS_SHARP
       (vertical grid fitting); flags 0 = font itself; the same (font, flags) pair always
       gives the same id
  unload(id) -> 0 / -1                 a runtime font and every font derived from it; the
       glyph cache is flushed; the id may be reused later
  count(), valid(id), hdr(font) (font_data), glyph(font, cp), has, adv, kern, kern_table,
  upem, metrics, metric, is_color, name, layers, glyph_box, glyph_path

Font ids 0..3 are the embedded fonts (never reused).  An unknown or unloaded id behaves
like font 0.
```

```rust
FONT_EMBEDDED: u32 = 4  // ids below this are the embedded fonts (never unloaded)
FONT_MAX_IDS: u32 = 4096  // registry capacity
```

- **`struct SynFont`**
  a font derived by font_style (TFK_SYN context)
  - `hdr: Hdr`
  - `fm: Metrics`
  - `base: u32`
  - `syn: u32`
  - `strength: f32` -- embolden strength in font units
  - `serial: u64`

**`impl Text`**

- **`fn register(&mut self, data: &'static [u8]) -> i32`**
  font_register(data) -> id / -1
- **`fn face_count(data: &[u8]) -> i32`**
  font_face_count(data) -> faces (1 for a plain sfnt, n for a TTC / OTC) / ERR_\*
- **`fn load_mem(&mut self, data: &[u8], face: u32) -> i32`**
  font_load_mem(data, face) -> id / ERR_\* (the data is copied)
- **`fn load_file(&mut self, path: &str, face: u32) -> i32`**
  font_load_file(path, face) -> id / ERR_\* (ERR_IO when it cannot be read)
- **`fn unload(&mut self, id: u32) -> i32`**
  font_unload(id) -> 0 / -1
- **`fn count(&self) -> u32`**
  font_count() -> ids in use are below this value
- **`fn valid(&self, id: u32) -> bool`**
  font_valid(id): the id names a registered font
- **`fn hdr(&self, font: u32) -> Hdr`**
  font_data: the SGF1-compatible header (an unknown id gives font 0)
- **`fn upem(&self, font: u32) -> i32`**
- **`fn glyph(&self, font: u32, cp: u32) -> u32`**
  font_glyph(font, cp) -> glyph (SGF1: fallback glyph, runtime: 0 = .notdef)
- **`fn has(&self, font: u32, cp: u32) -> bool`**
  font_has(font, cp)
- **`fn adv(&self, font: u32, g: u32) -> i32`**
  font_adv(font, glyph): advance in font units (synthetic bold included)
- **`fn kern(&self, font: u32, g1: u32, g2: u32) -> i32`**
  font_kern(font, g1, g2): kerning in font units (signed)
- **`fn kern_table(&self, font: u32, g1: u32, g2: u32) -> i32`**
  font_kern_table: the 'kern' table only (runtime fonts; others: font_kern)
- **`fn is_color(&self, font: u32) -> bool`**
  font_is_color: COLR layers or CBDT bitmaps
- **`fn layers(&self, font: u32, g: u32, cap: usize, out: &mut Vec<(u32, u32)>)`**
  font_layers(font, glyph, cap) into out: {glyph | LAYER_FG, argb}; empty = draw it
  normally
- **`fn name(&self, font: u32, which: u32) -> &[u8]`**
  font_name(font, FN_\*) -> UTF-8 ("" when unknown)
- **`fn metrics(&self, font: u32) -> Option<Metrics>`**
  font_metrics(font) -> FONTMETRICS (None when no font exists)
- **`fn metric(&self, font: u32, field: u32) -> i32`**
  font_metric(font, FM_\*) -> one FONTMETRICS field
- **`fn glyph_box(&mut self, font: u32, g: u32) -> Option<[i32; 4]>`**
  glyph_box(font, glyph) -> {xmin, ymin, xmax, ymax} (font units, control box); None =
  empty.  Runtime fonts leave the glyph's outline in the outline buffer.
- **`fn glyph_path(&mut self, font: u32, g: u32)`**
  glyph_path(font, glyph): append the outline to the path, transformed by gp (origin
  x / y in float pixels, sx / sy float pixels per unit)
- **`fn style(&mut self, font: u32, flags: u32) -> i32`**
  font_style(font, FS_\*) -> id / ERR_\*

### `text/ttf.rs`

Source: [`lib/text/ttf.rs`](../lib/text/ttf.rs)

```text
ttf -- sfnt loader for runtime fonts: TrueType (glyf), OpenType (CFF outlines via cff.rs),
collections (TTC / OTC), colour tables (colr.rs)                      [src/text/ttf.s]

  face_count(data) -> faces (1 for a plain sfnt, n for a TTC / OTC) / ERR_*
  TtFont::open(data, face, ol, serial) -> the font context / ERR_*
  glyph(cp), has(cp), adv(g), kern(g1, g2), kern_table(g1, g2), name(which)
  bbox(g, ol), path(g, ol, path, gp)       the outline through the outline buffer
  sfnt_name(table, id) -> UTF-8            best record: Windows Unicode German (0x0407),
       German variants, English (0x0409), other Windows languages, Unicode platform,
       Mac Roman English

Errors: ERR_TRUNC (too short / truncated directory), ERR_HDR (not an sfnt, bad head /
maxp), ERR_UNSUPPORTED (no usable outlines: e.g. CFF2 only), ERR_FAIL (face index out of
range), ERR_LIMIT (file >= 2 GB).

Tables: head, hhea, hmtx, maxp, loca, glyf (simple + composite: offsets, scale, x/y scale,
2x2, point matching, SCALED_COMPONENT_OFFSET; nesting <= 16, <= 4096 components per
glyph), cmap (formats 0, 4, 6, 12, 13; (3,10) > (0,x) fmt 12 > (3,1) > (0,x) > (3,0)
symbol > (1,0) Mac Roman), OS/2, post, name, kern (version 0 and Apple version 1, format
0), GPOS pair adjustment (lookup type 2 formats 1 and 2, extension type 9; x advance of
the first glyph; 'kern' feature of the DFLT and latn default language systems).  Variable
fonts: the default instance.  Every offset is checked against its table and the file;
malformed glyphs come out empty.  Like GDI / FreeType: glyf outlines are shifted by hmtx
lsb - xMin; symbol fonts ((3,0) cmap) also map U+0000..U+00FF and the Windows-1252
characters to U+F000 + code page byte; a CFF FontMatrix that is not exactly 1/unitsPerEm
scales the outline.
```

```rust
ERR_FAIL: i32 = -1
ERR_TRUNC: i32 = -1
ERR_HDR: i32 = -2
ERR_NOMEM: i32 = -8
ERR_UNSUPPORTED: i32 = -9
ERR_IO: i32 = -13
ERR_LIMIT: i32 = -15
FILE_MAX: u64 = 0x7FFF_FFF0
NAME_CAP: usize = 256
// table indices (TT_*)
TT_HEAD: usize = 0
TT_HHEA: usize = 1
TT_HMTX: usize = 2
TT_MAXP: usize = 3
TT_LOCA: usize = 4
TT_GLYF: usize = 5
TT_CMAP: usize = 6
TT_OS2: usize = 7
TT_POST: usize = 8
TT_NAME: usize = 9
TT_KERN: usize = 10
TT_GPOS: usize = 11
TT_CFF: usize = 12
TT_COLR: usize = 13
TT_CPAL: usize = 14
TT_CBDT: usize = 15
TT_CBLC: usize = 16
TT_FVAR: usize = 17
TT_COUNT: usize = 20
TFK_TTF: u32 = 1
TFK_CFF: u32 = 2
TFF_LOCA_LONG: u32 = 2
TFF_SYMBOL: u32 = 4
TFF_COLR: u32 = 8
TFF_CBDT: u32 = 16
TFF_MACCMAP: u32 = 32
```

- **`static MAC_ROMAN: [u16; 128]`**
  Mac OS Roman 0x80..0xFF -> Unicode

- **`struct Tb<'a>(pub &'a [u8])`**
  bounds-checked big-endian reads of one table (t16 / t32 of the assembly: -1 when the
  read would cross the table end; offsets are u32 and wrap like the 32-bit registers)

**`impl Tb<'_>`**

- **`fn len(&self) -> u32`**
- **`fn u8(&self, off: u32) -> i32`**
- **`fn u16(&self, off: u32) -> i32`**
  t16 -> u16 or -1
- **`fn u32(&self, off: u32) -> i64`**
  t32 -> u32 or -1

- **`fn face_count(d: &[u8]) -> i32`**
  font_face_count(data) -> faces / ERR_\*

- **`fn sfnt_name(tb: Tb, id: u32, cap: usize) -> Vec<u8>`**
  sfnt_name(table, id) -> UTF-8 (empty = not found), at most cap - 1 bytes

- **`struct TtFont`**
  a runtime font context (TrueType or CFF outlines)
  - `face_index: u32`
  - `data: Vec<u8>`
  - `hdr: Hdr`
  - `fm: Metrics`
  - `serial: u64` -- outline buffer owner identity
  - `kind: u32`
  - `flags: u32`
  - `nglyphs: u32`
  - `upem: u32`
  - `tab: [(u32, u32); TT_COUNT]`
  - `names: [Vec<u8>; 5]`
  - `cpal: u32`
  - `cpaln: u32`

**`impl TtFont`**

- **`fn open(data: Vec<u8>, face: u32, ol: &mut Outline, serial: u64) -> Result<Box<TtFont>, i32>`**
  ttf_open(data, face): the context, or ERR_\*
- **`fn glyph(&self, cp: u32) -> u32`**
  glyph for cp (0 = .notdef).  Symbol fonts ((3,0) cmap) map like GDI: U+0000..U+00FF
  and the Windows-1252 characters to U+F000 + their code page byte  [ttf_glyph]
- **`fn has(&self, cp: u32) -> bool`**
- **`fn adv(&self, g: u32) -> i32`**
  advance (hmtx; the last long metric repeats)  [ttf_adv]
- **`fn kern_table(&self, g1: u32, g2: u32) -> i32`**
  sum of the 'kern' format 0 values  [tt_kern_table]
- **`fn kern(&self, g1: u32, g2: u32) -> i32`**
  GPOS 'kern' when present, else 'kern'  [ttf_kern]
- **`fn name(&self, which: u32) -> &[u8]`**
  font_name of this font ("" for an unknown id)  [ttf_name]
- **`fn prep(&self, glyph: u32, ol: &mut Outline) -> bool`**
  the outline of glyph into the buffer (owner set either way): true = not empty
  [ttf_prep]
- **`fn bbox(&self, g: u32, ol: &mut Outline) -> Option<[i32; 4]>`**
  control box (font units) and the prepared outline  [ttf_box]
- **`fn path(&self, g: u32, ol: &mut Outline, path: &mut Path, gp: &Gp)`**
  append the outline to the path with the gp transform  [ttf_path]

## img_core

*image I/O dispatch, records, metadata, colour, resampling, image operations* -- crate root [`lib/img_core/lib.rs`](../lib/img_core/lib.rs)

```text
img_core -- the shared part of the image codecs: records of img.inc (decode options,
metadata, encode options, codec descriptor, format table), error codes, and the modules
every codec builds on                                              [ASMLIB/inc/img.inc]

  ImgDec        decode options (IMGDEC): flags IMGD_*, frame, hint, pixel budget, cancel
  ImgMeta       decoded metadata (IMGMETA); to_bytes() = the 128-byte record of the
                assembly with the blob pointers zeroed (codectest op 261 compares it),
                incl. IM_CHRM (PNG cHRM) and the DDS fields IM_DXGI / IM_TEXFL / IM_TEXN
  ImgOpt        encode options (IMGOPT); from_bytes() reads the 64-byte record
  Codec         a codec descriptor (IMGCODEC): probe / decode / encode, caps, iom; and
                for the core formats the functions the assembly calls directly: scan
                (png_meta / jpeg_meta / ...), decode_fl (png_decode_fl / ...: the classic
                decoders of img_decode and BMP's embedded PNG / JPEG), save (png_encode /
                jpeg_encode / ...: the classic encoders of img_save); Codec::EMPTY for
                struct update syntax
  FmtInfo       a format table entry (IMGFMT)

Modules (owners during the R6 wave, see rewrite/AGENTS.md):
  checksum, inflate, deflate, gzip, meta (imgmeta.s), exif, icc, io (imgio.s, imgreg.s,
  imgcore.s glue), bits (the bit readers of img_int.inc)  -- R6-core
  yuv                                                     -- R6-jpeg
  quantize                                                -- R6-bgt (bmp / gif / tga)
  resample, xform, mip, ops (imgops.s)                    -- R6-xf
  icc_xform (+ icc_eval, icc_bi: icc_xform.s, icc.inc)    -- R6-icc

Decoded images are gfx::Surface values (FMT_PARGB, or FMT_ARGB with IMGD_STRAIGHT).
```

### `img_core` crate root

Re-exports: `gfx::Surface`

- **`type ParallelFor = fn(usize, &(dyn Fn(usize) + Sync))`**
  runs f(i) for every i in 0..n, possibly on several threads (thr_parallel_for)

- **`fn set_parallel(f: ParallelFor)`**
  the loop the codecs' parallel parts run on (the asm's thr_parallel_for: ICC LUT grids, YUV
  bands); the apps pass a wrapper of plat::parallel_for -- img_core does not depend on plat.
  Without it everything runs on the calling thread (the same bytes)

- **`fn parallel() -> Option<ParallelFor>`**
  the loop set by set_parallel

*errors (jfgui.inc)*

```rust
ERR_FAIL: i32 = -1
ERR_TRUNC: i32 = -1  // input ends early
ERR_HDR: i32 = -2  // bad header / signature
ERR_BLOCK: i32 = -3  // bad deflate block or PNG filter
ERR_CODE: i32 = -4  // bad Huffman code
ERR_DIST: i32 = -5  // distance too far back / invalid
ERR_OVERFLOW: i32 = -6  // output larger than the buffer
ERR_CHECKSUM: i32 = -7  // Adler-32 mismatch
ERR_NOMEM: i32 = -8  // allocation failed
ERR_UNSUPPORTED: i32 = -9  // valid but unsupported variant
ERR_CRC: i32 = -10  // PNG chunk CRC mismatch
ERR_CHUNK: i32 = -11  // bad / missing PNG chunk
ERR_DIMS: i32 = -12  // dimensions out of range
ERR_IO: i32 = -13  // file could not be read / written
ERR_CANCEL: i32 = -14  // cancelled through a cancel flag
ERR_LIMIT: i32 = -15  // resource limit
```

*formats (jfgui.inc IMGF_\*)*

```rust
IMGF_PNG: u32 = 1
IMGF_JPEG: u32 = 2
IMGF_BMP: u32 = 3
IMGF_GIF: u32 = 4
IMGF_TIFF: u32 = 5
IMGF_TGA: u32 = 6
IMGF_DDS: u32 = 7
IMGF_WEBP: u32 = 8
IMGF_AVIF: u32 = 9
IMGF_HEIC: u32 = 10
IMGF_JXL: u32 = 11
IMGF_JXR: u32 = 12
IMGF_PDN: u32 = 13
IMG_DYN0: u32 = 14  // runtime formats (plugins), start inclusive
IMG_DYN_END: u32 = 22  // runtime formats, end exclusive
// Keep the eight established plugin slots (14 through 21) stable; new built-ins follow them.
IMGF_PSD: u32 = 22
IMG_EXTERNAL0: u32 = 23  // out-of-process codecs; native PNAPI IDs stay stable
IMGF_COUNT: u32 = 55
```

*limits*

```rust
IMG_MAXDIM: u32 = 65535  // largest width / height (ERR_DIMS)
IMG_MAXPX_DEF: u64 = 1 << 28  // default pixel budget (ERR_LIMIT)
```

*decode flags (ImgDec::flags)*

```rust
IMGD_STRAIGHT: u32 = 1  // FMT_ARGB instead of FMT_PARGB
IMGD_ICC: u32 = 2  // copy an embedded ICC profile into ImgMeta::icc
IMGD_EXIF: u32 = 4  // copy the EXIF block (TIFF structure)
IMGD_XMP: u32 = 8  // copy the XMP packet
IMGD_NO_ORIENT: u32 = 16  // do not apply the EXIF / container orientation
IMGD_PROBE: u32 = 32  // header + metadata only
IMGD_THUMB: u32 = 64  // an embedded preview / reduced size is acceptable
```

*metadata (ImgMeta)*

```rust
IMM_GRAY: u8 = 1  // colour models
IMM_RGB: u8 = 2
IMM_PAL: u8 = 3
IMM_CMYK: u8 = 4
IMM_YCC: u8 = 5
IMM_LAB: u8 = 6
IMM_YCCK: u8 = 7
IMF_ALPHA: u32 = 1  // flags
IMF_SRC_PREMUL: u32 = 2
IMF_LOSSLESS: u32 = 4
IMF_ANIMATED: u32 = 8
IMF_HDR: u32 = 16
IMF_ORIENT_APPLIED: u32 = 32
IMF_FLOAT: u32 = 64
IMF_INTERLACED: u32 = 128
IMF_SRGB: u32 = 256
IMR_NONE: u32 = 0  // resolution units
IMR_INCH: u32 = 1
IMR_CM: u32 = 2
```

*encode options (ImgOpt)*

```rust
IOF_LOSSLESS: u32 = 1

IOF_INTERLACE: u32 = 256

DDSF_BC2: u32 = 2

DDSF_BC5: u32 = 5
DDSF_BC6H: u32 = 6
DDSF_BC7: u32 = 7
DDSF_BGRA8: u32 = 8
DDSF_BGRX8: u32 = 9
DDSF_RGBA8: u32 = 10
DDSF_BGR5A1: u32 = 11
DDSF_BGRA4: u32 = 12
DDSF_B5G6R5: u32 = 13
DDSF_L8: u32 = 14
DDSF_A8: u32 = 15
DDSF_R8G8: u32 = 16
MIPF_BOX: u32 = 1
MIPF_LINEAR: u32 = 2
MIPF_CUBIC: u32 = 3
MIPF_NEAREST: u32 = 4
TIFC_NONE: u32 = 1
TIFC_LZW: u32 = 2
TIFC_DEFLATE: u32 = 3
TIFC_PACKBITS: u32 = 4
IOM_QUALITY: u32 = 1  // which ImgOpt fields an encoder honours
IOM_LOSSLESS: u32 = 2
IOM_RLE: u32 = 4
IOM_MIPMAPS: u32 = 8
IOM_BITDEPTH: u32 = 16
IOM_SAMPLEBITS: u32 = 32
IOM_DITHER: u32 = 64
IOM_ATHRESH: u32 = 128
IOM_CHROMA: u32 = 256
IOM_EFFORT: u32 = 512
IOM_DDSFMT: u32 = 1024
IOM_MIPFILTER: u32 = 2048
IOM_TIFFCOMP: u32 = 4096
IOM_SRGB: u32 = 8192

IOM_ICC: u32 = 32768
IOM_EXIF: u32 = 65536
IOM_XMP: u32 = 131072
```
