
*format table (FmtInfo::caps)*

```rust
IFC_DECODE: u32 = 1
IFC_ENCODE: u32 = 2
IFC_ALPHA: u32 = 4
IFC_LAYERS: u32 = 8
IFC_ANIM: u32 = 16
IFC_PAGES: u32 = 32
IFC_MIPS: u32 = 64
IFC_LOSSY: u32 = 128
IFC_LOSSLESS: u32 = 256
IFC_HDR: u32 = 512
IFC_ICC: u32 = 1024
IFC_EXIF: u32 = 2048
IFC_XMP: u32 = 4096
IFC_CORE: u32 = 8192
```

- **`struct ImgDec<'a>`**
  decode options (IMGDEC); Default = premultiplied output, orientation applied, frame 0,
  no hint, IMG_MAXPX_DEF pixels, no metadata blobs, no cancel flag
  - `flags: u32` -- IMGD_\*
  - `frame: u32` -- frame / page / mip level
  - `hint: u32` -- IMGF_\* expected from the file name (0 = none)
  - `maxpx: u64` -- max w \* h (0 = IMG_MAXPX_DEF) -> ERR_LIMIT
  - `maxmem: u64` -- memory budget for codec scratch (0 = no limit)
  - `cancel: Option<&'a AtomicU32>`

**`impl ImgDec<'_>`**

- **`fn max_pixels(&self) -> u64`**
  the pixel budget (maxpx, or the default)
- **`fn cancelled(&self) -> bool`**
  the cancel flag is set

- **`struct ImgMeta`**
  decoded metadata (IMGMETA)
  - `fmt: u32` -- IMGF_\*
  - `w: u32` -- stored width (before orientation)
  - `h: u32` -- stored height
  - `bits: u8` -- bits per sample of the source (1..16, 32 = float)
  - `model: u8` -- IMM_\*
  - `orient: u8` -- EXIF orientation 1..8
  - `channels: u8` -- samples per pixel incl. alpha (0 = unknown)
  - `flags: u32` -- IMF_\*
  - `frames: u32` -- frames / pages / mip levels
  - `dpix: u32` -- dots per inch 16.16 (0 = unknown)
  - `dpiy: u32`
  - `cicp: u32` -- primaries | transfer &lt;&lt; 8 | matrix &lt;&lt; 16 | full range &lt;&lt; 24
  - `gamma: u32` -- file gamma \* 100000 (PNG gAMA)
  - `icc: Vec<u8>` -- IMGD_ICC
  - `exif: Vec<u8>` -- IMGD_EXIF: TIFF structure without "Exif\\0\\0"
  - `xmp: Vec<u8>` -- IMGD_XMP
  - `delay: u32` -- ms (animations)
  - `loops: u32` -- animation loop count (0 = forever)
  - `quality: u32` -- estimated encoder quality 1..100
  - `resunit: u32` -- IMR_\*
  - `chrm: [u16; 8]` -- IM_CHRM: PNG cHRM white x, y, red x, y, green x, y, blue x, y / 50000
  - `dxgi: u16` -- IM_DXGI: DXGI_FORMAT of DDS pixel data
  - `texfl: u8` -- IM_TEXFL: TXF_\*
  - `texn: u32` -- IM_TEXN: DDS array size / cubes / depth

**`impl ImgMeta`**

- **`fn to_bytes(&self) -> [u8; 128]`**
  the 128-byte IMGMETA record of the assembly (blob pointers 0, lengths kept)

- **`struct ImgOpt<'a>`**
  encode options (IMGOPT); Default = the format's defaults
  - `fmt: u32` -- IMGF_\* (save: 0 = from the file extension)
  - `quality: u32` -- 1..100 (0 = format default)
  - `flags: u32` -- IOF_\*
  - `bitdepth: u32` -- bits per pixel 0 auto / 32 / 24 / 8
  - `samplebits: u32` -- bits per sample 8 / 10 / 12 (0 = 8)
  - `dither: u32` -- dithering level + 1 (0 = default 7, 1 = off .. 9 = level 8)
  - `athresh: u32` -- transparency threshold + 1 (0 = default 128)
  - `chroma: u32` -- 444 / 422 / 420 / 400 (0 = format default)
  - `effort: u32` -- 1..9 (0 = default); PNG: deflate level
  - `ddsfmt: u32` -- DDSF_\*
  - `mipfilter: u32` -- MIPF_\*
  - `tiffcomp: u32` -- TIFC_\*
  - `meta: Option<&'a ImgMeta>` -- metadata to embed
  - `cancel: Option<&'a AtomicU32>`

**`impl<'a> ImgOpt<'a>`**

- **`fn from_bytes(b: &[u8]) -> ImgOpt<'a>`**
  the scalar fields of a 64-byte IMGOPT record (IO_META / IO_CANCEL ignored)
- **`fn cancelled(&self) -> bool`**

- **`type ProbeFn = fn(&[u8], &mut ImgMeta) -> bool`**
  probe(data, meta) -> recognised (header + metadata scan, no pixels)

- **`type DecodeFn = fn(&[u8], &mut Surface, &ImgDec, &mut ImgMeta) -> i32`**
  decode(data, out, dec, meta) -> 0 / ERR_\*  (meta: the caller's ImgMeta, already
  cleared with fmt set; dec.flags tell which blobs to copy)

- **`type EncodeFn = fn(&Surface, &ImgOpt, &mut Vec<u8>) -> i32`**
  encode(surface, opt, out) -> 0 / ERR_\*  (FMT_XRGB / PARGB / ARGB / A8 input)

- **`type ScanFn = fn(&[u8], &mut ImgMeta, u32) -> i32`**
  scan(data, meta, flags) -> 0 / ERR_\*: the header / metadata scanner of a core format
  (png_meta, jpeg_meta, bmp_meta, gif_meta; flags = IMGD_\* of the blobs to copy)

- **`type DecodeFlFn = fn(&[u8], &mut Surface, u32) -> i32`**
  decode_fl(data, out, jdf) -> 0 / ERR_\*: the classic decoder (png_decode_fl, ...);
  jdf = JDF_STRAIGHT (FMT_ARGB) | JDF_NO_ORIENT, else FMT_PARGB

- **`type SaveFn = fn(&Surface, u32) -> Option<Vec<u8>>`**
  save(surface, quality) -> file bytes or None (out of memory, unsupported surface): the
  classic encoder of img_save (png_encode, jpeg_encode(quality), bmp_encode, gif_encode)

- **`struct Codec`**
  a codec descriptor (IMGCODEC); every codec crate exports one `pub static CODEC: Codec`
  (`Codec { probe: Some(..), decode: Some(..), ..Codec::EMPTY }`)
  - `probe: Option<ProbeFn>`
  - `decode: Option<DecodeFn>`
  - `encode: Option<EncodeFn>`
  - `caps: u32` -- IFC_DECODE / IFC_ENCODE really implemented
  - `iom: u32` -- IOM_\* the encoder honours
  - `scan: Option<ScanFn>` -- core formats: metadata scanner
  - `decode_fl: Option<DecodeFlFn>` -- core formats: classic decoder (img_decode)
  - `save: Option<SaveFn>` -- core formats: classic encoder (img_save)

**`impl Codec`**

- **`const EMPTY: Codec = Codec { probe: None, decode: None, encode: None, caps: 0, ...`**

*core codec options (img.inc CA)*

```rust
JDF_STRAIGHT: u32 = 1  // FMT_ARGB output
JDF_NO_ORIENT: u32 = 2  // keep the stored orientation
JDF_RGB: u32 = 4  // 3 components are R, G, B (TIFF Photometric 2)
JDF_YCC: u32 = 8  // 3 components are YCbCr (Photometric 6)
JDF_CMYK: u32 = 16  // 4 components are plain CMYK (Photometric 5)
PNG_IOM: u32 = IOM_BITDEPTH | IOM_DITHER | IOM_ATHRESH | IOM_EFFORT | IO ...
JPEG_IOM: u32 = IOM_QUALITY | IOM_CHROMA | IOM_ICC | IOM_EXIF | IOM_XMP
BMP_IOM: u32 = IOM_BITDEPTH | IOM_DITHER
GIF_IOM: u32 = IOM_DITHER | IOM_ATHRESH
TXF_CUBE: u8 = 1  // ImgMeta::texfl (DDS)
TXF_VOLUME: u8 = 2
TXF_ARRAY: u8 = 4
TXF_CROSS: u8 = 8
TXF_PREMUL: u8 = 16
TXF_LEGACY: u8 = 32
```

- **`struct FmtInfo`**
  a format table entry (IMGFMT)
  - `name: &'static str`
  - `exts: &'static str` -- ui file dialog entry "png=PNG" / "jpg;jpeg;...=JPEG"
  - `mime: &'static str`
  - `caps: u32` -- IFC_\* format properties | registered DECODE / ENCODE
  - `iom: u32` -- IOM_\* of the registered encoder
  - `codec: Option<&'static Codec>`

### `img_core/bits.rs`

Source: [`lib/img_core/bits.rs`](../lib/img_core/bits.rs)

```text
bits -- the bit readers of the codecs (MSB-first and LSB-first)  [ASMLIB/inc/img_int.inc]

  BrM (MSB first: JPEG entropy data without stuffing, HEVC, AV1 headers, JXR)
  BrL (LSB first: deflate, VP8L, JPEG XL)
  r = BrX::new(data, pos); r.refill(); r.get(n) / r.peek(n) / r.skip(n); r.overrun()

The state of the assembly macros: a 64-bit buffer, a bit count and a byte position that
keeps counting past the end of the data (zero bytes are shifted in there, nothing is
read).  After refill() at least 56 bits are available.  overrun() is BRx_OVERRUN: more
zero padding bytes consumed than bits are left.  Values of n: 1..56 (get / peek / skip).
```

- **`struct BrM<'a>`**
  MSB-first reader (BRM_\*)
  - `data: &'a [u8]`
  - `buf: u64`
  - `cnt: u32`
  - `pos: usize` -- may pass data.len()

**`impl<'a> BrM<'a>`**

- **`fn new(data: &'a [u8], pos: usize) -> Self`**
  BRM_INIT with the byte position pos
- **`fn refill(&mut self)`**
  BRM_REFILL: at least 56 bits in the buffer
- **`fn peek(&self, n: u32) -> u64`**
  BRM_PEEK: the next n bits
- **`fn skip(&mut self, n: u32)`**
  BRM_SKIP
- **`fn get(&mut self, n: u32) -> u64`**
  BRM_GET / BRM_GETV
- **`fn overrun(&self) -> bool`**
  BRM_OVERRUN: true when more padding bytes were consumed than bits are left

- **`struct BrL<'a>`**
  LSB-first reader (BRL_\*)
  - `data: &'a [u8]`
  - `buf: u64`
  - `cnt: u32`
  - `pos: usize` -- may pass data.len()

**`impl<'a> BrL<'a>`**

- **`fn new(data: &'a [u8], pos: usize) -> Self`**
  BRL_INIT with the byte position pos
- **`fn refill(&mut self)`**
  BRL_REFILL: at least 56 bits in the buffer
- **`fn peek(&self, n: u32) -> u64`**
  BRL_PEEK: the next n bits
- **`fn skip(&mut self, n: u32)`**
  BRL_SKIP
- **`fn get(&mut self, n: u32) -> u64`**
  BRL_GET / BRL_GETV
- **`fn overrun(&self) -> bool`**
  BRL_OVERRUN

### `img_core/checksum.rs`

Source: [`lib/img_core/checksum.rs`](../lib/img_core/checksum.rs)

```text
checksum -- CRC-32 (zlib / PNG polynomial) and Adler-32  [src/img/checksum.s]

  crc32(crc, data) -> u32        start with crc = 0 (like zlib's crc32())
  adler32(adler, data) -> u32    start with adler = 1

CRC: table driven (slice-by-8 here, slice-by-4 in the assembly: the same values).
Adler-32: blocks of at most 5552 bytes summed exactly in 64 bit, both sums reduced mod
65521 after every block (as the assembly's SSE2 loop); a call with no data returns the
start value unchanged.
```

- **`fn crc32(crc: u32, data: &[u8]) -> u32`**
  CRC-32 (zlib / PNG) of data, continuing crc (start with 0)  [crc32]

- **`fn adler32(adler: u32, data: &[u8]) -> u32`**
  Adler-32 (zlib) of data, continuing adler (start with 1)  [adler32]

### `img_core/deflate.rs`

Source: [`lib/img_core/deflate.rs`](../lib/img_core/deflate.rs)

```text
deflate -- DEFLATE (RFC 1951) / zlib (RFC 1950) compression  [src/img/deflate.s]

  zlib_deflate(src, level) -> Vec          zlib stream (level 0..9, < 0 = 6, > 9 = 9)
  deflate_raw(src, level) -> Vec           raw RFC 1951 data
  deflate_raw_ex(st, src, level, head, tail) -> Vec   head free bytes before the data
                                           (gzip writes its header there), tail reserved
                                           after it (not part of the Vec)
  DeflateState::new() -> Box               a reusable compressor state (~330 KB; one
                                           thread at a time); the same bytes as fresh calls
  zlib_deflate_st(st, src, level) / deflate_raw_st(st, src, level) -> Vec
  zlib_deflate_to(st, src, level, dst) / deflate_raw_to(...) -> Ok(bytes) /
                                           Err(ERR_OVERFLOW): into dst (no allocation with
                                           a state); overflow when the stream + 8 bytes do
                                           not fit (dst contents undefined then)
  deflate_bound(len) -> len + len / 512 + 128   a cap with which *_to never overflow

The algorithm and every table of the assembly: 3-byte multiplicative hash, head / prev
chains over "global" positions (input offset + base; every input starts GEN_GAP after
the previous one, so a reused state matches a fresh one), zlib's levels (greedy 1..3, lazy
4..9, chain depth / nice / lazy limits), TOO_FAR, 16K-symbol blocks written as the
cheapest of stored / fixed / dynamic by exact bit count, two-queue Huffman lengths with
miniz's Kraft repair, RLE-coded code lengths.  Inputs of 4 GiB - 64 KiB and more are
stored.  The output limit: a block that would end past cap - 8 stops the compressor
(ERR_OVERFLOW).  Speed (perf-inflate): the LZ77 loops keep their state in locals and
load the next position's hash head before the current chain walk (the walk is a chain of
dependent loads; the early load overlaps it) -- the same candidates in the same order.
```

- **`struct DeflateState`**
  a compressor state (DS_\*); reusable, one thread at a time

**`impl DeflateState`**

- **`fn new() -> Box<DeflateState>`**
  a zeroed state  [deflate_state_new]

- **`fn deflate_bound(len: usize) -> usize`**
  output capacity with which the \*\_to functions never report ERR_OVERFLOW  [deflate_bound]

- **`fn zlib_deflate_to(st: Option<&mut DeflateState>, src: &[u8], level: i32, dst: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, i32>`**
  zlib stream into dst  [zlib_deflate_to]

- **`fn deflate_raw_to(st: Option<&mut DeflateState>, src: &[u8], level: i32, dst: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, i32>`**
  raw deflate data into dst  [deflate_raw_to]

- **`fn zlib_deflate(src: &[u8], level: i32) -> Vec<u8>`**
  zlib stream  [zlib_deflate]

- **`fn zlib_deflate_st(st: Option<&mut DeflateState>, src: &[u8], level: i32) -> Vec<u8>`**
  zlib stream with a reusable state (None = a temporary one)  [zlib_deflate_st]

- **`fn deflate_raw(src: &[u8], level: i32) -> Vec<u8>`**
  raw deflate data  [deflate_raw]

- **`fn deflate_raw_st(st: Option<&mut DeflateState>, src: &[u8], level: i32) -> Vec<u8>`**
  raw deflate data with a reusable state  [deflate_raw_st without head / tail]

- **`fn deflate_raw_ex(st: Option<&mut DeflateState>, src: &[u8], level: i32, head: usize, tail: usize) -> Vec<u8>`**
  raw deflate data after `head` zero bytes (head, tail: 0..65536, larger values clamped);
  the Vec holds head + the compressed bytes, with room for `tail` more  [deflate_raw_st]

### `img_core/exif.rs`

Source: [`lib/img_core/exif.rs`](../lib/img_core/exif.rs)

```text
exif -- TIFF IFD reader (EXIF blocks; shared with the TIFF codec) and EXIF helpers
                                                                         [src/img/exif.s]

  Tifr::open(data) -> Ok(reader) / Err(ERR_TRUNC / ERR_HDR)
  tr.visit(off) -> 0 / ERR_LIMIT        record an IFD (a cycle or TIFR_MAXIFD IFDs: limit)
  tr.count(off) -> entries (clamped to what fits) / ERR_TRUNC
  tr.ifd(off) -> entries / ERR_*        visit + count
  tr.entry(off, i) -> Ok(TEnt) / Err(ERR_HDR unknown type / ERR_TRUNC)
  tr.find(off, tag) -> Some(TEnt)       first entry with the tag (bad entries skipped)
  tr.get(te, i) -> u64                  integer value i (signed types sign-extended,
                                        RATIONAL: numerator, FLOAT / DOUBLE: raw bits)
  tr.rational(te, i) -> (num, den)      RATIONAL / SRATIONAL value i (others: value / 1)
  tr.next(off) -> offset of the next IFD (0 = none)
  exif_read(blob) -> (0 / ERR_*, ExifInfo)   orientation, resolution, colour space
  exif_orientation(blob) -> 1..8
  exif_set_orientation(blob, o) -> bool      patch the IFD0 Orientation SHORT in place
  exif_copy_orient1(blob) -> Vec             copy with orientation 1

The reader works on a TIFF structure in memory: "II*\0" / "MM\0*" (classic, 4-byte
offsets, 12-byte entries) or BigTIFF "II+\0" / "MM\0+" (8-byte offsets, 20-byte
entries); every read is bounds-checked, value spans must lie inside the data.
```

```rust
TIFR_MAXIFD: usize = 1024  // IFDs per reader (chain + sub-IFDs), cycles detected
TIFR_MAXDEPTH: u32 = 3  // sub-IFD nesting callers must respect
```

- **`struct TEnt`**
  one decoded IFD entry (TENT)
  - `tag: u32`
  - `typ: u32` -- TIFF field type 1..18
  - `count: u64` -- number of values
  - `voff: u64` -- offset of the values in the TIFF structure
  - `bytes: u64` -- count \* type size (inside the data)

- **`struct Tifr<'a>`**
  a TIFF IFD reader (TIFR)
  - `data: &'a [u8]`
  - `be: bool` -- "MM"
  - `big: bool` -- BigTIFF
  - `ifd0: u64` -- offset of the first IFD

**`impl<'a> Tifr<'a>`**

- **`fn open(data: &'a [u8]) -> Result<Tifr<'a>, i32>`**
  start reading the TIFF structure: byte order, classic / BigTIFF, first IFD offset
  [tifr_open]
- **`fn visit(&mut self, off: u64) -> i32`**
  record that the IFD at off is being read: ERR_LIMIT when it was read before or
  TIFR_MAXIFD IFDs were read  [tifr_visit]
- **`fn count(&self, off: u64) -> i64`**
  entries of the IFD at off, clamped to the entries that fit, or ERR_TRUNC
  [tifr_count]
- **`fn ifd(&mut self, off: u64) -> i64`**
  visit + count  [tifr_ifd]
- **`fn entry(&self, off: u64, i: u64) -> Result<TEnt, i32>`**
  entry i (i &lt; count) of the IFD at off; the value span is checked to lie inside the
  data  [tifr_entry]
- **`fn find(&self, off: u64, tag: u32) -> Option<TEnt>`**
  the first entry of the IFD at off with the given tag (entries that fail entry() are
  skipped)  [tifr_find]
- **`fn get(&self, te: &TEnt, i: u64) -> u64`**
  value i of an entry as an integer: BYTE / ASCII / UNDEFINED / SHORT / LONG / IFD /
  LONG8 / IFD8 zero-extended, SBYTE / SSHORT / SLONG / SLONG8 sign-extended,
  RATIONAL: numerator, FLOAT / DOUBLE: raw bits; 0 when i >= count  [tifr_get]
- **`fn rational(&self, te: &TEnt, i: u64) -> (u64, u64)`**
  RATIONAL value i as (numerator, denominator) (SRATIONAL: sign-extended); other types
  give value / 1; i >= count: 0 / 0  [tifr_rational]
- **`fn next(&self, off: u64) -> u64`**
  offset of the IFD following the one at off (0 = none, or it lies outside the data)
  [tifr_next]

- **`struct ExifInfo`**
  what exif_read finds (EXIFINFO)
  - `orient: u32` -- 1..8 (1 when absent / invalid)
  - `unit: u32` -- ResolutionUnit (1 none, 2 inch, 3 cm; 0 absent)
  - `dpix: u32` -- 16.16 dpi (0 = unknown)
  - `dpiy: u32`
  - `cspace: u32` -- ColorSpace (1 = sRGB, 0xFFFF uncalibrated, 0 absent)
  - `orioff: u32` -- offset of the orientation SHORT in the blob (0 = none)

**`impl ExifInfo`**

- **`fn to_bytes(&self) -> [u8; 24]`**
  the 24-byte EXIFINFO record

- **`fn exif_read(blob: &[u8]) -> (i32, ExifInfo)`**
  fill an ExifInfo from an EXIF TIFF structure: IFD0 Orientation, X / YResolution,
  ResolutionUnit (default inch), the Exif IFD (ColorSpace; the Interop IFD is visited),
  then the IFD chain is walked until its end, a cycle or the limit (errors there are
  ignored) -> (0 / ERR_TRUNC / ERR_HDR / ERR_LIMIT, info)  [exif_read]

- **`fn exif_orientation(blob: &[u8]) -> u32`**
  the IFD0 orientation of an EXIF TIFF structure (1 when missing, invalid or unreadable)
  [exif_orientation]

- **`fn exif_set_orientation(blob: &mut [u8], orient: u32) -> bool`**
  overwrite the IFD0 Orientation SHORT in place (in the blob's byte order) -> true
  patched / false no orientation tag  [exif_set_orientation]

- **`fn exif_copy_orient1(blob: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8>`**
  a copy of an EXIF block with its orientation set to 1 (for pixels rotated already);
  empty for an empty blob  [exif_copy_orient1]

### `img_core/gzip.rs`

Source: [`lib/img_core/gzip.rs`](../lib/img_core/gzip.rs)

```text
gzip -- gzip (RFC 1952) members on top of inflate_raw / deflate_raw  [src/img/gzip.s]

  gzip_decode(src, dst) -> Ok((bytes, consumed)) / Err(ERR_*)   ONE member; consumed =
                                   the member length incl. the trailer
  gzip_decode_st(st, src, dst)     the same with an inflate state (None = temporary)
  gzip_encode(src, level) -> Vec   one member: 1F 8B 08, FLG 0, MTIME 0, XFL (2 for level 9,
                                   4 for level 1, else 0), OS 255, deflate, CRC32, ISIZE
  gzip_encode_st(st, src, level) -> Vec
  gzip_encode_to(st, src, level, dst) -> Ok(bytes) / Err(ERR_OVERFLOW)   into dst
                                   (deflate_bound(len) always suffices)
  gzip_isize(src) -> Ok(ISIZE) / Err(ERR_TRUNC)   the last 4 bytes of a single member

Decoding: ID1 ID2, CM 8, FLG (reserved bits 5..7 must be 0), MTIME, XFL, OS, then FEXTRA,
FNAME, FCOMMENT, FHCRC (low 16 bits of the CRC-32 of the header bytes), the deflate data,
CRC32 and ISIZE.  Errors: ERR_TRUNC, ERR_HDR, ERR_CHECKSUM and the inflate errors.
```

- **`fn gzip_decode(src: &[u8], dst: &mut [u8]) -> Result<(usize, usize), i32>`**
  decompress one gzip member into dst  [gzip_decode]

- **`fn gzip_decode_st(st: Option<&mut InflateState>, src: &[u8], dst: &mut [u8]) -> Result<(usize, usize), i32>`**
  gzip_decode with a reusable inflate state (None = a temporary one)  [gzip_decode_st]

- **`fn gzip_encode(src: &[u8], level: i32) -> Vec<u8>`**
  one gzip member  [gzip_encode]

- **`fn gzip_encode_st(st: Option<&mut DeflateState>, src: &[u8], level: i32) -> Vec<u8>`**
  gzip_encode with a reusable deflate state (None = a temporary one)  [gzip_encode_st]

- **`fn gzip_encode_to(st: Option<&mut DeflateState>, src: &[u8], level: i32, dst: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, i32>`**
  the member of gzip_encode into dst  [gzip_encode_to]

- **`fn gzip_isize(src: &[u8]) -> Result<u32, i32>`**
  the ISIZE field (uncompressed size mod 2^32) of a single-member stream  [gzip_isize]

### `img_core/icc.rs`

Source: [`lib/img_core/icc.rs`](../lib/img_core/icc.rs)

```text
icc -- ICC profile container helpers (no colour conversion: that is icc_xform)
                                                                          [src/img/icc.s]

  icc_check(data) -> profile size / ERR_TRUNC / ERR_HDR / ERR_UNSUPPORTED
  icc_jpeg_collect(data) -> (1 found / 0 none / ERR_CHUNK, profile)
  icc_jpeg_segments(icc) -> Ok(APP2 segments) / Err(ERR_LIMIT)

icc_check validates the 128-byte header and the tag table: declared size >= 132 and
<= len, 'acsp' at byte 36, major version 2..5, the tag count fits, every tag's data lies
inside the declared size.  JPEG embeds a profile in APP2 segments "ICC_PROFILE\0" + seq
(1-based) + count + data; icc_jpeg_collect walks the segments before the first SOS and
reassembles them in sequence order, rejecting (ERR_CHUNK) like libjpeg's
read_icc_profile: a count of 0, counts that differ, a sequence number of 0 or > count, a
duplicate, a missing one and an empty result.  icc_jpeg_segments produces the complete
APP2 segments (FF E2, length, header, <= 65519 data bytes each); more than 255 segments
-> ERR_LIMIT.
```

- **`fn icc_check(data: &[u8]) -> i64`**
  the declared profile size or ERR_TRUNC (shorter than the header / the declared size),
  ERR_HDR (bad signature, size, tag table), ERR_UNSUPPORTED (major version not 2..5)
  [icc_check]

- **`fn icc_jpeg_collect(data: &[u8]) -> (i32, Vec<u8>)`**
  reassemble the APP2 ICC_PROFILE segments of a JPEG file -> (1 profile / 0 no
  ICC_PROFILE segment / ERR_CHUNK inconsistent segments, profile bytes)  [icc_jpeg_collect]

- **`fn icc_jpeg_segments(icc: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, i32>`**
  the complete APP2 marker segments that embed an ICC profile in a JPEG (written after
  APP0 / APP1); empty for an empty profile  [icc_jpeg_segments]

### `img_core/icc_bi.rs`

Source: [`lib/img_core/icc_bi.rs`](../lib/img_core/icc_bi.rs)

```text
icc_bi -- the built-in ICC profiles and the profile description of icc_xform
                                                                  [src/img/icc_xform.s]

  icc_builtin(id) -> profile bytes | None     ICC_BI_SRGB / _ADOBE / _P3 / _PROPHOTO /
                                              _GRAY22  [icc_builtin]
  icc_describe(icc, buf) -> bytes             the 'desc' tag as UTF-8 (v2 desc; v4 mluc:
                                              German, else English, else the first
                                              record), NUL-terminated in buf; 0 = none
                                              (buf[0] = 0)  [icc_describe]

Built-in profiles (bi_make; own numbers, no copied files): ICC v4.3, class mntr, PCS XYZ,
D50 wtpt, desc / cprt as mluc, chad (Bradford from the space's white), colorants as
s15.16, TRC as para.  The assembly writes them into static buffers on the first call;
here the same function runs at compile time (const fn), so the bytes are static from the
start and every thread may ask for them.
```

- **`fn icc_builtin(id: u32) -> Option<&'static [u8]>`**
  the bytes of built-in profile id (ICC_BI_\*), None for an unknown id  [icc_builtin]

- **`fn icc_describe(icc: &[u8], buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize`**
  the 'desc' tag of a profile as UTF-8 into buf (cap = buf.len(), NUL-terminated) -> text
  bytes (0 = none)  [icc_describe]

### `img_core/icc_xform.rs`

Source: [`lib/img_core/icc_xform.rs`](../lib/img_core/icc_xform.rs)

```text
icc_xform -- ICC colour transforms                  [src/img/icc_xform.s, inc/icc.inc]

  IccXform::build(src, dst, intent, flags) -> IccXform | ERR_*
       src / dst = profile bytes (None = the built-in sRGB); intent ICC_* , flags
       ICF_BPC / ICF_NOIDENT / ICF_LUT.  ERR_TRUNC / ERR_HDR (broken profile, bad tag),
       ERR_UNSUPPORTED (CMYK / Lab / other device spaces, links, abstract, named colour,
       unknown tag types, gray LUT profiles, singular colorants, intent > 3), ERR_LIMIT
       (curv > 2^20 entries)  [icc_xform_build]
  xf.row(src, dst)        straight BGRA pixels (8 bit), alpha unchanged; any thread, no
                          allocation  [icc_xform_row]
  xf.row_in_place(px)     the same with src == dst
  xf.kind() -> ICX_*      [icc_xform_kind]
  icc_builtin(id) -> profile bytes (static) | None   ICC_BI_*  [icc_builtin]
  icc_describe(icc, buf) -> bytes   UTF-8 'desc' text, NUL-terminated  [icc_describe]
  set_parallel(f)         the loop that builds LUT grids (plat::parallel_for in the apps;
                          without one the grid planes are built one after the other)

Profiles: RGB and gray, v2 and v4, classes mntr / scnr / prtr / spac.  Matrix/TRC
(rXYZ gXYZ bXYZ + rTRC gTRC bTRC, gray kTRC; curv identity / gamma / table, para 0..4)
and LUT tags (A2B0..2 / B2A0..2 as lut8 / lut16 / mAB / mBA; PCS XYZ or Lab).  Tag choice
like LittleCMS: the intent's LUT tag (absolute: *1), else *0, else the matrix/TRC.
Media white for the absolute intent: wtpt (D50 for v2 display profiles, like lcms).

Paths (ICX_*): identical profiles -> copy; matrix/TRC on both sides -> ICX_MATRIX: per
channel f32 linearisation tables (256), one 3x3 f32 matrix (dst^-1 * adapt * src), linear
values clamped to 0..1, index round(sqrt(v) * 4096) into u8 inverse tables (4097 entries,
built by inverting 8193 forward samples of the destination TRC) -- a simd::Kernel, 4 (SSE2)
or 8 (AVX2) pixels per step, every lane computes what the assembly's SSE lane computes;
a result that maps every channel value to itself becomes ICX_IDENTITY.  Otherwise
ICX_LUT: the whole float64 pipeline (src -> PCS XYZ -> intent / BPC -> dst) sampled on a
33^3 grid whose node positions are (k/32)^2 per axis (dense near black), f32 per channel,
tetrahedral interpolation in f32 (grid planes on the worker pool, see set_parallel).  For
matrix/TRC (gray) destinations the grid holds the destination's LINEAR values, unclamped,
and every pixel ends like the matrix path (clamp, sqrt index, inverse TRC table); for B2A
destinations device values.  The tetrahedron is chosen without branches (a table over the
three comparisons of the assembly's decision tree, the weights as max / median / min of
the fractions): the same vertices and weights, no mispredictions on random pixels.

The handle of the assembly becomes a value (dropping it frees it); the parsing and the
float64 maths are in icc_eval, the built-in profiles and the description in icc_bi.
Deviation: out of memory aborts (Vec) instead of ERR_NOMEM.
```

*icc.inc*

```rust
ICC_PERCEPTUAL: u32 = 0  // rendering intents (ICC numbering)
ICC_RELATIVE: u32 = 1  // relative colorimetric
ICC_SATURATION: u32 = 2
ICC_ABSOLUTE: u32 = 3  // absolute colorimetric
ICF_BPC: u32 = 1  // black point compensation (not with ICC_ABSOLUTE)
ICF_NOIDENT: u32 = 2  // never take the identity shortcut (tests)
ICF_LUT: u32 = 4  // always use the 3D LUT path (tests)
ICX_IDENTITY: u32 = 0  // rows are copied
ICX_MATRIX: u32 = 1  // linearisation tables + 3x3 matrix + inverse tables
ICX_LUT: u32 = 2  // 33^3 3D LUT, tetrahedral interpolation
ICC_BI_SRGB: u32 = 0  // sRGB IEC61966-2.1 compatible
ICC_BI_ADOBE: u32 = 1  // Adobe RGB (1998) compatible
ICC_BI_P3: u32 = 2  // Display P3
ICC_BI_PROPHOTO: u32 = 3  // ProPhoto RGB (ROMM, gamma 1.8)
ICC_BI_GRAY22: u32 = 4  // gray, gamma 2.2, D50
ICC_BI_COUNT: u32 = 5
ICC_MON_PRIMARY: i32 = -1  // plat::monitor_icc: the primary monitor
```

- **`struct IccXform`**
  a colour transform between two profiles

- **`fn set_parallel(f: ParallelFor)`**
  the loop the LUT grids are built with = img_core::set_parallel (kept for its callers)

**`impl IccXform`**

- **`fn build(src: Option<&[u8]>, dst: Option<&[u8]>, intent: u32, flags: u32) -> Result<IccXform, i32>`**
  a transform from profile src to profile dst (None = the built-in sRGB)
  [icc_xform_build]
- **`fn kind(&self) -> u32`**
  ICX_IDENTITY / ICX_MATRIX / ICX_LUT  [icc_xform_kind]
- **`fn row(&self, src: &[u8], dst: &mut [u8])`**
  dst.len() / 4 straight BGRA pixels from src (at least as long) to dst, alpha
  unchanged  [icc_xform_row]
- **`fn row_in_place(&self, px: &mut [u8])`**
  row with src == dst: px.len() / 4 pixels converted in place

### `img_core/inflate.rs`

Source: [`lib/img_core/inflate.rs`](../lib/img_core/inflate.rs)

```text
inflate -- DEFLATE (RFC 1951) and zlib (RFC 1950) decompression  [src/img/inflate.s]

  zlib_inflate(src, dst) -> Ok(bytes written) / Err(ERR_*)
  inflate_raw(src, dst) -> Ok(bytes) / Err(ERR_*)
  inflate_raw_ex(src, dst) -> Ok((bytes, consumed)) / Err(ERR_*)   consumed = input bytes
                                    of the deflate data (up to the end of the final block)
  InflateState::new() -> Box      reusable decoder state (one thread at a time; an error
                                    never affects later calls)
  zlib_inflate_st(st, src, dst) / inflate_raw_st(st, src, dst)   the same with a state
                                    (None = a temporary one, like the classic calls)
  rev16(x)                        16-bit bit reversal (shared with deflate)
  LBASE / LEXT / DBASE / DEXT / CLORDER   the code tables (shared with deflate)

The output buffer has a fixed capacity: every write is bounds-checked (ERR_OVERFLOW),
every back reference is checked against the bytes produced so far (ERR_DIST), reading
past the end of the input gives ERR_TRUNC.  zlib header errors: ERR_HDR; a wrong
Adler-32: ERR_CHECKSUM.

The bit buffer behaves exactly as the assembly's (the error codes of corrupt input depend
on it): refilled 8 bytes at a time while 8 input bytes remain (the count becomes
56 + count % 8), byte by byte near the end with zero bytes counted as padding (more than
PAD_MAX of them = ERR_TRUNC); Huffman tables as stb_image (10-bit fast table, canonical
slow path); a code table's sorted-index arrays keep entries of an earlier build (the
slow path checks the length, as the assembly).

Speed (perf-inflate): the symbol loop runs in codes_fast while 8 input bytes remain at
every refill and a whole match fits the output: no slow refill, no overflow checks, no
call on the literal path (the state stays in registers), u32 fast tables (lx / dx) with
the length / distance base and extra-bit count folded in (one load and two shifts per
code), wide match moves.  It hands over to the careful loop before the symbol where a
condition fails; both follow the assembly's loop step by step.
```

- **`static LBASE: [u16; 32]`**

- **`static DBASE: [u16; 32]`**

- **`static LEXT: [u8; 32]`**

- **`static DEXT: [u8; 32]`**

- **`static CLORDER: [u8; 19]`**

- **`const fn rev16(x: u32) -> u32`**
  16-bit bit reversal  [zl_rev16]

- **`struct InflateState`**
  a reusable decoder state (IS_\*); only `pad` is reset per call, the fixed tables are built
  once per state, every other table is rebuilt before it is read

**`impl InflateState`**

- **`fn new() -> Box<InflateState>`**
  a zeroed state  [inflate_state_new]

- **`fn zlib_inflate(src: &[u8], dst: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, i32>`**
  zlib stream src -> dst  [zlib_inflate]

- **`fn zlib_inflate_st(st: Option<&mut InflateState>, src: &[u8], dst: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, i32>`**
  zlib_inflate with a reusable state (None = a temporary one)  [zlib_inflate_st]

- **`fn inflate_raw(src: &[u8], dst: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, i32>`**
  raw deflate data src -> dst  [inflate_raw]

- **`fn inflate_raw_ex(src: &[u8], dst: &mut [u8]) -> Result<(usize, usize), i32>`**
  inflate_raw that also gives the input bytes the deflate data occupied  [inflate_raw_ex]

- **`fn inflate_raw_st(st: Option<&mut InflateState>, src: &[u8], dst: &mut [u8]) -> Result<(usize, usize), i32>`**
  inflate_raw_ex with a reusable state (None = a temporary one)  [inflate_raw_st]

### `img_core/io.rs`

Source: [`lib/img_core/io.rs`](../lib/img_core/io.rs)

```text
io -- image files: format detection, codec dispatch, load and save; the format table and
the glue of the core codecs                    [src/img/imgio.s, imgreg.s, imgcore.s glue]

Classic API (FMT_PARGB, PNG / JPEG / BMP / GIF only, through the codecs' decode_fl / save):
  decode(data) -> Ok(surface) / Err(ERR_*)     PNG, JPEG, GIF or BMP by magic bytes
  load(path) -> Ok(surface) / Err(ERR_*)
  save(path, sf) / save_ex(path, sf, quality) -> 0 / ERR_*   by extension (.png .jpg .jpeg
                                               .jfif .jpe .bmp .dib .gif, any case)
  format(path) -> IMGF_* of those extensions (0 = unknown)
Extended API:
  detect(data, hint) -> IMGF_* / 0             magic bytes (TGA: hint or its probe)
  probe(data, meta) -> IMGF_* / 0              detect + header scan into meta
  decode_ex(data, dec, meta) -> Ok(surface) / Err(ERR_*)
  load_ex(path, dec, meta) -> Ok(surface) / Err(ERR_*)
  encode(sf, opt) -> Ok(file bytes) / Err(ERR_*)
  save_opt(path, sf, opt) -> 0 / ERR_*         atomic write (the file hook)
  opt_init(fmt) -> ImgOpt                      paint.net's defaults
  format_info(fmt) -> Option<FmtInfo>          format_from_path(path) -> IMGF_* / 0
  filter_list(need, cap) -> the ui_filedlg list "png=PNG\0jpg;...=JPEG\0...\0" (empty when
                                               it does not fit into cap bytes)
  register(fmt, codec) -> 0 / ERR_UNSUPPORTED  register_dyn(name, exts, mime, props, codec)
  set_file_io(FileIo)                          the file functions of load / save (img_core
                                               does not depend on plat: the app sets them)
Glue for the core codecs (imgcore.s):
  core_decode(data, sf, dec, meta, scan, decode_fl) -> 0 / ERR_*   scanner, frame / size
                                               checks, then the classic decoder
  core_probe(data, meta, scan) -> bool
  dpi_ppm(dpi) / ppm_dpi(ppm)                  16.16 dpi <-> pixels per metre

The format table has a static entry per built-in format (name, extensions, MIME,
properties); a codec crate's `CODEC` gets into it through register (img_all::
use_core_codecs / use_all_codecs).  Runtime formats (plugins) use the slots IMG_DYN0 ..
IMGF_COUNT - 1.

decode_ex: the codec gets a normalised copy of dec (maxpx filled in; ICC / EXIF / XMP
dropped when the caller passed no ImgMeta); the ImgMeta is cleared first and gets fmt,
orient 1, frames 1.  After a successful decode the orientation is applied (unless
IMGD_NO_ORIENT or the codec did it: IMF_ORIENT_APPLIED) and the pixels are converted to
FMT_ARGB (IMGD_STRAIGHT) or FMT_PARGB.  On an error the blobs are dropped (the other
metadata fields stay).
```

- **`fn format_info(fmt: u32) -> Option<FmtInfo>`**
  the format table entry of fmt (None for 0, out of range or a free runtime slot)
  [img_format_info]

- **`fn codec(fmt: u32) -> Option<&'static Codec>`**
  the registered codec of fmt  [img_codec]

- **`fn register(fmt: u32, c: Option<&'static Codec>) -> i32`**
  put a codec descriptor (or None to remove one) into the format table: caps = the
  format's properties | the codec's caps, iom = the codec's iom  [img_register]

- **`fn register_dyn(name: &'static str, exts: &'static str, mime: &'static str, props: u32, c: &'static Codec) -> u32`**
  a new runtime format (plugins): name "QOI", exts "qoi=QOI", mime, props (IFC_\* format
  properties; DECODE / ENCODE come from the codec) -> its IMGF (IMG_DYN0..) or 0 when every
  slot is taken or name / exts is empty  [img_register_dyn]

- **`fn register_external(name: &'static str, exts: &'static str, props: u32, c: &'static Codec) -> u32`**
  Separate slots for external codec hosts, preserving the native plugin ABI's IDs.

- **`fn filter_list(need: u32, cap: usize) -> Vec<u8>`**
  a ui_filedlg extension list "png=PNG\\0jpg;jpeg;...=JPEG\\0...\\0" of every format whose
  caps contain all bits of need; empty when it does not fit into cap bytes (incl. the
  final empty entry)  [img_filter_list]

- **`fn opt_init<'a>(fmt: u32) -> ImgOpt<'a>`**
  an ImgOpt with paint.net's defaults for fmt: quality 95 (JPEG / WebP / AVIF / HEIC), 90
  (JPEG XL / XR), dithering 7, transparency threshold 128, JPEG chroma 4:2:0, TGA 32 bit +
  RLE, DDS BC1, JPEG XL effort 7  [img_opt_init]

- **`fn format(path: &str) -> u32`**
  IMGF_PNG / JPEG / BMP / GIF from the file extension (any case), 0 when unknown
  [img_format]

- **`fn format_from_path(path: &str) -> u32`**
  IMGF_\* from the file extension (any case), 0 when unknown; the registered runtime
  formats after the built-in ones  [img_format_from_path]

- **`fn format_from_bytes(path: &[u8]) -> u32`**
  format_from_path of a path given as bytes (up to the first NUL)

- **`struct FileIo`**
  the file functions of load / save (img_core sits below plat)
  - `read: fn(&str) -> Option<Vec<u8>>`
  - `write: fn(&str, &[u8]) -> bool` -- atomic write

- **`fn set_file_io(io: FileIo)`**
  set the file functions (call once at start-up, e.g. with plat::read_file / write_file)

- **`fn decode(data: &[u8]) -> Result<Surface, i32>`**
  decode PNG, JPEG, GIF or BMP data (by its magic bytes) into a new FMT_PARGB surface
  [img_decode]

- **`fn load(path: &str) -> Result<Surface, i32>`**
  read a PNG, JPEG, GIF or BMP file into a new FMT_PARGB surface  [img_load]

- **`fn save(path: &str, sf: &Surface) -> i32`**
  write sf in the format of the file extension (see format); JPEG with quality 90
  [img_save]

- **`fn save_ex(path: &str, sf: &Surface, quality: i32) -> i32`**
  save with a JPEG quality (1..100, 0 = 90)  [img_save_ex]

*detection*

```rust
PNG_SIG: [u8; 8] = [0x89, b'P', b'N', b'G', 0x0D, 0x0A, 0x1A, 0x0A]
```

- **`fn detect(data: &[u8], hint: u32) -> u32`**
  the format of data by its magic bytes (order: PNG, JPEG, GIF, DDS, WebP, TIFF / BigTIFF,
  JPEG XR, ISOBMFF (AVIF / HEIC), JPEG XL container, PDN, gzip (legacy PDN), JPEG XL
  codestream, BMP, runtime formats, TGA by hint or its registered probe) -> IMGF_\* / 0
  [img_detect]

- **`fn probe(data: &[u8], meta: Option<&mut ImgMeta>) -> u32`**
  detect without a hint; with a meta: it is cleared (fmt, orient 1, frames 1 set) and the
  registered codec's probe reads the header into it (w = 0 when the header could not be
  read; no blobs)  [img_probe]

- **`fn decode_ex(data: &[u8], dec: Option<&ImgDec>, meta: Option<&mut ImgMeta>) -> Result<Surface, i32>`**
  decode any registered format into a new surface: FMT_ARGB with IMGD_STRAIGHT, else
  FMT_PARGB (see the module header)  [img_decode_ex]

- **`fn load_ex(path: &str, dec: Option<&ImgDec>, meta: Option<&mut ImgMeta>) -> Result<Surface, i32>`**
  read a file and decode_ex it; a zero dec.hint comes from the file extension; ERR_IO
  when unreadable  [img_load_ex]

- **`fn encode(sf: &Surface, opt: Option<&ImgOpt>) -> Result<Vec<u8>, i32>`**
  encode sf (FMT_XRGB, PARGB, ARGB or A8) as opt.fmt  [img_encode]
  Errors: ERR_UNSUPPORTED (format unknown / not registered / no encoder, or another
  surface format), ERR_DIMS (empty surface), ERR_CANCEL and the codec errors.

- **`fn save_opt(path: &str, sf: &Surface, opt: Option<&ImgOpt>) -> i32`**
  encode and write the file atomically; fmt 0 takes the format from the extension;
  ERR_IO when writing fails  [img_save_opt]

- **`fn core_decode(data: &[u8], sf: &mut Surface, dec: &ImgDec, meta: &mut ImgMeta, scan: ScanFn, decode_fl: DecodeFlFn) -> i32`**
  run the metadata scanner; with IMGD_PROBE return its result; else refuse frame > 0,
  check the header size (ERR_LIMIT) and call the classic decoder with JDF_STRAIGHT
  (IMGD_STRAIGHT) | JDF_NO_ORIENT (IMGD_NO_ORIENT)  [core_decode]

- **`fn core_probe(data: &[u8], meta: &mut ImgMeta, scan: ScanFn) -> bool`**
  the scanner without blobs: recognised (meta filled)  [core_probe]

- **`fn dpi_ppm(dpi: u32) -> u32`**
  pixels per metre from a 16.16 dpi value (rounded, at most 0x7FFFFFFF)  [dpi_ppm]

- **`fn ppm_dpi(ppm: u32) -> u32`**
  16.16 dpi from pixels per metre (0 -> 0)  [ppm_dpi]

### `img_core/meta.rs`

Source: [`lib/img_core/meta.rs`](../lib/img_core/meta.rs)

```text
meta -- shared helpers of the codecs: metadata blobs, size limits, straight <->
premultiplied conversion, orientation, sample depth conversion, JPEG segment walking
                                                                     [src/img/imgmeta.s]

  meta_blob(meta, Blob, src)            copy src into the ICC / EXIF / XMP blob (len 0: nothing)
  meta_free(meta)                       drop the blobs (the other fields stay)
  dims_check(w, h, dec) -> 0 / ERR_DIMS / ERR_LIMIT    w, h 1..IMG_MAXDIM, w * h <= maxpx
  mem_check(bytes, dec) -> 0 / ERR_LIMIT               against dec.maxmem (0 = no limit)
  surf_alloc(w, h, fmt, dec) -> Ok(surface) / Err(ERR_*)   dims_check + a zeroed surface
  premultiply(sf) / unpremultiply(sf)   FMT_ARGB <-> FMT_PARGB in place (others untouched)
  convert(sf, fmt) -> 0 / ERR_UNSUPPORTED   in place to FMT_PARGB / FMT_ARGB from XRGB,
                                        PARGB, ARGB
  surf_convert(src, fmt) -> Ok(surface) / Err(ERR_*)   a new surface from any get_row source
  get_row(sf, y, dst, fmt)              row y as w straight (FMT_ARGB) / premultiplied
                                        (FMT_PARGB) BGRA pixels from XRGB (alpha 255),
                                        PARGB, ARGB, A8 (grey); other formats: transparent
  orient(sf, o) -> 0                    apply an EXIF orientation 2..8 (5..8 swap w and h)
  u16_to_u8(v)                          round(v / 257)
  row_u16_to_u8(dst, src, be)           16-bit samples (bytes, LE / BE) -> bytes
  half_to_f32(h)                        IEEE half -> single precision bits
  row_half_to_u8(dst, src) / row_f32_to_u8(dst, src)   round(clamp(v, 0, 1) * 255), NaN -> 0
  row_f32_to_srgb8(dst, src)            linear floats -> sRGB bytes (4096-entry table)
  jpg_seg_next(data, js) -> marker      JPEG marker segment iterator (JSeg)
  srgb_lin(v) / SRGB_LIN_BITS           sRGB byte -> linear light (f32)
  all_opaque(sf) -> bool                every alpha of a 32-bit surface is 255  [png.s]
  unpremul(p)                           premultiplied -> straight, one pixel  [png.s]

Straight -> premultiplied: round(c * a / 255); premultiplied -> straight: min(255,
(c * ceil(255 * 2^24 / a) + 2^23) >> 24), a = 0 gives 0 (gfx::straight's kernels have
exactly these rules).
```

- **`enum Blob`**
  the blobs of an ImgMeta (IM_ICC / IM_EXIF / IM_XMP)
  - `Icc`
  - `Exif`
  - `Xmp`

- **`fn meta_blob(meta: &mut ImgMeta, which: Blob, src: &[u8])`**
  store a copy of src as the blob (replacing one already there); an empty src stores
  nothing  [img_meta_blob]

- **`fn meta_free(meta: &mut ImgMeta)`**
  drop the ICC / EXIF / XMP blobs (the other fields stay)  [img_meta_free]

- **`fn dims_check(w: u32, h: u32, dec: &ImgDec) -> i32`**
  w and h must be 1..IMG_MAXDIM (ERR_DIMS), w \* h at most dec's pixel budget (ERR_LIMIT)
  [img_dims_check]

- **`fn mem_check(bytes: u64, dec: &ImgDec) -> i32`**
  bytes against dec's memory budget (0 = no limit)  [img_mem_check]

- **`fn surf_alloc(w: u32, h: u32, fmt: u8, dec: &ImgDec) -> Result<Surface, i32>`**
  dims_check, then a zeroed surface  [img_surf_alloc]

- **`fn get_row(sf: &Surface, y: usize, dst: &mut [u8], fmt: u8)`**
  row y of sf as w BGRA pixels in dst: fmt FMT_ARGB = straight, FMT_PARGB =
  premultiplied  [img_get_row]

- **`fn premultiply(sf: &mut Surface)`**
  FMT_ARGB -> FMT_PARGB in place (other formats are left alone)  [img_premultiply]

- **`fn unpremultiply(sf: &mut Surface)`**
  FMT_PARGB -> FMT_ARGB in place (other formats are left alone)  [img_unpremultiply]

- **`fn convert(sf: &mut Surface, fmt: u8) -> i32`**
  convert sf in place to fmt (FMT_PARGB or FMT_ARGB) from FMT_XRGB (alpha 255), FMT_PARGB
  or FMT_ARGB -> 0 / ERR_UNSUPPORTED  [img_convert]

- **`fn surf_convert(src: &Surface, fmt: u8) -> Result<Surface, i32>`**
  a new w x h surface (FMT_PARGB or FMT_ARGB) holding src through get_row
  [img_surf_convert]

- **`fn orient(sf: &mut Surface, o: u32) -> i32`**
  transform a 32-bit surface for display by its EXIF orientation: 2 mirror, 3 rotate
  180, 4 flip, 5 transpose, 6 rotate 90 clockwise, 7 transverse, 8 rotate 90
  counter-clockwise (1 and invalid values: nothing); 5..8 swap width and height (a new
  pixel buffer; opacity and blend mode kept) -> 0 / ERR_NOMEM  [img_orient]

- **`fn all_opaque(sf: &Surface) -> bool`**
  every alpha byte of a 32-bit surface is 255  [img_all_opaque, png.s]

- **`fn unpremul(p: u32) -> u32`**
  premultiplied -> straight of one pixel  [unpremul, png.s]

- **`fn u16_to_u8(v: u32) -> u8`**
  round(v / 257) for v = 0..65535  [img_u16_to_u8]

- **`fn row_u16_to_u8(dst: &mut [u8], src: &[u8], be: bool)`**
  16-bit samples (be: big endian) -> bytes round(v / 257); n = dst.len() (bounded by the
  source)  [img_row_u16_to_u8]

- **`fn half_to_f32(h: u32) -> u32`**
  IEEE 754 half (low 16 bits of h) -> single precision bits (subnormals, infinities and
  NaN preserved)  [img_half_to_f32]

- **`fn row_half_to_u8(dst: &mut [u8], src: &[u16])`**
  IEEE halves -> bytes round(clamp(v, 0, 1) \* 255), NaN -> 0  [img_row_half_to_u8]

- **`fn row_f32_to_u8(dst: &mut [u8], src: &[f32])`**
  floats -> bytes round(clamp(v, 0, 1) \* 255), NaN -> 0  [img_row_f32_to_u8]

- **`fn row_f32_to_srgb8(dst: &mut [u8], src: &[f32])`**
  linear-light floats -> sRGB-encoded bytes (clamp 0..1, NaN -> 0, 4096-entry table)
  [img_row_f32_to_srgb8]

- **`fn srgb_lin(v: u8) -> f32`**
  sRGB decode (IEC 61966-2-1) of v / 255  [g_img_srgb_lin]

- **`struct JSeg`**
  a JPEG segment walker (JSEG): pos = next read offset (start: 2, after SOI); ptr / len =
  the payload of the current segment (offset into the data)
  - `pos: usize`
  - `ptr: usize`
  - `len: usize`

**`impl JSeg`**

- **`fn new() -> JSeg`**

- **`fn jpg_seg_next(data: &[u8], js: &mut JSeg) -> u32`**
  the next JPEG marker segment from js.pos: skips bytes that are not 0xFF, fill bytes,
  standalone markers (TEM, RSTn); returns the marker (0xC0..0xFE) with js.ptr / js.len =
  the payload (after the 2 length bytes) and js.pos behind it; 0xD9 (EOI) and 0xD8 (SOI)
  come with len 0; 0 = end of data, a truncated segment or a length &lt; 2  [jpg_seg_next]

- **`static SRGB_LIN_BITS: [u32; 256]`**
  g_img_srgb_lin[v] = sRGB decode of v / 255 as f32 bits

### `img_core/mip.rs`

Source: [`lib/img_core/mip.rs`](../lib/img_core/mip.rs)

```text
mip -- display mip levels: 2 x 2 box filter in premultiplied space        [src/img/mip.s]

  mip_down(src, dst, x0, y0, x1, y1) -> 0 / ERR_*
       update the part of dst (the next level of src) covered by the source rectangle
       x0, y0 .. x1, y1 (exclusive; clamped to src)                            [mip_down]
  mip_down_rows(src, band, x0, x1, dy0, dy1) -> 0 / ERR_*
       the same for dst rows dy0 .. dy1-1 (of a Band of dst; workers split the dst rows) and
       the source columns x0 .. x1-1                                       [mip_down_rows]
  MipChain::alloc(w, h, levels) -> Option<MipChain>
       levels 1 .. n below a w x h base (levels 0 = until 1 x 1; at most MIP_MAX)
                                                                        [mip_chain_alloc]
  chain.update(src, rect) -> 0 / ERR_*   propagate the dirty rectangle rect ([x0, y0, x1,
       y1]; None = everything) of the base image src through every level [mip_chain_update]
  chain.level(k) -> Option<&Surface>     level k (1 .. n)                [mip_chain_level]

src: FMT_ARGB (straight) or FMT_PARGB; dst: FMT_PARGB of (w + 1) / 2 x (h + 1) / 2
(ERR_UNSUPPORTED / ERR_DIMS otherwise).  dst(i, j) averages src columns 2i, min(2i + 1,
w - 1) and rows 2j, min(2j + 1, h - 1) (odd edges repeat the last pixel):
  FMT_PARGB   v = (p00 + p01 + p10 + p11 + 2) >> 2 per channel
  FMT_ARGB    a = (sum a + 2) >> 2;  t = (sum c * a + 2) >> 2,  c = min(a, ((t + 128) * 257) >> 16)
Thread safe (no global state); mip_down_rows calls on disjoint bands may run in parallel.
The level surfaces are ordinary FMT_PARGB surfaces owned by the chain (drop = mip_chain_free).
```

```rust
MIP_MAX: u32 = 16  // levels below the base (65535 -> 1 needs 16)
```

- **`fn mip_down_rows(src: &Surface, dst: &mut Band, x0: i32, x1: i32, dy0: i32, dy1: i32) -> i32`**
  dst rows dy0 .. dy1-1 (clamped to the dst and to the band) from the source columns
  x0 .. x1-1: ERR_UNSUPPORTED (formats), ERR_DIMS (dst not the half size)  [mip_down_rows]

- **`fn mip_down(src: &Surface, dst: &mut Surface, x0: i32, y0: i32, x1: i32, y1: i32) -> i32`**
  the part of dst covered by the source rectangle x0, y0 .. x1, y1  [mip_down]

- **`struct MipChain`**
  a mip chain: levels 1 .. n (FMT_PARGB) below a w x h base  [MC_\*]
  - `n: u32`
  - `w: i32`
  - `h: i32`

**`impl MipChain`**

- **`fn alloc(w: i32, h: i32, levels: i32) -> Option<MipChain>`**
  levels 1 .. MIP_MAX as given, else (0, negative, too many) as many as reach 1 x 1;
  None when w or h is outside 1 .. 65535  [mip_chain_alloc]
- **`fn level(&self, k: u32) -> Option<&Surface>`**
  level k (1 .. n)  [mip_chain_level]
- **`fn update(&mut self, src: &Surface, rect: Option<[i32; 4]>) -> i32`**
  propagate the dirty rectangle of the base image src (None = all of it) through every
  level: the next level's rectangle is (x0 >> 1, y0 >> 1, (x1 + 1) >> 1, (y1 + 1) >> 1)
  [mip_chain_update]

### `img_core/ops.rs`

Source: [`lib/img_core/ops.rs`](../lib/img_core/ops.rs)

```text
ops -- whole-image operations on 32-bit surfaces (PARGB / ARGB / XRGB)  [src/img/imgops.s]

  flip_h(sf)                                   mirror left <-> right (in place)  [img_flip_h]
  flip_v(sf)                                   mirror top <-> bottom (in place)  [img_flip_v]
  rotate180(sf)                                (in place)                     [img_rotate180]
  rotate90(src, dir) -> Result<Surface, i32>   h x w; dir >= 0 clockwise, < 0 counter-
                                               clockwise                      [img_rotate90]
  crop(src, x, y, w, h) -> Result<Surface>     w x h; parts outside src transparent [img_crop]
  resize_canvas(src, w, h, fill) -> Result<>   w x h filled with fill (premultiplied like
                                               Dc::clear), src copied to its top-left
                                                                         [img_resize_canvas]
  blit_copy(dst, src, x, y)                    copy src to (x, y), no blending, clipped to
                                               dst only (not to a draw context's clip)
                                                                             [gfx_blit_copy]
  scale(src, w, h, bilinear) -> Result<>       16.16 fixed-point nearest / bilinear (7-bit
                                               fractions), edges clamped        [img_scale]
  skew(src, kx, ky, fill) -> Result<>          x' = x + kx y / 1000, y' = y + ky x / 1000,
                                               nearest, forward mapped; dst grows to fit
                                                                                 [img_skew]
  Band, Band::whole(sf), bands(sf, k)          disjoint row ranges of a surface, so worker
                                               threads can write different rows at once
                                               (the bands of rstest's rt_bands)

Results keep the source format; the Err value is ERR_FAIL (-1, the assembly's -1) when a
surface size is out of range.  Rows are 4 bytes per pixel (blit_copy, scale, skew and the
flips copy whole pixels).
```

- **`struct Band<'a>`**
  rows y0 .. y1 (exclusive) of a surface of w x h pixels: `px` holds them from row y0 on,
  row y at (y - y0) \* stride.  What one worker of a parallel loop may write.
  - `px: &'a mut [u8]`
  - `w: i32`
  - `h: i32`
  - `fmt: u8`
  - `stride: usize`
  - `y0: i32`
  - `y1: i32`

**`impl<'a> Band<'a>`**

- **`fn whole(sf: &'a mut Surface) -> Band<'a>`**
  every row of sf
- **`fn row(&mut self, y: i32) -> &mut [u8]`**
  row y (y0 &lt;= y &lt; y1) from its first pixel to the end of the band

- **`fn bands(sf: &mut Surface, k: usize) -> Vec<Band<'_>>`**
  k bands of rows i \* h / k .. (i + 1) \* h / k (k &lt; 1: one band)

- **`fn flip_h(sf: &mut Surface)`**
  mirror left &lt;-> right  [img_flip_h]

- **`fn flip_v(sf: &mut Surface)`**
  mirror top &lt;-> bottom  [img_flip_v]

- **`fn rotate180(sf: &mut Surface)`**
  [img_rotate180]

- **`fn rotate90(src: &Surface, dir: i32) -> Result<Surface, i32>`**
  a new h x w surface: clockwise dst(H-1-y, x) = src(x, y) (dir >= 0), counter-clockwise
  dst(y, W-1-x) = src(x, y)  [img_rotate90]

- **`fn blit_copy(dst: &mut Surface, src: &Surface, x: i32, y: i32)`**
  copy src to (x, y) of dst without blending, clipped to dst  [gfx_blit_copy]

- **`fn crop(src: &Surface, x: i32, y: i32, w: i32, h: i32) -> Result<Surface, i32>`**
  the w x h part of src at (x, y); outside parts transparent  [img_crop]

- **`fn resize_canvas(src: &Surface, w: i32, h: i32, fill: u32) -> Result<Surface, i32>`**
  w x h filled with fill, src copied to the top-left corner  [img_resize_canvas]

- **`fn scale(src: &Surface, w: i32, h: i32, bilinear: bool) -> Result<Surface, i32>`**
  w x h nearest / bilinear copy (16.16 steps, pixel centres)  [img_scale]

- **`fn skew(src: &Surface, kx: i32, ky: i32, fill: u32) -> Result<Surface, i32>`**
  shear by kx, ky in 1/1000 (nearest, every source pixel mapped forward); the dst grows by
  |kx h / 1000| x |ky w / 1000| and is filled with fill first  [img_skew]

- **`fn bpp(fmt: u8) -> usize`**
  bytes per pixel of a format (A8: 1, else 4)

### `img_core/quantize.rs`

Source: [`lib/img_core/quantize.rs`](../lib/img_core/quantize.rs)

```text
quantize -- colour quantization: palette + index image (the 8-bit encoders: GIF, BMP 8 bit,
PNG palette)                                                        [src/img/quantize.s]

  quantize(sf, max_colors, dither, pal, idx) -> (colours, transparent index or -1)
  quantize_ex(sf, max_colors, cfg, pal, idx) -> (colours, transparent index or -1)
  quantize_alloc(sf, max_colors, cfg, pal) -> Some((colours, transparent index, idx))
  opt_qcfg(opt) -> cfg from ImgOpt::dither / athresh                [imgcore.s opt_qcfg]

Input: FMT_PARGB (un-premultiplied), FMT_ARGB (straight), FMT_XRGB, FMT_A8 (grey).
max_colors is clamped to 2..256.
cfg (quantize_ex) = dither level 0..8 in the low byte (0 = none, 8 = full Floyd-Steinberg;
the diffused error is scaled by level / 8; > 8 counts as 8)
  | threshold << IQ_THRESH_SHIFT: pixels with alpha < threshold (0..256) become
    transparent, all others opaque with their straight colour
  | IQF_WHITE: composite over white first (c + (255 - a) premultiplied), no transparency
  | IQF_KEEPALPHA: exact palette only, alpha kept per entry (straight ARGB, every alpha-0
    pixel counts as 0x00000000); more than max_colors colours -> 0 colours (no Wu fallback;
    dither and threshold are ignored)
quantize(sf, max, dither, ...) = quantize_ex with level 8 when dither != 0 (else 0) and
threshold 128.

Exact palette: when the image has at most max_colors distinct colours (the transparent
pixels count as one colour) the palette holds exactly those, in order of first appearance.
Otherwise: Wu's variance-minimizing quantizer (X. Wu, Graphics Gems II, 1991) on a
32 x 32 x 32 histogram of the opaque pixels (exact 64-bit moments) splits RGB space into
max_colors boxes (max_colors - 1 when there are transparent pixels); a palette colour is the
rounded mean of its box.  Pixels map to the nearest palette colour (squared RGB distance,
lowest index on ties, searched for the centre of the pixel's 64 x 64 x 64 cell and cached per
cell), with serpentine Floyd-Steinberg error diffusion (7/16, 3/16, 5/16, 1/16; the
accumulated error e (in 16ths) is applied as (e * level + 64) >> 7) when the level is not 0.
Transparent pixels get the last palette entry (0x00000000) and take no part in the diffusion.

  pal  u32[256] straight 0xAARRGGBB: alpha 255, or 0x00000000 for the transparent entry
       (IQF_KEEPALPHA: any alpha); entries past the count are set to 0.  Left untouched
       when the result is 0 colours.
  idx  w * h bytes, rows packed (stride w).  As in the assembly, a failed exact palette
       (IQF_KEEPALPHA) leaves the indices written before the failing pixel.
Result: (number of palette entries 1..max_colors, index of the transparent entry or -1);
(0, -1) for an empty surface, an unknown format or an index buffer smaller than w * h.
```

```rust
IQ_THRESH_SHIFT: u32 = 8  // cfg: threshold << IQ_THRESH_SHIFT
IQF_KEEPALPHA: u32 = 1 << 20  // exact palette with per-entry alpha only
IQF_WHITE: u32 = 1 << 21  // composite over white first (no transparent entry)
```

- **`fn quantize(sf: &Surface, max_colors: i32, dither: u32, pal: &mut [u32; 256], idx: &mut [u8]) -> (u32, i32)`**
  img_quantize: cfg = level 8 (dither != 0) or 0, threshold 128

- **`fn quantize_alloc(sf: &Surface, max_colors: i32, cfg: u32, pal: &mut [u32; 256]) -> Option<(u32, i32, Vec<u8>)>`**
  img_quantize_alloc: quantize_ex into a new index image (w \* h bytes); None on failure

- **`fn opt_qcfg(opt: &ImgOpt) -> u32`**
  opt_qcfg: the quantize_ex cfg from the biased ImgOpt::dither (0 = level 7, n = level n - 1,
  at most 8) and ImgOpt::athresh (0 = 128, n = threshold n - 1, at most 255)  [imgcore.s]

- **`fn quantize_ex(sf: &Surface, max_colors: i32, cfg: u32, pal_out: &mut [u32; 256], idx: &mut [u8]) -> (u32, i32)`**
  img_quantize_ex (see the module header)

### `img_core/resample.rs`

Source: [`lib/img_core/resample.rs`](../lib/img_core/resample.rs)

```text
resample -- separable image resampling (resize) of 32-bit surfaces       [src/img/resample.s]

  resize(src, dst, filter, flags) -> 0 / ERR_*            resample src into dst (dst's size
                                                          is the target size)  [rs_resize]
  Resize::prepare(src, dst, filter, flags) -> Result<Resize, i32>
       validate and build the weight tables (dst is only measured)              [rs_prepare]
  ctx.rows(band, y0, y1) -> 0 / ERR_*                     dst rows y0 .. y1-1 (clamped) into
       a Band of dst; several threads may run it at once on disjoint bands  [rs_resize_rows]
  axis_weights(s, d, filter) -> Result<AxisWeights, i32>  the table of one axis
                                                                         [rs_axis_weights]
  to_work(w, src, n, fmt, flags)      n 8-bit pixels -> working row             [rs_to_work]
  from_work(dst, w, n, fmt, flags)    n working pixels -> 8-bit row           [rs_from_work]

Formats: src and dst may be FMT_ARGB (straight) or FMT_PARGB (premultiplied), in any
combination (ERR_UNSUPPORTED otherwise); sizes 1 .. 65535 (ERR_DIMS).  Filters and flags:
RS_* below.

Pipeline (filters other than RS_NEAREST):
  1. Working values: every source row becomes 4 x i16 per pixel, premultiplied, a full
     channel = RS_PONE = 16320 (= 255 * 64):
       FMT_PARGB   P = v << 6 (all four channels)
       FMT_ARGB    P_a = a << 6,  P_c = ((c * a + 2) * 16448) >> 16
       RS_GAMMA    straight s (FMT_PARGB: s = min(255, (v * 255 + a / 2) / a), a = 0 -> 0),
                   lin = LIN16[s] = round(16320 * srgb_to_linear(s / 255)),
                   P_c = (lin * a * 257 + 32768) >> 16,  P_a = a << 6
  2. Horizontal pass: H = (sum_k w_k * P[x0 + k] + 8192) >> 14, saturated to i16
     (arithmetic shift = floor); results are NOT clamped between the passes.
  3. Vertical pass: V = (sum_j w_j * H[y0 + j] + 8192) >> 14, saturated to i16.
  4. Output: A = max(P_a, 0) (may exceed 16320: ringing at alpha edges), colours clamped
     to 0 .. A, a = (min(A, 16320) + 32) >> 6; the colour ratio P_c / A is kept:
       FMT_ARGB    c = rint_even(f32(P_c * 255) / f32(max(A, 1)))
       FMT_PARGB   c = (P_c + 32) >> 6, or the straight c above when A > 16320 (a = 255)
       RS_GAMMA    A = 0 -> pixel 0; else idx = (P_c * 8190 + A) / (2 A),
                   s = SRGB8[idx] = round(255 * linear_to_srgb(idx / 4095)); straight: c = s,
                   premultiplied: c = ((s * a + 128) * 257) >> 16
RS_NEAREST copies the source pixel whose centre is nearest: sx = ((2x + 1) S) / (2 D), no
premultiplication.  When both axes are identities (same size and an interpolating filter:
everything except RS_BICUBIC_SMOOTH) the rows are copied.  Format changes on the copy /
nearest paths use gfx::straight premul / unpremul.

Weight tables (per axis, per output pixel i, all f64 with IEEE double semantics):
  scale = S / D; fs = max(scale, 1) for widened filters, else 1; R = support * fs
  (support: triangle 1, cubics 2, Lanczos 3).  center = ((2i + 1) S) / (2 D);
  j = floor(center - R) .. ceil(center + R): w_j = K(((j + 0.5) - center) / fs).
  RS_FANT: lo = (i S) / D, hi = ((i + 1) S) / D, j = floor(lo) .. ceil(hi) - 1:
  w_j = min(hi, j + 1) - max(lo, j).
  total = sum w_j (in j order); every w_j is added to pixel clamp(j, 0, S - 1) (clamp to
  edge); q = floor(w / total * 16384 + 0.5); 16384 - sum(q) is added to the first largest
  q; zero taps at both ends are dropped.  Kernels (x = |x|):
    triangle  max(0, 1 - x)
    cubic a   x < 1: ((a + 2) x - (a + 3)) x x + 1;  x < 2: ((a x - 5a) x + 8a) x - 4a
              (Catmull-Rom a = -0.5; RS_ADAPTIVE growing: a = -0.75)
    Mitchell  x < 1: (7/6 x - 2) x x + 8/9;  x < 2: ((-7/18 x + 2) x - 10/3) x + 16/9
    Lanczos-3 x = 0: 1; x < 3: sinpi(x) sinpi(x / 3) / (pi x (pi x / 3)) with x / 3 computed
              as x * (1/3); sinpi: n = floor(t + 0.5), z = pi (t - n), Taylor polynomial
              of degree 13 (Horner in z^2), negated for odd n
  RS_ADAPTIVE: per axis Lanczos-3 when S > D, else cubic a = -0.75.
Record of a table (AxisWeights::tab, the bytes of the assembly): +0 i32 first source pixel
x0, +4 i32 taps used, +8 8 zero bytes, +16 n/2 pairs of 16 bytes {w_2k, w_2k+1} x 4 (i16)
-- the pmaddwd operand.  n = the largest tap count rounded up to even; x0 is moved left so
that x0 + n <= S when possible.

Threads: no global state.  rows() allocates its scratch per call (about (S_w + n) * 24 +
K * (D_w + 4) * 8 bytes; K = taps of the vertical filter, at most RS_RING_BUDGET bytes of
ring rows; more taps are summed in groups through i32 accumulators).  All sums are i32
(wrapping), so groups, band splits and register widths never change a result.  The
horizontal kernels keep the SSE2 layout of the assembly (pair rows); the vertical pass,
to_work and from_work work on SSE2 or AVX2 registers (simd::Kernel), pixel by pixel equal.
```

```rust
RS_NEAREST: u32 = 0  // point sampling (no premultiplication, pixels copied)
RS_BILINEAR: u32 = 1  // triangle, widened by the scale factor when downscaling
RS_BILINEAR_LQ: u32 = 2  // triangle, never widened (plain 2 x 2)
RS_BICUBIC: u32 = 3  // Catmull-Rom (Keys a = -0.5), widened when downscaling
RS_BICUBIC_SMOOTH: u32 = 4  // Mitchell-Netravali B = C = 1/3, widened
RS_LANCZOS3: u32 = 5  // sinc(x) sinc(x/3), |x| < 3, widened when downscaling
RS_FANT: u32 = 6  // exact area average
RS_ADAPTIVE: u32 = 7  // per axis: Lanczos-3 shrinking, Keys a = -0.75 growing
RS_FILTER_COUNT: u32 = 8
RS_MULTISAMPLE: u32 = 8  // xform only: 4 x 4 supersampled bilinear
RS_GAMMA: u32 = 1  // flag: filter in linear light
RS_WONE: i32 = 16384  // sum of every output's weights
RS_PONE: i32 = 16320  // working value of a full channel (255 * 64)
```

- **`struct AxisWeights`**
  the weight table of one axis (S source -> D destination pixels)
  - `tab: Vec<u8>` -- D records of rb bytes (format: module header)
  - `n: u32` -- taps per record (even)
  - `rb: u32` -- bytes per record = n \* 8 + 16
  - `nmax: u32` -- largest real tap count
  - `id: bool` -- S == D with one tap per output (an identity)

- **`fn axis_weights(s: i32, d: i32, filter: u32) -> Result<AxisWeights, i32>`**
  the weight table of one axis: ERR_DIMS (sizes outside 1 .. 65535), ERR_UNSUPPORTED
  (RS_NEAREST or an unknown filter)  [rs_axis_weights]

- **`fn to_work(w: &mut [i16], src: &[u8], n: usize, fmt: u8, flags: u32)`**
  n pixels of an 8-bit row (FMT_ARGB / FMT_PARGB) to working values (4 x i16 per pixel,
  premultiplied, full = 16320); RS_GAMMA in flags: linear light.  Writes w[..n \* 4].
  [rs_to_work]

- **`fn from_work(dst: &mut [u8], w: &[i16], n: usize, fmt: u8, flags: u32)`**
  n working pixels (4 x i16, any values) to an 8-bit row of FMT_ARGB / FMT_PARGB (module
  header).  Reads w[..n \* 4], writes dst[..n \* 4].  [rs_from_work]

- **`struct Resize<'a>`**
  a prepared resize of src into a destination of the measured size; read-only while
  rows() runs (Sync)  [rs_prepare context]

**`impl<'a> Resize<'a>`**

- **`fn prepare(src: &'a Surface, dst: &Surface, filter: u32, flags: u32) -> Result<Resize<'a>, i32>`**
  validate and build the weight tables: ERR_UNSUPPORTED (filter, formats), ERR_DIMS
  (sizes outside 1 .. 65535)  [rs_prepare]
- **`fn rows(&self, band: &mut Band, y0: i32, y1: i32) -> i32`**
  dst rows y0 .. y1-1 (clamped to 0 .. dst h) into band, which must hold them  [rs_resize_rows]

- **`fn resize(src: &Surface, dst: &mut Surface, filter: u32, flags: u32) -> i32`**
  resample src into dst (its size is the target size): prepare + every row  [rs_resize]

### `img_core/xform.rs`

Source: [`lib/img_core/xform.rs`](../lib/img_core/xform.rs)

```text
xform -- affine resampling (move / rotate / scale / skew) and 2 x 3 matrix helpers
[src/img/xform.s]

  affine(src, dst, m, filter, flags, clip) -> 0 / ERR_*                         [rs_affine]
       dst(x, y) = src sampled at m * (x + 0.5, y + 0.5) for every dst pixel inside clip
       ([x0, y0, x1, y1] exclusive, or None = all of dst); pixels outside clip are untouched
  Affine::prepare(src, dst, m, filter, flags, clip) -> Result<Affine, i32>
       validate; dst is only measured                                 [rs_affine_prepare]
  xa.rows(band, y0, y1) -> 0           dst rows y0 .. y1-1 (clipped) into a Band of dst;
                                       disjoint bands may run on several threads
                                                                         [rs_affine_rows]
  Matrix, Matrix::IDENTITY, m.apply(x, y), sincos(angle)    (gfx::xform: xf_identity,
                                       xf_apply, xf_sincos)
  mul(a, b) -> Matrix                  a * b (apply b first, then a)              [xf_mul]
  invert(m) -> Option<Matrix>          None: singular or not finite            [xf_invert]
  translate(&mut m, tx, ty)            m = m * T(tx, ty)                      [xf_translate]
  scale(&mut m, sx, sy)                m = m * S(sx, sy)                          [xf_scale]
  rotate(&mut m, angle, cx, cy)        m = m * R(angle about cx, cy)             [xf_rotate]
  CUBIC_LUT                            Catmull-Rom taps -1, 0, 1, 2 for phase p / 256 (i16,
                                       sum 16384)                            [xf_cubic_lut]

Matrix {a, b, c, d, e, f}: x' = a x + b y + c, y' = d x + e y + f.  affine maps DESTINATION
pixel centres to continuous SOURCE coordinates (source pixel (i, j) covers [i, i + 1) x
[j, j + 1)); to draw an image transformed by a forward matrix F, pass invert(F).  Angles in
radians; with y pointing down a positive angle turns clockwise on screen: R = {cos, -sin,
0, sin, cos, 0}.

Sampling (per destination pixel, xc = x + 0.5, yc = y + 0.5, all f64 in this order):
  qx = a xc + (b yc + c),  qy = d xc + (e yc + f)
  RS_NEAREST     pixel (floor(clamp(qx)), floor(clamp(qy))), copied (format converted with
                 gfx::straight premul / unpremul when src and dst formats differ)
  RS_BILINEAR    U = floor(clamp((qx - 0.5) * 256 + 0.5)) (24.8 fixed point, same for V);
                 x0 = U >> 8, phase fx = U & 255.  Phase 0 in both axes: the pixel (x0, y0)
                 is copied exactly (like nearest).  Else the working values of the 2 x 2
                 pixels (resample::to_work: premultiplied, linear light with RS_GAMMA) are
                 mixed: wx = fx * 64: T = (P00 (16384 - wx) + P10 wx + 8192) >> 14, B
                 likewise, P = (T (16384 - wy) + B wy + 8192) >> 14, then from_work.
  RS_BICUBIC     same phases, 4 x 4 Catmull-Rom taps x0 - 1 .. x0 + 2 with the weights
                 CUBIC_LUT[phase] (exact copy at phase 0); rows first:
                 R = sat16((sum w P + 8192) >> 14), P = sat16((sum w R + 8192) >> 14)
  RS_MULTISAMPLE 16 bilinear samples at xc = x + o_s, yc = y + o_t, o = 1/8, 3/8, 5/8, 7/8
                 (no exact-copy shortcut), P = (sum of the 16 sample P + 8) >> 4
  clamp(v) limits v to +-2^30 (NaN -> -2^30).  An integer translation (a = e = 1, b = d = 0,
  c and f integers below 2^30) with NEAREST / BILINEAR / BICUBIC gives exactly the copies
  above and takes a row-copy path.  Edge modes (flags bits 8..9): outside pixels are
  transparent 0 (XF_TRANSPARENT), the nearest edge pixel (XF_CLAMP), j mod n (XF_WRAP) or
  mirrored with period 2n, edge pixels repeated (XF_REFLECT).

Threads: no global state; rows() allocates about 17 KB of scratch per call.  The 2 x 2 /
4 x 4 mixing keeps the assembly's SSE2 layout (one output per register).
```

Re-exports: `gfx::xform::{Matrix, sincos}`

```rust
XF_TRANSPARENT: u32 = 0  // outside the source = transparent black
XF_CLAMP: u32 = 1  // repeat the edge pixels
XF_WRAP: u32 = 2  // tile
XF_REFLECT: u32 = 3  // mirror (edge pixels duplicated)
XF_EDGE_SHIFT: u32 = 8
XF_EDGE_MASK: u32 = 0x300
```

- **`fn mul(a: &Matrix, b: &Matrix) -> Matrix`**
  a \* b (b applied first)  [xf_mul]

- **`fn invert(m: &Matrix) -> Option<Matrix>`**
  the inverse of m; None when the determinant is 0 or not finite  [xf_invert]

- **`fn translate(m: &mut Matrix, tx: f64, ty: f64)`**
  m = m \* T(tx, ty): c += a tx + b ty, f += d tx + e ty  [xf_translate]

- **`fn scale(m: &mut Matrix, sx: f64, sy: f64)`**
  m = m \* S(sx, sy)  [xf_scale]

- **`fn rotate(m: &mut Matrix, angle: f64, cx: f64, cy: f64)`**
  m = m \* T(cx, cy) \* R(angle) \* T(-cx, -cy)  [xf_rotate]

- **`struct Affine<'a>`**
  a prepared affine resampling of src into a destination of the measured size (the XFORM
  record); read-only while rows() runs

**`impl<'a> Affine<'a>`**

- **`fn prepare(src: &'a Surface, dst: &Surface, m: &Matrix, filter: u32, flags: u32, clip: Option<[i32; 4]>) -> Result<Affine<'a>, i32>`**
  validate: ERR_UNSUPPORTED (filter, formats), ERR_DIMS (sizes outside 1 .. 65535)
  [rs_affine_prepare]
- **`fn prepare_projective(src: &'a Surface, dst: &Surface, m: &[f64; 9], filter: u32, flags: u32, clip: Option<[i32; 4]>) -> Result<Affine<'a>, i32>`**
  Prepare an inverse 3x3 homography, in row-major order. Uses the same alpha-aware
  filters and edge modes as affine resampling; no intermediate image is allocated.
- **`fn rows(&self, band: &mut Band, y0: i32, y1: i32) -> i32`**
  dst rows y0 .. y1-1 inside the clip into band, which must hold them  [rs_affine_rows]

- **`fn affine(src: &Surface, dst: &mut Surface, m: &Matrix, filter: u32, flags: u32, clip: Option<[i32; 4]>) -> i32`**
  dst = src resampled through m inside clip (None = all of dst)  [rs_affine]

- **`static CUBIC_LUT: [[i16; 4]; 256]`**
  Catmull-Rom taps -1, 0, 1, 2 for phase p / 256 (i16, sum 16384)  [xf_cubic_lut]

### `img_core/yuv.rs`

Source: [`lib/img_core/yuv.rs`](../lib/img_core/yuv.rs)

```text
yuv -- YUV -> RGB conversion for HEIC / AVIF (u16 sample planes)       [src/img/yuv.s]

  coef(yp) -> YuvCoef / ERR_UNSUPPORTED      the fixed-point gains (yuv_coef)
  to_argb(yp, sf, dx, dy) -> 0 / ERR_*       YUV image into sf at (dx, dy) (clipped), opaque
  alpha(yp, sf, dx, dy) -> 0                 replace the alpha bytes with yp's Y plane
  prem_clamp(sf)                             limit R, G, B of every pixel to its alpha
  argb_to_yuv() -> ERR_UNSUPPORTED           the inverse (not implemented, as in the assembly)

Conversion (tools/heic_check.py has the same model): the range maps Y to Y' = (Y - yoff) *
255 / ymax and chroma to C' = (C - coff) * 255 / cmax (full range: yoff 0, ymax = cmax =
2^bits - 1, coff 2^(bits - 1); limited: yoff 16 << (bits - 8), ymax 219 << (bits - 8), coff
128 << (bits - 8), cmax 224 << (bits - 8); identity uses the luma range for all three).
YCbCr: R = Y' + (2 - 2Kr) Cr', G = Y' - 2Kb(1 - Kb) / Kg Cb' - 2Kr(1 - Kr) / Kg Cr', B = Y'
+ (2 - 2Kb) Cb'; YCgCo: R = Y' - Cg' + Co', G = Y' + Cg', B = Y' - Cg' - Co'; identity:
G = Y', B = U', R = V'.  The gains are computed in double precision (fixed order of
operations) and rounded once: Ky = floor(255 / ymax * 2^32 + 0.5), Kc = +-floor(255 / cmax
* coef * 2^28 + 0.5).

Chroma upsampling (linear, weights summing to 4 per direction, so upsampled chroma carries
a factor 16): horizontally co-sited (location types 0 / 2 / 4: even x = 4 c[x/2], odd x =
2 c[(x-1)/2] + 2 c[(x+1)/2]) or centred (1 / 3 / 5: 3 c[k] + c[k -+ 1], k = x >> 1);
vertically centred (types 0 / 1 / 4 / 5) or co-sited (2 / 3), the same way; neighbours
clamp at the plane edge.  YPF_NEAREST: c[x / subw][y / subh].

The assembly converts bands of 32 rows on the worker pool; img_core has no thread pool
(no plat dependency), so the bands run one after the other here -- every band depends only
on its rows, the bytes are the same.
```

```rust
YPF_NEAREST: u32 = 1  // nearest-neighbour chroma upsampling (default: linear)
```

- **`struct YuvPlanes<'a>`**
  a YUV image (YP_\*): u16 samples (also for 8-bit content), strides in samples; the chroma
  planes are ceil(w / subw) x ceil(h / subh) samples
  - `pl: [&'a [u16]; 3]` -- Y, U (Cb / Cg / B), V (Cr / Co / R); U empty: monochrome
  - `st: [u32; 3]`
  - `w: u32`
  - `h: u32`
  - `subw: u32` -- horizontal chroma subsampling 1 / 2
  - `subh: u32` -- vertical chroma subsampling 1 / 2
  - `bits: u32` -- luma bit depth 8..16
  - `bitsc: u32` -- chroma bit depth 8..16
  - `cicp: u32` -- primaries | transfer &lt;&lt; 8 | matrix &lt;&lt; 16 | full range &lt;&lt; 24
  - `flags: u32` -- YPF_\*
  - `chloc: u32` -- chroma sample location type 0..5 (HEVC VUI / H.273; 0 default)

- **`struct YuvCoef`**
  the gains of coef (YK): out = clamp((y' \* Ky + u' \* Ku + v' \* Kv + 2^31) >> 32) with
  y' = Y - yoff (Ky Q32) and u' / v' = 16 \* upsampled chroma - coff (Q28)
  - `r: [i64; 3]` -- Ky, Ku, Kv of red
  - `g: [i64; 3]`
  - `b: [i64; 3]`
  - `yoff: i64` -- luma offset
  - `coff: i64` -- 16 \* chroma offset
  - `ky: i64` -- Q32 luma gain (monochrome: R = G = B)

- **`fn coef(yp: &YuvPlanes) -> Result<YuvCoef, i32>`**
  yuv_coef: the fixed-point gains of yp's matrix, range and bit depths

- **`fn to_argb(yp: &YuvPlanes, sf: &mut Surface, dx: u32, dy: u32) -> i32`**
  yuv_to_argb: convert the YUV image into sf at (dx, dy) (clipped to sf) as opaque BGRA
  (alpha 255; alpha replaces it) -> 0 / ERR_UNSUPPORTED

- **`fn alpha(yp: &YuvPlanes, sf: &mut Surface, dx: u32, dy: u32) -> i32`**
  yuv_alpha: replace the alpha bytes of sf at (dx, dy) (clipped) with yp's Y plane (bit
  depth bits, always full range: a = floor(Y \* 255 / (2^bits - 1) + 1/2) in Q32) -> 0

- **`fn prem_clamp(sf: &mut Surface)`**
  yuv_prem_clamp: limit R, G, B of every pixel to its alpha (premultiplied sources: the
  result is valid FMT_PARGB)

- **`fn argb_to_yuv() -> i32`**
  argb_to_yuv: the inverse, for future encoders (not implemented yet)

## img_all

*registration of every codec* -- crate root [`lib/img_all/lib.rs`](../lib/img_all/lib.rs)

```text
img_all -- registration of the codec crates in the format table  [src/img/imgreg.s, imgio.s]

  use_core_codecs()   PNG, JPEG, BMP, GIF (the assembly registers them statically: call it
                      once at start-up, before worker threads decode)
  use_all_codecs()    every codec of the library, the core formats included
                      (img_use_all_codecs)

Each codec crate exports `pub static CODEC: img_core::Codec`; the codec agents add one
registration line for their crate below.
```

### `img_all` crate root

- **`fn use_core_codecs()`**
  register PNG, JPEG, BMP and GIF

- **`fn use_all_codecs()`**
  register every codec of the library (the core formats included)  [img_use_all_codecs]

## img_png

*PNG* -- crate root [`lib/img_png/lib.rs`](../lib/img_png/lib.rs)

```text
img_png -- the PNG codec                                  [src/img/png.s, imgcore.s PNG parts]

  CODEC                          the codec descriptor (probe / decode_ex / encode_opt; scan,
                                 decode_fl, save for the classic API and BMP's embedded PNG)
  dec::decode_fl(data, sf, jdf)  png_decode_fl (dec.rs: decoder)
  enc::encode_cfg(sf, cfg)       png_encode_cfg (enc.rs: encoder, PngCfg, put_chunk)
  glue::scan / probe / decode_ex / encode_opt   imgcore.s (glue.rs)
  encode(px, w, h, stride, grey) the screenshot writer of ui (--shot): rows of 32-bit
                                 B, G, R, x (RGB) or 8-bit grey -> png_encode's file
  decode_pargb(data)             png_decode: a FMT_PARGB surface or None (the hook of
                                 text::Text::png_decode, CBDT colour glyphs)
```

### `img_png` API

- **`static CODEC: Codec`**
  the PNG codec (png_codec)

- **`fn decode_pargb(data: &[u8]) -> Option<Surface>`**
  png_decode: the file as a new FMT_PARGB surface, None on any error

- **`fn encode(px: &[u8], w: usize, h: usize, stride: usize, grey: bool) -> Vec<u8>`**
  the PNG file of w x h pixels: 32-bit B, G, R, x rows (RGB) or, with `grey`, 8-bit rows;
  `stride` = bytes per row (png_encode of an XRGB / A8 surface)

From [`lib/img_png/dec.rs`](../lib/img_png/dec.rs) (`img_png::dec`):

- **`fn decode_fl(data: &[u8], out: &mut Surface, flags: u32) -> i32`**
  decode a PNG file into a new surface: FMT_PARGB, or FMT_ARGB with JDF_STRAIGHT; on an
  error out is left as it is  [png_decode_fl]

From [`lib/img_png/enc.rs`](../lib/img_png/enc.rs) (`img_png::enc`):

```rust
/// PngCfg::depth: png_encode's rule (A8 grey, XRGB RGB, else RGB when opaque / RGBA)
PNGD_CLASSIC: u32 = 0xFFFF_FFFF
```

- **`struct PngCfg<'a>`**
  the options of encode_cfg (PNGCFG)
  - `level: u32` -- deflate level 0..9
  - `depth: u32` -- 0 auto / 32 / 24 / 8 / PNGD_CLASSIC
  - `qcfg: u32` -- quantize_ex cfg for 8 bit (dither level | threshold &lt;&lt; 8)
  - `chunks: &'a [u8]` -- complete chunks written after IHDR
  - `cancel: Option<&'a AtomicU32>`

- **`fn put_chunk(out: &mut Vec<u8>, typ: &[u8; 4], data: &[u8])`**
  append a chunk: length, type (4 bytes), data, CRC-32 of type + data  [png_put_chunk]

- **`fn encode_cfg(sf: &Surface, cfg: &PngCfg) -> Result<Vec<u8>, i32>`**
  encode sf (FMT_XRGB, PARGB, ARGB or A8) with a PngCfg  [png_encode_cfg]

| Module | Content |
|---|---|
| [`img_png::dec`](../lib/img_png/dec.rs) | the PNG decoder |
| [`img_png::enc`](../lib/img_png/enc.rs) | the PNG encoder |
| [`img_png::glue`](../lib/img_png/glue.rs) | PNG behind the codec interface: metadata scanner, probe, decode_ex, encode_opt |

## img_jpeg

*JPEG* -- crate root [`lib/img_jpeg/lib.rs`](../lib/img_jpeg/lib.rs)

```text
img_jpeg -- JPEG decoder and encoder  [src/img/jpeg_dec.s, jpeg_enc.s, jpeg_dsp.s,
                                       imgcore.s (JPEG parts)]

  decode(data) -> Surface / ERR_*                        jpeg_decode (FMT_PARGB, opaque)
  decode_fl(data, flags) -> Surface / ERR_*              jpeg_decode_fl, JDF_* flags
  decode_abbrev(tables, data, flags) -> Surface / ERR_*  jpeg_decode_abbrev (JPEG-in-TIFF)
  encode(sf, quality) -> file / None                     jpeg_encode
  encode_ex(sf, quality, cfg) -> file / None             jpeg_encode_ex (EncCfg = JENCCFG)
  meta(data, m, flags) -> 0 / ERR_*                      jpeg_meta (also used for BMP's
                                                         embedded JPEG)
  probe / decode_ex / encode_opt                         the codec interface (CODEC)

Modules: dec (jpeg_dec.s), enc (jpeg_enc.s), dsp (jpeg_dsp.s: IDCT / FDCT / colour
conversion / resampling kernels and the tables), glue (imgcore.s: metadata scan, IMGDEC /
IMGOPT glue).
```

### `img_jpeg` API

Re-exports: `img_core::{JDF_CMYK, JDF_NO_ORIENT, JDF_RGB, JDF_STRAIGHT, JDF_YCC, JPEG_IOM}`

- **`fn decode(data: &[u8]) -> Result<Surface, i32>`**
  jpeg_decode: decode a JPEG file into a new FMT_PARGB surface

- **`static CODEC: Codec`**
  the codec descriptor (jpeg_codec); scan / decode_fl / save serve img_core::io's classic
  paths (img_decode, img_save) and the BMP / TIFF embeddings

From [`lib/img_jpeg/dec.rs`](../lib/img_jpeg/dec.rs) (`img_jpeg::dec`):
