- **`fn decode_abbrev(tables: &[u8], data: &[u8], flags: u32) -> Result<Surface, i32>`**
  jpeg_decode_abbrev: an abbreviated image stream (JPEG-in-TIFF strip / tile) with the
  tables of a tables-only stream (TIFF JPEGTables; empty = none); the orientation is never
  applied

From [`lib/img_jpeg/enc.rs`](../lib/img_jpeg/enc.rs) (`img_jpeg::enc`):

- **`struct EncCfg`**
  JENCCFG: chroma subsampling 0 (4:4:4 from quality 90, else 4:2:0) / 444 / 422 / 420 / 400;
  JFIF density unit 0 = keep 96 x 96 dpi, 1 dpi, 2 dots per cm; x / y density 1..65535
  - `chroma: u32`
  - `unit: u32`
  - `xd: u32`
  - `yd: u32`

  jpeg_encode

- **`fn encode_ex(sf: &Surface, quality: i32, cfg: Option<&EncCfg>) -> Option<Vec<u8>>`**
  jpeg_encode_ex: jpeg_encode with an EncCfg (None = defaults)

From [`lib/img_jpeg/glue.rs`](../lib/img_jpeg/glue.rs) (`img_jpeg::glue`):

- **`fn meta(data: &[u8], m: &mut ImgMeta, flags: u32) -> i32`**

- **`fn probe(data: &[u8], m: &mut ImgMeta) -> bool`**
  jpeg_probe: header + metadata scan, no pixels

- **`fn decode_ex(data: &[u8], out: &mut Surface, d: &ImgDec, m: &mut ImgMeta) -> i32`**
  jpeg_decode_ex: JPEG through decode_fl, which applies the EXIF orientation itself unless
  IMGD_NO_ORIENT: IMF_ORIENT_APPLIED is set when it did

- **`fn encode_opt(sf: &Surface, opt: &ImgOpt, out: &mut Vec<u8>) -> i32`**
  jpeg_encode_opt: encode_ex with the IMGOPT options and metadata segments (see the file
  header)

*BMP, ICO, CUR* -- crate root [`lib/img_bmp/lib.rs`](../lib/img_bmp/lib.rs)

```text
img_bmp -- Windows bitmap (BMP) decoder and encoder, and the BMP parts of the core-format
glue                                             [src/img/bmp.s, src/img/imgcore.s]

  decode(data, sf) -> 0 / ERR_*                  bmp_decode: FMT_PARGB
  decode_fl(data, sf, flags) -> 0 / ERR_*        bmp_decode_fl: JDF_STRAIGHT -> FMT_ARGB
  encode(sf) -> Some(file) / None                bmp_encode (the classic rule)
  encode_cfg(sf, cfg, out) -> 0 / ERR_*          bmp_encode_cfg (BmpCfg = BMPCFG)
  meta(data, meta, flags) -> 0 / ERR_*           bmp_meta (imgcore.s)
  probe(data, meta) -> recognised                bmp_probe
  decode_ex(data, sf, dec, meta) -> 0 / ERR_*    bmp_decode_ex
  encode_opt(sf, opt, out) -> 0 / ERR_*          bmp_encode_opt
  CODEC                                          bmp_codec

Decoder: BITMAPCOREHEADER (12) and BITMAPINFOHEADER..V5 (40..124) headers; 1/4/8-bit palette,
16-bit (default 5-5-5 or bit fields), 24-bit, 32-bit (BI_RGB = opaque, BI_BITFIELDS /
BI_ALPHABITFIELDS with any masks and an optional alpha mask); bottom-up and top-down rows.
Channels narrower than 8 bits are scaled with rounding (v * 255 / max), wider ones keep their
top 8 bits.  Palette indices past the palette give opaque black.
BI_RLE8 / BI_RLE4: encoded runs, absolute runs (word aligned), end of line, end of bitmap,
delta; pixels the stream skips (delta, early end of line / bitmap, data that ends early) stay
transparent; runs past the right edge are clipped, everything is bounds-checked.  BI_JPEG /
BI_PNG: the embedded file (bfOffBits, biSizeImage bytes or up to the end of the data) is
decoded by img_jpeg / img_png (the flags are passed on).  OS/2 2.x (64-byte header) Huffman
1D and RLE24, CMYK and other compressions: ERR_UNSUPPORTED.  Result: FMT_PARGB surface, or
FMT_ARGB with JDF_STRAIGHT (the file's straight values: colour kept at alpha 0).

Encoder (encode): 24-bit BI_RGB when the surface is opaque (XRGB, all-opaque PARGB / ARGB,
A8 as grey), else 32-bit BITMAPV4HEADER with BI_BITFIELDS and an alpha mask (straight alpha).
Rows bottom-up, 72 dpi.  encode_cfg with a BmpCfg: depth 0 auto (not opaque -> 32; opaque
with <= 256 colours -> exact 8-bit palette; else 24), 32, 24 (colour composited over white),
8 (quantize_ex with qcfg's dither level, over white: BMP palettes have no alpha),
BMPD_CLASSIC (encode's rule); ppmx / ppmy pixels per metre (0 = 2835, 72 dpi).

Metadata (bmp_meta): header (size, depth, compression, alpha mask), pels per metre -> dpi,
V4/V5 colour space (sRGB flag, embedded profile for IMGD_ICC); BI_JPEG / BI_PNG: the embedded
file's own header (size, depth, model, channels, alpha / lossless / interlace / HDR flags).
```

### `img_bmp` API

```rust
BMPD_CLASSIC: u32 = 0xFFFF_FFFF  // BmpCfg::depth: encode's rule
```

- **`static CODEC: Codec`**
  bmp_codec

- **`struct BmpCfg<'a>`**
  encoder options (BMPCFG)
  - `depth: u32` -- 0 auto / 32 / 24 / 8 / BMPD_CLASSIC
  - `qcfg: u32` -- quantize_ex cfg for 8 bit (IQF_WHITE is added)
  - `ppmx: u32` -- pixels per metre (0 = 2835, 72 dpi)
  - `ppmy: u32`
  - `cancel: Option<&'a AtomicU32>`

- **`fn decode(data: &[u8], sf: &mut Surface) -> i32`**
  bmp_decode: a BMP file as a new FMT_PARGB surface (sf is replaced; untouched on error)

- **`fn decode_fl(d: &[u8], sf: &mut Surface, flags: u32) -> i32`**
  bmp_decode_fl: bmp_decode with JDF_\* flags (JDF_STRAIGHT: FMT_ARGB output; passed on to an
  embedded JPEG / PNG)

- **`fn encode(sf: &Surface) -> Option<Vec<u8>>`**
  bmp_encode: BMP file, 24-bit when opaque, else 32-bit BITMAPV4HEADER with alpha (straight)

- **`fn encode_cfg(sf: &Surface, cfg: &BmpCfg, out: &mut Vec<u8>) -> i32`**
  bmp_encode_cfg: encode sf (FMT_XRGB, FMT_PARGB, FMT_ARGB or FMT_A8) with a BmpCfg (see the
  module header); out is empty on error.  Errors: ERR_DIMS, ERR_UNSUPPORTED (surface format),
  ERR_NOMEM (8-bit quantization failed), ERR_CANCEL

- **`fn meta(d: &[u8], m: &mut ImgMeta, flags: u32) -> i32`**
  bmp_meta: file and info header -> 0 / ERR_TRUNC / ERR_HDR (see the module header)

- **`fn probe(data: &[u8], m: &mut ImgMeta) -> bool`**
  bmp_probe: header + metadata scan, no pixels

- **`fn decode_ex(data: &[u8], sf: &mut Surface, dec: &ImgDec, m: &mut ImgMeta) -> i32`**
  bmp_decode_ex: BMP through decode_fl (core_decode: the metadata scan, IMGD_PROBE, frame 0
  only, the size check, the cancel flag)

- **`fn encode_opt(sf: &Surface, opt: &ImgOpt, out: &mut Vec<u8>) -> i32`**
  bmp_encode_opt: encode_cfg with ImgOpt::bitdepth (0 / 32 / 24 / 8), ImgOpt::dither (8 bit)
  and the pixels per metre of ImgOpt::meta's dpi (none: 72 dpi)

## img_gif

*GIF* -- crate root [`lib/img_gif/lib.rs`](../lib/img_gif/lib.rs)

```text
img_gif -- GIF decoder and encoder, and the GIF parts of the core-format glue
                                                 [src/img/gif.s, src/img/imgcore.s]

  decode(data, sf) -> 0 / ERR_*                  gif_decode: FMT_PARGB
  decode_fl(data, sf, flags) -> 0 / ERR_*        gif_decode_fl: JDF_STRAIGHT -> FMT_ARGB
  encode(sf) -> Some(file) / None                gif_encode
  encode_q(sf, qcfg) -> Some(file) / None        gif_encode_q (quantize_ex cfg)
  meta(data, meta, flags) -> 0 / ERR_*           gif_meta (imgcore.s)
  probe(data, meta) -> recognised                gif_probe
  decode_ex(data, sf, dec, meta) -> 0 / ERR_*    gif_decode_ex
  encode_opt(sf, opt, out) -> 0 / ERR_*          gif_encode_opt
  CODEC                                          gif_codec

Decoder: GIF87a / GIF89a, global and local colour tables, LZW minimum code sizes 1..8,
clear codes anywhere (early clear, deferred clear = codes after the table filled up without
a clear, full 4096-entry tables), interlace, the Graphic Control Extension transparency
index.  Only the first image is decoded (animations show their first frame); the metadata
scanner counts the frames.  The image is placed at its offset on a transparent canvas of the
logical screen size, grown to the frame extent (left + width, top + height) where the screen
is smaller; a 0-sized frame takes the screen size.  Application / comment / plain-text and
unknown extensions are skipped (a GCE counts for the image only when it is the last one
before it).
Colours: index -> opaque palette colour; index past the colour table -> opaque black; no
colour table at all -> grey ramp (index i -> i,i,i); the GCE transparent index -> 0 (also in
FMT_ARGB output: GIF has only binary transparency, so both formats hold the same bytes).
Code size rule (giflib / Wuffs): after every code other than a clear code the width grows by
one bit when next_code >= 2^width (max 12 bits).
Errors: ERR_TRUNC (data ends before the image descriptor is complete), ERR_HDR (no GIF87a /
GIF89a signature), ERR_CHUNK (trailer or an unknown block before the first image), ERR_DIMS
(canvas w or h > 32768, w * h > 2^28, or a 0-sized frame on a 0-sized screen),
ERR_UNSUPPORTED (LZW minimum code size outside 1..8).  Once the image descriptor has been
read the result is always 0: a truncated local colour table, missing, truncated or corrupt
LZW data end the image and the pixels not decoded stay transparent.

Encoder: GIF89a, one image.  img_core::quantize builds the palette: exact when the image has
at most 256 colours (transparent counts as one), else Wu quantization to 256 colours (255 +
transparent) with serpentine Floyd-Steinberg dithering (encode: level 8, encode_q: the cfg's
level 0..8).  Alpha < threshold (encode: 128) becomes transparent (GCE written only when such
pixels exist).  Input FMT_PARGB / FMT_ARGB / FMT_XRGB / FMT_A8.  Global colour table of the
next power of two >= the colour count (min 2), LZW minimum code size max(2, table bits),
variable code width up to 12 bits, a clear code whenever the 4096-entry table is full.

Metadata (gif_meta): logical screen, colour table depth, frame count (walks all blocks),
first frame delay, NETSCAPE loop count, transparency, interlace of the first frame, an ICC
profile from an "ICCRGBG1" "012" application extension (IMGD_ICC).
```

### `img_gif` API

- **`static CODEC: Codec`**
  gif_codec

- **`fn decode(data: &[u8], sf: &mut Surface) -> i32`**
  gif_decode: the first image as a new FMT_PARGB surface (sf is replaced; untouched on error)

- **`fn decode_fl(data: &[u8], sf: &mut Surface, flags: u32) -> i32`**
  gif_decode_fl: gif_decode; flags JDF_STRAIGHT: FMT_ARGB surface (same bytes)

- **`fn encode(sf: &Surface) -> Option<Vec<u8>>`**
  gif_encode: GIF89a file (dither level 8, threshold 128) or None (empty or larger than
  65535 x 65535, unknown format)

- **`fn encode_q(sf: &Surface, qcfg: u32) -> Option<Vec<u8>>`**
  gif_encode_q: gif_encode with a quantize_ex cfg (dither level | threshold &lt;&lt;
  IQ_THRESH_SHIFT; IQF_\* flags are ignored)

- **`fn meta(d: &[u8], m: &mut ImgMeta, flags: u32) -> i32`**
  gif_meta: logical screen and a walk over all blocks -> 0 / ERR_TRUNC / ERR_HDR

- **`fn probe(data: &[u8], m: &mut ImgMeta) -> bool`**
  gif_probe: header + metadata scan, no pixels

- **`fn decode_ex(data: &[u8], sf: &mut Surface, dec: &ImgDec, m: &mut ImgMeta) -> i32`**
  gif_decode_ex: the first frame through decode_fl (core_decode); meta.w / h become the canvas
  size decode_fl produced

- **`fn encode_opt(sf: &Surface, opt: &ImgOpt, out: &mut Vec<u8>) -> i32`**
  gif_encode_opt: encode_q with the dither level ImgOpt::dither and the threshold
  ImgOpt::athresh -> 0 / ERR_NOMEM (also for a surface encode_q refuses) / ERR_CANCEL

## img_tga

*TGA* -- crate root [`lib/img_tga/lib.rs`](../lib/img_tga/lib.rs)

```text
img_tga -- Truevision TGA reader / writer                            [src/img/tga.s]

  probe(data, meta) -> recognised                tga_probe
  decode(data, sf, dec, meta) -> 0 / ERR_*       tga_decode
  encode(sf, opt, out) -> 0 / ERR_*              tga_encode
  CODEC                                          tga_codec

TGA has no magic number, so the format detection only reports TGA when the caller's hint
(file extension) says so or probe accepts the header.  probe is deliberately strict: colour
map type 0 / 1, image type 1 / 2 / 3 / 9 / 10 / 11, a colour map exactly when the type needs
one (entry size 15 / 16 / 24 / 32, length > 0; without one all colour map fields 0), width and
height > 0, a pixel depth that fits the type (colour mapped 8; true colour 15 / 16 / 24 / 32;
grey 8 / 16), descriptor bits 6..7 zero, alpha bits 0 / 1 (16 bit), 0 / 8 (32 bit, 16-bit
grey), 0 otherwise, at least one byte of pixel data after the ID and the colour map, and for
uncompressed types all pixels present.

Decoder (more lenient than the probe): image types 1 / 2 / 3 and RLE 9 / 10 / 11 (types 32 /
33 and 0: ERR_UNSUPPORTED); colour mapped with 8- or 16-bit indices and 15 / 16 / 24 / 32-bit
entries starting at the first-entry index (indices outside the map: opaque black); true colour
15 / 16 / 24 / 32 bit; grey 8 bit and 16 bit (grey + alpha byte); a colour map of a
true-colour image is skipped; the ID field is skipped; all four origins (descriptor bit 4
right-to-left, bit 5 top-to-bottom); RLE packets may cross rows; interleaved images
(descriptor bits 6..7) ERR_UNSUPPORTED.  5-bit channels expand by bit replication.
Alpha: with a v2 footer ("TRUEVISION-XFILE.") and an extension area (size >= 495) the
attributes type decides: 3 = straight alpha, 4 = premultiplied (FMT_PARGB result, colours
clamped to alpha, IMF_SRC_PREMUL), anything else = opaque.  Without one the storage decides:
32-bit pixels / entries and 16-bit grey carry alpha, 16-bit pixels / entries when the
descriptor has alpha bits; then an alpha channel that is zero everywhere counts as opaque.
Missing or truncated pixel data: ERR_TRUNC (the minimum size -- RLE: one packet per 128
pixels -- is checked before the surface is allocated).  Result: FMT_ARGB (straight).

Encoder: 32-bit BGRA with straight alpha (descriptor alpha bits 8) or 24-bit BGR (bitdepth
24; transparent pixels are composited over white), bitdepth 0 / 32 = 32 bit, others
ERR_UNSUPPORTED; IOF_RLE -> type 10 with packets that never cross a row (runs of >= 2 equal
pixels become run packets); rows bottom-up (origin lower left); a v2 extension area
(attributes type 3 for 32 bit, 0 for 24 bit) and footer.  Width / height above 65535:
ERR_DIMS.  Codec iom: IOM_RLE | IOM_BITDEPTH.
```

### `img_tga` API

- **`static CODEC: Codec`**
  tga_codec

- **`fn probe(d: &[u8], m: &mut ImgMeta) -> bool`**
  tga_probe: the strict header check described in the module header; meta: size, depth,
  model, alpha (w = h = 0 when not recognised)

- **`fn decode(d: &[u8], sf: &mut Surface, dec: &ImgDec, m: &mut ImgMeta) -> i32`**
  tga_decode: decode (see the module header); meta is filled from the header; sf holds the
  (partial) surface also when a later error is returned

- **`fn encode(sf: &Surface, opt: &ImgOpt, out: &mut Vec<u8>) -> i32`**
  tga_encode: TGA file (see the module header) -> 0 / ERR_DIMS / ERR_UNSUPPORTED / ERR_CANCEL;
  out is empty on error

```text
                               [src/img/webp.s, webp_vp8.s, webp_vp8l.s, webp_vp8_enc.s, webp_vp8l_enc.s]

  CODEC                         the IMGCODEC descriptor (probe, decode, encode, caps, iom)
```

### `img_webp` API

*WebP encoder options (img.inc CE)*

```rust
IO_WEBPPRESET: usize = 36  // ImgOpt field (the IO_DDSFMT slot; ImgOpt::ddsfmt)
IOM_PRESET: u32 = 1 << 19  // the preset is honoured
WPP_DEFAULT: u32 = 0  // paint.net "Standard" (libwebp WEBP_PRESET_DEFAULT)
WPP_PICTURE: u32 = 1
WPP_PHOTO: u32 = 2
WPP_DRAWING: u32 = 3
WPP_ICON: u32 = 4
WPP_TEXT: u32 = 5
IOF_WEBP_NOEXACT: u32 = 0x20000  // RGB under alpha 0 may change (default: kept exactly)
IOF_WEBP_SHARPYUV: u32 = 0x40000  // lossy: sharp RGB -> YUV 4:2:0
IOF_WEBP_1THREAD: u32 = 0x80000  // no worker threads (the output is the same either way)
// WEBPENC flags (Cfg::flags)
WEF_NOEXACT: u32 = 1
WEF_NOHDR: u32 = 2  // VP8L: header-less stream (ALPH chunk)
WEF_1THREAD: u32 = 4
WEF_SHARPYUV: u32 = 8
// test-only flags (codectest ops 126..128): force a configuration / predictor mode, switch
// off stages
WEF_T_CFG_SHIFT: u32 = 8  // 4 bits: VP8L configuration + 1 (0 = automatic)
WEF_T_NOCROSS: u32 = 0x1000
WEF_T_DUMPM: u32 = 0x2000  // return the main image's match array (u32 dist | (len - 1) << 20)
WEF_T_MODE_SHIFT: u32 = 16  // 4 bits: predictor mode + 1 for every tile
WEF_T_NOCACHE: u32 = 0x10_0000
WEF_T_NOMETA: u32 = 0x20_0000
WEF_T_NODP: u32 = 0x40_0000
WEF_T_NOGREEDY: u32 = 0x80_0000
WEF_T_NORLE: u32 = 0x100_0000
WEF_T_KEEPDP: u32 = 0x200_0000
WEF_T_CACHE_SHIFT: u32 = 26  // 4 bits: colour cache bits + 1 (0 = estimate)
WEF_T_REPORT: u32 = 0x4000_0000  // return 0x1000000 + (cache bits | groups << 8), no stream
```

- **`struct Cfg<'a>`**
  the WEBPENC record (WE_\*): effort 1..9 (0 = 5), lossy quality 0..100 (0 = 95), WEF_\*
  flags, WPP_\* preset, cancel flag
  - `effort: u32`
  - `quality: u32`
  - `flags: u32`
  - `preset: u32`
  - `cancel: Option<&'a AtomicU32>`

**`impl Cfg<'_>`**

- **`fn cancelled(&self) -> bool`**

- **`static CODEC: Codec`**
  the codec descriptor  [webp_codec]

```text
img_hevc -- HEVC (H.265) intra decoder and encoder for HEIC still images
                                 [src/img/hevc.s, hevc_ps.s, hevc_ctu.s, hevc_rec.s, hevc_lf.s,
```

### `img_hevc` API

From [`lib/img_hevc/dec.rs`](../lib/img_hevc/dec.rs):

*flags (hevc_new)*

```rust
HVF_NODEBLOCK: u32 = 1  // skip the deblocking filter (test hook)
HVF_NOSAO: u32 = 2  // skip SAO (test hook)
```

- **`struct Pic<'p>`**
  the decoded picture (HEVCPIC): planes owned by the decoder
  - `w: u32` -- output size (conformance window applied)
  - `h: u32`
  - `x0: u32` -- luma offset of the window in the planes
  - `y0: u32`
  - `bd: u32` -- BitDepthY / C
  - `bdc: u32`
  - `chroma: u32` -- chroma_format_idc
  - `subw: u32`
  - `subh: u32`
  - `vuicol: u32` -- colour description present (VUI)
  - `prim: u32`
  - `trc: u32`
  - `mc: u32`
  - `full: u32` -- video_full_range_flag
  - `planes: [&'p [u16]; 3]`
  - `pw: [u32; 3]` -- plane width (= stride in samples) / height
  - `ph: [u32; 3]`

- **`struct Hevc<'a>`**
  the decoder record (HV)

**`impl<'a> Hevc<'a>`**

- **`fn new(flags: u32, maxctu: u32) -> Hevc<'a>`**
  hevc_new: a fresh (zeroed) record
- **`fn set_cancel(&mut self, flag: Option<&'a AtomicU32>)`**
  hevc_set_cancel: a flag polled per CTU (None = none)
- **`fn newpic(&mut self) -> i32`**
  hevc_newpic: allocate the planes and maps of a picture (SPS / PPS active; the
  encoder uses the record for prediction / reconstruction)
- **`fn nal(&mut self, nal: &[u8]) -> i32`**
  hevc_nal: one NAL unit (see the file header)
- **`fn annexb(&mut self, data: &[u8]) -> i32`**
  hevc_annexb: split at 00 00 01 start codes (trailing zero bytes of a NAL dropped) and
  feed every NAL unit to nal
- **`fn finish(&mut self) -> Result<Pic<'_>, i32>`**
  hevc_finish: deblocking, SAO and the picture (see the file header)

From [`lib/img_hevc/enc.rs`](../lib/img_hevc/enc.rs) (`img_hevc::enc`):

```rust
HEF_LOSSLESS: u32 = 1  // cu_transquant_bypass for every CU (QP ignored)
HEF_NODEBLOCK: u32 = 2  // pps_deblocking_filter_disabled (tests)
```

- **`struct EncCfg<'a>`**
  the input of encode (HECFG)
  - `pl: [&'a [u16]; 3]` -- source planes (u16 samples, 8-bit values)
  - `st: [u32; 3]` -- strides in samples
  - `w: u32` -- picture width (even for 4:2:0)
  - `h: u32` -- picture height (even for 4:2:0)
  - `chroma: u32` -- chroma_format_idc 0 / 1 / 3
  - `qp: u32` -- QP 0..51
  - `flags: u32` -- HEF_\*
  - `vui: u32` -- primaries | transfer &lt;&lt; 8 | matrix &lt;&lt; 16 | full range &lt;&lt; 24
  - `cancel: Option<&'a AtomicU32>` -- polled per CTU

- **`struct EncOut`**
  the result of encode (HEOUT)
  - `nal: [Vec<u8>; 4]` -- VPS, SPS, PPS, slice: NAL units (header, emulation prevention)
  - `ptl: [u8; 12]` -- general profile / tier / level bytes (hvcC)

- **`fn encode(cfg: &EncCfg) -> Result<EncOut, i32>`**
  hevc_encode: encode the picture of cfg (see the file header)

The codec's building blocks (public for the codec test app `codectest`; see their source headers):

| Module | Content |
|---|---|
| [`img_hevc::enc`](../lib/img_hevc/enc.rs) | HEVC intra encoder for HEIC (model: tools/hevc_enc_ref.py, byte-exact) |

## img_av1

*AV1 intra coding* -- crate root [`lib/img_av1/lib.rs`](../lib/img_av1/lib.rs)

```text
img_av1 -- AV1 intra decoder for AVIF still images (AV1 bitstream specification; model:
tools/av1_ref.py, bit-exact)                                  [src/img/av1*.s]

  Av1Dec::new() -> decoder                  av1_new (av1_free = drop)
  dec.set_cancel(flag)                      av1_set_cancel: polled per superblock
  dec.set_stage(stage)                      av1_set_stage: test hook, AVS_* (output after that step)
  dec.set_nograin(flag)                     av1_set_nograin: 1 = do not apply film grain
  dec.obus(data) -> Ok / Err(ERR_*)         av1_obus: OBUs in the low-overhead format: sequence
                                            header, frame header / frame / tile groups of the
                                            first shown frame (decoded when its last tile
                                            arrives); temporal delimiters, metadata, padding and
                                            everything after the first frame are ignored
  dec.finish() -> Pic / Err(ERR_*)          av1_finish: the decoded frame (film grain applied)
  dec.qindex(seg, curq) -> qindex           av1_qindex: get_qindex( curq < 0, seg )

Supported: profiles 0..2 (8 / 10 / 12 bits, 4:0:0 / 4:2:0 / 4:2:2 / 4:4:4), key frames and
intra-only frames with every intra tool, operating point 0.  ERR_UNSUPPORTED: inter / switch
frames, show_existing_frame, frames that are not shown, intra block copy, large scale tile.

Modules: dec (av1.s: OBUs, headers, frame buffers, tile groups, the post filter chain), sym
(av1_sym.s), blk (av1_blk.s: tile / block syntax), coef (av1_coef.s), ipred (av1_ipred.s), itx
(av1_itx.s), txfn (av1_txfn.s, generated), lf (av1_lf.s), cdef (av1_cdef.s), lr (av1_lr.s:
superres, loop restoration), fg (av1_fg.s), tab (av1_tab.s, generated by tools/gen_av1.py).
The deblocking filter, CDEF and loop restoration run on the worker pool (plat::parallel_for)
like the assembly's; every item writes its own rows (or columns), so the bytes never depend
on the thread count.
```

### `img_av1` API

*test stages (av1_set_stage): output after that step  [av1.inc AVS_\*]*

```rust
AVS_FINAL: u32 = 0
AVS_RECON: u32 = 1  // reconstruction, no in-loop filter
AVS_LF: u32 = 2  // after the deblocking filter
AVS_CDEF: u32 = 3
AVS_SR: u32 = 4  // after superres (of the CDEF output)
AVS_LR: u32 = 5  // after loop restoration (film grain not applied)
```

- **`struct Pic<'a>`**
  the decoded frame (A1P of av1.inc): u16 sample planes owned by the decoder
  - `w: u32` -- output size (UpscaledWidth x FrameHeight)
  - `h: u32`
  - `bd: u32` -- BitDepth
  - `subx: u32` -- subsampling (1 / 1 for monochrome)
  - `suby: u32`
  - `mono: u32`
  - `cdp: u32` -- color_description_present_flag
  - `prim: u32`
  - `trc: u32`
  - `mc: u32`
  - `full: u32` -- color_range
  - `csp: u32` -- chroma_sample_position
  - `planes: [&'a [u16]; 3]` -- (empty for the chroma of a monochrome frame)
  - `strides: [u32; 3]` -- in samples
  - `rw: u32` -- render size
  - `rh: u32`

From [`lib/img_av1/dec.rs`](../lib/img_av1/dec.rs):

- **`struct Av1Dec<'a>`**
  the AV1 intra decoder (AV)

**`impl<'a> Av1Dec<'a>`**

- **`fn new() -> Av1Dec<'a>`**
  av1_new
- **`fn set_cancel(&mut self, c: Option<&'a AtomicU32>)`**
  av1_set_cancel: a flag polled per superblock (!= 0 cancels)
- **`fn set_stage(&mut self, stage: u32)`**
  av1_set_stage: test hook, AVS_\* (output after that step)
- **`fn set_nograin(&mut self, flag: u32)`**
  av1_set_nograin: 1 = do not apply film grain
- **`fn qindex(&self, seg: u32, curq: i32) -> u32`**
  av1_qindex: get_qindex( curq &lt; 0, seg ) with CurrentQIndex = curq
- **`fn obus(&mut self, data: &[u8]) -> Result<(), i32>`**
  av1_obus: OBUs in the low-overhead format
- **`fn finish(&mut self) -> Result<Pic<'_>, i32>`**
  av1_finish: the decoded frame

From [`lib/img_av1/enc.rs`](../lib/img_av1/enc.rs) (`img_av1::enc`):

- **`struct EncIn<'a>`**
  av1_encode's input (A1E)
  - `w: u32` -- frame size (1 .. 65536)
  - `h: u32`
  - `bd: u32` -- bit depth: 8 / 10
  - `chroma: u32` -- 0 (4:0:0) / 1 (4:2:0) / 3 (4:4:4)
  - `qidx: u32` -- base_q_idx 0 .. 255 (0 = lossless)
  - `cicp: u32` -- primaries | transfer &lt;&lt; 8 | matrix &lt;&lt; 16 | full range &lt;&lt; 24
  - `tile_sb: u32` -- smallest tile side in superblocks (0: 4)
  - `flags: u32` -- (reserved, 0)
  - `cancel: Option<&'a AtomicU32>`
  - `planes: [&'a [u16]; 3]` -- u16 samples; chroma (w + subX) >> subX wide
  - `strides: [u32; 3]` -- in samples

- **`struct EncOut`**
  av1_encode's output (A1O)
  - `data: Vec<u8>` -- OBUs: sequence header OBU, frame OBU
  - `seqlen: u32` -- bytes of the sequence header OBU (the av1C configOBUs)
  - `profile: u32` -- seq_profile
  - `level: u32` -- seq_level_idx

- **`fn encode(cfg: &EncIn) -> Result<EncOut, i32>`**
  av1_encode

| Module | Content |
|---|---|
| [`img_av1::eec`](../lib/img_av1/eec.rs) | AV1 encoder: range encoder (libaom's od_ec_enc, the inverse of the specification's symbol decoder) and symbol output / cost estimation of the tile encoder |

## img_avif

*AVIF* -- crate root [`lib/img_avif/lib.rs`](../lib/img_avif/lib.rs)

```text
img_avif -- AVIF (AV1 still images in HEIF): the codec entry points and the writer
                                                   [src/img/avif.s, avif_enc.s]

  probe(data, meta) -> recognised           avif_probe = heic_probe
  decode(data, sf, dec, meta) -> 0 / ERR_*  avif_decode = heic_decode
  enc::encode(sf, opt, out) -> 0 / ERR_*    avif_encode (the AV1 encoder img_av1::encode)
  CODEC                                     avif_codec

Reading is the HEIF reader of img_heif (it handles av01 items, grids, alpha,
transformations, colour and metadata the same way as for HEIC; the AV1 decoder is img_av1).
The dispatcher picks this codec for files with an AVIF brand (img_core::io bmff kind).
Writing: enc (avif_enc.s).
```

```rust
/// the encoder options the AVIF writer honours (AVIF_IOM)
AVIF_IOM: u32 = IOM_QUALITY | IOM_LOSSLESS | IOM_CHROMA | IOM_ICC | IOM_E ...
```

## img_jxl

*JPEG XL* -- crate root [`lib/img_jxl/lib.rs`](../lib/img_jxl/lib.rs)

```text
img_jxl -- JPEG XL reader (ISO/IEC 18181) and writer (lossless modular, lossy VarDCT)
                                                          [src/img/jxl.s, jxl_*.s]

  CODEC                         the codec descriptor (jxl_codec)
  probe(data, meta) -> bool     jxl_probe
  decode(data, sf, dec, meta) -> 0 / ERR_*   jxl_decode: displayed frame dec.frame
                                (ERR_LIMIT past the last) as FMT_ARGB, 8 bits; meta.delay of
                                that frame; ICC, EXIF (Exif box), XMP (xml box) blobs;
                                ERR_UNSUPPORTED: float extra channels, PQ / HLG / 709
                                output.  jxl-plus: DC frames (progressive DC); the groups of
                                a frame decode on the worker pool (same pixels).  jxl-tools:
                                splines (spline.rs: libjxl 0.7's rendering, same pixels)
  open(data) -> Dec / ERR_*     jx_open: codestream copy (bare FF 0A or the box container:
                                jxlc / jxlp), image headers
  jx_frames(jx, br) -> u32      displayed frames (walks the frame headers)
  jx_meta(jx, br, meta)         image properties into an ImgMeta
  enc::encode(sf, opt, out) -> 0 / ERR_*   jxl_encode: the jxl-plus writer (crate img_jxl_enc:
                                enew.rs lossless modular, evdct.rs lossy VarDCT, options
                                below) when the app has one (writer.rs, jxl-module: linked in
                                with img_jxl_enc::use_static(), or the module file jxlenc.dll /
                                .so with writer::use_module); with IOF_JXL_COMPAT, without a
                                new writer or without the module file the assembly's writer
                                (enc.rs / venc.rs)
  jpeg::reconstruct(data) -> JPEG bytes / ERR_*   jxl-plus: the original JPEG file of a JPEG
                                transcode (jbrd box; byte-identical to libjxl's); jpeg::has_jbrd
  box_contents(data, box)       jxl-plus: an Exif / xml box (Brotli decompressed when it is a
                                brob box: libjxl's JPEG transcodes; decode reads them as EXIF /
                                XMP too)

Writer options (ImgOpt) -- the save dialog's controls                        [jxl-plus]
  quality   1..99 lossy (VarDCT), 100 lossless; 0 = 90.  Butteraugli distance as libjxl:
            q >= 30: 0.1 + (100 - q) * 0.09, else 53 / 3000 q^2 - 23 / 20 q + 25
  effort    1..9 like libjxl's (0 = 7, 1 works like 2); higher = smaller, slower.  What
            each effort adds:
              lossless  1-2 gradient predictor, fixed tree; 3 weighted predictor, fixed
                        tree; 1-4 YCoCg; 4+ learned MA tree (both predictors); 5+ local
                        palettes, RCT search per group, patches (jxl-tools: repeated
                        text glyphs / UI pieces in a reference frame; 9 keeps the smaller
                        file with / without); 8+ WP parameter search, LZ77, more tree
                        properties
              lossy     1-3 DCT8, one quant value; 4 near-lossless DC, block context
                        model, own coefficient orders; 5 adaptive quantization, AC
                        strategy search (DCT2 .. DCT64, identity), chroma from luma,
                        gaborish; 6 DCT4x8 / AFV, unaligned blocks; 7 (default) better
                        CfL, chroma scaling from the pixels, patches (jxl-tools; from
                        distance 3 also dots when there are no text-like patches); 8
                        learned DC / metadata trees, the Butteraugli quantization loop
                        (jxl-tools: 2 round trips of the frame, libjxl's
                        FindBestQuantization); 9 LZ77, 4 round trips
  flags     IOF_LOSSLESS          lossless whatever the quality
            IOF_EMBED_ICC         write meta.icc (RGB / gray profiles).  Lossy: an sRGB
                                  profile is written as plain sRGB; other profiles keep
                                  their values (coded as YCbCr, no XYB without colour
                                  management) and the profile
            IOF_EMBED_EXIF / _XMP meta.exif / meta.xmp as Exif / xml boxes (container)
            IOF_JXL_PROGRESSIVE   0x400000 (enew.rs): lossy: a 1:8 DC frame and three AC
                                  passes; lossless: squeeze (coarse to fine).  A few %
                                  larger
            IOF_JXL_1THREAD       0x200000: no worker threads (the same file, slower)
            IOF_JXL_COMPAT        0x100000: the assembly-compatible writer (byte-identical
                                  to the asm; quality / lossless / EXIF / XMP only)
            IOF_JXL_NOPATCHES     0x800000 (jxl-tools): no patches at any effort
            bits 24..31           (jxl-tools, IOF_JXL_NOISE_SHIFT / _MASK) photon noise:
                                  ISO / 100 (0 = off, the default; 16 = ISO 1600): lossy XYB
                                  frames carry libjxl's noise LUT for that ISO (cjxl
                                  --photon_noise_iso), the decoder adds the grain; lossless
                                  and YCbCr frames ignore it
  cancel    polled between the stages: ERR_CANCEL
The file never depends on the number of threads.  Extra channels other than alpha, more
than 8 bits, animation: not written; splines only by the test-only espline.rs (codectest
op 197).

Modules (one per assembly file): bits (jxl_bits.s), hdr (jxl_hdr.s), icc (jxl_icc.s), ent
(jxl_ent.s), frm (jxl_frm.s), dec (jxl_dec.s), modular (jxl_mod.s), mtr (jxl_mtr.s), vdc
(jxl_vdc.s), vac (jxl_vac.s), vqt (jxl_vqt.s), vrc (jxl_vrc.s), dct (jxl_dct.s), rnd
(jxl_rnd.s), epf (jxl_epf.s), blend (jxl_blend.s), noise (jxl_noise.s), tab (jxl_tab.s,
generated by tools/gen_jxl.py); the writer: ewr (jxl_ewr.s), enc (jxl_enc.s), venc
(jxl_venc.s).  jxl-plus (no asm): emath (libjxl's fast math), jpeg (JPEG reconstruction) with
brotli (RFC 7932 decoder; brotli_tab.rs / brotli_dict.bin generated by
tools/gen_brotli_dict.py), par (the pool helpers).  jxl-tools (no asm): spline (decoder:
splines).  jxl-module: writer (the new writer's options, samples and hook).  The new writer
itself (libjxl 0.11.1's encoder: enew, evdct, emod, ema, eans, efrm, eicc, etx, dctk, eaq,
eacs, ecfl, epatch, edots, espline, enoise, efeat, ebutter, ebq) is the crate img_jxl_enc
(lib/img_jxl_enc/lib.rs), linked into codectest and built into the module jxlenc
(modules/jxlenc) that PaintCraft loads; Recon and decode_linear below are its reconstruction
helpers.
```

### `img_jxl` API

- **`struct Dec`**
  an opened file: the codestream copy (with JXB_PAD zero bytes) and the decoder record
  - `cs: Vec<u8>`
  - `jx: Jx`

**`impl Dec`**

- **`fn reader(&self) -> Jb<'_>`**
  the header reader at the first frame header

- **`fn box_contents(data: &[u8], b: (usize, usize, bool)) -> Result<Vec<u8>, i32>`**
  the contents of a metadata box (jxl-plus): (start, size, brob) from Jx.exifbox / xmlbox;
  a brob box is Brotli decompressed

- **`fn open(data: &[u8]) -> Result<Dec, i32>`**
  jx_open: codestream copy and image headers

- **`fn jx_frames(jx: &Jx, br: &mut Jb) -> u32`**
  jx_frames: displayed frames (0 on a damaged frame list); br is the header reader

- **`fn jx_meta(jx: &Jx, br: &mut Jb, m: &mut ImgMeta)`**
  jx_meta: image properties into meta

- **`fn probe(data: &[u8], meta: &mut ImgMeta) -> bool`**
  jxl_probe

- **`fn decode(data: &[u8], sf: &mut Surface, dec: &ImgDec, meta: &mut ImgMeta) -> i32`**
  jxl_decode (see the module header)

- **`fn decode_opt(data: &[u8], sf: &mut Surface, dec: &ImgDec, meta: Option<&mut ImgMeta>) -> i32`**
  jxl_decode with an optional ImgMeta (the assembly's ID_META may be 0: then the image
  properties are not read, which leaves the header reader's state alone)

- **`struct Recon`**
  the writer's reconstruction for its EPF sharpness search (jxl-plus; img_jxl_enc): the displayed frame
  decoded once up to the EPF; planes(sharpness) runs the EPF with that sharpness per block
  on a copy

**`impl Recon`**

- **`fn new(cs: &[u8]) -> Result<Recon, i32>`**
- **`fn planes(&mut self, sharp: &[u8]) -> Result<(u32, u32, usize, [&[f32]; 3]), i32>`**
  (width, height, row stride, X / Y / B) after the EPF with this sharpness per block
  (jxl-speed: the padding of the frame for the first stage is made once, the output
  planes are reused: every sample the EPF writes is rewritten each call, the rest is the
  frame's as in a fresh copy)

- **`fn decode_linear(cs: &[u8], w: usize, h: usize) -> Result<[Vec<f32>; 3], i32>`**
  the writer's Butteraugli round trips (jxl-tools): the shown frame of a codestream of the
  writer (XYB, no blending) decoded to linear sRGB planes of w x h

- **`static CODEC: Codec`**
  the codec descriptor  [jxl_codec]

From [`lib/img_jxl/jpeg.rs`](../lib/img_jxl/jpeg.rs) (`img_jxl::jpeg`):

- **`fn has_jbrd(data: &[u8]) -> bool`**
  has_jbrd: whether data is a container with a JPEG reconstruction box

- **`fn reconstruct(data: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, i32>`**
  reconstruct: the JPEG file of a JPEG transcode (see the module header)

From [`lib/img_jxl/writer.rs`](../lib/img_jxl/writer.rs) (`img_jxl::writer`):

```rust
/// IO_FLAGS bit: the assembly-compatible writer (byte-identical to the asm reference)
IOF_JXL_COMPAT: u32 = 0x10_0000
/// IO_FLAGS bit: no worker threads (the output is the same either way)
IOF_JXL_1THREAD: u32 = 0x20_0000
/// IO_FLAGS bit: progressive (VarDCT: DC + 2 AC passes; lossless: squeeze, responsive)
IOF_JXL_PROGRESSIVE: u32 = 0x40_0000
/// IO_FLAGS bit (jxl-tools): no patches (libjxl's patches = 0; for comparisons)
IOF_JXL_NOPATCHES: u32 = 0x80_0000
/// IO_FLAGS bits 24..31 (jxl-tools): photon noise ISO / 100 (lossy only; 0 = off)
IOF_JXL_NOISE_SHIFT: u32 = 24
/// the mask of the photon noise bits (jxl-tools)
IOF_JXL_NOISE_MASK: u32 = 0xFF00_0000
```

- **`struct Opts<'a>`**
  the encoder options
  - `effort: u32`
  - `quality: u32`
  - `flags: u32`
  - `icc: Option<&'a [u8]>`
  - `exif: Option<&'a [u8]>`
  - `xmp: Option<&'a [u8]>`
  - `cancel: Option<&'a AtomicU32>`
  - `splines: Option<&'a crate::spline::Quantized>` -- jxl-tools: the test-only spline writer

**`impl<'a> Opts<'a>`**

- **`fn from_imgopt(opt: &ImgOpt<'a>) -> Opts<'a>`**
  the options of an ImgOpt: the ICC profile with IOF_EMBED_ICC, EXIF / XMP when present
  (the container writer looks at IOF_EMBED_EXIF / \_XMP)
- **`fn effort(&self) -> u32`**
  the effort 1..9 (0 = 7)
- **`fn threads(&self) -> bool`**
  worker threads allowed (no IOF_JXL_1THREAD)
- **`fn lossless(&self) -> bool`**
  lossless: IOF_LOSSLESS or quality 100 and more
- **`fn cancelled(&self) -> bool`**
  the cancel flag is set
- **`fn noise_iso(&self) -> u32`**
  the photon noise ISO (0 = none)  [jxl-tools]
- **`fn patches(&self, lossless: bool) -> bool`**
  patches allowed at this effort (libjxl: lossless 5+, lossy 7+)  [jxl-tools]

- **`enum Kind`**
  which writer saves JPEG XL files without IOF_JXL_COMPAT
  - `Static` -- the writer linked into the app (img_jxl_enc)
  - `Module` -- the loadable module (jxlenc.dll / jxlenc.so next to the exe)
  - `Compat` -- none: the assembly-compatible writer

- **`fn set_static(f: WriterFn)`**
  the writer linked into the app (img_jxl_enc::use_static() installs enew::encode_px)

- **`fn use_module(path: Option<&str>)`**
  the writer of the module file: None = &lt;exe folder>/jxlenc.dll (.so), Some(path) = that file,
  Some("off") = none (the assembly-compatible writer; tests).  Loaded on first use.

- **`fn kind() -> Kind`**
  which writer saves (Module only when the module file loads: this loads it)

- **`fn write(sf: &Surface, o: &Opts) -> Option<Result<Vec<u8>, i32>>`**
  the new writer on sf: Some(file / ERR_\*), None when there is none (no writer installed, or
  the module file is missing / refused)

### `img_jxl` modules

The codec's building blocks (public for the codec test app `codectest`; see their source headers):

| Module | Content |
|---|---|
| [`img_jxl::bits`](../lib/img_jxl/bits.rs) | JPEG XL bit reader and field coding (fields.cc; model: tools/jxl_ref.py Bits) |
| [`img_jxl::blend`](../lib/img_jxl/blend.rs) | JPEG XL reference frames, frame blending and patches (blending.cc, stage_blending.cc, dec_patch_dictionary.cc; model: tools/jxl_ref.py perform_blending, ... |
| [`img_jxl::brotli`](../lib/img_jxl/brotli.rs) | Brotli decompression (RFC 7932) of the JPEG reconstruction data (jbrd box) of JPEG transcodes |
| [`img_jxl::brotli_tab`](../lib/img_jxl/brotli_tab.rs) | Brotli static dictionary layout and the 121 word transforms (RFC 7932 appendices A and B); generated by tools/gen_brotli_dict.py, do not edit |
| [`img_jxl::dct`](../lib/img_jxl/dct.rs) | JPEG XL VarDCT transforms (dct-inl.h IDCT1DImpl / DCT1DImpl, dec_transforms-inl.h TransformToPixels / LowestFrequenciesFromDC; model: tools/jxl_ref.py ... |
| [`img_jxl::dec`](../lib/img_jxl/dec.rs) | JPEG XL frame decoding driver (dec_frame.cc, dec_modular.cc; model: tools/jxl_ref.py Frame.decode, decode_global_modular, decode_group, pass_bracket) |
| [`img_jxl::emath`](../lib/img_jxl/emath.rs) | float helpers of the new encoder (libjxl base/fast_math-inl.h, rational_polynomial-inl.h) |
| [`img_jxl::enc`](../lib/img_jxl/enc.rs) | JPEG XL writer, lossless modular (model: tools/jxl_enc_ref.py encode_lossless; byte-exact) |
| [`img_jxl::ent`](../lib/img_jxl/ent.rs) | JPEG XL entropy decoding (dec_ans.cc, ans_common.cc, dec_huffman.cc, dec_context_map.cc; model: tools/jxl_ref.py read_histogram .. Reader) |

## img_jxl_enc

*the new JPEG XL writer (linked into codectest; PaintCraft loads it as the module jxlenc)* -- crate root [`lib/img_jxl_enc/lib.rs`](../lib/img_jxl_enc/lib.rs)

```text
img_jxl_enc -- the new JPEG XL writer (jxl-plus: a port of libjxl 0.11.1's encoder; jxl-tools,
jxl-speed), split out of img_jxl so that PaintCraft can load it as a module (jxl-module)
                                                                      [jxl-plus: no asm]

  use_static()                   the app saves JPEG XL with this writer, linked in (codectest,
                                 the check tools): img_jxl::writer::set_static(enew::encode_px)
  enew::encode / encode_px / encode_with_splines   the writer itself (options: the header
                                 of img_jxl/lib.rs)

Who links what: img_jxl (reader, the assembly-compatible writer, the writer's options and
samples in writer.rs) is in every app that registers JPEG XL; this crate only where the
app calls use_static() or one of its functions (codectest).  PaintCraft does not: it calls
img_jxl::writer::use_module(), and the module jxlenc (modules/jxlenc: this crate built
as a no_std cdylib over the module runtime) runs encode_px on the samples the host read.
The output is the same byte for byte either way, for any number of threads.

Modules (moved from img_jxl, same names; `crate::<decoder module>` paths resolve through
the re-exports below): enew (options, lossless frame), evdct (VarDCT frame), emod (modular
streams, transforms), ema (MA tree learning), eans (entropy coding: ANS / prefix codes,
clustering, LZ77), efrm (headers, TOC), eicc (ICC stream), etx (forward transforms, quant
tables), dctk (the transforms as simd kernels; jxl-speed), eaq (adaptive quantization,
gaborish), eacs (AC strategy search), ecfl (chroma from luma); jxl-tools: epatch (patch
search, packing, dictionary), edots (dots for the dictionary), espline (test-only spline
writer), enoise (photon noise LUT), efeat (the frame's feature sections), ebutter
(Butteraugli), ebq (the effort 8 / 9 quantization loop).
```

Re-exports: `img_jxl::{ bits, blend, brotli, dct, dec, emath, enc, ent, epf, ewr, frm, hdr, icc, jpeg, modular, mtr, noise, par, rnd, spline, tab, vac, vdc, venc, vqt, vrc, writer, }`

Re-exports: `img_jxl::{decode_linear, Recon}`

  cannot write splines; the decoder's checks use this)

## img_jxr

*JPEG XR* -- crate root [`lib/img_jxr/lib.rs`](../lib/img_jxr/lib.rs)

```text
img_jxr -- JPEG XR (ITU-T T.832 / ISO 29199-2, HD Photo) decoder and encoder: tag-based
container (Annex A), codestream decode driver, metadata              [src/img/jxr.s]

  probe(data, meta) -> recognised                    jxr_probe (container + IMAGE_HEADER
                                                     fields into meta)
  decode(data, out, dec, meta) -> 0 / ERR_*          jxr_decode
  enc::encode(sf, opt, out) -> 0 / ERR_*             jxr_encode (jxr_enc.s)
  dbg_planes(data, mode) -> (0 / ERR_*, blob)        jxr_dbg_planes -- test hook: the decoded
      planes of the primary codestream as {u32 w, h, ml, mt, npl; per plane u32 cf, nch,
      scaled; per component u32 width, height, i32 samples...}; mode 0 = reconstructed
      samples, 1 = before the inverse transform (HP coefficients in the planes), 2 = the DC
      grids after decoding, 3 = {u32 w, h; i32 [h][w][5]} output samples of the pixel format
      (color.rs, before 8-bit)
  CODEC                                              jxr_codec

Modules: ent (jxr_ent.s: headers, entropy decoding, prediction, dequantisation), pct
(jxr_pct.s: inverse transform, overlap filters), color (jxr_color.s: output formatting), enc
(jxr_enc.s: the writer), tab (generated tables, tools/gen_jxr.py).
```

## img_dds

*DDS and BC1..BC7 block compression* -- crate root [`lib/img_dds/lib.rs`](../lib/img_dds/lib.rs)

```text
img_dds -- DirectDraw Surface reader / writer and the BC1..BC7 block codecs
                        [src/img/dds.s, bc_dec.s, bc_enc.s, bc6h_enc.s, bc7_enc.s]

Modules:
  read     DDS reader: probe, decode (headers, DX10, legacy pixel formats, mipmaps,
           cube maps as a 4 x 3 cross, arrays, volumes)                     [dds.s CD]
  write    DDS writer: encode (legacy / DX10 headers, BC and raw formats, mip chains,
           cube crosses)                                                    [dds.s CB]
  dec      BC1..BC7 block decoders, decode_to / decode                      [bc_dec.s]
  enc      BC1..BC5 block encoders, the surface driver (block rows on the thread pool)
           and the float pixel-set helpers of the BC6H / BC7 encoders       [bc_enc.s]
  bc6h     BC6H block encoder                                               [bc6h_enc.s]
  bc7      BC7 block encoder                                                [bc7_enc.s]

  CODEC    the codec descriptor (dds_codec): probe, decode, encode
```

### `img_dds` API

*img.inc (BC / DDS parts)*

```rust
/// block formats of the BC codecs (BCF_*); 1..7 equal DDSF_BC1..BC7
BCF_BC1: u32 = 1
BCF_BC2: u32 = 2
BCF_BC3: u32 = 3
BCF_BC4: u32 = 4  // BC4 unsigned (ATI1 / BC4U)
BCF_BC5: u32 = 5  // BC5 unsigned (ATI2 / BC5U)
BCF_BC6H: u32 = 6  // BC6H UF16
BCF_BC7: u32 = 7
BCF_BC4S: u32 = 8  // BC4 signed
BCF_BC5S: u32 = 9  // BC5 signed
BCF_BC6HS: u32 = 10  // BC6H SF16
BCF_COUNT: u32 = 11
/// encoder configuration (cfg of encode_block / encode_to)
BCQ_LEVEL: u32 = 3  // mask: 0 default (= normal), BCQ_FAST, BCQ_NORMAL, BCQ_SLOW
BCQ_FAST: u32 = 1  // BC7: mode 6 only; BC1..5: one refinement
BCQ_NORMAL: u32 = 2  // BC7: modes 1, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7
BCQ_SLOW: u32 = 3  // BC7: all modes / rotations / p-bits
BCQ_UNIFORM: u32 = 4  // BC1..BC3 colour: uniform RGB error (else perceptual)
BCQ_ATHR_SHIFT: u32 = 8  // BC1: bits 8..16 = alpha threshold + 1 (0 = default 128)
/// more ImgOpt::ddsfmt values (DDSF_BC1 .. DDSF_R8G8 are in img_core)
DDSF_BC4S: u32 = 17  // BC4 signed (legacy FourCC "BC4S")
DDSF_BC5S: u32 = 18  // BC5 signed ("BC5S")
DDSF_BC6HS: u32 = 19  // BC6H SF16 (DX10)
DDSF_R8: u32 = 20  // DXGI R8_UNORM (grey, DX10)
DDSF_BGR8: u32 = 21  // legacy 24-bit R8G8B8
DDSF_COUNT: u32 = 22
/// more ImgOpt::mipfilter values (0 = MIPF_FANT)
MIPF_FANT: u32 = 5  // area average
MIPF_LANCZOS: u32 = 6  // Lanczos-3
/// ImgOpt::flags: uniform error metric for BC1..BC3 colour
IOF_DDS_UNIFORM: u32 = 0x10000
```

- **`static CODEC: Codec`**
  the codec descriptor  [dds_codec]

From [`lib/img_dds/dec.rs`](../lib/img_dds/dec.rs) (`img_dds::dec`):

- **`fn block_bytes(fmt: u32) -> usize`**
  bytes of one block of bcfmt (0 = unknown format)  [bc_block_bytes]

- **`fn decode_block(fmt: u32, src: &[u8], dst: &mut [u8], stride: usize) -> i32`**
  decode one 4x4 block (8 / 16 bytes at src) into 4 rows of 4 straight BGRA pixels at dst,
  rows stride bytes apart -> 0 / ERR_UNSUPPORTED  [bc_decode_block]

- **`fn decode_to(src: &[u8], fmt: u32, px: &mut [u8], stride: usize, w: u32, h: u32, cancel: Option<&AtomicU32>) -> i32`**
  decode ceil(w / 4) x ceil(h / 4) blocks (raster order) into the 32-bit pixels at px (row
  y at y \* stride, w x h pixels) -> 0 / ERR_TRUNC / ERR_CANCEL / ERR_UNSUPPORTED; the
  cancel flag is polled once per block row  [bc_decode_to]

- **`fn decode(src: &[u8], w: u32, h: u32, fmt: u32) -> Result<Surface, i32>`**
  decode a whole BCn image (blocks in raster order) into a new FMT_ARGB surface (w, h
  1..65535, at most IMG_MAXPX_DEF pixels: ERR_DIMS / ERR_LIMIT; ERR_TRUNC,
  ERR_UNSUPPORTED)  [bc_decode]

- **`fn bc6h_decode_half(src: &[u8], signed: bool, dst: &mut [u16; 64])`**
  decode one BC6H block (signed: SF16, else UF16) into 16 x (r, g, b, a) IEEE halves
  (a = 1.0); reserved modes -> 0  [bc6h_decode_half]

From [`lib/img_dds/enc.rs`](../lib/img_dds/enc.rs) (`img_dds::enc`):

**`impl Scratch`**

- **`fn encode_block(fmt: u32, px: &[u8; 64], dst: &mut [u8], cfg: u32) -> i32`**

- **`fn encode_to(sf: &Surface, fmt: u32, cfg: u32, dst: &mut [u8], cancel: Option<&AtomicU32>) -> i32`**

## img_pdn

*paint.net documents (.pdn)* -- crate root [`lib/img_pdn/lib.rs`](../lib/img_pdn/lib.rs)

```text
img_pdn -- paint.net documents (.pdn): read and write, compatible with paint.net 5.1, and
the MS-NRBF (.NET BinaryFormatter) parser / writer they are built on
                                                   [src/img/pdn.s, src/img/nrbf.s]

  CODEC                              the IMGCODEC (pdn_codec): probe, flat decode (the
                                     composite), flat encode (one-layer document)
  decode(data, dec) -> PdnDoc        pdn_decode: the file with every layer
  decode_ex(data, dec, flags)        pdn_decode_ex: PDND_PROBE = size, resolution and
                                     metadata only (no layers)
  encode(doc, opt) -> file bytes     pdn_encode (IO_EFFORT = deflate level 1..9, default 1)
  encode_ex(doc, opt, flags)         pdn_encode_ex: PDNE_BLENDOP = the 3.x blendOp classes
  flatten(doc) -> Surface            pdn_flatten: the composite of the visible layers
                                     (FMT_ARGB, paint.net blend modes and opacity)
  probe / decode_flat / encode_flat  pdn_probe / pdn_decode_flat / pdn_encode_flat
  nrbf::Nrbf, nrbf::Nw               the NRBF reader / writer (nrbf.s)

The document records of the assembly (img.inc PDNDOC / PDNLAYER) are PdnDoc / PdnLayer:
the reader returns an owned PdnDoc (pdn_free = drop).  The writer takes a borrowed view,
DocRef / LayerRef, so a caller hands its own layers over without copying pixels (PaintCraft's
save_pdn borrows its layer surfaces the same way); PdnDoc::view() makes one of a PdnDoc.
PaintCraft (R11): open_pdn = decode(data, Some(&dec)), then move each layer's surface, name
and metadata blob out of doc.layers (core::mem::take) and use doc.unit / dpux / dpuy;
save_pdn = a DocRef over its layers (LayerRef per layer) and encode(&docref, Some(&opt)).
Field semantics are those of the asm records: opacity / visible / blend / isbg as stored,
blend values > PDNB_XOR mean Normal, the metadata blobs are {u32 klen, key, u32 vlen,
value}* in file order, the writer accepts FMT_XRGB / PARGB / ARGB / A8 layers.
Threads: deferred payloads with several chunks are decoded, and every layer's chunks are
compressed, on the plat::parallel_for pool (as the assembly's Z1 worker-pool loops).

File format (confirmed on paint.net 5.1.12 files):
  "PDN3" | u24 LE n | n bytes UTF-8 XML header (size + thumbnail PNG in base64) |
  00 01 | MS-NRBF object graph | the deferred MemoryBlock payloads, in the order of the
  MemoryBlock objects in the stream: u8 format (0 gzip, 1 raw), u32 BE chunk size, then
  ceil(length / chunk) chunks in any order {u32 BE index, u32 BE size, data}.
  Legacy: "PDN3" + header + 1F 8B = gzip of everything else; a bare gzip file (<= 2.0).
Resolution and the ICC profile live in the document metadata as EXIF items
("$exif.tagN[0]" = <exif id="296|282|283|34675" ... value="base64" />).

Modules: nrbf (nrbf.s), dec (the reader: pdn.s decoding), enc (the writer: pdn.s encoding),
template (the NRBF class templates, generated by tools/gen_pdn_template.py).
```

### `img_pdn` API

*constants (img.inc)*

```rust
PDU_PIXEL: u32 = 1  // PdnDoc::unit (paint.net MeasurementUnit values)
PDU_INCH: u32 = 2
PDU_CM: u32 = 3
PDNB_NORMAL: u8 = 0  // PdnLayer::blend: paint.net LayerBlendMode = gfx BM_*
PDNB_MULTIPLY: u8 = 1
PDNB_ADDITIVE: u8 = 2
PDNB_COLORBURN: u8 = 3
PDNB_COLORDODGE: u8 = 4
PDNB_REFLECT: u8 = 5
PDNB_GLOW: u8 = 6
PDNB_OVERLAY: u8 = 7
PDNB_DIFFERENCE: u8 = 8
PDNB_NEGATION: u8 = 9
PDNB_LIGHTEN: u8 = 10
PDNB_DARKEN: u8 = 11
PDNB_SCREEN: u8 = 12
PDNB_XOR: u8 = 13
PDND_PROBE: u32 = 1  // decode_ex: size, resolution and metadata only (no layers)
PDNE_BLENDOP: u32 = 1  // encode_ex: BitmapLayerProperties.blendOp = the UserBlendOps
```

- **`static CODEC: Codec`**
  the codec descriptor  [pdn_codec]: IO_EFFORT = deflate level of the pixel chunks,
  IO_META's ICC profile with IOF_EMBED_ICC

- **`struct PdnDoc`**
  a paint.net document (PDNDOC)
  - `w: u32`
  - `h: u32`
  - `unit: u32` -- PDU_\* (paint.net MeasurementUnit)
  - `dpux: f64` -- dots per unit (IEEE double, as stored by paint.net)
  - `dpuy: f64`
  - `layers: Vec<PdnLayer>` -- bottom layer first (PD_N = layers.len(); none after a PDND_PROBE decode)
  - `nvc: Vec<u8>` -- Document.Metadata, serialized: items {u32 klen, key, u32 vlen, value} (UTF-8, the
    KeyValuePair / NameValueCollection entries in file order); empty = none
  - `meta: Option<ImgMeta>` -- decoded: IMGF_PDN, size, 8-bit RGBA, resolution (dpix / dpiy / resunit from the
    metadata EXIF items 296 / 282 / 283) and the ICC profile (34675); for view(): the
    ICC profile to embed
  - `xml: Vec<u8>` -- the file's XML header (UTF-8)

- **`struct PdnLayer`**
  a layer (PDNLAYER)
  - `sf: Surface` -- decoded: FMT_ARGB straight (paint.net ColorBgra); for the writer any of FMT_XRGB,
    FMT_PARGB, FMT_ARGB, FMT_A8 with the document's size
  - `name: Vec<u8>` -- UTF-8 name; the writer (and the assembly's NUL-terminated strings) end it at the
    first NUL, the reader cuts it there
  - `opacity: u8` -- 0..255
  - `visible: u8` -- 0 / 1
  - `blend: u8` -- PDNB_\*
  - `isbg: u8` -- 1 = background layer
  - `meta: Vec<u8>` -- layer metadata, serialized like PdnDoc::nvc; empty = none

- **`struct DocRef<'a>`**
  the writer's view of a document: everything borrowed
  - `w: u32`
  - `h: u32`
  - `unit: u32`
  - `dpux: f64`
  - `dpuy: f64`
  - `layers: Vec<LayerRef<'a>>`
  - `nvc: &'a [u8]`
  - `icc: &'a [u8]` -- the ICC profile to embed (EXIF item 34675); empty = none

- **`struct LayerRef<'a>`**
  the writer's view of a layer
  - `sf: &'a Surface`
  - `name: &'a [u8]`
  - `opacity: u8`
  - `visible: u8`
  - `blend: u8`
  - `isbg: u8`
  - `meta: &'a [u8]`

**`impl PdnLayer`**

- **`fn view(&self) -> LayerRef<'_>`**

**`impl PdnDoc`**

- **`fn view(&self) -> DocRef<'_>`**
  the document as the writer sees it (ICC profile from meta)

- **`fn flatten(doc: &DocRef) -> Result<Surface, i32>`**
  a new FMT_ARGB surface with the composite of the visible layers (paint.net blend modes
  and opacity, k_bm_row); layers of any surface format with the document's size (ERR_DIMS
  otherwise)  [pdn_flatten]

From [`lib/img_pdn/dec.rs`](../lib/img_pdn/dec.rs):

- **`fn decode(data: &[u8], dec: Option<&ImgDec>) -> Result<PdnDoc, i32>`**
  read a .pdn: every layer  [pdn_decode]

- **`fn decode_ex(data: &[u8], dec: Option<&ImgDec>, flags: u32) -> Result<PdnDoc, i32>`**
  pdn_decode; flags PDND_PROBE: only size, resolution and metadata (no layers)
  [pdn_decode_ex]

- **`fn probe(data: &[u8], m: &mut ImgMeta) -> bool`**
  "PDN3" header: width / height from the XML, 8-bit straight BGRA; legacy gzip files are
  recognised without a size  [pdn_probe]

- **`fn decode_flat(data: &[u8], out: &mut Surface, dec: &ImgDec, meta: &mut ImgMeta) -> i32`**
  img_decode_ex's entry: the composite of the visible layers (FMT_ARGB); IMGD_PROBE:
  metadata only; IMGD_ICC: the profile of the document metadata  [pdn_decode_flat]

From [`lib/img_pdn/enc.rs`](../lib/img_pdn/enc.rs):

- **`fn encode(doc: &DocRef, opt: Option<&ImgOpt>) -> Result<Vec<u8>, i32>`**
  write the document as a .pdn file (opt: IO_EFFORT = deflate level 1..9, default 1)
  [pdn_encode]

- **`fn encode_ex(doc: &DocRef, opt: Option<&ImgOpt>, flags: u32) -> Result<Vec<u8>, i32>`**
  pdn_encode with PDNE_\* flags; errors: ERR_DIMS (sizes, a layer of another size, no
  layers), ERR_UNSUPPORTED (surface format), ERR_LIMIT (XML header > 16 MB, the composite
  over the default pixel budget), ERR_CANCEL  [pdn_encode_ex]

- **`fn encode_flat(sf: &Surface, opt: &ImgOpt, out: &mut Vec<u8>) -> i32`**
  img_encode's entry: sf as the background layer of a one-layer document (resolution from
  opt.meta dpix / dpiy, ICC profile with IOF_EMBED_ICC)  [pdn_encode_flat]

The codec's building blocks (public for the codec test app `codectest`; see their source headers):

| Module | Content |
|---|---|
| [`img_pdn::nrbf`](../lib/img_pdn/nrbf.rs) | MS-NRBF (.NET BinaryFormatter) reader and template-driven writer |

## fx

*effects and adjustments* -- crate root [`lib/fx/lib.rs`](../lib/fx/lib.rs)

```text
fx -- the effect and adjustment kernels: the FXDESC registry, prepare / render on ROIs from
worker threads, deterministic per pixel                       [src/fx/*.s, inc/fx.inc]

The contract (fx.inc).  An application (PaintCraft, efftest) runs an effect by
  1. copying the source layer (FMT_ARGB, straight alpha) -> FxArgs::src,
  2. calling desc.prepare once when it has one (global statistics, LUTs, noise tables):
     Ok(Some(aux)) / Ok(None) / Err(ERR_*) -- ERR_CANCEL when the job was cancelled,
  3. calling desc.render for many rectangles (ROIs) -- from several worker threads at once
     -- until the ROIs cover the area to change; every call gets the aux of step 2,
  4. blending the destination back through the selection mask itself
     (gfx::straight::lerp_mask_row).
Rules: render writes the destination only inside its ROI (Dst holds exactly those pixels)
and may read the whole source; the result of a pixel depends only on (source, params, aux)
-- never on the ROI split or the thread count (bit-identical); render polls the job's
cancel flag once per row (FxArgs::cancelled) and returns ERR_CANCEL at once.  Pixels are
straight ARGB (0xAARRGGBB = bytes B, G, R, A).  Random effects use hash32(x, y, seed,
stream), never a sequential generator.  FXF_BANDS: render wants full-width row bands (the
runner splits by rows only); FXF_WHOLE: one call over the whole image.

API (the asm records as Rust types):
  FxDesc   FXDESC: id (FX_*), flags (FXF_*), name (ASCII key), prepare / render (fn
           pointers; FXD_FREE is the Drop of the aux), props (FXPROP records), category
           (FXC_*), user (free for the registering code, e.g. a plugin index)
  FxProp   FXPROP: typ (FP_*), dec, slot, flags (FPF_*), name, min, max, def (FP_COLOR:
           the f64 bits are the ARGB colour -- color()), def_y (FP_PAN: the y default),
           choices (FP_CHOICE keys)
  Params   the parameter block: FX_NSLOTS u64 slots (i64 for FP_INT / BOOL / CHOICE / SEED,
           f64 bits for FP_DEC / ANGLE / PAN, ARGB in the low half for FP_COLOR, raw bytes
           for FP_BLOB); defaults(desc, &mut params) fills it
  FxArgs   FXARGS without the destination: src, ROI x0 y0 x1 y1, params, aux, job (a
           Cancel flag: the worker pool's JobCtl, an AtomicBool / AtomicU32), sel (the
           selection TMask: statistics may be limited to it)
  Dst      the destination pixels of one render call: one &mut [u8] per ROI row with the
           pixels x0..x1 -- of_surface (one call owns the whole rectangle), tiles (disjoint
           rectangles of one surface for parallel calls; the row slices of all of them live
           in one caller-owned Vec), strided (rows of a tile buffer), or new (a row list)
  Aux      Box<dyn Any + Send + Sync>: the result of prepare, read by render (aux::<T>())
  registry use_all() registers the built-in groups (menu order), register(group) adds a
           group (&'static [FxDesc], e.g. plugin effects), count(), get(i), find(name),
           find_id(id), defaults(desc, params)
  hash32(x, y, seed, stream)   deterministic per-pixel random bits (fx_common.s)
  rows(a, dst, f)              fx_rows: f(dst_row, src_row, x0, y) for every ROI row

Modules: lut (fx_lut.s), hist (fx_hist.s), adj (fx_adj.s), color (fx_color.s), stylize
(fx_stylize.s), conv (fx_conv.s), lochist (fx_lochist.s), blur (fx_blur.s), artistic
(fx_artistic.s), noise (fx_noise.s), photo (fx_photo.s), object (fx_object.s), warp
(fx_warp.s), perlin (fx_perlin.s), distort (fx_distort.s), render (fx_render.s).
```

### `fx` crate root

Re-exports: `gfx::tmask::TMask`

Re-exports: `gfx::Surface`

Re-exports: `plat::JobCtl`

*constants (fx.inc)*

```rust
FX_NSLOTS: usize = 32  // parameter slots per effect
FXF_ADJ: u32 = 1  // adjustment (Korrekturen) rather than effect
FXF_PREPARE: u32 = 2  // has a prepare step (statistics over the source / selection)
FXF_SEED: u32 = 4  // uses a random seed (has an FP_SEED property)
FXF_BANDS: u32 = 8  // render wants full-width row bands
FXF_WHOLE: u32 = 16  // render must get the whole image in one call
FXF_NO_DIALOG: u32 = 32  // applied immediately (e.g. Invert Colors, Sepia)
FXF_SLOW: u32 = 64  // hint: show progress
FXC_ADJUST: u32 = 0
FXC_ARTISTIC: u32 = 1
FXC_BLUR: u32 = 2
FXC_COLOR: u32 = 3
FXC_DISTORT: u32 = 4
FXC_NOISE: u32 = 5
FXC_OBJECT: u32 = 6
FXC_PHOTO: u32 = 7
FXC_RENDER: u32 = 8
FXC_STYLIZE: u32 = 9
FP_INT: u8 = 1  // slot: i64
FP_DEC: u8 = 2  // slot: f64
FP_BOOL: u8 = 3  // slot: i64 0/1
FP_CHOICE: u8 = 4  // slot: i64 index
FP_ANGLE: u8 = 5  // slot: f64 degrees
FP_PAN: u8 = 6  // slots: f64 x, f64 y (normalised -1..1, 0 = centre)
FP_COLOR: u8 = 7  // slot: u32 straight ARGB in the low half
FP_SEED: u8 = 8  // slot: i64 seed
FP_BLOB: u8 = 9  // slots slot .. slot + max - 1 hold raw bytes (default all 0)
FPF_LOG: u8 = 1  // slider maps logarithmically
FX_AUTO_LEVEL: u32 = 1  // Korrekturen
FX_BLACK_WHITE: u32 = 2
FX_BRIGHT_CONTRAST: u32 = 3
FX_CURVES: u32 = 4
FX_EXPOSURE: u32 = 5
FX_HIGHLIGHTS_SHADOWS: u32 = 6
FX_HUE_SATURATION: u32 = 7
FX_INVERT_ALPHA: u32 = 8
FX_INVERT_COLORS: u32 = 9
FX_LEVELS: u32 = 10
FX_POSTERIZE: u32 = 11
FX_SEPIA: u32 = 12
FX_TEMP_TINT: u32 = 13

FX_OIL_PAINTING: u32 = 21
FX_PENCIL_SKETCH: u32 = 22
FX_BOKEH_BLUR: u32 = 30  // Weichzeichnen
FX_FRAGMENT_BLUR: u32 = 31
FX_GAUSSIAN_BLUR: u32 = 32
FX_MEDIAN: u32 = 33
FX_MOTION_BLUR: u32 = 34
FX_RADIAL_BLUR: u32 = 35
FX_SKETCH_BLUR: u32 = 36
FX_SQUARE_BLUR: u32 = 37
FX_SURFACE_BLUR: u32 = 38
FX_UNFOCUS: u32 = 39
FX_ZOOM_BLUR: u32 = 40
FX_QUANTIZE: u32 = 50  // Farbe
FX_BULGE: u32 = 60  // Verzerren
FX_CRYSTALIZE: u32 = 61
FX_DENTS: u32 = 62
FX_FROSTED_GLASS: u32 = 63
FX_MORPHOLOGY: u32 = 64
FX_PIXELATE: u32 = 65
FX_POLAR_INVERSION: u32 = 66
FX_TILE_REFLECTION: u32 = 67

FX_REDUCE_NOISE: u32 = 71
FX_DROP_SHADOW: u32 = 80  // Objekt
FX_GLOW: u32 = 90  // Foto
FX_RED_EYE: u32 = 91
FX_SHARPEN: u32 = 92
FX_SOFTEN_PORTRAIT: u32 = 93
FX_STRAIGHTEN: u32 = 94
FX_VIGNETTE: u32 = 95
FX_CLOUDS: u32 = 100  // Rendern
FX_JULIA: u32 = 101
FX_MANDELBROT: u32 = 102
FX_TURBULENCE: u32 = 103
FX_EDGE_DETECT: u32 = 110  // Stilisieren
FX_EMBOSS: u32 = 111

ERR_NOMEM: i32 = -8

```

- **`type Aux = Box<dyn Any + Send + Sync>`**
  the result of prepare (read-only in render; FXD_FREE = its Drop)

- **`type PrepareFn = fn(&FxArgs) -> Result<Option<Aux>, i32>`**
  FXD_PREPARE: Ok(Some(aux)) / Ok(None) / Err(ERR_\*)

- **`type RenderFn = fn(&FxArgs, &mut Dst) -> i32`**
  FXD_RENDER: 0 / ERR_CANCEL / ERR_\*

- **`struct FxDesc`**
  an effect (FXDESC)
  - `id: u32`
  - `flags: u32`
  - `name: &'static str`
  - `prepare: Option<PrepareFn>`
  - `render: RenderFn`
  - `props: &'static [FxProp]`
  - `category: u32`
  - `user: usize` -- free for the code that registers the group (0 for the built-in effects)

- **`struct FxProp`**
  a property (FXPROP); slot i of the parameter block belongs to property i, except FP_PAN
  (2 slots) and FP_BLOB (max slots)
  - `typ: u8`
  - `dec: u8`
  - `slot: u8`
  - `flags: u8`
  - `name: &'static str`
  - `min: f64`
  - `max: f64`
  - `def: f64` -- the default (FP_COLOR: the bits of this f64 are the ARGB colour)
  - `def_y: f64` -- FP_PAN: the y default (FXP_CHOICES of the asm record)
  - `choices: &'static [&'static str]`

**`impl FxProp`**

- **`const fn new(typ: u8, dec: u8, slot: u8, flags: u8, name: &'static str, min: f64, max: f64, def: f64) -> FxProp`**
  an FP_INT / DEC / BOOL / ANGLE / SEED / BLOB property (FXPROP)
- **`const fn choice(slot: u8, name: &'static str, max: f64, def: f64, choices: &'static [&'static str]) -> FxProp`**
  an FP_CHOICE property
- **`const fn color(slot: u8, name: &'static str, argb: u32) -> FxProp`**
  an FP_COLOR property (FXPROPC: the default's bits are the colour)
- **`const fn pan(slot: u8, name: &'static str, dec: u8, min: f64, max: f64, x: f64, y: f64) -> FxProp`**
  an FP_PAN property (default x, y)
- **`fn def_color(&self) -> u32`**
  the default colour of an FP_COLOR property

- **`struct Params(pub [u64; FX_NSLOTS])`**
  the parameter block (FX_NSLOTS 8-byte slots)

**`impl Params`**

- **`fn i(&self, slot: usize) -> i64`**
  slot as i64
- **`fn f(&self, slot: usize) -> f64`**
  slot as f64
- **`fn int(&self, slot: usize, lo: i32, hi: i32) -> i32`**
  the i64 slot clamped to lo..=hi  [prm_int]
- **`fn dbl(&self, slot: usize, lo: f64, hi: f64) -> f64`**
  the f64 slot clamped to lo..=hi, NaN -> lo  [prm_f64]
- **`fn byte(&self, slot: usize, i: usize) -> u8`**
  byte i of the block counted from slot `slot` (FP_BLOB)
- **`fn set_i(&mut self, slot: usize, v: i64)`**
- **`fn set_f(&mut self, slot: usize, v: f64)`**
- **`fn set_byte(&mut self, slot: usize, i: usize, v: u8)`**

- **`trait Cancel`**
  a cancel flag render / prepare poll (JOB_CANCEL of FXA_JOB): the pool's JobCtl, or any
  flag of the caller's
  - `fn cancelled(&self) -> bool`

- **`struct FxArgs<'a>`**
  the arguments of prepare / render (FXARGS without the destination)
  - `src: &'a Surface`
  - `x0: i32`
  - `y0: i32`
  - `x1: i32`
  - `y1: i32`
  - `params: &'a Params`
  - `aux: Option<&'a (dyn Any + Send + Sync)>`
  - `job: Option<&'a (dyn Cancel + Sync)>`
  - `sel: Option<&'a TMask>`

**`impl<'a> FxArgs<'a>`**

- **`fn new(src: &'a Surface, roi: [i32; 4], params: &'a Params) -> FxArgs<'a>`**
  arguments for the ROI (x0, y0) - (x1, y1) without aux, job and selection
- **`fn cancelled(&self) -> bool`**
  the job's cancel flag is set (JOB_CANCEL)
- **`fn aux<T: 'static>(&self) -> Option<&'a T>`**
  the aux of prepare as T
- **`fn src_row(&self, y: i32) -> &'a [u8]`**
  source row y (w \* 4 bytes)

- **`fn row(sf: &Surface, y: i32) -> &[u8]`**
  row y of a surface (w \* 4 bytes)

- **`fn px(b: &[u8], i: usize) -> u32`**
  pixel i of a row

- **`fn put(b: &mut [u8], i: usize, v: u32)`**
  set pixel i of a row

- **`struct Dst<'a>`**
  the destination pixels of one render call: row k holds the pixels x0..x1 of row y0 + k
  - `x0: i32`
  - `y0: i32`
  - `x1: i32`
  - `y1: i32`

**`impl<'a> Dst<'a>`**

- **`fn new(roi: [i32; 4], rows: Vec<&'a mut [u8]>) -> Dst<'a>`**
  a destination from row slices (each (x1 - x0) \* 4 bytes, y1 - y0 of them)
- **`fn strided(roi: [i32; 4], buf: &'a mut [u8], stride: usize) -> Dst<'a>`**
  a destination in one buffer: row y0 + k at k \* stride (e.g. a tile buffer with stride
  (x1 - x0) \* 4)
- **`fn of_surface(sf: &'a mut Surface, roi: [i32; 4]) -> Dst<'a>`**
  the rectangle of a surface (clamped to it) as one destination
- **`fn row(&mut self, y: i32) -> &mut [u8]`**
  the pixels x0..x1 of row y (y0 &lt;= y &lt; y1)

- **`fn tiles<'a>(sf: &'a mut Surface, rects: &[[i32; 4]], store: &'a mut Vec<&'a mut [u8]>) -> Vec<Dst<'a>>`**
  disjoint rectangles of a surface as destinations (clamped to the surface; empty ones are
  empty destinations); panics when two rectangles overlap.  `store` holds the row slices of
  all destinations (one allocation, cleared first; pass a new or reused empty Vec):
  ```text
  let mut store = Vec::new();
  let dsts = fx::tiles(&mut surface, &rects, &mut store);
  ```

- **`fn rows(a: &FxArgs, dst: &mut Dst, mut f: impl FnMut(&mut [u8], &[u8], i32, i32)) -> i32`**
  fx_rows: f(dst_row, src_row, x0, y) for every row of the ROI (rows = the pixels x0..x1);
  polls the cancel flag before every row  -> 0 | ERR_CANCEL

- **`fn register(group: &'static [FxDesc]) -> i32`**
  add a group of effects; a group already registered is ignored -> 0 | ERR_LIMIT

- **`fn use_all()`**
  register every built-in group in menu order (adjustments first)  [fx_use_all]

- **`fn count() -> usize`**
  the number of registered effects

- **`fn get(i: usize) -> Option<&'static FxDesc>`**
  the i-th registered effect (group order, then table order)

- **`fn find(name: &str) -> Option<&'static FxDesc>`**
  the effect with this ASCII key

- **`fn find_id(id: u32) -> Option<&'static FxDesc>`**
  the effect with this FX_\* id

- **`fn defaults(desc: &FxDesc, p: &mut Params)`**
  every property's default into its slot(s); slots without a property are 0

- **`fn hash32(x: u32, y: u32, seed: u32, stream: u32) -> u32`**
  deterministic per-pixel random bits: a murmur3-style finaliser over the inputs  [fx_hash32]

### `fx/adj.rs`

Source: [`lib/fx/adj.rs`](../lib/fx/adj.rs)

```text
adj -- the 13 adjustments (Korrekturen) of paint.net 5.1 as pure kernels    [src/fx/fx_adj.s]

  FXTAB            the group table (menu order of paint.net)

Pixels are straight ARGB; every adjustment keeps alpha (except Invert Alpha) and changes the
colour of transparent pixels like any other.  Intensity I = (7471 B + 38470 G + 19595 R) >> 16.

  auto_level          prepare: histogram (alpha > 0, selection > 0) -> paint.net
                      HistogramRgb.MakeLevelsAuto -> Level lut
  black_white         B = G = R = I
  brightness_contrast paint.net 3.x BrightnessAndContrastAdjustment (integer tables)
  curves              natural cubic spline luts (Luminosity: c + curve[I] - I, or R/G/B)
  exposure            linear light: out = enc(dec(c) * 2^ev) per channel lut
  highlights_shadows  blurred alpha-weighted intensity grid (prepare) -> gain
                      2^(2 s (1-m)^2 - 2 h m^2) on linear light
  hue_saturation      paint.net HueSaturationLightness (f32 lanes as the asm's SSE code)
  invert_alpha        A = 255 - A
  invert_colors       B, G, R = 255 - c
  levels              paint.net UnaryPixelOps.Level (lut::levels_lut) with a channel mask
  posterize           paint.net PosterizePixel levels per channel
  sepia               Desaturate, then Level with gamma B 1.2, G 1.0, R 0.8 (constant tables)
  temperature_tint    luminance-normalised linear-light channel gains
```

- **`static FXTAB: [FxDesc; 13]`**
  the group table (menu order of paint.net)  [fxtab_adj]

### `fx/artistic.rs`

Source: [`lib/fx/artistic.rs`](../lib/fx/artistic.rs)

```text
artistic -- effect group "artistic" (Künstlerisch): ink_sketch, oil_painting, pencil_sketch
                                                                       [src/fx/fx_artistic.s]

  FXTAB            the group table

  oil_painting   brush size b (1..8), coarseness L (3..255): paint.net 3's oil painting: the
                 (2b+1)^2 square window bucketed by intensity FastScale(I, L); the fullest
                 bucket (lowest index on ties) gives the pixel: alpha = sum A / n, colour =
                 sum (c A) / sum A
  pencil_sketch  tip t (1..20), colour range c: D = desaturate(invert(BrightnessContrast(
                 GaussianBlur(src, t), c, -c))); dst = ColorDodge(desaturate(src), D)
  ink_sketch     outline k (0..99), colouring c: Glow (radius 6, b = c = -(c - 50) * 2)
                 screened over the source, then a 5x5 edge kernel (centre 24, others -1) on
                 the premultiplied source darkens it: d = min(255, I (k + 5) >> 6)
```

- **`static FXTAB: [FxDesc; 3]`**
  the group table  [fxtab_artistic]
