### `fx/blur.rs`

Source: [`lib/fx/blur.rs`](../lib/fx/blur.rs)

```text
blur -- effect group "blur" (Weichzeichnen)                              [src/fx/fx_blur.s]

  FXTAB            the group table

Effects: bokeh_blur, fragment_blur, gaussian_blur, median, motion_blur, radial_blur,
sketch_blur, square_blur, surface_blur, unfocus, zoom_blur
  gaussian_blur  radius r (0..200): Gaussian with 12 sigma^2 = 2 r (r + 2), premultiplied,
                 edges clamped (conv::sep_run)
  square_blur    mean of the (2r+1)^2 square (box, running sums)
  unfocus        mean over the paint.net disc (conv::disc_run)
  median         per channel percentile of the disc (lochist; colour histograms skip alpha 0)
  motion_blur    N = (1 + d) * 3 / 2 bilinear samples (1/16 px) along the direction, mean of
                 the valid ones (interior pixels: collapsed taps, the same integer sum)
  zoom_blur      paint.net 3's zoom blur in 64-bit fixed point, ColorBgra.Blend
  radial_blur    paint.net 3's radial blur (64-bit), ColorBgra.Blend
  fragment_blur  paint.net 3's FragmentEffect
  surface_blur   histogram bilateral filter over the disc
  bokeh_blur     disc mean in linear light with boosted highlights (f32 sums)
  sketch_blur    three layers of stroke cells, 2R + 1 bilinear samples along each cell's
                 direction
```

- **`static FXTAB: [FxDesc; 11]`**
  the group table  [fxtab_blur]

### `fx/color.rs`

Source: [`lib/fx/color.rs`](../lib/fx/color.rs)

```text
color -- Farbe: Quantize                                                 [src/fx/fx_color.s]

  FXTAB            the group table

quantize: colors 2..256, algorithm octree | median_cut, dithering 0..8, alpha keep | binary.
prepare (single thread, polls the cancel flag per row) does everything over the ROI and keeps
the result image in aux; render copies rows (so tiles / threads cannot change the result).
  considered pixels (palette statistics): inside the ROI, selection coverage > 0, alpha > 0
    (keep) / alpha >= 128 (binary); row-major order
  palette: <= K distinct colours -> exactly those (first appearance); else
    octree  Gervautz-Purgathofer: 8 levels, child = bit (7-L) of R, G, B; after every pixel,
            while leaves > K the most recently created internal node of the deepest level
            with internal nodes absorbs its children; palette = leaves depth first
    median_cut  Heckbert on a 32^3 histogram: split the box with the most pixels (ties:
            first) along its longest cell extent after the first slice s with 2 * count(lo..s)
            >= count, boxes shrunk to their occupied cells; colour = round(sum / count)
  mapping: nearest palette colour (squared RGB distance, ties -> lowest index, through lazily
    built candidate lists per 16^3 cell), Floyd-Steinberg (7 / 3 / 5 / 1 in 16ths) when
    dithering L > 0: v = clamp(c + ((E L + 64) >> 7)); only "opaque" pixels take and pass
    on error
  alpha: keep -> unchanged; binary -> 255 when alpha >= 128, else 0
```

- **`static FXTAB: [FxDesc; 1]`**
  the group table  [fxtab_color]

### `fx/conv.rs`

Source: [`lib/fx/conv.rs`](../lib/fx/conv.rs)

```text
conv -- shared convolution helpers of the fx kernels                     [src/fx/fx_conv.s]

  exp(x) / ln(x) / pow(x, y)       f64 e^x (Cody-Waite + degree 12 Taylor), ln (2 atanh), x^y
  roi(a) -> Option<[i32; 4]>       the ROI clamped to the image (fxc_roi), None when empty
  copy_roi(a, dst) -> 0 | ERR_CANCEL      destination ROI = source ROI
  pint(a, slot, lo, hi) / pdbl(a, slot)   clamped i64 / f64 parameters (pdbl: NaN / inf -> 0,
                                   clamped to +-1e6)
  load_row(w16, src, y, x0, x1, mode)     working pixels (4 x i16 premultiplied, full 16320)
                                   of (x0 .. x1-1, y); outside: edge pixels or 0 (FXL_ZERO);
                                   FXL_GAMMA: linear light
  var12(r), Kern::gauss(var12), Kern::boxk(h)   separable kernels (exact FIR for sigma <= 6,
                                   else a cascade of 3 boxes)
  sep_run(sep, io) -> 0 | ERR_*    separable convolution of a ROI in strips: H pass of the
                                   rows (clamped / loaded), V pass, io.emit per row
  disc_run(disc, io) -> 0 | ERR_*  sums over the paint.net disc of every ROI pixel (prefix
                                   sums per row, i32 or f32 accumulation)
  disc_edges(r) -> (E, area)       half widths of the disc rows
  bac_init(b, c) -> (tab, mode), bac_row, desat_row   paint.net 3 Brightness / Contrast
  blurbac(a, dst, r, b, c, f)      Gaussian blur then Brightness / Contrast of the ROI;
                                   f(dst, y, x0, row) finishes each output row

Exactness: every sum is an integer (running box sums, FIR dot products), so the result of
a pixel never depends on the ROI split; box means are rounded through f32
(rint(f32(S) * f32(1 / w))), FIR sums as (S + 8192) >> 14 saturated to i16.
```

```rust
FXL_GAMMA: u32 = 1  // linear light (RS_GAMMA)
FXL_ZERO: u32 = 2  // outside the image = transparent (default: clamp to the edge)
KT_COPY: u32 = 0
KT_FIR: u32 = 1
KT_BOX: u32 = 2
KN_MAXTAPS: usize = 255
KN_MAXBOX: usize = 8
DSF_F32: u32 = 1  // disc: accumulate the per-row sums in f32
```

- **`fn exp(x: f64) -> f64`**
  e^x: k = rint(x / ln 2), r = x - k ln2 (Cody-Waite), degree 12 Taylor  [fxc_exp]

- **`fn ln(x: f64) -> f64`**
  ln x: x = m 2^e, ln m = 2 atanh((m - 1) / (m + 1)); x &lt;= 0 or denormal -> -745  [fxc_ln]

- **`fn pow(x: f64, y: f64) -> f64`**
  x^y = exp(y ln x) for x > 0, else 0  [fxc_pow]

- **`fn roi(a: &FxArgs) -> Option<[i32; 4]>`**
  the ROI clamped to the image (x0, y0 >= 0, x1 &lt;= w, y1 &lt;= h); None when empty  [fxc_roi]

- **`fn copy_roi(a: &FxArgs, dst: &mut Dst) -> i32`**
  destination ROI = source ROI  [fxc_copy_roi]

- **`fn dst_px<'d>(dst: &'d mut Dst, y: i32, x0: i32, n: i32) -> &'d mut [u8]`**
  the destination pixels x0 .. x0 + n of row y

- **`fn pint(a: &FxArgs, slot: usize, lo: i32, hi: i32) -> i32`**
  i64 parameter slot clamped to [lo, hi]  [fxc_pint]

- **`fn pdbl(a: &FxArgs, slot: usize) -> f64`**
  f64 parameter slot; NaN / infinite -> 0, clamped to -1e6 .. 1e6  [fxc_pdbl]

- **`fn load_row(dst: &mut [i16], src: &Surface, y: i32, x0: i32, x1: i32, mode: u32)`**
  w16 = working pixels of (x0 .. x1-1, y); outside columns / rows: the edge pixel (clamp)
  or 0 (mode & FXL_ZERO); mode & FXL_GAMMA: linear light  [fxc_load_row]

- **`fn var12(r: i32) -> i32`**
  paint.net-like blur radius -> 12 sigma^2 = 2 r (r + 2) (r &lt;= 0 -> 0)  [fxc_var12]

- **`struct Kern`**
  a separable 1-D kernel (KERN)
  - `typ: u32`
  - `halo: i32`
  - `n: i32` -- FIR taps (odd) / number of boxes
  - `w: [i16; 256]` -- FIR weights (+ a 0 pad tap)
  - `h: [i32; KN_MAXBOX]` -- box half widths

**`impl Kern`**

- **`fn copy() -> Kern`**
  the identity
- **`fn boxk(h: i32) -> Kern`**
  one box of width 2h + 1 (h &lt;= 0: identity)  [fxc_kern_box]
- **`fn gauss(var12: i32) -> Kern`**
  Gaussian with 12 sigma^2 = var12 (&lt;= 0: identity)  [fxc_kern_gauss]
  ```text
  FIR (var12 <= 432): h = ceil(sqrt(0.75 var12)) (1..18), g_i = exp(-6 i^2 / var12),
  sum = g_0 + 2 g_1 + ..., q_i = trunc(g_i (16384 / sum) + 0.5), the centre absorbs
  16384 - (q_0 + 2 sum q_i).  Boxes: wl = largest odd w with 3 w^2 <= var12 + 3,
  m = (2 num + den) / (2 den) (num = 3 wl^2 + 12 wl + 9 - var12, den = 4 wl + 4,
  m <= 3): m boxes of width wl, then 3 - m of wl + 2.
  ```

- **`struct Sep<'a>`**
  a separable run (SEP): source, job, kernels, the output ROI, loader mode
  - `src: &'a Surface`
  - `job: Option<&'a (dyn Cancel + Sync)>`
  - `kh: &'a Kern`
  - `kv: Option<&'a Kern>`
  - `x0: i32`
  - `y0: i32`
  - `x1: i32`
  - `y1: i32`
  - `mode: u32`
  - `custom_load: bool` -- io.load replaces fxc_load_row (rows are then not clamped to the image)

**`impl<'a> Sep<'a>`**

- **`fn new(a: &FxArgs<'a>, k: &'a Kern) -> Option<Sep<'a>>`**
  the standard run of an effect: source / job / ROI of a (None when the ROI is empty)
  [fxc_sep_init]

- **`trait SepIo`**
  the row source and sink of sep_run (SP_LOAD / SP_EMIT)
  - `fn load(&mut self, _sep: &Sep, _w16: &mut [i16], _y: i32, _x0: i32, _x1: i32)`
    a custom loader (when Sep::custom_load): w16 of (x0 .. x1-1, y)
  - `fn emit(&mut self, sep: &Sep, y: i32, row: &[i16])`
    output row y (columns sep.x0 .. sep.x1-1) as working pixels

- **`struct EmitArgb<'d, 'e>(pub &'d mut Dst<'e>)`**
  the standard emitter: working row -> straight ARGB in the destination  [fxc_emit_argb]

- **`fn sep_run(sep: &Sep, io: &mut impl SepIo) -> i32`**
  separable convolution of a ROI -> 0 | ERR_CANCEL  [fxc_sep_run]
  Strips of S rows: H pass for the rows sy0 - HY .. sy1 + HY (clamped rows computed once),
  then the V pass over a row table, then io.emit per row.

- **`fn disc_edges(r: i32) -> (Vec<i32>, i32)`**
  half widths of the disc rows E[0..r] (paint.net LocalHistogramEffect: largest u with
  u^2 + v^2 &lt;= ((2r+1)^2 + 2) / 4) and the area  [fxc_disc_edges]

- **`struct Disc<'a>`**
  a disc run (DSC)
  - `src: &'a Surface`
  - `job: Option<&'a (dyn Cancel + Sync)>`
  - `r: i32`
  - `flags: u32`
  - `x0: i32`
  - `y0: i32`
  - `x1: i32`
  - `y1: i32`
  - `mode: u32`
  - `area: i32` -- pixels in the disc (set by disc_run)
  - `custom_prep: bool` -- io.prep replaces the default row preparation

**`impl<'a> Disc<'a>`**

- **`fn new(a: &FxArgs<'a>, r: i32) -> Option<Disc<'a>>`**
  the standard run of an effect (None when the ROI is empty)  [fxc_disc_init]

- **`enum DiscRow<'r>`**
  the summed row of a disc run: i32 x 4 per pixel, or f32 x 4 with DSF_F32
  - `I(&'r mut [i32])`
  - `F(&'r mut [f32])`

- **`trait DiscIo`**
  the row source and sink of disc_run (DS_PREP / DS_EMIT)
  - `fn prep(&mut self, _d: &Disc, _v: &mut [i32], _y: i32, _x0: i32, _x1: i32)`
    custom preparation (Disc::custom_prep): i32 x 4 values of (x0 .. x1-1, y)
  - `fn emit(&mut self, d: &Disc, y: i32, row: DiscRow)`

- **`struct DiscArgb<'d, 'e>(pub &'d mut Dst<'e>)`**
  the standard emitter (i32 sums): W16 = rint(f32(sum) \* f32(1 / area)), then straight ARGB
  [fxc_disc_emit_argb]

- **`fn disc_run(d: &mut Disc, io: &mut impl DiscIo) -> i32`**
  disc sums of a ROI -> 0 | ERR_CANCEL  [fxc_disc_run]
  Per source row a prefix sum P (i32, wrapping) makes each disc row one difference
  P[x + e + 1] - P[x - e]; rows are clamped to the image; the rows are summed in i32 or (DSF_F32)
  in f32 in the order v = -r .. r.

- **`fn bac_init(b: i32, c: i32) -> ([i32; 256], u32)`**
  paint.net 3 BrightnessAndContrast as used inside Glow / Soften Portrait / Pencil / Ink:
  (tab, mode) -- mode 0 (div = 100) / 1: shift tables, mode 2 (contrast 100): threshold
  [fxc_bac_init]

- **`fn bac_row_inplace(row: &mut [u8], tab: &[i32; 256], mode: u32)`**
  apply bac_init's table to a straight ARGB row (dst may be src: pass the same slice twice
  through bac_row_inplace)  [fxc_bac_row]

- **`fn desat_row(dst: &mut [u8], src: &[u8])`**
  B = G = R = intensity, alpha kept  [fxc_desat_row]

- **`fn desat_inplace(row: &mut [u8])`**
  desat_row in place

- **`fn blurbac(a: &FxArgs, dst: &mut Dst, r: i32, b: i32, c: i32, mut f: impl FnMut(&mut Dst, i32, i32, &mut [u8])) -> i32`**
  the paint.net 3 chain "Gaussian blur (radius r) of the source, then Brightness /
  Contrast" for the ROI; f(dst, y, x0, row) receives the straight ARGB result of each row
  (columns x0 ..) and writes the destination itself -> 0 | ERR_\*  [fxc_blurbac]

- **`fn w16_of(p: u32) -> [i16; 4]`**
  working values of one straight pixel  (rs_to_work, no gamma)

### `fx/distort.rs`

Source: [`lib/fx/distort.rs`](../lib/fx/distort.rs)

```text
distort -- effect group "distort" (Verzerren): Bulge, Crystalize, Dents, Frosted Glass,
Morphology, Pixelate, Polar Inversion, Tile Reflection, Twist           [src/fx/fx_distort.s]

  FXTAB            the group table

The warp effects are small mapping functions for the warp framework (warp.rs); Pixelate and
Morphology have their own loops.  Every pixel depends only on (source, parameters, aux).
  bulge          a = amount / 100, R = default radius: |p| < R: p (1 - a (1 - |p| / R)^2)
  twist          R = size * default radius, k = sign(amount) amount^2 / 100: p rotated by
                 k (1 - |p| / R)^3
  polar_inversion  p lerp(1, R^2 / |p|^2, amount)
  tile_reflection  (s, t) = p rotated by -angle, s += k tan(s pi / size) (t likewise), back
  dents          p + d (-sin a, cos a), a = theta * 4 octaves of perlin noise
  frosted_glass  samples moved by r ((1 - s) w + s n): w random point of the unit disc, n two
                 perlin fields
  crystalize     Voronoi cells of a jittered grid: the colour of the source pixel under the
                 nearest site
  pixelate       blocks filled with their premultiplied average (exact integers)
  morphology     max (dilate) / min (erode) / open / close of the premultiplied channels over
                 a circle / square / diamond (exact window maxima by doubling)
```

- **`static FXTAB: [FxDesc; 9]`**
  the group table  [fxtab_distort]

### `fx/hist.rs`

Source: [`lib/fx/hist.rs`](../lib/fx/hist.rs)

```text
hist -- histogram helpers of the effect kernels (thread safe, explicit buffers)  [src/fx/fx_hist.s]

  hist_rgba(a, hist) -> count | ERR_CANCEL    u32[4][256] (B, G, R, A) of the ROI pixels with
       alpha != 0 and (with a selection) coverage != 0; polls the cancel flag once per row
  hist_percentile(h, total, fraction) -> j    smallest j with h[0] + .. + h[j] > total *
       fraction (f64 compare); 0 when there is none (paint.net HistogramRgb.GetPercentile)
  hist_mean(h) -> f64                         f32(sum j h[j]) / f32(sum h[j]) (paint.net GetMean)
  lochist(a, dst, rad, f) -> 0 | ERR_CANCEL   paint.net LocalHistogramEffect.RenderRect: for
       every ROI pixel the histograms (LocHist) of the source pixels (x+u, y+v) inside the
       image with u^2 + v^2 <= ((2 rad + 1)^2 + 2) / 4; dst = f(hist, area, srcpixel).
       Histograms start at the ROI's left edge of every row and slide to the right (with
       paint.net's edge loops), so the result does not depend on the ROI split.
```

```rust
FX_LOCHIST_MAX: i32 = 1024
```

- **`struct LocHist`**
  histograms B, G, R, A and their block sums (block k = bins 16k .. 16k + 15)
  - `h: [[u32; 256]; 4]`
  - `blk: [[u32; 16]; 4]`

- **`fn hist_rgba(a: &FxArgs, hist: &mut [[u32; 256]; 4]) -> i64`**
  histograms of the ROI (alpha > 0, selection > 0) -> pixels counted | ERR_CANCEL  [fx_hist_rgba]

- **`fn hist_percentile(h: &[u32; 256], total: i64, fraction: f64) -> u32`**
  smallest j with h[0] + .. + h[j] > total \* fraction, else 0  [fx_hist_percentile]

- **`fn hist_mean(h: &[u32; 256]) -> f64`**
  f32(sum j h[j]) / f32(sum h[j]) as f64, 0 when empty  [fx_hist_mean]

- **`fn lochist(a: &FxArgs, dst: &mut Dst, rad: i32, mut f: impl FnMut(&LocHist, u32, u32) -> u32) -> i32`**
  paint.net LocalHistogramEffect.RenderRect over the ROI -> 0 | ERR_CANCEL  [fx_lochist]

### `fx/lochist.rs`

Source: [`lib/fx/lochist.rs`](../lib/fx/lochist.rs)

```text
lochist -- sliding local histograms for median / percentile / oil / surface style effects
                                                                        [src/fx/fx_lochist.s]

  lh_run(a, dst, r, shape, alpha0, f)    for every ROI pixel (x, y): the histograms (Hist) of
                                   the window around it (LHS_DISC = paint.net disc of radius
                                   r, LHS_SQUARE = (2r+1)^2), then dst(x, y) = f(hist)
  lh_run_oil(a, dst, r, shape, levels, f)  the same with pixels bucketed by intensity (Oil)
  lh_percentile(hist, ch, target)  smallest v with count(<= v) >= max(target, 1) (255 when
                                   fewer pixels)
  lh_rank(hist, ch, v)             pixels with value < v
  lh_edges(r, shape) -> (E, area)  half widths of the window rows

Window: pixels outside the image are not counted.  alpha0 (LHF_ALPHA0) keeps pixels with
alpha 0 out of the colour histograms (ncol counts the rest; the alpha histogram sees every
pixel, nall).  Oil: bucket = FastScale(I, levels), alpha-0 pixels count as transparent black.
Traversal: the histogram of the first ROI column slides down one row per ROI row, is
copied, then slides right along the row; every result depends on the window content only.
```

```rust
LHS_DISC: u32 = 0
LHS_SQUARE: u32 = 1
```

- **`struct Hist`**
  the per-channel histograms of a window (HIST): fine counts B, G, R, A, coarse counts (bins
  v >> 4), pixel counts and the centre pixel
  - `fine: [[u32; 256]; 4]`
  - `coarse: [[u32; 16]; 4]`
  - `nall: u32`
  - `ncol: u32`
  - `src: u32`

- **`struct Oil`**
  the intensity buckets of a window (OIL): pixels per bucket, per bucket sum B\*A, G\*A, R\*A, A
  - `cnt: [u32; 256]`
  - `sum: [[u32; 4]; 256]`
  - `n: u32`
  - `src: u32`

- **`fn lh_edges(r: i32, shape: u32) -> (Vec<i32>, i32)`**
  half widths of the window rows E[0..r] and the area  [lh_edges]

- **`fn lh_percentile(h: &Hist, ch: usize, target: i32) -> u32`**
  smallest v with count(&lt;= v) >= max(target, 1) in channel ch, 255 when fewer  [lh_percentile]

- **`fn lh_rank(h: &Hist, ch: usize, v: i32) -> u32`**
  pixels with channel value &lt; v (v clamped to 0..256)  [lh_rank]

- **`fn lh_run(a: &FxArgs, dst: &mut Dst, r: i32, shape: u32, alpha0: bool, f: impl FnMut(&Hist, i32, i32) -> u32) -> i32`**
  histograms of every ROI pixel's window -> dst = f(hist, x, y)  [lh_run, LHK_HIST]

- **`fn lh_run_oil(a: &FxArgs, dst: &mut Dst, r: i32, shape: u32, levels: i32, f: impl FnMut(&Oil, i32, i32) -> u32) -> i32`**
  intensity buckets of every ROI pixel's window -> dst = f(oil, x, y)  [lh_run, LHK_OIL]

### `fx/lut.rs`

Source: [`lib/fx/lut.rs`](../lib/fx/lut.rs)

```text
lut -- shared helpers of the effect kernels: per-channel tables, intensity, sRGB, Levels
and spline tables, the x87 maths                                          [src/fx/fx_lut.s]

  lut_row(dst, src, lut)        per channel table lookup; lut = 4 x 256 bytes B, G, R, A
  lut_ident(lut)                four identity tables
  luma(argb) -> u32             paint.net intensity (7471 B + 38470 G + 19595 R) >> 16
  luma_row(dst8, src)           the intensity of every pixel as a byte
  pow / exp2 / log2 / cos / sin x87 maths (rt::x87; fx_pow, fx_exp2, fx_log2, fx_cos, fx_sin)
  srgb_dec(c) / srgb_enc(l)     sRGB 0..1 <-> linear 0..1 (IEC 61966-2-1, f64)
  lin_table(out)                f32[256]: srgb_dec(i / 255)
  srgb_table(out, n)            u8[n]: round(255 srgb_enc(i / (n - 1))), n >= 2
  levels_lut(lut, lvl)          paint.net UnaryPixelOps.Level as a 1024-byte lut
  spline_lut(lut, pts)          u8[256] from control points {x, y}: paint.net
                                SplineInterpolator (natural cubic spline), ClampToByte

Levels, channel i (B, G, R) and input j: v = f32(j) - in_lo; v < 0 -> out_lo; j >= in_hi
-> out_hi; else trunc(clamp(out_lo + (out_hi - out_lo) pow(f64(f32(v / f32(in_hi -
in_lo))), f64(gamma)), 0, 255)).  A channel with in_hi <= in_lo is the identity; gamma is
clamped to 0.1 .. 10 (NaN -> 1).
```

Re-exports: `rt::x87::{cos, exp2, log2, pow, sin}`

```rust
FX_SPLINE_MAX: usize = 64
```

- **`fn lut_row(dst: &mut [u8], src: &[u8], lut: &[u8; 1024])`**
  dst[i] = {lut[B], lut[256 + G], lut[512 + R], lut[768 + A]}  [fx_lut_row]

- **`fn lut_ident(lut: &mut [u8])`**
  four identity tables  [fx_lut_ident]

- **`fn luma(c: u32) -> u32`**
  (7471 B + 38470 G + 19595 R) >> 16  [fx_luma]

- **`fn luma_row(dst: &mut [u8], src: &[u8])`**
  the intensity of every pixel  [fx_luma_row]

- **`fn srgb_dec(c: f64) -> f64`**
  c &lt;= 0.04045 ? c / 12.92 : ((c + 0.055) / 1.055)^2.4  [fx_srgb_dec]

- **`fn srgb_enc(l: f64) -> f64`**
  l &lt;= 0.0031308 ? 12.92 l : 1.055 l^(1/2.4) - 0.055, clamped to 0..1  [fx_srgb_enc]

- **`fn lin_table(out: &mut [f32])`**
  f32[256] linear values of the sRGB bytes  [fx_lin_table]

- **`fn srgb_table(out: &mut [u8], n: usize)`**
  u8[n]: trunc(255 srgb_enc(i / (n - 1)) + 0.5)  [fx_srgb_table]

- **`struct Lvl`**
  the LVL record of levels_lut (channel order B, G, R)
  - `inlo: [u8; 4]`
  - `inhi: [u8; 4]`
  - `outlo: [u8; 4]`
  - `outhi: [u8; 4]`
  - `gamma: [f32; 3]`

- **`fn levels_lut(lut: &mut [u8; 1024], lvl: &Lvl)`**
  paint.net UnaryPixelOps.Level as a 1024-byte lut (alpha identity)  [fx_levels_lut]

- **`fn spline_lut(lut: &mut [u8], pts: &[u8], n: usize)`**
  u8[256] from up to FX_SPLINE_MAX control points {x, y} (pts = 2 n bytes, any order, a
  repeated x: the last wins); n = 0: identity, n = 1: constant  [fx_spline_lut]

### `fx/noise.rs`

Source: [`lib/fx/noise.rs`](../lib/fx/noise.rs)

```text
noise -- effect group "noise" (Rauschen): add_noise, reduce_noise         [src/fx/fx_noise.s]

  FXTAB            the group table

  add_noise     intensity (0..100), colour saturation (0..400), coverage (0..100), seed:
                paint.net 3's Add Noise with per-pixel hashes h_k = hash32(x, y, seed, k):
                changed when h_0 mod 100 < coverage; n_k = ((sum of the 4 bytes of h_k) -
                510) * 1774 >> 8 for R (1), G (2), B (3); i = (4899 r + 9618 g + 1867 b) >> 14,
                c = i + (((c - i) sat) >> 12), sat = saturation * 4096 / 100, dev = I^2 / 4,
                channel' = clamp(channel + ((c dev + 32768) >> 16)); alpha kept
  reduce_noise  radius (0..200), strength (0..1): per channel the median m of the disc,
                f = strength (1 - 0.75 I), v' = trunc(clamp(v + f (m - v), 0, 255))
```

- **`static FXTAB: [FxDesc; 2]`**
  the group table  [fxtab_noise]

```text
object -- effect group "object" (Objekt): drop_shadow                    [src/fx/fx_object.s]

  FXTAB            the group table

  drop_shadow  colour, angle (deg, 0 = right, 90 = down), distance d (0..100), blur radius r
               (0..100), opacity (0..100), shadow only: offset o = (rint(d cos a), rint(d sin a));
               shadow alpha S = Gaussian blur (Kern::gauss(2 r (r + 2)), W16) of the source
               alpha moved by o (transparent outside the image); shadow pixel = colour with
               alpha (S opacity colour.a + 816000) / 1632000; result = source over shadow
               (Normal blend), or the shadow alone
```

  the group table  [fxtab_object]

### `fx/perlin.rs`

Source: [`lib/fx/perlin.rs`](../lib/fx/perlin.rs)

```text
perlin -- deterministic 2-D gradient noise for the fx kernels (Clouds, Turbulence, Dents,
Frosted Glass)                                                            [src/fx/fx_perlin.s]

  perlin(x, y, seed, stream) -> f64        gradient noise, about -1 .. 1
  perlin_row(rec, acc)                     acc[j] += amp * noise(x_j, y) along a row

Improved Perlin noise (quintic fade 6t^5 - 15t^4 + 10t^3) on the integer lattice; the gradient
of lattice point (ix, iy) is G[h & 15], h = hash32(ix, iy, seed, stream), G_k = sqrt(2)
(cos, sin)(2 pi (k + 0.5) / 16).  g00 = G00.x fx + G00.y fy, g10 = G10.x (fx - 1) + G10.y fy,
g01 = G01.x fx + G01.y (fy - 1), g11 = G11.x (fx - 1) + G11.y (fy - 1); a = g00 + u (g10 -
g00), b = g01 + u (g11 - g01), noise = a + v (b - a), u / v = fade(fx / fy) computed as
((((f 6) - 15) f + 10) f) f f.  Coordinates are clamped to +-2^30; fixed operation order:
perlin and perlin_row give bit-identical values.
```

- **`fn perlin(x: f64, y: f64, seed: u32, stream: u32) -> f64`**
  gradient noise at (x, y)  [fxn_perlin]

- **`struct PerlinRow`**
  a row of noise samples (PERLIN_ROW): x_j = (x0 + j step) \* inv + ofsx
  - `n: usize`
  - `x0: i32`
  - `step: i32`
  - `abs: bool`
  - `seed: u32`
  - `stream: u32`
  - `inv: f64`
  - `ofsx: f64`
  - `y: f64`
  - `amp: f64`

- **`fn perlin_row(r: &PerlinRow, acc: &mut [f64])`**
  acc[j] += amp \* noise(x_j, y) (|noise| when abs); the lattice gradients are reused while
  consecutive x_j stay in one cell  [fxn_perlin_row]

```text
photo -- effect group "photo" (Foto): glow, red_eye_removal, sharpen, soften_portrait,
straighten, vignette                                                      [src/fx/fx_photo.s]

  FXTAB            the group table

  glow             paint.net 3's Glow: G = BrightnessContrast(GaussianBlur(src, r)), dst =
                   Screen(lhs = src, rhs = G)
  soften_portrait  G = BrightnessContrast(GaussianBlur(src, 3 softness), lighting,
                   -lighting / 2); L = warm grey of src; dst = Overlay(L, G), alpha = src
  sharpen          local median m of the disc (radius = amount): clamp((3 s - m) >> 1)
  red_eye_removal  paint.net 3's RedEyeRemove op on every ROI pixel
  straighten       rotation about the image centre (img_core::xform bicubic); zoom scales
                   by cos|a| + max(w / h, h / w) sin|a| (edges clamped), else transparent
                   corners
  vignette         smoothstep darkening towards a colour in linear light
```

### `fx/render.rs`

Source: [`lib/fx/render.rs`](../lib/fx/render.rs)

```text
render -- effect group "render" (Rendern): Clouds, Julia Fractal, Mandelbrot Fractal,
Turbulence                                                                [src/fx/fx_render.s]

  FXTAB            the group table

All four are per-pixel functions of (x, y, image size, parameters).
  clouds      paint.net 3.x algorithm, own lattice noise: dx = 2x - w, dy = 2y - h; octaves
              i = 0, 1, ... while i < 12, amp > 0.03 and div > 0 (amp = power^i, div = scale
              >> i): val += perlin(dx / div + ox_i, dy / div + oy_i, seed, i) amp;
              t = clamp((val + 1) / 2, 0, 1)
  turbulence  octaves i < detail while div > 0, amp = roughness^i; val = sum |perlin| amp;
              t = clamp(2 val / sum amp, 0, 1)
              Colour = round(c1 + (c2 - c1) t) per channel (f32), blended over the source
              with the blend mode (gfx::blendmode)
  julia_fractal / mandelbrot_fractal  paint.net 3.x algorithms, count samples per pixel,
              escape iteration per sample (the asm runs 6 / 12 packed lanes; every sample
              does the same IEEE operations), colour schemes classic / grayscale / palette /
              rainbow, channel sums divided by count (truncating)
```

- **`static FXTAB: [FxDesc; 4]`**
  the group table  [fxtab_render]

### `fx/stylize.rs`

Source: [`lib/fx/stylize.rs`](../lib/fx/stylize.rs)

```text
stylize -- Stilisieren: Edge Detect, Emboss, Outline, Relief           [src/fx/fx_stylize.s]

  FXTAB            the group table

Edge Detect, Emboss and Relief are paint.net 3.x ColorDifferenceEffect kernels: a 3 x 3
weight matrix rotated by the angle (r = angle * 2 * pi / 360, dr = pi / 4):
      cos(r + dr)  cos(r + 2dr)  cos(r + 3dr)
      cos(r)       CENTER        cos(r + 4dr)
      cos(r - dr)  cos(r - 2dr)  cos(r - 3dr)
Weights are quantised to wq = floor(w * 16384 + 0.5) (integer sums, bit-exact, tile
independent); taps outside the image count as 0.  Per channel v = trunc(sum wq * c / 16384)
(towards zero) + OFFSET, clamped to 0..255; alpha = the centre pixel's alpha.
  edge_detect  CENTER 0, OFFSET 128, channels B, G, R        (angle default 45)
  relief       CENTER 1, OFFSET 0, channels B, G, R          (angle default 45)
  emboss       CENTER 0, OFFSET 128, on the intensity, output B = G = R = v (angle default 0)
  outline      paint.net 3.x OutlineEffect (hist::lochist): per channel over the circle of
               radius `thickness`: skip empty bins, count up to area (100 - intensity) / 200
               -> c1, on up to area (100 + intensity) / 200 -> c2, c = 255 - (c2 - c1);
               alpha = a2 (the alpha scan skips on the BLUE histogram like paint.net)
```

- **`static FXTAB: [FxDesc; 4]`**
  the group table  [fxtab_stylize]

### `fx/warp.rs`

Source: [`lib/fx/warp.rs`](../lib/fx/warp.rs)

```text
warp -- warp framework of the distort effects: inverse mapping per destination pixel, n x n
supersampling, bilinear sampling in premultiplied space, edge behaviour; the f64 maths the
fx kernels share                                                          [src/fx/fx_warp.s]

  prepare(a) -> aux                bounds for centre / radius (the selection's bounding box)
  Warp::new(a)                     the WARP record of a render call (fxw_init)
  wr.center(x, y)                  centre from an FP_PAN pair (fxw_center)
  wr.run(a, dst, map) -> 0 | ERR_CANCEL      render the ROI through map (fxw_run)
  sincos(x) -> (sin, cos)          f64, |x| <= 1e9 (fdlibm kernels)        [fxm_sincos]
  log(x)                           f64 ln, x clamped to [DBL_MIN, DBL_MAX]   [fxm_log]

Coordinates: pixel (x, y) is the POINT (x, y) (pixel centres at integers); the image covers
[-0.5, w - 0.5) x [-0.5, h - 0.5).  For every destination pixel of the ROI run takes n x n
sample points p = (x - cx + ox_i, y - cy + oy_i) relative to the centre (paint.net's rotated
grid: oy_i = (i + 1) / (n*n + 1) - 0.5, ox_i = frac(n (i + 1) / (n*n + 1)) - 0.5; n = 1:
one sample at the centre) and calls map(wr, cur, pt) -> identity?  pt = the point relative to
the centre on entry, the SOURCE point on return (identity: the sample is the pixel's own
source pixel, exactly).  A source point is sampled bilinearly from the 2 x 2 pixels around it
with f32 weights, alpha-weighted (each tap contributes w (c a, a)); taps outside the image
follow the edge mode (CLAMP / WRAP / REFLECT per tap; PRIMARY / SECONDARY / TRANSPARENT /
ORIGINAL replace a sample outside the image area by that colour).  The pixel = sum of its
samples: a = round(S_a / (n n)), c = round(S_c / S_a); a = 0 -> 0.  Pixels farther than
rin + 0.75 from the centre (rin > 0) are copied unchanged.
```

```rust
FXW_CLAMP: i32 = 0  // clamp taps to the image
FXW_WRAP: i32 = 1  // tile the image
FXW_REFLECT: i32 = 2  // mirror (period 2n, edge pixels repeated)
FXW_PRIMARY: i32 = 3  // samples outside the image = col1
FXW_SECONDARY: i32 = 4  // samples outside the image = col2
FXW_TRANSPARENT: i32 = 5  // samples outside the image = transparent
FXW_ORIGINAL: i32 = 6  // samples outside the image = the destination's own pixel
FXW_NEDGE: i32 = 7
```

- **`struct Bounds(pub [i32; 4])`**
  the aux of prepare: the bounds x0, y0, x1, y1

- **`fn prepare(a: &FxArgs) -> Result<Option<Aux>, i32>`**
  the bounding box of the selection (clipped to the image) or the whole image  [fxw_prepare]

- **`struct Warp<'a>`**
  the WARP record of one render call
  - `src: &'a Surface`
  - `w: i32`
  - `h: i32`
  - `x0: i32`
  - `y0: i32`
  - `x1: i32`
  - `y1: i32`
  - `job: Option<&'a (dyn Cancel + Sync)>`
  - `q: i32` -- quality 1..5: n x n samples per pixel
  - `edge: i32`
  - `col1: u32`
  - `col2: u32`
  - `cx: f64`
  - `cy: f64`
  - `rin: f64` -- radius of influence (0 = map every pixel)
  - `rdef: f64` -- default radius = min(bounds w, h) / 2
  - `bx0: i32`
  - `by0: i32`
  - `bx1: i32`
  - `by1: i32`
  - `p: [f64; 16]` -- effect parameters (WR_P)
  - `seed: u32`

- **`struct Cur`**
  the pixel and sample a map function is called for (WR_CURX / CURY / CURS)
  - `x: i32`
  - `y: i32`
  - `s: i32`

**`impl<'a> Warp<'a>`**

- **`fn new(a: &FxArgs<'a>) -> Warp<'a>`**
  the record from the arguments: ROI clipped to the image, bounds (aux of prepare or the
  image), rdef, centre of the bounds, q = 1, edge CLAMP, rin = 0  [fxw_init]
- **`fn center(&mut self, x: f64, y: f64)`**
  centre from an FP_PAN value (-1 = left / top edge of the bounds, 1 = right / bottom;
  clamped to -2 .. 2): cx = bx0 - 0.5 + bw (1 + x) / 2  [fxw_center]
- **`fn run(&mut self, dst: &mut Dst, mut map: impl FnMut(&Warp, Cur, &mut [f64; 2]) -> bool) -> i32`**
  render the ROI through map -> 0 | ERR_CANCEL  [fxw_run]

- **`fn sincos(x: f64) -> (f64, f64)`**
  (sin x, cos x): k = rint(x 2/pi), Cody-Waite reduction, fdlibm kernels; x clamped to
  +-1e9 (NaN -> 0)  [fxm_sincos]

- **`fn log(x: f64) -> f64`**
  ln x (fdlibm e_log.c); x clamped to [DBL_MIN, DBL_MAX] (NaN, 0, negatives -> ln DBL_MIN)
  [fxm_log]

## plat

*the platform layer: process, files, threads, windows (Windows and Linux)* -- crate root [`lib/plat/lib.rs`](../lib/plat/lib.rs)

```text
plat -- the platform layer: one module per OS with the same functions  [src/plat]

  process: entry point (calls the app's jfgui_main), args(), arg_value(name), exit(code),
           write_stdout / write_stderr, time_us(), sleep_ms(ms), date()
  environment: env(name) (= getenv), setenv(name, Some / None), config_dir(), temp_dir(), cwd()
  files:   read_file(path), write_file(path, data) (native: exclusive sibling + rename),
           File::open(path, FO_*) / open_temp(dir) -> read_at / write_at / size / sync,
           file_exists(path) (0 / 1 file / 2 dir), mkdir (with parents), delete, rename,
           file_exists_checked(path) (Some(0) missing, None error), FileLock::acquire(path),
           dir_list(path, f(name, is_dir, size_lo)) -- checked against the app's
           permissions first (guarded.rs); a refused path behaves like a missing one
  policy:  the app's permissions (apps/NAME/PERMISSIONS): install, the fs / process /
           url / plugin / print checks, grants for the user's files      (policy.rs)
  threads: thread_spawn(stack, f) -> Thread (join), thread_yield(), Sem (post / wait),
           wake() (any thread: E_WAKE on the UI thread), wait_events(deadline, on_event)
           (headless), cpu_count(), mem_total()
  pool:    thr_init / thr_count / thr_shutdown / thr_self, parallel_for, Job   (pool.rs)
  sync:    Mutex, Condvar, RwLock, channel (futex / WaitOnAddress)          (sync.rs)
  rand:    fill_random, random_bytes, random_u64 (the OS CSPRNG)             (rand.rs)
  net:     TcpListener, TcpStream, UdpSocket, SocketAddr, resolve            (net.rs)
  fs:      stat / lstat / read_dir / links / set_times / free_space /
           write_file_private / local_zone_id                                (fs.rs)
  cpu:     cpu_features() (CPU_*), cpu_brand()                                 (cpu.rs)
  crash:   crash_hook(Some(f)): f(&CrashInfo) before exit code 3 -- hardware faults (VEH /
           signals, the stderr line of ASMLIB) and panics; crash_test() crashes on purpose
  windows: the window-indexed API of the assembly (win_create / win_show / win_present /
           wait ..., popups, clipboard, drops, pen, cursors; consts.rs, evq.rs, pwin0.rs)
  (plat-shared) print(job) + page layout / PDF writer (print.rs; win_print.rs,
           linux_print.rs), plugin libraries lib_open / lib_sym / lib_close / lib_seg and
           the crash guard guard_call / guard_run / guard_tag (plib.rs; win_lib.rs,
           linux_elfload.rs, linux_guard.rs), monitor_icc / monitor_key (icc.rs), the
           portal's file dialog / OpenURI / Print (linux_portal.rs)
  (jxl-module) the loadable JPEG XL writer module: jxlenc_set_path / jxlenc_ready /
           jxlenc_encode (modhost.rs; the records in modabi.rs)

The layer knows events, pixel rows, files and threads only -- no UI, no gfx.  One of the
four crates that may contain `unsafe` (rt, simd, plat, plugin); nothing unsafe leaves it.
```

### `plat` crate root

Re-exports: `rt::os::{exit, write_stderr, write_stdout}`

Re-exports: `rt::crash::{CK_ACCESS, CK_BUS, CK_DIVIDE, CK_ILLEGAL, CK_OTHER, CK_PANIC, CK_STACK, CrashHook, CrashInfo}`

- **`struct Event`**
  one platform event (the EV_\* record): type, button, modifiers, window index (0 = the
  main window), position (px; E_RESIZE: size), data
  - `typ: u8`
  - `btn: u8`
  - `mods: u8`
  - `win: u8`
  - `x: i32`
  - `y: i32`
  - `data: u32`

- **`struct Frame<'a>`**
  the pixels to present: rows of 32-bit B, G, R, x
  - `px: &'a [u8]`
  - `w: i32`
  - `h: i32`
  - `stride: i32`

*files*

```rust
FO_READ: u32 = 0  // existing file, read only
FO_WRITE: u32 = 1  // create or truncate, read + write
FO_RW: u32 = 2  // open or create (contents kept), read + write
```

- **`struct Date`**
  a date and time (date(): Windows local time, Linux UTC)
  - `year: u16`
  - `month: u16`
  - `day: u16`
  - `hour: u16`
  - `min: u16`
  - `sec: u16`
  - `ms: u16`
  - `wday: u16` -- 0 = Sunday

- **`fn arg_value(name: &str) -> Option<String>`**
  the argument after `name` on the command line (argument 0, the program, is skipped)  [arg_value]

- **`fn crash_hook(f: Option<CrashHook>)`**
  f(&CrashInfo) runs in the crash handler, after the stderr line, before exit code 3; also
  for panics (CK_PANIC)  [plat_crash_hook]

- **`extern "C" fn crash_test() -> !`**
  a deliberate crash for tests: `mov eax, 16` (5 bytes), then a 4-byte write to address 0x10
  -- the faulting instruction is at crash_test + 5

- **`extern "C" fn crash_test_caller() -> !`**
  (plat-win) crash_test called from a known place (call rel32): the return address
  crash_test_caller + 5 must be among CrashInfo::frames (plattest's call chain check)

- **`extern "C" fn crash_test() -> !`**
  Deliberate trap for browser runtime diagnostics.

- **`extern "C" fn crash_test_caller() -> !`**
  Deliberate trap through the compatibility caller API.

- **`extern "C" fn crash_test() -> !`**
  Deliberate termination on Android, where no hardware exception translator is installed.

- **`extern "C" fn crash_test_caller() -> !`**
  Compatibility caller for the Android diagnostic termination.

### `plat/process.rs`

Source: [`lib/plat/process.rs`](../lib/plat/process.rs)

```text
Native child processes for tools and application helpers; no assembly counterpart.
run_elevated / elevated: a program with administrator or root rights (a service install).
```

- **`struct Command<'a>`**
  Shell-free child invocation. Environment entries override inherited values.
  - `program: &'a str` -- Executable path, or a program found in PATH.
  - `args: &'a [&'a str]` -- Arguments excluding argv[0]; empty arguments are preserved.
  - `cwd: Option<&'a str>` -- Child working directory, or None to inherit.
  - `env: &'a [(&'a str, Option<&'a str>)]` -- Environment overrides; None removes a variable.

- **`fn run_elevated(program: &str, args: &[&str]) -> Result<i32, String>`**
  Run a program with administrator / root rights and wait for it -> its exit code.  Windows:
  the UAC prompt (ShellExecuteEx "runas", hidden window, no pipes); Linux: directly when this
  process is root, otherwise through pkexec (the desktop's password prompt).  Err when the
  user refused or no way exists (the text says what to do).

- **`fn elevated() -> bool`**
  Whether this process runs with administrator (Windows) / root (Linux) rights.

**`impl Command<'_>`**

- **`fn spawn(&self) -> Result<Child, u32>`**
  Start a hidden process with pipes. Dropping its handle terminates its tree.

### `plat/process_win.rs` (Windows)

Source: [`lib/plat/process_win.rs`](../lib/plat/process_win.rs)

```text
Windows child pipes and job ownership; no console window and only explicit handles inherited.
```

**`impl Child`**

- **`fn read_stdout(&mut self, bytes: &mut [u8]) -> Result<Option<usize>, u32>`**
  Read stdout without waiting; None means no data, Some(0) means EOF.
- **`fn read_stderr(&mut self, bytes: &mut [u8]) -> Result<Option<usize>, u32>`**
  Read stderr without waiting; None means no data, Some(0) means EOF.
- **`fn write_stdin(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<Option<usize>, u32>`**
  Write a small chunk to stdin. Call from an IO worker, not the UI thread.
- **`fn close_stdin(&mut self)`**
  Close stdin to request orderly helper shutdown.
- **`fn try_wait(&mut self) -> Result<Option<i32>, u32>`**
  Return the child exit status without blocking.
- **`fn terminate(&mut self)`**
  Terminate all processes in the owned job.

### `plat/process_linux.rs` (Linux)

Source: [`lib/plat/process_linux.rs`](../lib/plat/process_linux.rs)

```text
Linux child pipes, exec and owned process groups. All allocations precede fork.
```

- **`struct Child`**
  Native child with independent output pipes; drop terminates its process group.

**`impl Child`**

- **`fn read_stdout(&mut self, bytes: &mut [u8]) -> Result<Option<usize>, u32>`**
  Read stdout without waiting; None means no data, Some(0) means EOF.
- **`fn read_stderr(&mut self, bytes: &mut [u8]) -> Result<Option<usize>, u32>`**
  Read stderr without waiting; None means no data, Some(0) means EOF.
- **`fn write_stdin(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<Option<usize>, u32>`**
  Write available bytes; retry None or the unwritten suffix.
- **`fn close_stdin(&mut self)`**
  Close stdin to request orderly helper shutdown.
- **`fn try_wait(&mut self) -> Result<Option<i32>, u32>`**
  Poll exit status; signals map to 128 + signal.
- **`fn terminate(&mut self)`**
  Terminate the owned process group and reap its leader.

### `plat/sync.rs`

Source: [`lib/plat/sync.rs`](../lib/plat/sync.rs)

```text
sync -- blocking primitives for worker threads (servers, background jobs)

  Mutex<T> (lock / try_lock -> MutexGuard)     Condvar (wait / wait_timeout / notify_*)
  RwLock<T> (read / write; writers first)      channel(cap) -> (Sender<T>, Receiver<T>)

One primitive per OS: wait on a 32-bit word while it holds a value, wake one / all waiters
(Windows WaitOnAddress, Linux private futexes).  Mutex and Condvar are the futex algorithms
of Rust's std; RwLock and the channel are built on them.  A lock is not poisoned by a panic
(panics abort the process here).
Not here: rt::SpinLock (rt keeps spin locks for the allocator and for modules, where plat is
replaced).
```

- **`struct Mutex<T: ?Sized>`**
  a mutual-exclusion lock around a value

**`impl<T> Mutex<T>`**

- **`const fn new(v: T) -> Mutex<T>`**

**`impl<T: ?Sized> Mutex<T>`**

- **`fn try_lock(&self) -> Option<MutexGuard<'_, T>>`**
- **`fn get_mut(&mut self) -> &mut T`**
  the value without locking (the borrow proves exclusivity)

- **`struct MutexGuard<'a, T: ?Sized>`**
  the locked value; unlocks when dropped

- **`struct Condvar`**
  a condition variable for Mutex

**`impl Condvar`**

- **`const fn new() -> Condvar`**

  unlock, sleep until notified (or spuriously), lock again
- **`fn wait_timeout<'a, T: ?Sized>(&self, g: MutexGuard<'a, T>, ms: Option<u32>) -> (MutexGuard<'a, T>, bool)`**
  as wait, at most `ms` milliseconds (None: forever) -> (guard, timed out)
- **`fn wait_while<'a, T: ?Sized>(&self, mut g: MutexGuard<'a, T>, mut cond: impl FnMut(&mut T) -> bool) -> MutexGuard<'a, T>`**
  wait while `cond` holds (re-checked after every wake-up)
- **`fn notify_one(&self)`**
- **`fn notify_all(&self)`**

- **`struct RwWriteGuard<'a, T: ?Sized>`**

  a bounded multi-producer, single-consumer queue (cap >= 1)

  why recv_timeout returned nothing
  - `Timeout`

**`impl<T> Sender<T>`**

  queue a value, waiting while the queue is full -> Err(v) when the receiver is gone
- **`fn try_send(&self, v: T) -> Result<(), T>`**
  queue a value if there is room -> Err(v) when full or the receiver is gone

**`impl<T> Receiver<T>`**

  the next value, waiting for one -> None when the queue is empty and every sender is gone

  the next value, waiting at most `ms` milliseconds
- **`fn try_recv(&self) -> Option<T>`**
  a value if one is queued
- **`fn len(&self) -> usize`**
  values queued right now

### `plat/net.rs`

Source: [`lib/plat/net.rs`](../lib/plat/net.rs)

```text
net -- TCP, UDP and Unix domain sockets (IPv4 and IPv6) with timeouts

  IpAddr / SocketAddr: parse ("10.0.0.5:9443", "[::1]:80"), Display, loopback / unspecified
  TcpListener::bind(&addr) / accept() / accept_timeout(ms) / local_addr()
  TcpStream::connect(&addr, timeout_ms) / read / write / write_all / read_exact,
            set_read_timeout / set_write_timeout (ms, None = block), set_nodelay,
            shutdown_write, peer_addr / local_addr, wait_readable(ms)
  UdpSocket::bind / send_to / recv_from(buf, timeout_ms)
  UnixListener::bind(path) / accept() / accept_timeout(ms); connect_unix(path, timeout_ms)
            -> UnixStream (the stream calls of TcpStream; addresses and nodelay do not apply)
            Linux, and Windows 10 1803 and later (AF_UNIX)
  resolve(host, port): numeric addresses, "localhost"; Windows also the system resolver
            (GetAddrInfoW).  Linux names go through netio::dns (no libc here).
  NetError: the kinds callers act on, with the OS code

Timeouts are readiness waits (WSAPoll / poll) before each call, never SO_RCVTIMEO: after a
timed-out blocking receive Windows leaves the connection in an undefined state.  Sockets are
not inherited by child processes; sends never raise SIGPIPE.  Every method takes &self, so
one thread may read while another writes (Arc<TcpStream>).
```

### `plat/fs.rs`

Source: [`lib/plat/fs.rs`](../lib/plat/fs.rs)

```text
fs -- file system access for backup and restore

  stat(path) / lstat(path) -> Option<Meta>     kind, size, times (ns), mode / attributes,
                                                owner, device and file id, link count
  read_dir(path) -> Option<Vec<DirEntry>>      names and kinds ("." / ".." left out)
  read_link / symlink(target, link, dir) / hard_link(existing, new)
  set_times(path, atime_ns, mtime_ns)          on the link itself, never its target
  set_mode(path, mode) (Linux) / set_attrs(path, attrs) (Windows) / set_owner (Linux)
  free_space(path) -> Option<(free, total)>    sync_dir(dir) (Linux: fsync of the directory)
  write_file_private(path, data)               owner-only file (0600 / protected DACL),
                                               fsync, atomic rename, directory sync
  mkdir_private(path)                          owner-only directory (with parents)
  local_zone_id() -> "Europe/Berlin" | "win:W. Europe Standard Time"
  long_path(path) (Windows): the \\?\ form every call here uses

Windows: reparse points count as links only for symbolic links and junctions; other tags
(deduplication, cloud placeholders) are ordinary files whose data is read.  Files are
opened with backup semantics (directories, and backup privileges when the process holds
them).
```

  - `atime_ns: i64`
  - `ctime_ns: i64` -- Linux: status change; Windows: creation
  - `mode: u32` -- Linux: st_mode (type and permission bits); Windows: 0
  - `uid: u32`
  - `gid: u32`
  - `attrs: u32` -- Windows: FILE_ATTRIBUTE_\*; Linux: 0
  - `dev: u64` -- device (Windows: volume serial number) and file id (inode / file index)

### `plat/service.rs`

Source: [`lib/plat/service.rs`](../lib/plat/service.rs)

```text
service -- programs that run as a system service

  run_service(name, run) -> exit code
      Windows: started by the service control manager, run() runs while the service reports
      RUNNING; a stop or shutdown request sets stop_requested(), reports STOP_PENDING and gives
      run() 20 s before the process ends.  Started any other way (a console): run() directly.
      Linux: run() directly (systemd starts the program like any other; SIGTERM ends it, the
      programs keep their data crash-safe).
  stop_requested() -> bool
  install(&ServiceSpec) / uninstall(name) / start(name) / stop(name) / state(name)
      Windows: the service control manager (automatic start as LocalSystem, restart after a
      failure); Linux: a systemd unit in /etc/systemd/system and systemctl.  Both need
      administrator / root rights; the errors say so.
  unit_text(&ServiceSpec) -> the systemd unit install writes (Linux)
```

  whether the service manager asked the program to stop

- **`struct ServiceSpec<'a>`**

  - `display: &'a str`

  - `exe: &'a str` -- the program (absolute) and its arguments

### `plat/watch.rs`

Source: [`lib/plat/watch.rs`](../lib/plat/watch.rs)

```text
watch -- changes below folders, as they happen (continuous data protection)

  Watcher::new(&roots) -> Result<Watcher, String>   recursive; one thread watches
  watcher.take(wait_ms) -> Changes                   the paths changed since the last take
                                                     (waits up to wait_ms for the first);
                                                     overflow: events were lost, rescan
  dropping the watcher stops it

Windows: ReadDirectoryChangesW per root (subtree, overlapped, 64 KiB buffer; an empty
answer means the buffer overflowed).  Linux: inotify with a watch per folder; new folders
are added (and reported whole), a full queue or the watch limit reports an overflow.
Paths are absolute, in the form of the platform; the folder of a change is enough for a
caller that walks the tree again.
```

- **`struct Changes`**
  what changed since the last take
  - `paths: Vec<String>`
  - `overflow: bool` -- events were lost: everything below the roots may have changed

- **`struct Watcher`**

**`impl Watcher`**

- **`fn new(roots: &[String]) -> Result<Watcher, String>`**
- **`fn take(&self, wait_ms: u32) -> Changes`**
  the changes so far (waiting up to wait_ms when there are none yet)

```text
certs -- the operating system's trusted certificate authorities

  verify_tls_server(chain, host) -> Option<Result<(), String>>
       Windows: CryptoAPI builds and checks the chain (leaf first, DER) for a TLS server of
       that name, with the machine's and user's roots, enterprise policy and automatic root
       updates; Some(Err(text)) when it refuses.  Linux: None (netio checks against the
       bundle).
  root_bundle() -> Option<Vec<u8>>

       openSUSE, Alpine).  Windows: None.

Revocation is not checked (no network access during the handshake).
```

```text
rand -- the operating system's cryptographic random number generator

There is no fallback: when the system RNG fails the process stops (a key made from weak
```

```text
cpu -- CPU feature detection for SIMD dispatch                             [src/core/cpu.s]
```

### `plat/capabilities.rs`

Source: [`lib/plat/capabilities.rs`](../lib/plat/capabilities.rs)

```text
Platform service availability, shared by app commands, agent schemas and browser hosts.
```

```rust
/// Read files through the platform's file namespace.
CAP_FILE_READ: u64 = 1 << 0
/// Write files through the platform's file namespace.
CAP_FILE_WRITE: u64 = 1 << 1
/// Confirm durable storage, rather than a temporary in-memory write.
CAP_PERSISTENT_STORAGE: u64 = 1 << 2
/// Exchange plain UTF-8 clipboard text.
CAP_CLIPBOARD_TEXT: u64 = 1 << 3
/// Exchange native/RTF/HTML rich clipboard representations.
/// Browser hosts provide an instance-local clipboard, without OS rich-format access.
CAP_CLIPBOARD_RICH: u64 = 1 << 4
/// Exchange clipboard images.
CAP_CLIPBOARD_IMAGE: u64 = 1 << 5
/// Submit a print job through the host.
CAP_PRINT: u64 = 1 << 6
/// Discover fonts installed in the operating system.
CAP_SYSTEM_FONTS: u64 = 1 << 7
/// Load native shared libraries with the platform plugin ABI.
CAP_NATIVE_PLUGINS: u64 = 1 << 8
/// Connect through a user-local IPC transport after explicit activation.
CAP_LOCAL_IPC: u64 = 1 << 9
/// Display windows, including library windows inside a browser canvas.
CAP_WINDOWING: u64 = 1 << 10
/// Run parallel operating-system threads.
CAP_THREADS: u64 = 1 << 11
/// Install operating-system shortcuts or integrations.
CAP_SHELL_INTEGRATION: u64 = 1 << 12
```

- **`struct Capabilities`**
  Snapshot of services actually provided by the current host.
  - `available: u64` -- Bit set of the `CAP_*` services.

**`impl Capabilities`**

- **`const fn supports(self, required: u64) -> bool`**
  Whether every requested service is available.
- **`fn unavailable_reason(self, required: u64) -> Option<&'static str>`**
  A concrete explanation for the first missing service, or no limitation.

- **`fn capabilities() -> Capabilities`**
  Current native service set, less what the app's permissions leave out (policy.rs).
  Browser permissions are supplied by its host adapter.

### `plat/policy.rs`

Source: [`lib/plat/policy.rs`](../lib/plat/policy.rs)

```text
policy -- the app's permissions (apps/NAME/PERMISSIONS, docs/PERMISSIONS.md): parsed once at
start-up, then checked by the public file, process, shell, plugin and print functions

  install(text) -> Result<Key, String>   parse and activate a manifest (once per process);
                                         before that, and in apps without one, all is allowed
  active() -> bool
  fs_allowed(path, FS_READ | FS_WRITE) / process_allowed(program) / url_allowed(url) /
  plugin_allowed(path) / print_allowed() -> bool
                                         a refusal is recorded (denials) and printed once
                                         per target to stderr: "PERMDENY kind target"
  Key::grant(path, FS_*) / Key::grant_dir(dir, FS_*)   a file / folder the user handed over
                                         (file dialog, drop, command line); needs fs.user
  Key::trusted(f)                        f runs on this thread without the file checks (the
                                         library's own file dialog and test scripts)
  denials() -> Vec<Denial>               the last DENIALS_MAX refusals (audit)
  normalize(path) -> Option<String>      absolute, '/' separators, no "." / ".." / empty
                                         segments; Windows: upper-case drive letter, no
                                         device, stream or reserved names; Linux: no ".."
                                         at all (the kernel applies it after symbolic
                                         links); None = refused
  normalize_for(path, windows, cwd)      the same rules for either OS (tests, tools)
  font_dirs() -> [folder]                the system font folders in scan order (fontdb)
  cap_mask() -> CAP_* bits               what the policy leaves of capabilities()

Manifest: one rule per line, '#' starts a comment, a key may repeat:
  fs.read PATTERN..     read files below PATTERN
  fs.write PATTERN..    write (and read) files below PATTERN
  fs.user               files the user chooses (dialog, drop, command line): read + write
  process PATTERN|NAME..   programs Command::spawn / run_elevated may start (NAME: no '/')
  url SCHEME..|*        open_url schemes (https, mailto, ...)
  plugins PATTERN..     native libraries for lib_open
  print                 plat::print
A PATTERN is an absolute path (X:/.. and //server/share/.. count on Windows only, /.. on
Linux only), or starts with $CONFIG $TEMP $HOME $EXE $FONTS (a variable without a value
drops the pattern); '*' / '?' match inside one segment, a '**' segment any
number of segments ("dir/**" also matches dir itself); "**" alone matches every path.
Windows compares without case.  Always readable: the font folders, the exe folder and on
Linux /etc/resolv.conf and /etc/hosts (netio's resolver).

The checks are lexical: a symbolic link inside an allowed folder can point outside it.  On
Linux a path with a ".." segment is refused: "allowed/link/../x" would leave the folder.
```

```rust
/// read access ([`fs_allowed`], [`Key::grant`])
FS_READ: u32 = 1
/// write access; implies [`FS_READ`]
FS_WRITE: u32 = 2
/// refusals kept for [`denials`]
DENIALS_MAX: usize = 64
```

- **`struct Denial`**
  one refused access
  - `kind: &'static str` -- "fs.read", "fs.write", "process", "url", "plugins" or "print"
  - `target: String` -- the path, program, URL or "" (print)

- **`struct Key(())`**
  The right to widen the installed policy for the user's own files and to run trusted
  library code; only [`install`] creates one (the UI runtime keeps it to itself).

- **`fn install(text: &str) -> Result<Key, String>`**
  parse and activate a manifest; Err names the first bad line.  Only the first call of a
  process can succeed.

- **`fn active() -> bool`**
  whether a policy restricts this process

- **`fn fs_allowed(path: &str, mode: u32) -> bool`**
  whether path may be read (FS_READ) or written (FS_WRITE)

- **`fn process_allowed(program: &str) -> bool`**
  whether Command::spawn / run_elevated may start program (a path, or a name found in PATH)

- **`fn url_allowed(url: &str) -> bool`**
  whether open_url may open url (its scheme)

- **`fn plugin_allowed(path: &str) -> bool`**
  whether lib_open may load the native library at path

- **`fn print_allowed() -> bool`**
  whether plat::print may print

- **`fn denials() -> Vec<Denial>`**
  the last refusals, oldest first

- **`fn cap_mask() -> u64`**
  the CAP_\* bits the policy leaves (all without a policy)

**`impl Key`**

- **`fn grant(&self, path: &str, mode: u32) -> bool`**
  let the app read (FS_READ) or read and write (FS_WRITE) the file at path, which the
  user chose; false without fs.user or for a path normalize refuses
- **`fn grant_dir(&self, dir: &str, mode: u32) -> bool`**
  the same for a folder and everything below it

  on Linux also ':'), else the Windows / XDG folders

- **`fn normalize_for(path: &str, windows: bool, cwd: &str) -> Option<String>`**

### `plat/pool.rs`

Source: [`lib/plat/pool.rs`](../lib/plat/pool.rs)

```text
pool -- the worker thread pool: parallel loops and background jobs     [src/core/thread.s]

  thr_init(n) -> workers          start the pool once: n workers (0 = cpu_count), at most
                                  THR_MAX; `--threads N` on the command line wins.  Later
                                  calls return the count; every function below starts it
  thr_count() -> workers          thr_shutdown()   stop and join the workers (no job running)
  parallel_for(count, &f) -> Ok / Err(Cancelled)
       f(index, job) for 0..count on the workers AND the calling thread; returns when every
       item is done.  f may call job.cancel() to skip the remaining items.  Nested loops
       (from inside f) are fine.
  Job: a background job -- set(count, f), start() (false: still running), wait(),
       cancel(), done(), items_done().  When it finishes the pool calls wake() (E_WAKE).
  thr_self() -> slot              0..n-1 on worker k, n on any other thread: per-thread
                                  arrays of thr_count() + 1 entries

Design (as in ASMLIB): one queue of runnable jobs under a short spin lock (loops at the
head, background jobs at the tail).  A thread takes part in a job by counting itself in
`active` under the lock, then claims item indexes with fetch_add on `next`.  The thread
that sees every index handed out (or the cancel flag) unlinks the job; the last one to
leave finishes it: state DONE (from then on the owner may drop the record), wake() for
background jobs, and every thread sleeping in a job wait is woken.  Idle workers spin
briefly, then sleep on a semaphore; waiters spin, then sleep on a second one at most
WAIT_POLL_MS at a time (a semaphore post may be taken by another waiter).  Work depends
only on the item index, so results are the same for any number of threads.

The loop record lives on the caller's stack and holds a pointer to the borrowed closure:
parallel_for returns only after the last participant left, which is what makes handing
the borrow to the workers sound (the unsafe below).
```

```rust
THR_MAX: usize = 64
```

- **`struct Cancelled`**
  a loop or job ended through cancel()

- **`struct JobCtl`**
  a job as its item functions see it: cancel the rest, look at the progress

**`impl JobCtl`**

- **`fn cancel(&self)`**
  skip the items not started yet (callable from inside an item)  [thr_job_cancel]
- **`fn cancelled(&self) -> bool`**
  long items poll this
- **`fn count(&self) -> u64`**
- **`fn items_done(&self) -> u64`**
  items finished so far (progress = items_done / count)

- **`fn thr_init(n: u32) -> u32`**
  start the pool (once): the number of worker threads  [thr_init]

- **`fn thr_count() -> u32`**
  the number of worker threads (starts the pool on first use)  [thr_count]

- **`fn thr_shutdown()`**
  stop the workers once the queue is empty, join them; a later call starts a new pool

- **`fn parallel_for(count: usize, f: &(dyn Fn(usize, &JobCtl) + Sync)) -> Result<(), Cancelled>`**
  f(index, job) for every index in 0..count, on the workers and this thread  [thr_parallel_for]

- **`struct Job`**
  a background job: count items of f on the workers; wake() when it is finished

**`impl Job`**

- **`fn new() -> Job`**
- **`fn set(&mut self, count: u64, f: impl Fn(usize, &JobCtl) + Send + Sync + 'static) -> bool`**
  the work of the next start: count items of f (false while the job runs)
- **`fn set_count(&mut self, count: u64) -> bool`**
  another item count for the same function (false while the job runs)
- **`fn start(&self) -> bool`**
  run it in the background; false: it is still running  [thr_job_start]
- **`fn wait(&self) -> Result<(), Cancelled>`**
  block until the job is finished (this thread helps with items not taken yet)
- **`fn cancel(&self)`**
  skip the items not started yet; the job still finishes (wake)
- **`fn done(&self) -> bool`**
  finished (or never started)
- **`fn items_done(&self) -> u64`**
- **`fn count(&self) -> u64`**

- **`fn thr_self() -> usize`**
  the calling thread's slot: k on worker k, the worker count on any other thread  [thr_self]

### `plat/win_api.rs` (Windows)

Source: [`lib/plat/win_api.rs`](../lib/plat/win_api.rs)

```text
win_api -- the window-indexed platform API on Windows: windows (records), input, the event
wait, presentation, cursors                                     [src/plat/win/win_window.s]
(popups: win_popup.rs; clipboard, IME, drops: win_clip.rs; file dialog: win_filedlg.rs;
 pens: win_pen.rs; shell, theme, home, drives, spawn_self: win_shell.rs)

  win_create(title, w_dip, h_dip, flags, owner, x, y) -> index / -1
       owner PW_NOOWNER: a top-level window (WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW, preferably index 0); else
       a window owned by window `owner`: WS_POPUP | WS_CAPTION | WS_SYSMENU (+ WS_THICKFRAME
       with PWF_RESIZABLE), WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW for PWF_TOOL (no taskbar button, not in
       Alt+Tab), WS_EX_NOACTIVATE for PWF_NOACTIVATE.  Created hidden; x, y = client origin
       (PW_DEFAULT: system default / owner client origin + 32 px).  Without PWF_SYSFRAME the
       caption is removed (the library draws the frame; DWM keeps shadow and corners).
  win_destroy(w)                    win_show(w, PWS_HIDE / SHOW / SHOWNA)
  win_activate(w)                   win_enable(w, on)
  win_set_rect(w, x, y, cw, ch)     client origin (screen px) and size; PW_KEEP
  win_get_rect(w) -> [x, y, w, h]   client origin + client size (None: no window)
  win_work_area(w) -> [x, y, w, h]  work area of the window's monitor
  win_set_title(w, utf8)            win_state(w) -> WST_* (WST_ACTIVE: the app is active)
  win_cmd(w, WCMD_*)                win_begin_move(w)  win_begin_resize(w, edges)
  win_size(w) -> (w, h)             win_dpi(w) -> 96 = 100 %
  win_set_min_size(w, w_dip, h_dip) (also before the window exists)
  win_present(w, frame, rects)      rects x, y, w, h; None = all.  The window keeps a copy
                                    of the presented pixels: WM_PAINT repaints from it.
  win_set_cursor(w, shape)          CUR_* or a custom cursor CUR_USER0..CUR_TABLE-1
  cursor_create(px, w, h, hot_x, hot_y, dpi) -> CUR_USER0 + k / -1   a cursor from
                                    premultiplied ARGB pixels (rows of w * 4 bytes,
                                    1..CUR_MAX_PX; CreateIconIndirect)
  cursor_destroy(id)                cursor_dpi(dpi) -> the dpi to draw cursors for (the
                                    window's own on Windows)
  win_hit_hook(f)                   f(w, x, y) -> HB_* flags of the UI at a client point
                                    (WM_NCHITTEST, WM_MOUSEACTIVATE)
  wait(deadline_us) -> queued events   0 = poll, u64::MAX = forever, else plat::time_us()
  set_refresh(f)                    f() redraws during the modal move / size loop
  win_test_send(w, msg, wp, lp)     (test aid) SendMessageW to window w (input, hit test,
                                    activation, size-move messages: no pointers); more test
                                    aids in win_test.rs (win_test, file_dialog_autoclose)
(the single-window window_create / present / ... are in pwin0.rs)

Library frame (no PWF_SYSFRAME, AF_CUSTOM_TITLEBAR for the main window): the caption is
removed (WM_NCCALCSIZE) while the resize borders, shadow and rounded corners stay;
WM_NCHITTEST asks the UI hit boxes (hit hook): HB_DRAG moves the window, HB_MAX gives snap
layouts (main window), the caption buttons are drawn by the UI.
Every event carries its window (Event::win).  The window procedure never acts on input: it
only records events into the ring (evq.rs); it re-enters (SendMessage, SetWindowPos), so
the window of the events it pushes is saved and restored.
The owner keeps looking active while one of its own windows is active: WM_NCACTIVATE (FALSE)
is drawn active unless the whole app loses the activation (WM_ACTIVATEAPP).
Tool windows are not activated by clicks (WM_MOUSEACTIVATE -> MA_NOACTIVATE) unless the
click hits a text box: the keyboard stays with the main window.

State: everything lives on the UI thread (the thread that creates the windows runs their
window procedure); the records are Cells, so the re-entrant window procedure and the
callbacks (refresh, hit hook) never hold a reference across a Win32 call.
```

- **`fn win_create(title: &str, w_dip: i32, h_dip: i32, flags: u32, owner: i32, x: i32, y: i32) -> i32`**
  a hidden window (see the file header) -> index / -1  [plat_win_create]

- **`fn win_hit_hook(f: Option<fn(i32, i32, i32) -> u32>)`**
  f(w, x, y) -> HB_\* flags at a client point (None = no hit tests)

- **`fn set_refresh(f: Option<fn()>)`**
  f() redraws during the modal move / size loop

- **`fn wait_refresh(deadline_us: u64, refresh: &mut dyn FnMut()) -> u32`**
  wait() with a redraw closure for the modal move / size loop: refresh() runs (instead of the
  set_refresh hook) whenever the window must redraw before the loop returns  [main agent]

- **`fn wait(deadline_us: u64) -> u32`**
  pump the OS until an event is queued or the deadline (plat::time_us; 0 = poll without
  blocking, u64::MAX = forever) passes -> events queued (wake() makes it return at once)

- **`fn win_present(w: i32, f: &Frame, rects: Option<&[[i32; 4]]>)`**
  show the frame's dirty rectangles (x, y, w, h; None = the whole frame); the window keeps a
  copy of the pixels (WM_PAINT repaints from it)  [plat_win_present]

- **`fn win_destroy(w: i32)`**
  close the window (w = 0: ends the message loop)  [plat_win_destroy]

- **`fn win_show(w: i32, mode: u32)`**
  PWS_HIDE / PWS_SHOW (activates) / PWS_SHOWNA

- **`fn win_activate(w: i32)`**
  make the window the active one (keyboard focus)

- **`fn win_enable(w: i32, on: bool)`**
  a disabled window takes no input (modal dialogs disable the windows below them)

- **`fn win_set_rect(w: i32, x: i32, y: i32, cw: i32, ch: i32)`**
  client origin (screen px) and client size; PW_KEEP keeps a field

- **`fn win_get_rect(w: i32) -> Option<[i32; 4]>`**
  client origin (screen px) and client size (minimized: where it was); None: no window

- **`fn win_work_area(w: i32) -> Option<[i32; 4]>`**
  work area (without the taskbar) of the monitor the window is on: x, y, w, h (screen px)

- **`fn win_set_title(w: i32, title: &str)`**

- **`fn win_state(w: i32) -> u32`**
  WST_\* bits (WST_ACTIVE: one of the app's windows is active)

- **`fn win_cmd(w: i32, cmd: u32)`**
  WCMD_MINIMIZE / MAXIMIZE / RESTORE / CLOSE (asks, like the system menu does)

- **`fn win_begin_move(w: i32)`**
  let the system move the window (call on a button press)

- **`fn win_begin_resize(w: i32, edges: u32)`**
  let the system resize the window from an edge (EDGE_\* bits)

- **`fn win_size(w: i32) -> (i32, i32)`**
  client width, height in px ((0, 0) without a window)

- **`fn win_dpi(w: i32) -> i32`**
  96 = 100 %

- **`fn win_set_min_size(w: i32, w_dip: i32, h_dip: i32)`**
  minimum client size (also before the window exists)

- **`fn win_test_send(w: i32, msg: u32, wp: usize, lp: isize) -> isize`**
  (test aid) SendMessageW to window w -> its result (0 without the window or for a message
  that could carry a pointer)

- **`fn win_set_cursor(w: i32, shape: u32)`**
  CUR_\* shape while the pointer is over window w

- **`fn cursor_create(px: &[u8], w: i32, h: i32, hot_x: i32, hot_y: i32, _dpi: i32) -> i32`**
  a cursor from w x h premultiplied 0xAARRGGBB pixels (rows of w \* 4 bytes), hot spot in px:
  a 32-bit top-down DIB section with straight alpha + an empty AND mask -> CreateIconIndirect;
  Windows shows it at its size (dpi is not needed here) -> CUR_USER0 + k / -1

- **`fn cursor_destroy(id: i32)`**
  free a custom cursor; windows showing it fall back to the arrow (their shape is forgotten,
  so the next win_set_cursor applies even with the same id)

- **`fn cursor_dpi(dpi: i32) -> i32`**
  the dpi to draw a cursor for a window of this dpi (Windows: the window's own)

### `plat/win_clip.rs` (Windows)

Source: [`lib/plat/win_clip.rs`](../lib/plat/win_clip.rs)

```text
win_clip -- Windows clipboard, file drops (WM_DROPFILES) and the IME caret position
                                         [src/plat/win/win_clip.s; docs/modules/CLIPBOARD_DND.md]

  clip_set(fmt, data) -> false on failure
       CLIP_TEXT: UTF-8 text (converted to CF_UNICODETEXT, "\n" -> "\r\n")
       CLIP_PNG:  PNG file bytes (registered clipboard format "PNG", the one browsers and
                  Office exchange; no CF_DIBV5 rendering)
       CLIP_IMAGE (K1): data = a CLIPIMG record (clipimg_pack: PNG + BMP file): "PNG" +
                  CF_DIBV5 (the BMP's V5 header and pixels) + CF_DIB (BITMAPINFOHEADER,
                  32 bit BI_RGB)
  clip_get(fmt) -> bytes   text as UTF-8 with "\r\n" -> "\n"; CLIP_IMAGE (K1): "PNG", else
       "image/png", else CF_DIBV5, else CF_DIB as a BMP file (clip_dib_to_bmp: header checks,
       paint.net's alpha rule)
  clip_has(fmt)            the clipboard offers the format (no data is read; K1)
  win_drop_files(hdrop) -> (files, x, y)   (K1) WM_DROPFILES: the paths go to the pending
       drop list (drop_*); shell32 (DragQueryFileW, DragQueryPoint, DragFinish) is loaded on
       the first drop.  The windows get WS_EX_ACCEPTFILES.
  win_drop_selftest(hwnd)  (K1, test aid) jfgui_TEST_DROPFILES="path|path..." posts a
       WM_DROPFILES with a real HDROP (GlobalAlloc'd DROPFILES, wide paths, point 100, 100) to
       the new main window, so the whole shell32 path runs in a real window
  ime_pos(x, y, h)         place a hidden system caret at client px (x, y) with height h so
       IME composition / candidate windows open next to the text caret; h <= 0 removes it

The clipboard is opened for the main window; opening is retried for a moment while another
program holds it.  Every block of another program is checked (sizes, NUL, surrogates).
```

### `plat/win_pen.rs` (Windows)

Source: [`lib/plat/win_pen.rs`](../lib/plat/win_pen.rs)

```text
win_pen -- (PN1) pen input on Windows: WM_POINTER* of pen pointers (Windows 8+)
                                                   [src/plat/win/win_pen.s; PEN_INPUT.md]

  win_pointer(hwnd, msg, wparam, w) -> true handled (the window procedure returns 0), false
       = on to DefWindowProc.  Called by the window procedure (win_api.rs) for
       WM_POINTERUPDATE .. WM_POINTERCAPTURECHANGED with the event window = w.
  pen_devices() -> pens / pen tablets attached (GetPointerDevices; -1 = the API is missing)
  pen_api_set(Some(api))   (test aid) the pointer API through the given functions instead
       of user32's (plattest's fakes); None = back to user32 (loaded again on first use)

Only pointers of type PT_PEN are handled.  EnableMouseInPointer is never called, so the
mouse keeps sending WM_MOUSEMOVE / WM_*BUTTON*; touch and every pointer the API cannot
describe go on to DefWindowProc, which turns them into mouse messages as before.  A handled
pen message produces no mouse message.  With pen input off (pen_enable(false)) nothing is
handled: pens arrive as the system's mouse emulation.
  WM_POINTERUPDATE   every coalesced sample since the last message (GetPointerPenInfoHistory,
                     oldest first, at most PEN_HIST) -> E_MOVE with pen data
  WM_POINTERDOWN     tip contact -> E_DOWN BTN_LEFT, or BTN_RIGHT while the barrel button is
                     held (PEN_FLAG_BARREL / POINTER_FLAG_SECONDBUTTON), like paint.net
  WM_POINTERUP       E_UP of the button of the press
  WM_POINTERLEAVE    E_LEAVE (no button held)    WM_POINTERCAPTURECHANGED  E_UP if held
Pen data (evq_pen_data): pressure 0..1024 -> 1..PRESSURE_ONE (PEN_MASK_PRESSURE, else none),
PEN_FLAG_INVERTED / PEN_FLAG_ERASER -> PTR_ERASER, tilt x / y in degrees.  The API's results
are not trusted: counts are clamped, positions go through ScreenToClient.
```

```rust
PEN_HIST: usize = 16  // samples per WM_POINTERUPDATE
```

- **`struct PenInfo`**
  one sample of the pointer API: the POINTER_PEN_INFO fields the pen path reads
  - `flags: u32` -- POINTER_INFO.pointerFlags
  - `x: i32` -- ptPixelLocation (screen px)
  - `y: i32`
  - `pen_flags: u32` -- PEN_FLAG_\*
  - `pen_mask: u32` -- PEN_MASK_\*
  - `pressure: u32` -- 0..1024
  - `tilt_x: i32` -- -90..90 degrees
  - `tilt_y: i32`

- **`struct PenApi`**
  the pointer API (functions that are missing are None)
  - `ptype: Option<fn(u32) -> Option<u32>>` -- GetPointerType(id) -> the POINTER_INPUT_TYPE (None: failed)
  - `info: Option<fn(u32) -> Option<PenInfo>>` -- GetPointerPenInfo(id) -> the newest sample
  - `hist: Option<fn(u32, &mut u32, &mut [PenInfo]) -> bool>` -- GetPointerPenInfoHistory(id, &count, out): newest first, at most \*count (the room)
    records; \*count = the samples available -> false: failed
  - `devs: Option<fn(&mut u32, Option<&mut [u32]>) -> bool>` -- GetPointerDevices(&count, types): \*count = the devices; with Some(types) (\*count of
    them) their POINTER_DEVICE_TYPEs -> false: failed

- **`fn pen_api_set(api: Option<PenApi>)`**
  (test aid) the pointer API through `api` (None: user32's again, loaded on first use)

- **`fn pen_devices() -> i32`**
  pens / pen tablets attached, -1 = no pointer API  [plat_pen_devices]

### `plat/win_popup.rs` (Windows)

Source: [`lib/plat/win_popup.rs`](../lib/plat/win_popup.rs)

```text
win_popup -- OS popup windows on Windows (U3)                    [src/plat/win/win_popup.s]

Popups (menus, combo lists, tooltips) of a secondary UI window that do not fit into it are
shown in borderless popup windows owned by that window (WS_POPUP, WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW |
WS_EX_NOACTIVATE, class style CS_DROPSHADOW, a rounded window region).  They never take the
keyboard; their pointer input is pushed as events of the OWNER window in owner client
coordinates, so the UI hit-tests them like the rest of the window.

  popup(slot, owner, rect, frame)
       show / move / present popup slot `slot` (0 .. PPW_MAX-1): rect = x, y, w, h in px of
       the owner's client area (popup pixel 0, 0 = owner pixel x, y); the slot keeps a copy
       of the frame's pixels (WM_PAINT repaints from it).  rect None hides it.
  win_popup_wheel(hwnd, sx, sy, data) -> true if hwnd is one of the popups: the wheel event
       (screen point sx, sy) is pushed for its owner (win_api.rs: Windows sends the wheel to
       the focus window)
  win_popup_under(owner) -> true if the pointer is over a shown popup of owner (the owner's
       WM_MOUSELEAVE is then no leave)
```

- **`fn popup(slot: u32, owner: i32, rect: Option<[i32; 4]>, f: Option<&Frame>)`**
  show / move / present popup slot over window owner (rect None hides it)  [plat_popup]

```text
win_shell -- the shell and process level: open a folder / a URL, the system theme, the home
folder, the drives, start this program again
                      [src/plat/win/win_shell.s, win_files.s (home, drives), plat_win.s
                       (theme, spawn_self)]

  open_folder(path) -> 0 started, -1 failed (no such path, no shell32, error)
       path: UTF-8, '/' or '\' separators.  A folder opens in Explorer (ShellExecuteW "open"
       on the folder); a file is shown selected in its folder (ShellExecuteW "open"
       explorer.exe with /select,"path").  shell32 and ole32 are loaded at run time (COM
       initialised once, as for the file dialog).  jfgui_OPEN_DRYRUN=1: nothing is started;
       the call is printed to stdout and 0 returned (tests).
  open_url(url) -> 0 / -1   a web page (http / https / mailto ...) in the default
       application (ShellExecuteW "open" on the URL); the same dry run
  theme() -> 0 light, 1 dark, -1 unknown (HKCU ... Personalize\AppsUseLightTheme)
  home() -> %USERPROFILE% (or "C:/")       drives() -> "C:/", "D:/", ...
  spawn_self(args) -> 0 / -1   this executable again: "exe" "arg1" "arg2" ... (every
       argument quoted with the Microsoft rules), no inherited handles
```
