### `netio/server.rs`

Source: [`lib/netio/server.rs`](../lib/netio/server.rs)

```text
server -- a blocking HTTP/1.1 server: one accept thread, a pool of worker threads, each
worker serves one connection at a time (keep-alive, pipelined requests in order)

  Server::start(&addr, ServerConfig, handler) -> NetResult<Server>
  Server::start_with(.., Some(wrap))   wrap turns an accepted TcpStream into the Stream HTTP
                                       runs over (TLS) and the identity it proved
  server.local_addr() / server.stop()

The handler runs on a worker thread; it must not panic (panics abort the process).
Requests with both Content-Length and Transfer-Encoding are refused (request smuggling).
```

- **`struct ServerConfig`**
  - `threads: usize` -- worker threads (connections served at once)
  - `max_body: usize` -- the largest request body
  - `idle_ms: u32` -- how long an idle keep-alive connection stays open
  - `read_ms: u32` -- the longest wait for the next bytes of a request in progress
  - `write_ms: u32`
  - `max_requests: usize` -- requests per connection

- **`type Handler = Arc<dyn Fn(&Request) -> Response + Send + Sync>`**

- **`type Wrap = Arc<dyn Fn(TcpStream) -> Option<(Box<dyn Stream>, Option<String>)> + Send + Sync>`**
  turns an accepted connection into the stream HTTP runs over and the identity it proved
  (None: drop the connection, e.g. a failed TLS handshake)

- **`struct Server`**

**`impl Server`**

- **`fn start(addr: &SocketAddr, cfg: ServerConfig, handler: Handler) -> NetResult<Server>`**
- **`fn start_with(addr: &SocketAddr, cfg: ServerConfig, handler: Handler, wrap: Option<Wrap>) -> NetResult<Server>`**
- **`fn local_addr(&self) -> SocketAddr`**
- **`fn stop(self)`**
  stop accepting, finish the requests in progress, wait for the threads

### `netio/smtp.rs`

```text
smtp -- sending mail (RFC 5321) for notifications

  send(&SmtpConfig, &Mail) -> Result<String, String>   the server's final answer or the
                                                       reason it failed (German, for people)
  Security::StartTls (port 587) / Tls (465) / None (25; refuses to authenticate in clear)
  AUTH PLAIN or LOGIN (whatever the server offers; PLAIN first)
  message(&Mail, host, now) -> the RFC 5322 text: UTF-8 subject as an encoded word, the body
  text/plain (and text/html as multipart/alternative) in base64, Date and Message-ID

TLS checks the server like a browser (the system's certificate authorities, plus extra CAs
for a company's own); after STARTTLS nothing the server sent before the switch is used
(no command injection across the switch).
```

- **`enum Security`**
  - `StartTls`

**`impl Security`**

  - `helo: String` -- our name in EHLO
  - `extra_cas: Vec<Cert>` -- CAs trusted in addition to the system's
  - `timeout_ms: u32`

- **`struct Mail`**
  - `from: String`
  - `to: Vec<String>`

  - `text: String`

### `netio/ssh.rs`

Source: [`lib/netio/ssh.rs`](../lib/netio/ssh.rs)

```text
ssh -- SSH-2 for running commands on a machine (RFC 4253 / 4252 / 4254)

  Client::connect(stream, HostKeyCheck) -> Client      version, key exchange, host key check
  client.host_key()                                    type, blob, "SHA256:..." fingerprint
  client.auth_password(user, pw) / auth_key(user, &Keypair)
  client.exec(command, stdin, max_out) -> Exec { status, stdout, stderr }
  Transport::accept(stream, &host_key)                 the server side of the transport
                                                       (tests drive it with OpenSSH's client)
  private_key(text) -> Keypair      an unencrypted Ed25519 key ("OPENSSH PRIVATE KEY")
  public_key_line(&Keypair, comment) -> "ssh-ed25519 AAAA... comment" (authorized_keys)
  fingerprint(blob) -> "SHA256:..." the way ssh-keygen -l shows it

Algorithms: curve25519-sha256 (also @libssh.org) with OpenSSH's strict key exchange (against
the Terrapin prefix truncation), ssh-ed25519 host and user keys, chacha20-poly1305@openssh.com.
The host key is checked by the caller's rule: HostKeyCheck::Pinned(fingerprint) or Any (only
to learn the fingerprint).  Not here: RSA / ECDSA keys, compression, port forwarding, PTYs,
rekeying (a peer that starts a new key exchange ends the session; commands here are short).
```

```rust
MSG_DISCONNECT: u8 = 1
MSG_IGNORE: u8 = 2
MSG_UNIMPLEMENTED: u8 = 3
MSG_DEBUG: u8 = 4
MSG_SERVICE_REQUEST: u8 = 5
MSG_SERVICE_ACCEPT: u8 = 6
MSG_EXT_INFO: u8 = 7
MSG_KEXINIT: u8 = 20
MSG_NEWKEYS: u8 = 21
MSG_KEX_ECDH_INIT: u8 = 30
MSG_KEX_ECDH_REPLY: u8 = 31
MSG_USERAUTH_REQUEST: u8 = 50
MSG_USERAUTH_FAILURE: u8 = 51
MSG_USERAUTH_SUCCESS: u8 = 52
MSG_USERAUTH_BANNER: u8 = 53
MSG_USERAUTH_PK_OK: u8 = 60
MSG_GLOBAL_REQUEST: u8 = 80
MSG_REQUEST_FAILURE: u8 = 82
MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN: u8 = 90
MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN_CONFIRMATION: u8 = 91
MSG_CHANNEL_OPEN_FAILURE: u8 = 92
MSG_CHANNEL_WINDOW_ADJUST: u8 = 93
MSG_CHANNEL_DATA: u8 = 94
MSG_CHANNEL_EXTENDED_DATA: u8 = 95
MSG_CHANNEL_EOF: u8 = 96
MSG_CHANNEL_CLOSE: u8 = 97
MSG_CHANNEL_REQUEST: u8 = 98
MSG_CHANNEL_SUCCESS: u8 = 99
MSG_CHANNEL_FAILURE: u8 = 100
```

- **`enum SshError`**
  - `Net(NetError)`
  - `Protocol(String)` -- the peer broke the protocol or offers nothing we speak
  - `HostKey(String)` -- the host key is not the expected one (the fingerprint it showed)
  - `Auth` -- the user name / password / key was refused
  - `Disconnected(String)` -- the peer ended the session (its reason)

- **`struct W(pub Vec<u8>)`**
  an SSH message under construction

**`impl W`**

- **`fn msg(t: u8) -> W`**

- **`fn str(self, v: &str) -> W`**
- **`fn mpint(self, v: &[u8]) -> W`**
  an unsigned big-endian integer as mpint
- **`fn raw(mut self, v: &[u8]) -> W`**

- **`struct R<'a>`**
  reading an SSH message

**`impl<'a> R<'a>`**

- **`fn new(b: &'a [u8]) -> R<'a>`**
- **`fn u8(&mut self) -> Option<u8>`**
- **`fn u32(&mut self) -> Option<u32>`**
- **`fn bool(&mut self) -> Option<bool>`**
- **`fn bytes(&mut self) -> Option<&'a [u8]>`**
- **`fn str(&mut self) -> Option<&'a str>`**
- **`fn skip(&mut self, n: usize) -> Option<()>`**
- **`fn rest(&self) -> &'a [u8]`**

- **`fn fingerprint(blob: &[u8]) -> String`**
  "SHA256:&lt;base64 without padding>" of a public key blob

- **`fn public_key_line(k: &Keypair, comment: &str) -> String`**
  "ssh-ed25519 AAAA... comment"

- **`fn private_key(text: &str) -> Option<Keypair>`**
  an unencrypted Ed25519 key in OpenSSH's format ("-----BEGIN OPENSSH PRIVATE KEY-----")

- **`struct HostKey`**
  the host key a server showed
  - `key_type: String`

- **`enum HostKeyCheck`**
  - `Pinned(String)` -- exactly this key ("SHA256:...")
  - `Any` -- any key (to read its fingerprint for a decision)

- **`struct Transport`**

  - `session_id: Vec<u8>`

**`impl Transport`**

- **`fn send(&mut self, payload: &[u8]) -> Result<(), SshError>`**

- **`fn recv(&mut self) -> Result<Vec<u8>, SshError>`**

  ends the session, a new key exchange is not supported
- **`fn accept(s: Box<dyn Stream>, host: &Keypair) -> Result<Transport, SshError>`**
  the server side: version, key exchange with this host key
- **`fn disconnect(&mut self, reason: &str)`**

- **`struct Exec`**
  what a command did
  - `status: Option<u32>` -- the exit code (None: it ended by a signal or the server did not say)
  - `stdout: Vec<u8>`
  - `stderr: Vec<u8>`

- **`struct Client`**

**`impl Client`**

- **`fn connect(s: Box<dyn Stream>, check: HostKeyCheck) -> Result<Client, SshError>`**

- **`fn auth_key(&mut self, user: &str, key: &Keypair) -> Result<(), SshError>`**
- **`fn exec(&mut self, command: &str, stdin: &[u8], max_out: usize) -> Result<Exec, SshError>`**
  run a command, feed it `stdin`, collect at most `max_out` bytes of each output

### `netio/tls/mod.rs`

Source: [`lib/netio/tls/mod.rs`](../lib/netio/tls/mod.rs)

```text
tls -- TLS for BackupCraft's own servers and agents and for public servers (RFC 8446, 5246)

  Own deployments (both sides): TLS 1.3, TLS_CHACHA20_POLY1305_SHA256 · X25519 · Ed25519
  certificates (own CA) · mutual authentication · HelloRetryRequest · KeyUpdate.
  Public servers (client side, Trust::System): also TLS_AES_128_GCM_SHA256, RSA / ECDSA
  certificates of the system's CAs, and TLS 1.2 with ECDHE (X25519) and AEAD suites.
  Not here: PSK / resumption, 0-RTT, renegotiation, revocation checks.

  connect(stream, ClientConfig) -> TlsStream     accept(stream, ServerConfig) -> TlsStream
  TlsStream implements Stream (HTTP runs over it unchanged); peer_certs() after the handshake
  Trust::Anchors(CAs) / Pinned(SHA-256 of the leaf) / Any (trust on first use: the caller
  shows the fingerprint and pins it) / System(extra CAs) (the operating system's CAs)

conn.rs is the I/O-free core (feed bytes, take bytes); this file drives it over a Stream.
```

- **`struct Identity`**
  a certificate chain (DER, leaf first) and the leaf's private key
  - `chain: Vec<Vec<u8>>`
  - `key: Keypair`

- **`enum Trust`**
  - `Anchors(Vec<Cert>)` -- certificates issued by one of these CAs
  - `Pinned(Vec<[u8; 32]>)` -- exactly these leaf certificates (SHA-256 of the DER)
  - `Any` -- anything (only to read the fingerprint for a trust decision)
  - `System(Vec<Cert>)` -- the operating system's certificate authorities, and these as well (a company CA);
    allows TLS 1.2 and the public suites (crate::roots)

- **`struct ClientConfig`**
  - `trust: Trust`
  - `identity: Option<Identity>`
  - `server_name: String` -- the name the certificate must carry (DNS name or IP address; "" = not checked)

- **`struct ServerConfig`**
  - `identity: Identity`
  - `client_auth: Option<Trust>` -- ask for a client certificate trusted like this
  - `client_auth_optional: bool` -- a client without a certificate is accepted too (one that sends one must pass the
    check); for a port that consoles and agents share

- **`enum TlsError`**
  - `Alert(u8, &'static str)` -- we ended the connection with this alert
  - `PeerAlert(u8)` -- the peer ended it with this alert
  - `Net(NetError)`
  - `Closed`

- **`fn alert_name(a: u8) -> String`**

- **`struct TlsStream`**
  TLS over a stream

**`impl TlsStream`**

- **`fn peer_certs(&self) -> Vec<Cert>`**
  the peer's certificate chain (leaf first)
- **`fn last_error(&self) -> Option<TlsError>`**
- **`fn version(&self) -> (&'static str, u16)`**
  "TLS 1.3" / "TLS 1.2" and the cipher suite number

- **`fn connect(inner: Box<dyn Stream>, cfg: ClientConfig) -> Result<TlsStream, TlsError>`**
  client side: handshake over `inner`

- **`fn accept(inner: Box<dyn Stream>, cfg: ServerConfig) -> Result<TlsStream, TlsError>`**
  server side: handshake over `inner`

### `netio/tls/conn.rs`

Source: [`lib/netio/tls/conn.rs`](../lib/netio/tls/conn.rs)

```text
conn -- the TLS handshake and the record stream, without I/O

  Conn::client(cfg) / Conn::server(cfg)
  feed(bytes)          bytes from the peer (any split)
  take_out() -> bytes  to send to the peer
  read_plain / write_plain / close
  established(), peer_cert(), version(), suite()

Our own servers and agents (private PKI): TLS 1.3 with TLS_CHACHA20_POLY1305_SHA256, X25519,
Ed25519 signatures and certificates, a HelloRetryRequest when the client offered X25519
without a key share (or a cookie), mutual authentication.  The server side speaks only that.

A client that trusts the system's certificate authorities (Trust::System: mail servers,
update servers, web hooks) also offers TLS_AES_128_GCM_SHA256, RSA (PKCS #1 v1.5 / PSS) and
ECDSA (P-256 / P-384) signatures and the P-256 key exchange (after a HelloRetryRequest),
and falls back to TLS 1.2 with ECDHE (X25519 or P-256) and AEAD suites (AES-128-GCM,
ChaCha20-Poly1305), the extended master secret and the TLS 1.3 downgrade check.  Private
connections never fall back.

No PSK, no 0-RTT, no session tickets (a client ignores them), no renegotiation;
post-handshake KeyUpdate is supported.  Every protocol error ends the connection with the
matching alert.
```

```rust
AL_CLOSE_NOTIFY: u8 = 0
AL_UNEXPECTED_MESSAGE: u8 = 10
AL_BAD_RECORD_MAC: u8 = 20
AL_RECORD_OVERFLOW: u8 = 22
AL_HANDSHAKE_FAILURE: u8 = 40
AL_BAD_CERTIFICATE: u8 = 42
AL_UNSUPPORTED_CERTIFICATE: u8 = 43
AL_CERTIFICATE_EXPIRED: u8 = 45
AL_ILLEGAL_PARAMETER: u8 = 47
AL_UNKNOWN_CA: u8 = 48
AL_DECODE_ERROR: u8 = 50
AL_DECRYPT_ERROR: u8 = 51
AL_PROTOCOL_VERSION: u8 = 70
AL_INTERNAL_ERROR: u8 = 80
AL_NO_RENEGOTIATION: u8 = 100
AL_MISSING_EXTENSION: u8 = 109
AL_CERTIFICATE_REQUIRED: u8 = 116
```

```text
Derive-Secret, traffic keys and finished keys; the TLS 1.2 PRF with SHA-256 (RFC 5246 5)
```

### `netio/tls/record.rs`

Source: [`lib/netio/tls/record.rs`](../lib/netio/tls/record.rs)

```text
record -- the TLS record layer: TLS 1.3 (RFC 8446) and, for public servers, TLS 1.2 (RFC
5246 with AEAD suites only: RFC 5288 AES-GCM, RFC 7905 ChaCha20-Poly1305)

  plaintext record    type | 0x0303 | length | fragment          (handshake before keys,
                                                                   alerts, CCS)
  TLS 1.3 protected   23 | 0x0303 | length | AEAD(content ‖ type ‖ zero padding)
                      nonce = IV xor sequence number, additional data = the record header
  TLS 1.2 protected   type | 0x0303 | length | [explicit nonce (GCM)] AEAD(content)
                      additional data = sequence ‖ type ‖ 0x0303 ‖ content length;
                      GCM nonce = 4-byte salt ‖ explicit 8 bytes (we send the sequence),
                      ChaCha20 nonce = IV xor sequence number
```

```rust
CHANGE_CIPHER_SPEC: u8 = 20
ALERT: u8 = 21
HANDSHAKE: u8 = 22
APPLICATION_DATA: u8 = 23
/// the largest plaintext fragment
MAX_PLAIN: usize = 1 << 14
/// the largest record body on the wire (protected: plaintext + type + tag, some slack)
MAX_RECORD: usize = MAX_PLAIN + 256
/// the cipher suites (TLS 1.3, then TLS 1.2 ECDHE)
TLS_AES_128_GCM_SHA256: u16 = 0x1301
TLS_CHACHA20_POLY1305_SHA256: u16 = 0x1303
ECDHE_ECDSA_AES128_GCM: u16 = 0xC02B
ECDHE_RSA_AES128_GCM: u16 = 0xC02F
ECDHE_RSA_CHACHA20: u16 = 0xCCA8
ECDHE_ECDSA_CHACHA20: u16 = 0xCCA9
```

- **`fn key_iv_len(suite: u16) -> Option<(usize, usize)>`**
  key and IV lengths of a suite (TLS 1.2: the IV is the implicit part)

- **`struct Cipher`**
  one direction's keys

**`impl Cipher`**

- **`fn new(key: [u8; 32], iv: [u8; 12]) -> Cipher`**
  TLS 1.3 with ChaCha20-Poly1305
- **`fn for_suite(suite: u16, key: &[u8], iv: &[u8], v12: bool) -> Option<Cipher>`**
  the keys of a suite (key and IV of key_iv_len); None for an unknown suite
- **`fn seal(&mut self, ty: u8, content: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8>`**
  a protected record of `content` of type `ty`
- **`fn open(&mut self, hdr: &[u8; 5], body: &[u8]) -> Option<(u8, Vec<u8>)>`**
  open a protected record body (header given) -> (type, content)

- **`fn plain(ty: u8, content: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8>`**
  a plaintext record

### `netio/trust.rs`

Source: [`lib/netio/trust.rs`](../lib/netio/trust.rs)

```text
trust -- https to a server with its own private CA (Ed25519): the client either has the CA
(a PEM file), has pinned the CA's fingerprint, or learns it on the first connection and lets
the user decide (trust on first use, like SSH host keys)

  presented_ca(&chain, name, now)  the CA at the end of a server's chain that issued the
                                   server certificate, valid now and for `name`
  connector(ServerTrust, identity, seen)   for Client::with_connector; a CA that is unknown
                                   (Ask) or not the pinned one ends the handshake with
                                   HttpError::Tls(UNTRUSTED) and lands in `seen`
  fingerprint_text(&digest)        "AB:CD:..." for people to compare
  system_connector(extra_cas)      https to public servers: the system's CAs (and these),
                                   TLS 1.3 or 1.2 (mail servers, update servers, web hooks)
```

```rust
/// the text of HttpError::Tls when the server's CA needs the user's decision
UNTRUSTED: &str = "untrusted-ca"
```

### `netio/x509.rs`

Source: [`lib/netio/x509.rs`](../lib/netio/x509.rs)

```text
x509 -- certificates (RFC 5280): the private PKI of BackupCraft (Ed25519, RFC 8410; built
and signed here) and the public web PKI (RSA and P-256 / P-384 keys; RSA PKCS #1 v1.5 / PSS
and ECDSA signatures with SHA-256 / 384 / 512), parsed strictly

  Cert::parse(der) -> Option<Cert>        subject / issuer, validity, key, CA flag, names,
                                          key and extended key usage, name constraints,
                                          unknown critical extensions noticed
  cert.fingerprint() -> SHA-256 (hex)     cert.signed_by(&issuer) -> bool
  cert.verify(alg, data, sig)             a signature made with the certificate's key
  verify_chain(&[leaf, ...], &anchors, now, name, usage) -> Result<(), String>   builds a
                                          path through the given intermediates to an anchor
  CertBuilder { .. }.sign(&issuer_cert_or_self, &issuer_key) -> DER

Not checked: revocation (CRL / OCSP), certificate policies; name constraints cover DNS
names and IP addresses (the names a TLS server is reached by).
```

```rust
OID_ED25519: &[u64] = &[1, 3, 101, 112]
/// key usage bits as they appear in the first byte

KU_KEY_CERT_SIGN: u8 = 0x04

```

## cas

*content-addressed backup repositories: chunking, packs, signed encrypted snapshots, GC* -- crate root [`lib/cas/lib.rs`](../lib/cas/lib.rs)

```text
cas -- a content-addressed, deduplicating, encrypted chunk store (BackupCraft's repository
format "bcrepo-1")

  Repo::create(path, &CreateOptions) / Repo::open(path, Unlock::{Master, Passphrase,
       Recovery})                       one process holds a repository at a time (LOCK)
  repo.writer() -> Writer               put(kind, bytes) / put_stream(reader) -> chunk ids,
                                        finish() (packs and indexes are durable);
                                        .codec(c, level): this writer's compression
                                        (the repository's own is the default)
  repo.read_chunk(id) / read_tree(id)   repo.save_snapshot / load_snapshot / snapshot_ids /
                                        delete_snapshot (refused while immutable)
  repo.check(read_data, progress)       snapshots, signatures, references, every blob
  repo.gc(progress)                     mark & sweep, repack sparse packs
  repo.add_passphrase / set_recovery / set_immutable_days / trust_signer

Layout:  repo.json  data/<xx>/<pack>.pack  index/<pack>.idx  snapshots/<id>.snap  LOCK
Keys:    one random repository key, wrapped in repo.json for the server's master key, for
         passphrases (Argon2id) and for the recovery kit (sealed box); per purpose keys are
         derived from it with BLAKE3 derive_key (chunk ids, data, index, snapshots, gear).
Chunks:  id = BLAKE3-keyed(id key, plain) -> identical data is stored once, the ids reveal
         nothing without the key.  Commit order of a backup: packs (fsync) -> indexes
         (fsync) -> snapshot (atomic rename, directory sync); a crash leaves at most
         unreferenced packs, which gc removes.
```

### `cas` crate root

```rust
FORMAT: &str = "bcrepo-1"
```

- **`type ChunkId = [u8; 32]`**

```rust
KIND_DATA: u8 = 0
KIND_TREE: u8 = 1
```

- **`enum CasError`**
  - `Io(String)`
  - `Corrupt(String)` -- damaged or forged data
  - `Locked` -- none of the unlock methods fits
  - `InUse` -- another process uses the repository
  - `NotARepository`
  - `NotEmpty`
  - `Immutable(i64)` -- a snapshot that may not be deleted yet
  - `NotFound(String)`
  - `Cancelled`
  - `TooLarge`

- **`type CasResult<T> = Result<T, CasError>`**

- **`fn hex_id(id: &ChunkId) -> String`**

- **`fn parse_id(s: &str) -> Option<ChunkId>`**

- **`struct Keys`**
  the per-purpose keys of a repository
  - `id: [u8; 32]`
  - `data: [u8; 32]`
  - `index: [u8; 32]`
  - `snapshot: [u8; 32]`
  - `gear: [u8; 32]`

**`impl Keys`**

- **`fn chunk_id(&self, data: &[u8]) -> ChunkId`**

- **`enum Unlock<'a>`**
  how to open a repository
  - `Master(&'a [u8; 32])` -- the server's repository wrap key
  - `Passphrase(&'a str)`
  - `Recovery(&'a [u8; 32])` -- the recovery kit's X25519 secret

- **`struct CreateOptions`**
  - `id: String`
  - `chunk_avg: usize` -- average chunk size (a power of two; 1 MiB)
  - `compression: Codec`
  - `master: [u8; 32]` -- the server's wrap key
  - `recovery: Option<[u8; 32]>`
  - `signer: [u8; 32]` -- the public key whose snapshots this repository trusts
  - `immutable_days: u32`

- **`struct WriteStats`**
  statistics of a writer
  - `chunks: u64`
  - `raw: u64`
  - `new_chunks: u64`
  - `new_raw: u64`
  - `new_stored: u64` -- bytes added to pack files

- **`struct CheckReport`**
  - `snapshots: u64`
  - `bad_snapshots: Vec<String>`
  - `chunks_referenced: u64`
  - `missing: u64`
  - `damaged: u64`
  - `packs: u64`
  - `orphan_packs: u64` -- packs without an index (left by an interrupted write)
  - `bytes_read: u64`

**`impl CheckReport`**

- **`fn ok(&self) -> bool`**

- **`struct GcReport`**
  - `packs_deleted: u64`
  - `packs_repacked: u64`
  - `bytes_freed: u64`
  - `chunks_removed: u64`

- **`type Progress<'a> = &'a mut dyn FnMut(u64, u64) -> bool`**
  progress: (done, total) -> false to cancel

- **`struct Repo`**

**`impl Repo`**

- **`fn create(path: &str, o: &CreateOptions) -> CasResult<Repo>`**
  create a repository in an empty (or new) folder
- **`fn open(path: &str, unlock: Unlock) -> CasResult<Repo>`**
  open a repository
- **`fn path(&self) -> &str`**
- **`fn id(&self) -> String`**
- **`fn immutable_days(&self) -> u32`**
- **`fn chunker(&self) -> &Chunker`**
- **`fn keys(&self) -> &Keys`**
- **`fn chunk_count(&self) -> usize`**
  chunks in the index
- **`fn has_chunk(&self, id: &ChunkId) -> bool`**
- **`fn stored_bytes(&self) -> u64`**
  bytes of all pack files
- **`fn add_passphrase(&self, pass: &str, label: &str, repo_key_from: Unlock) -> CasResult<()>`**
  let a passphrase open the repository (Argon2id, 64 MiB)
- **`fn has_passphrase(&self, label: &str) -> bool`**
  whether a passphrase with this label opens the repository
- **`fn set_recovery(&self, pk: &[u8; 32], repo_key_from: Unlock) -> CasResult<()>`**
  seal the repository key for a (new) recovery kit
- **`fn set_immutable_days(&self, days: u32) -> CasResult<()>`**
  raise the immutability (never lowers it)
- **`fn trust_signer(&self, pk: &[u8; 32]) -> CasResult<()>`**
  trust snapshots signed by another key too (a restored server)
- **`fn writer(&self) -> Writer<'_>`**
  a writer for new chunks (backups run several at once; gc waits for them)
- **`fn read_chunk(&self, id: &ChunkId) -> CasResult<Vec<u8>>`**
  the plain content of a chunk
- **`fn read_tree(&self, id: &ChunkId) -> CasResult<Tree>`**
- **`fn save_snapshot(&self, snap: &Snapshot, signer: &ed25519::Keypair) -> CasResult<()>`**
  store a snapshot (after its writer finished); signed by `signer`
- **`fn snapshot_ids(&self) -> Vec<String>`**
- **`fn load_snapshot(&self, id: &str) -> CasResult<Snapshot>`**
  a snapshot, checked (signature by a trusted key, authentic encryption)
- **`fn delete_snapshot(&self, id: &str, now: i64) -> CasResult<()>`**
  delete a snapshot (its chunks go with the next gc) -- refused while it is immutable
- **`fn referenced(&self, snap: &Snapshot, out: &mut BTreeSet<ChunkId>) -> CasResult<()>`**
  every chunk a snapshot references (trees and data)
- **`fn check(&self, read_data: bool, progress: Progress) -> CasResult<CheckReport>`**
  check the repository: every snapshot opens and verifies, every referenced chunk is
  indexed; with `read_data` every blob is read, decrypted and compared with its id
- **`fn gc(&self, progress: Progress) -> CasResult<GcReport>`**
  remove what no snapshot references: empty packs are deleted, sparse packs (less than
  60 % in use) are rewritten with their live blobs
- **`fn repair_index(&self) -> CasResult<u64>`**
  rebuild missing index files from the packs' trailers

- **`fn peek_id(path: &str) -> CasResult<String>`**
  the id of a repository folder without opening it

- **`struct Writer<'a>`**
  writes new chunks into packs
  - `stats: WriteStats`

**`impl Writer<'_>`**

- **`fn codec(mut self, codec: Codec, level: i32) -> Self`**
  compress this writer's data chunks with `codec` (`level`: zstd's level); trees stay LZ4
- **`fn put(&mut self, kind: u8, data: &[u8]) -> CasResult<ChunkId>`**
  store a chunk (once) -> its id
- **`fn put_stream(&mut self, read: &mut dyn FnMut(&mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, String>, cancel: &dyn Fn() -> bool) -> CasResult<(Vec<ChunkId>, u64)>`**
  chunk a stream and store it -> (chunk ids, size); `read` fills the buffer, Ok(0) = end
- **`fn put_tree(&mut self, t: &Tree) -> CasResult<ChunkId>`**
- **`fn finish(mut self) -> CasResult<WriteStats>`**
  write the last pack; every chunk put so far is durable afterwards

### `cas/chunker.rs`

Source: [`lib/cas/chunker.rs`](../lib/cas/chunker.rs)

```text
chunker -- content-defined chunking (FastCDC with normalized chunking, level 2)

Boundaries depend on the content only, so an insertion early in a file moves one boundary
and leaves the later chunks (and their deduplication) intact.  The gear table is derived
from the repository key: chunk sizes do not reveal known files to someone who sees the
repository without its key.
```

- **`struct Chunker`**
  - `min: usize`
  - `avg: usize`
  - `max: usize`

**`impl Chunker`**

- **`fn new(gear_key: &[u8; 32], avg: usize) -> Chunker`**
  avg must be a power of two; min = avg / 4, max = avg \* 4
- **`fn cut(&self, data: &[u8], last: bool) -> Option<usize>`**
  the length of the first chunk of `data` (`last`: no more data follows)
- **`fn split(&self, data: &[u8]) -> Vec<usize>`**
  split a whole buffer into chunk lengths

### `cas/pack.rs`

Source: [`lib/cas/pack.rs`](../lib/cas/pack.rs)

```text
pack -- blobs, pack files and their indexes

  blob     kind:u8 codec:u8 | nonce:24 | XChaCha20-Poly1305(data key, nonce,
           aad = chunk id ‖ kind ‖ codec, payload)       payload: plain, an LZ4 block or a
                                                         zstd frame (the codec byte says which)
  pack     "BCK1" | blobs ... | sealed index (as below) | len:u32 | "BCKI"
           (the trailer repeats the index: a lost index file can be rebuilt from the pack)
  index    "BCI1" | nonce:24 | XChaCha20-Poly1305(index key, aad = pack id, JSON
           [[id hex, offset, length, kind, raw length], ...])

Pack files are written to a temporary name, synced, renamed; then their index.  A crash
leaves at most a pack without an index (garbage that check reports and gc removes).
```

```rust
PACK_MAGIC: &[u8; 4] = b"BCK1"
PACK_TRAILER: &[u8; 4] = b"BCKI"
INDEX_MAGIC: &[u8; 4] = b"BCI1"
/// a pack is closed when it reaches this size
PACK_TARGET: usize = 32 << 20
BLOB_OVERHEAD: usize = 2 + 24 + 16
```

- **`type PackId = [u8; 16]`**

- **`struct Loc`**
  where a chunk lives
  - `pack: PackId`
  - `off: u32`
  - `len: u32`
  - `kind: u8`
  - `raw: u32` -- the plain length

- **`fn seal_blob(keys: &Keys, id: &ChunkId, kind: u8, data: &[u8], codec: Codec, level: i32) -> Vec<u8>`**
  seal one chunk into a blob (compressed with `codec` when that saves something; `level`:
  zstd's level)

- **`fn open_blob(keys: &Keys, id: &ChunkId, blob: &[u8], raw_len: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>, CasError>`**
  open a blob: the plain chunk (checked against its id)

- **`fn seal_index(keys: &Keys, pack: &PackId, entries: &[(ChunkId, Loc)]) -> Vec<u8>`**
  an index (entries of one pack) as sealed bytes

- **`fn open_index(keys: &Keys, pack: &PackId, b: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<(ChunkId, Loc)>, CasError>`**
  the entries of a sealed index

- **`fn trailer_index(keys: &Keys, pack: &PackId, b: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<(ChunkId, Loc)>, CasError>`**
  the index stored in a pack's trailer

- **`struct PackBuf`**
  a pack being filled
  - `id: PackId`
  - `data: Vec<u8>`
  - `entries: Vec<(ChunkId, Loc)>`

**`impl PackBuf`**

- **`fn new() -> PackBuf`**
- **`fn push(&mut self, id: ChunkId, kind: u8, raw: usize, blob: &[u8]) -> Loc`**
- **`fn is_empty(&self) -> bool`**
- **`fn finish(&mut self, keys: &Keys) -> Vec<u8>`**
  the complete pack file (with its trailer)

- **`fn pack_hex(p: &PackId) -> String`**

- **`fn parse_pack(s: &str) -> Option<PackId>`**

### `cas/snapshot.rs`

Source: [`lib/cas/snapshot.rs`](../lib/cas/snapshot.rs)

```text
snapshot -- what a restore point holds: a tree of nodes per backed-up root, stored as
chunks (an unchanged directory produces the same tree chunk and is stored once), and the
snapshot record that names the roots, signed with Ed25519 and encrypted

  snapshot file  "BCN1" | nonce:24 | XChaCha20-Poly1305(snapshot key, aad = snapshot id,
                 signature:64 ‖ signer public key:32 ‖ JSON of the Snapshot)
```

```rust
SNAP_MAGIC: &[u8; 4] = b"BCN1"
```

- **`enum NodeKind`**
  - `File`
  - `Dir`
  - `Symlink`
  - `Other`

**`impl NodeKind`**

- **`fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str`**
- **`fn parse(s: &str) -> NodeKind`**

- **`struct Node`**
  one entry of a directory (or a root)
  - `name: String`
  - `kind: NodeKind`
  - `size: u64`
  - `mtime_ns: i64`
  - `atime_ns: i64`
  - `ctime_ns: i64`
  - `mode: u32`
  - `uid: u32`
  - `gid: u32`
  - `attrs: u32`
  - `ino: u64`
  - `dev: u64`
  - `link: String` -- symbolic links: the target as stored in the link
  - `chunks: Vec<ChunkId>` -- files: the content chunks in order
  - `tree: Option<ChunkId>` -- directories: the tree chunk of their entries
  - `error: String` -- a message when the entry could not be read completely (locked file, permission)
  - `count: u64` -- directories: the files below (with `size` = their bytes); 0 in older snapshots

**`impl Node`**

- **`fn to_json(&self) -> Value`**
- **`fn from_json(v: &Value) -> Option<Node>`**

- **`struct Tree`**
  the entries of a directory, sorted by name
  - `nodes: Vec<Node>`

**`impl Tree`**

- **`fn encode(&self) -> Vec<u8>`**
- **`fn decode(b: &[u8]) -> Option<Tree>`**
- **`fn find(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&Node>`**

- **`struct SnapStats`**
  totals of a snapshot
  - `files: u64`
  - `dirs: u64`
  - `bytes: u64` -- the plain size of all files
  - `new_bytes: u64` -- stored bytes added by this snapshot (after dedupe, compression, encryption)
  - `new_chunks: u64`
  - `new_raw: u64` -- plain bytes of the new chunks
  - `errors: u64` -- files that could not be read (completely)

- **`struct Snapshot`**
  a restore point
  - `id: String`
  - `created: i64`
  - `job_id: String`
  - `job_name: String`
  - `host: String`
  - `roots: Vec<Node>` -- one node per backed-up source; its name is the full source path
  - `parent: String`
  - `full: bool`
  - `stats: SnapStats`
  - `immutable_until: i64` -- not deletable before this time (seconds)
  - `meta: Value` -- the caller's extra data (session id, ...)

**`impl Snapshot`**

- **`fn to_json(&self) -> Value`**
- **`fn from_json(v: &Value) -> Option<Snapshot>`**

- **`fn seal(key: &[u8; 32], signer: &crypto::ed25519::Keypair, snap: &Snapshot) -> Result<Vec<u8>, CasError>`**
  seal a snapshot (signed by `signer`)

- **`fn open(key: &[u8; 32], id: &str, b: &[u8]) -> Result<(Snapshot, [u8; 32]), CasError>`**
  open a snapshot file: the snapshot and its signer (the signature is checked; whether the
  signer is trusted is the caller's decision)

## archive

*bounded ZIP32 containers (stored and DEFLATE)* -- crate root [`lib/archive/lib.rs`](../lib/archive/lib.rs)

```text
In-memory ZIP32 interchange. New native library code, independent of document/UI code.
Raw DEFLATE and CRC reuse the existing safe codec primitives; no paths are extracted.
```

### `archive` crate root

- **`struct Entry`**
  One decoded archive part with a normalized, relative UTF-8 name.
  - `name: String`
  - `data: Vec<u8>`

- **`struct Limits`**
  Bounds are checked before decompression or allocation of advertised sizes.
  - `entries: usize`
  - `part_bytes: usize`
  - `total_bytes: usize`
  - `archive_bytes: usize`

- **`enum Error`**
  Structural, integrity, feature or resource-limit failure.
  - `Invalid`
  - `Unsupported`
  - `Checksum`
  - `Limit`

- **`fn read(b: &[u8], limits: Limits) -> Result<Vec<Entry>, Error>`**
  Read stored/DEFLATE ZIP32 files. Reject encryption, multi-disk/ZIP64, ambiguous names,
  duplicate parts, overlapping local records, trailing payloads and CRC mismatches.

- **`fn write(entries: &[Entry], limits: Limits) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error>`**
  Deterministic stored ZIP32 writer (UTF-8 names, fixed DOS date 1980-01-01).

## doc_core

*structured documents, sources, citations and undo/redo* -- crate root [`lib/doc_core/lib.rs`](../lib/doc_core/lib.rs)

```text
Structured document and source model, with atomic edits and bounded undo/redo.
New native functionality, independent of UI, file formats and operating system.
```

### `doc_core` crate root

- **`type Id = u64`**
  Stable identity inside a document. IDs are never reused by an editing session.

- **`enum ParagraphChange`**
  A paragraph command applied atomically to a selection. Individual properties preserve
  each paragraph's other overrides; Format replaces the complete set of overrides.
  - `Style(ParagraphStyle)`
  - `Format(ParagraphFormat)`
  - `Properties(ParagraphFormat)`
  - `Tabs(TabStops)` -- Replace the effective tab list, preserving explicit suppression of inherited stops.
  - `Alignment(Alignment)`
  - `PageBreakBefore(bool)`

- **`enum ParagraphStyle`**
  Stable paragraph-style identity. Definitions and inheritance belong to the document.
  - `Normal`
  - `Title`
  - `Heading1`
  - `Heading2`
  - `Named(u32)`

- **`enum Inline`**
  Text or a structured citation; a citation remains linked to a source after edits.
  - `Text(String)`
  - `StyledText { text: String, format: TextFormat, }`
  - `Citation { source: Id, locator: String, }`
  - `CitationCluster(Vec<CitationItem>)`
  - `Formula(Formula)` -- Bounded live arithmetic, using the containing table for cell references.
  - `Bibliography`
  - `NoteReference(Id)` -- Stable note identity; a note has exactly one anchor, in the main body.

**`impl Inline`**

- **`fn text(&self) -> Option<&str>`**
  Editable text, excluding generated citation and bibliography fields.
- **`fn format(&self) -> TextFormat`**
  Character overrides; plain text and fields inherit the paragraph style.
- **`fn with_format(text: String, format: TextFormat) -> Self`**
  Canonical text node: inherited formatting uses the compact plain-text variant.

- **`struct Paragraph`**
  A paragraph with stable identity and structured inline content.
  - `id: Id`
  - `style: ParagraphStyle`
  - `format: ParagraphFormat`
  - `content: Vec<Inline>`

- **`struct Document`**
  Document data. Public for codecs; validate() is mandatory at trust boundaries.
  - `tables: Vec<Table>`
  - `table_styles: Vec<TableStyle>`
  - `styles: Vec<StyleDefinition>`
  - `paragraphs: Vec<Paragraph>`
  - `sources: Vec<Source>`
  - `notes: Vec<Note>`
  - `page: PageSettings`

- **`enum Error`**
  Invalid structure, dangling citation, source in use, or a resource budget exceeded.
  - `Invalid`
  - `Missing`
  - `InUse`
  - `Limit`

**`impl Document`**

- **`fn source(&self, id: Id) -> Option<&Source>`**
  Lookup by stable source identity.
- **`fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), Error>`**
  Validate identities, source references, XML-compatible text and conservative budgets.
- **`fn byte_size(&self) -> usize`**
  Approximate retained payload size for history budgets (including node overhead).
- **`fn citation_text(&self, source: Id, locator: &str) -> String`**
  Cached display text for a citation; this basic author/year rendering is not CSL/APA.
- **`fn paragraph_text(&self, p: &Paragraph) -> String`**
  Paragraph text including live citation labels.
- **`fn plain_text(&self) -> String`**
  Plain-text view used for counts and explicit text exports.
- **`fn bibliography(&self) -> Vec<String>`**
  Deterministic basic list of cited sources, including notes at their anchor position.

- **`enum Edit`**
  One atomic edit; invalid edits leave document, redo history and dirty state untouched.
  - `InsertFormula { paragraph:Id, inline:usize, offset:usize, formula:Formula }` -- Insert a live formula at a UTF-8 caret, retaining adjacent text and formatting.
  - `SetFormula { paragraph:Id, inline:usize, formula:Formula }` -- Replace a calculation field without touching its neighbours, as one undo step.
  - `InsertTable { paragraph: Id, offset: usize, rows: usize, columns: usize }`
  - `ChangeTable { table: Id, change: TableChange }`
  - `SetTableStyle { style: TableStyle, table: Option<Id> }`
  - `SetStyleDefinition(StyleDefinition)` -- Create or replace one definition; validation rejects cycles and dangling bases atomically.
  - `RemoveField { paragraph: Id, inline: usize, }` -- Remove one generated field by native position, including zero-width bibliography.
    Removing a note anchor removes its body in the same undo transaction.
  - `ReplaceStoryFragment { paragraph: Id, start: usize, end: usize, fragment: DocumentFragment, }` -- Replace a rich selection, including complete fields, in one atomic transaction.
    Imported identities are remapped and exactly identical sources are reused.
  - `FormatStoryParagraphs { paragraph: Id, start: usize, end: usize, change: ParagraphChange, }` -- Change all paragraphs touched by a story selection without changing their inline
    objects. An end caret at the beginning of the next paragraph excludes that paragraph.
  - `ReplaceStoryText { paragraph: Id, start: usize, end: usize, text: String, paragraph_breaks: bool, }` -- Replace a UTF-8 selection in one complete story (main body or one note). Newlines
    optionally create paragraphs; false keeps them as soft line breaks inside a run.
    Crossing generated fields is rejected atomically, including the paragraph structure.
  - `FormatStoryText { paragraph: Id, start: usize, end: usize, format: TextFormat, }` -- Apply character overrides over several paragraphs as a single history step.
  - `ReplaceStoryFormat { paragraph: Id, start: usize, end: usize, format: TextFormat, }` -- Replace all character overrides in a story range as one undoable edit.
    Default formatting clears overrides and restores paragraph-style inheritance.
    Text, paragraph styles and generated fields retain their original structure;
    selections intersecting generated fields are rejected before committing.
  - `ReplaceParagraphText { paragraph: Id, start: usize, end: usize, text: String, }` -- Replace a UTF-8 range across adjacent formatted runs; generated fields are protected.
  - `FormatParagraphText { paragraph: Id, start: usize, end: usize, format: TextFormat, }` -- Apply explicit character overrides across adjacent formatted runs as one transaction.
  - `SplitParagraphText { paragraph: Id, start: usize, end: usize, }` -- Split a selection across adjacent formatted runs into a new paragraph atomically.
  - `SplitParagraph { paragraph: Id, inline: usize, start: usize, end: usize, }` -- Replace a selection in one text run with a paragraph boundary. Formatting and all
    following fields/notes move intact; returns the new paragraph identity. A heading
    split at its end continues in Normal style. The new half never repeats a page break.
  - `JoinParagraphs { first: Id, second: Id, }` -- Join adjacent paragraphs in one story, retaining the first identity/style and every
    inline object. Matching character overrides at the seam coalesce into one text run.
  - `InsertNote { paragraph: Id, inline: usize, offset: usize, kind: NoteKind, text: String, }` -- Insert one note at a main-body UTF-8 text caret, preserving both text formatting and suffix.
  - `SetNoteKind { note: Id, kind: NoteKind, }` -- Convert a note without changing its identity, anchor or body.
  - `RemoveNote(Id)` -- Remove a note and its sole anchor in one undo transaction.
  - `FormatText { paragraph: Id, inline: usize, start: usize, end: usize, format: TextFormat, }` -- Format a UTF-8 range in one text run, preserving its prefix/suffix and field neighbors.
  - `SetParagraphFormat { paragraph: Id, format: ParagraphFormat, }`
  - `SetPage(PageSettings)`
  - `ReplaceMatch { found: TextMatch, expected: String, replacement: String, }` -- Replace a previously found range only if its text still matches the expected value.
  - `ReplaceAll { query: String, replacement: String, options: SearchOptions, }` -- One undo transaction for all matching text, excluding generated fields.
  - `SetText { paragraph: Id, inline: usize, text: String, }` -- Replace an editable run. An empty paragraph exposes virtual run zero; writing a
    nonempty value creates its first text run, while an empty value preserves the structure.
  - `SetStyle { paragraph: Id, style: ParagraphStyle, }`
  - `AddParagraph { after: Id, }`
  - `InsertBibliography { after: Id, }`
  - `RemoveParagraph(Id)`
  - `PutSource(Source)`
  - `ImportSources(Vec<Source>)` -- Import a complete list atomically, allocating IDs and skipping exact content duplicates.
  - `MergeSources(SourceMerge)` -- Merge two existing sources and redirect all citation occurrences in one transaction.
  - `RemoveSource(Id)`
  - `Cite { paragraph: Id, source: Id, locator: String, }`
  - `InsertCitation { paragraph: Id, inline: usize, offset: usize, items: Vec<CitationItem>, }` -- Insert a citation at a UTF-8 caret inside one editable run, preserving its formatting.
    The suffix is always retained as editable text, including insertion at the run end.
  - `UpdateCitation { paragraph: Id, inline: usize, items: Vec<CitationItem>, }` -- Replace an existing citation's items in a single undo transaction.
  - `ConvertCitationToText { paragraph: Id, inline: usize, }` -- Replace one citation field by its current display text, disconnecting its sources.
  - `RemoveCitation { paragraph: Id, inline: usize, }`

- **`struct Editor`**
  Editing session with a saved checkpoint and bounded snapshot history.

**`impl Editor`**

- **`fn new(doc: Document) -> Result<Self, Error>`**
  Create a validated editing session; loading starts with an empty history.
- **`fn document(&self) -> &Document`**
  Current immutable document.
- **`fn revision(&self) -> u64`**
  Changes on every successful content edit/undo/redo; suitable for a layout cache.
- **`fn state_id(&self) -> u64`**
  Identity of this history state. Unlike revision(), undo/redo restore this identity,
  allowing a view to restore transaction-associated caret positions without model data.
  Identities are local to one Editor session; no-op/failed edits do not change them.
- **`fn dirty(&self) -> bool`**
  Content differs from the last successfully saved checkpoint.
- **`fn mark_saved(&mut self)`**
  Call only after a successful durable write.
- **`fn can_undo(&self) -> bool`**
  Whether an undo step exists.
- **`fn can_redo(&self) -> bool`**
  Whether a redo step exists.
- **`fn undo(&mut self) -> bool`**
  Undo a complete model edit, including source/citation changes.
- **`fn redo(&mut self) -> bool`**
  Redo a complete model edit.
- **`fn apply(&mut self, edit: Edit) -> Result<Id, Error>`**
  Apply an edit; returns the created/affected identity. New sources use id=0.
- **`fn apply_checked(&mut self, edit:Edit,check:impl FnOnce(&Document)->Result<(),Error>)->Result<Id,Error>`**
  Validate an additional shared constraint before committing a transaction.
  A rejected edit leaves identities, history, saved state and revision untouched.

### `doc_core/citation.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_core/citation.rs`](../lib/doc_core/citation.rs)

```text
Structured multi-source citation fields, rendering and source-usage lookup.
New shared document functionality, independent of external citation processors.
```

- **`struct CitationItem`**
  One source reference in an ordered citation field. Affixes are kept verbatim.
  - `source: Id` -- Stable document-local source ID; zero is only an unbound draft value.
  - `locator: String` -- Page or other location, currently rendered with the basic style's page prefix.
  - `prefix: String` -- Literal text before this reference, including any desired whitespace.
  - `suffix: String` -- Literal text after this reference, including any desired whitespace.
  - `suppress_author: bool` -- Hide personal or corporate authors in this reference.
  - `suppress_year: bool` -- Hide the publication year in this reference.
  - `suppress_title: bool` -- Hide the title fallback when this source has no authors.

**`impl CitationItem`**

- **`fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), Error>`**
  Validate field-code-safe text and the source ID range; references require a document.

- **`struct CitationLocation`**
  Location of one citation field, even when that field cites a source more than once.
  - `paragraph: Id` -- Stable paragraph ID containing the field.
  - `inline: usize` -- Zero-based inline index of the field within its paragraph.

**`impl Inline`**

- **`fn citation(items: Vec<CitationItem>) -> Result<Self, Error>`**
  Create a bounded citation field, keeping simple single references in the legacy form.
  Source existence is checked when validating or applying the containing document.
- **`fn citation_items(&self) -> Option<Vec<CitationItem>>`**
  Owned edit draft for either citation representation; other inline nodes return None.

**`impl Document`**

- **`fn citation_cluster_text(&self, items: &[CitationItem]) -> String`**
  Basic author/year rendering of an ordered multi-source field; this is not CSL/APA.
  Prefixes/suffixes are literal. Author suppression hides people/corporations; title
  suppression hides the title fallback of anonymous sources. Locators use "S. ".
- **`fn cited_source_ids(&self) -> Vec<Id>`**
  Distinct cited source IDs in first-use order, scanning the document only once.
  Bibliography fields and unreferenced sources do not add IDs. Validate the document
  at trust boundaries; for valid documents at most 1,024 IDs are returned.
- **`fn source_uses(&self, source: Id) -> Vec<CitationLocation>`**
  Citation fields using a source, once per field in document order.

### `doc_core/format.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_core/format.rs`](../lib/doc_core/format.rs)

```text
Document formatting in typographic units; independent of pixels, GUI styles and codecs.
```

- **`enum TextColor`**
  Direct WordprocessingML text color; automatic remains distinct from inherited color.
  - `Auto`
  - `Rgb(u32)` -- Opaque RGB (0x000000..=0xFFFFFF), without an alpha channel.

- **`struct TextFormat`**
  Explicit character overrides. None inherits the paragraph style; false can disable bold headings.
  - `font_family: Option<FontFamily>`
  - `bold: Option<bool>`
  - `italic: Option<bool>`
  - `underline: Option<bool>`
  - `strike: Option<bool>`
  - `color: Option<TextColor>`
  - `size_half_points: Option<u16>`
  - `highlight: Option<Highlight>`
  - `positioning: Option<TextPositioning>`

**`impl TextFormat`**

- **`fn common(self, other: Self) -> Self`**
  Keep only equal, effective properties when summarizing a selection. A missing
  property in the result means mixed formatting, not an instruction to change text.
- **`fn explicit(self, style: ParagraphStyle) -> Self`**
  Capture the original built-in defaults. For a document's named or modified styles,
  use `Document::resolve_text(...).explicit()` to include the document's inheritance.
- **`fn overlay(self, direct: Self) -> Self`**
  Apply only properties present in `direct`, preserving all other overrides.
- **`fn validate(self) -> Result<(), Error>`**
  Supported size range: 4–144 points, in half-point increments.
- **`fn resolve(self, style: ParagraphStyle) -> ResolvedFormat`**
  Resolve the original built-in defaults. Use `Document::resolve_text` for style graphs.

- **`struct ResolvedFormat`**
  Fully resolved attributes consumed by layout and rendering.
  - `font_family: FontFamily`
  - `bold: bool`
  - `italic: bool`
  - `underline: bool`
  - `strike: bool`
  - `size_half_points: u16`
  - `color: u32`
  - `highlight: Highlight`
  - `positioning: TextPositioning`

**`impl ResolvedFormat`**

- **`fn explicit(self) -> TextFormat`**
  Capture all resolved properties as direct formatting for cross-style transfers.
- **`fn display_size_half_points(self) -> u16`**
  Shared typographic policy: scripts use two thirds of the nominal size, rounded to
  half points. The nominal size remains unchanged in the document and file formats.
- **`fn baseline_shift(self) -> i32`**
  Baseline displacement in twips, positive downwards. GUI zoom never enters this value.

- **`enum Alignment`**
  Horizontal alignment of a paragraph's lines.
  - `Left`
  - `Center`
  - `Right`
  - `Justify`

- **`struct ParagraphFormat`**
  Paragraph overrides. Distances are twips (1/20 point); line spacing is in 1/240 lines.
  - `explicit: u16` -- Explicit default-valued overrides: alignment, before, after, line, left, right,
    first-line, page break, tabs (bits 0..8). Non-default values are always overrides.
  - `alignment: Alignment`
  - `before: u16`
  - `after: u16`
  - `line: u16`
  - `left: u16`
  - `right: u16`
  - `first_line: i32` -- Additional first-line indent in twips; negative values create a hanging indent.
    Applies once per paragraph, including paragraphs that continue on another page.
  - `page_break_before: bool`
  - `tabs: crate::TabStops` -- Custom tab declarations, merged by position through the style hierarchy (bit 8).

**`impl ParagraphFormat`**

- **`fn select(self, mask: u16) -> Self`**
  Retain only selected properties as an explicit patch (bits follow `explicit`).
- **`fn overlay(self, patch: Self) -> Self`**
  Merge a property patch without making untouched inherited values explicit.
- **`fn mask(self) -> u16`**
  The properties this value overrides. This retains compatibility with existing
  callers that construct non-default properties with a struct update.
- **`fn over(self, mut base: Self) -> Self`**
  Apply this set of overrides to an inherited paragraph format.
- **`fn validate(self, page: PageSettings) -> Result<(), Error>`**
  Validate resolved spacing and ensure that the paragraph retains a usable text area.

- **`struct PageSettings`**
  One document-wide page setup in twips. A4 portrait with 25 mm margins by default.
  - `width: u32`
  - `height: u32`
  - `top: u32`
  - `right: u32`
  - `bottom: u32`
  - `left: u32`

**`impl PageSettings`**

- **`fn content_width(self) -> u32`**
  Remaining line width after page margins; saturating for unvalidated public models.
- **`fn content_height(self) -> u32`**
  Remaining text height after page margins.
- **`fn validate(self) -> Result<(), Error>`**
  Keep arithmetic bounded and at least two inches available for text in each direction.

### `doc_core/tabs.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_core/tabs.rs`](../lib/doc_core/tabs.rs)

- **`enum TabAlignment`**
  Tab alignment. `Clear` removes an inherited stop at the same position.
  - `Left` -- Tab alignment. `Clear` removes an inherited stop at the same position.
  - `Center` -- Tab alignment. `Clear` removes an inherited stop at the same position.
  - `Right` -- Tab alignment. `Clear` removes an inherited stop at the same position.
  - `Decimal` -- Tab alignment. `Clear` removes an inherited stop at the same position.
  - `Bar` -- Tab alignment. `Clear` removes an inherited stop at the same position.
  - `Clear` -- Tab alignment. `Clear` removes an inherited stop at the same position.

- **`enum TabLeader`**
  Repeated visual fill between the cursor and a tab target (never document text).
  - `None` -- Repeated visual fill between the cursor and a tab target (never document text).

### `doc_core/fragment.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_core/fragment.rs`](../lib/doc_core/fragment.rs)

```text
Structured clipboard ranges, independent of platform clipboard ownership and file codecs.
```

- **`struct DocumentFragment`**
  A self-contained rich selection. Paragraph boundaries distinguish hard paragraph marks
  from soft line breaks; sources and anchored notes travel with their native fields.
  The first and last paragraph can be partial. IDs are local to this fragment and are
  remapped when inserted. Public codec data must pass document.validate() before use.
  - `document: Document`

**`impl Document`**

- **`fn copy_story_range( &self, paragraph: Id, start: usize, end: usize, ) -> Result<DocumentFragment, Error>`**
  Copy a story range with native formatting and complete fields. Selecting only part
  of a generated field fails; no display text is silently substituted for its identity.

**`impl Editor`**

- **`fn remove_field(&mut self, paragraph: Id, inline: usize) -> Result<(Id, usize), Error>`**
  Remove a native field and return its surviving insertion caret. Citation and note
  removal can change bibliography or marker lengths before and after that caret;
  the returned paragraph-local UTF-8 offset is measured in the updated document.
- **`fn replace_story_fragment( &mut self, paragraph: Id, start: usize, end: usize, fragment: DocumentFragment, ) -> Result<(Id, usize), Error>`**
  Insert a rich fragment as one undo transaction and return its trailing caret as
  (paragraph identity, paragraph-local UTF-8 offset). Live field lengths are measured
  after insertion, so source reuse and note renumbering cannot misplace the caret.
- **`fn replace_story_fragment_checked( &mut self, paragraph:Id, start:usize, end:usize, fragment:DocumentFragment, check:impl FnOnce(&Document)->Result<(),Error>, )->Result<(Id,usize),Error>`**
  Insert a fragment with an additional pre-commit constraint, preserving its exact caret.
  Rejection leaves the document, identity allocation and undo/redo history unchanged.

- **`struct ParagraphText`**
  A lossless editing projection. Field text is displayed but never converted into plain runs.
  - `paragraph: Id`
  - `text: String`
  - `spans: Vec<TextSpan>`

**`impl ParagraphText`**

- **`fn position(&self, offset: usize) -> Result<TextPosition, Error>`**
  Resolve a caret to a text run, preferring the preceding editable run at a seam.
- **`fn offset(&self, inline: usize, offset: usize) -> Option<usize>`**
  Map a native inline caret back to paragraph coordinates.
- **`fn selection(&self, start: usize, end: usize) -> Result<TextMatch, Error>`**
  Resolve a selection across adjacent text runs; field intersections are rejected.

**`impl Document`**

- **`fn text_projection(&self, id: Id) -> Result<ParagraphText, Error>`**
  Displayed paragraph text with UTF-8 source ranges for editable text and generated fields.

### `doc_core/story_range.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_core/story_range.rs`](../lib/doc_core/story_range.rs)

```text
Continuous story coordinates; paragraph structure never lives in the GUI's text buffer.
```

- **`struct StoryParagraph`**
  One paragraph's projection and starting UTF-8 byte offset in its story.
  - `start: usize`
  - `projection: ParagraphText`

- **`struct StoryText`**
  A main body or one note, separated by single LF paragraph marks. Existing soft line
  breaks also display as LF; paragraph provenance distinguishes them during every edit.
  - `text: String`
  - `paragraphs: Vec<StoryParagraph>`

**`impl StoryText`**

- **`fn position(&self, offset: usize) -> Result<(Id, usize), Error>`**
  Resolve a story caret to paragraph identity and paragraph-local byte offset.
  The caret immediately before a paragraph mark belongs to the preceding paragraph.
- **`fn offset(&self, paragraph: Id, offset: usize) -> Option<usize>`**
  Translate an existing paragraph-local caret into the story buffer.

**`impl Document`**

- **`fn story_character_common(&self, paragraph: Id, start: usize, end: usize) -> Result<TextFormat, Error>`**
  Effective properties shared by every editable run in a selection. `None` marks
  mixed values. A caret samples its insertion format; fields remain protected.
- **`fn story_character_format(&self, paragraph: Id, start: usize, end: usize) -> Result<TextFormat, Error>`**
  Sample effective character formatting at the start of a UTF-8 story selection.
  Empty selections use caret affinity; nonempty selections use the selected run.
  Protected fields and invalid scalar boundaries are rejected consistently with edits.
- **`fn story_projection(&self, paragraph: Id) -> Result<StoryText, Error>`**
  Project the entire story containing this paragraph. Note bodies remain separate from
  the main body and from other notes, even when their lines share the same printed page.

Source: [`lib/doc_core/search.rs`](../lib/doc_core/search.rs)

```text
Literal Unicode search over adjacent text runs, without searching generated citation fields.
```

  - `case_sensitive: bool`
  - `whole_word: bool`

- **`struct TextMatch`**
  UTF-8 byte positions in one paragraph, possibly crossing adjacent formatting runs.

  - `end_inline: usize`
  - `end: usize`

**`impl Document`**

- **`fn find_text(&self, query: &str, options: SearchOptions) -> Result<Vec<TextMatch>, Error>`**
  Find non-overlapping literal matches. Formatting boundaries are transparent; fields and
  paragraph boundaries are not. Each main paragraph is followed by its anchored note bodies
  in anchor order, independent of note storage order. At most 10,000 matches and 4,096 query
  bytes are accepted.

### `doc_core/source_audit.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_core/source_audit.rs`](../lib/doc_core/source_audit.rs)

```text
Advisory bibliography completeness checks; new native functionality without a reference.
```

- **`enum SourceIssueKind`**
  Missing bibliographic metadata, independent of any particular citation style.
  - `MissingCreators` -- Neither personal authors nor a corporate author were supplied.
  - `MissingYear` -- The publication year is blank.
  - `MissingPublisher` -- A book has no publisher.
  - `MissingJournal` -- A journal article has no journal name.
  - `MissingUrl` -- A website has no URL.
  - `IncompleteAccessDate` -- Access-date components skip a preceding year or month component.

- **`struct SourceIssue`**
  A completeness hint associated with a stable source identity.
  - `source: Id` -- Source containing the incomplete metadata.
  - `kind: SourceIssueKind` -- Missing field or incomplete component relationship.

**`impl Document`**

- **`fn audit_sources(&self) -> Vec<SourceIssue>`**
  Inspect all sources, including uncited ones, without changing document or history.
  Hints never block source saving, mark the document dirty or claim style compliance.
  Results follow source order, then creators, year, type-specific field and access date.
  Whitespace-only fields are blank. Access dates are checked only for missing preceding
  components, not calendar validity; year-only and year/month dates are accepted.
  A valid document has at most 1,024 sources and fewer than 6,144 returned hints.

  - `Import(usize)`

- **`enum SourceMatch`**

  - `Identical(SourceTarget)`
  - `Similar(SourceTarget)`

- **`fn matches_query(&self, query: &str) -> bool`**

**`impl Document`**

### `doc_core/source_library.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_core/source_library.rs`](../lib/doc_core/source_library.rs)

```text
Reusable source-library transactions and identity allocation; new native functionality.
Storage belongs to doc_io: this model never accesses a filesystem or a document.
```

- **`struct SourceLibrary`**
  Independent source collection with a persisted, monotonically increasing ID watermark.
  Public for codecs; validate at trust boundaries. IDs have no meaning in other containers.
  - `sources: Vec<Source>` -- Ordered source records, each with an identity local to this library.
  - `next_id: Id` -- First never-issued ID, or 0x7fff_ffff when the identity space is exhausted.

**`impl SourceLibrary`**

- **`fn source(&self, id: Id) -> Option<&Source>`**
  Find a source by its library-local identity.
- **`fn byte_size(&self) -> usize`**
  Estimated retained payload, including source, creator and string storage.
- **`fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), Error>`**
  Check metadata, unique local IDs, allocation watermark and collection budgets.

- **`enum LibraryEdit`**
  Atomic source-library operations; imported identities are always external to the library.
  - `Import(Vec<Source>)` -- Import records, ignoring their IDs and skipping exact metadata duplicates.
    Returns the final record's existing or new library ID, or zero for an empty import.
  - `Put(Source)` -- ID zero explicitly creates a source; a nonzero ID updates an existing source.
    Unlike import, explicit creation preserves intentionally identical records.
  - `Remove(Id)` -- Remove an existing library source without affecting copies in documents.

- **`struct SourceLibraryEditor`**
  Atomic editor with at most 64 retained history snapshots and an 8 MiB history budget.
  Undo and redo retain the allocation watermark, including when a library is saved/reloaded.

**`impl SourceLibraryEditor`**

- **`fn new(library: SourceLibrary) -> Result<Self, Error>`**
  Start an editing session from a validated library, without inherited history.
- **`fn library(&self) -> &SourceLibrary`**
  Current immutable collection, ready for a library codec or copying to a document.
- **`fn revision(&self) -> u64`**
  Changes for every successful content edit, undo or redo; unchanged by no-ops/errors.
- **`fn can_undo(&self) -> bool`**
  Whether a complete transaction can be undone.
- **`fn can_redo(&self) -> bool`**
  Whether an undone transaction can be reapplied.
- **`fn undo(&mut self) -> bool`**
  Undo one transaction without making any previously issued identity reusable.
- **`fn redo(&mut self) -> bool`**
  Redo one transaction, preserving the same source identities and allocation watermark.
- **`fn apply(&mut self, edit: LibraryEdit) -> Result<Id, Error>`**
  Validate and apply a whole transaction. Errors and no-ops preserve history/revision.

### `doc_core/source_merge.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_core/source_merge.rs`](../lib/doc_core/source_merge.rs)

```text
Explicit source reconciliation with complete citation remapping; new native functionality.
```

- **`enum SourceField`**
  Selectable source metadata; personal and corporate creators form one exclusive field.
  - `Kind`
  - `Creators`
  - `Title`
  - `Year`
  - `Publisher`
  - `City`
  - `Edition`
  - `Journal`
  - `Volume`
  - `Issue`
  - `Pages`
  - `Website`
  - `Url`
  - `StandardNumber`
  - `YearAccessed`
  - `MonthAccessed`
  - `DayAccessed`
  - `ShortTitle`
  - `Comments`
  - `ExternalId`
  - `Language`

**`impl SourceField`**

- **`const ALL: [Self; 21] = [ Self::Kind, Self::Creators, Self::Title, Self::Year, Se ...`**
  Every reconcilable field in stable form order; source identity is never selectable.

- **`struct SourceMerge`**
  Explicit field choices: selected fields come from remove, all others remain from keep.
  An incoming empty field deliberately clears the kept value. Creator choices are atomic.
  - `keep: Id` -- Existing source identity retained by the transaction.
  - `remove: Id` -- Existing source removed after its citation references are redirected.
  - `incoming: Vec<SourceField>` -- Fields explicitly copied from remove, including deliberately selected empty values.

- **`struct SourceCandidate`**
  An advisory duplicate candidate. Similarity never authorizes an automatic merge.
  - `source: Id` -- Candidate's stable identity in the same document.
  - `identical: bool` -- True when every modeled field matches, ignoring only the local source ID.

**`impl Source`**

- **`fn field_text(&self, field: SourceField) -> String`**
  Readable comparison value; exact equality additionally preserves creator structure.
- **`fn field_equals(&self, other: &Self, field: SourceField) -> bool`**
  Exact metadata comparison, including creator mode, components and entered order.
- **`fn field_empty(&self, field: SourceField) -> bool`**
  True only for absent metadata. Kind is always present; whitespace is retained data.
- **`fn merge_fields(&self, incoming: &Self, fields: &[SourceField]) -> Result<Self, Error>`**
  Copy explicitly selected metadata from another scope, retaining this source's ID.
  Creator mode and ordered people change together. Duplicate choices and invalid
  resulting metadata are rejected; selecting an empty value deliberately clears it.

**`impl Document`**

- **`fn plan_source_merge(&self, keep: Id, remove: Id) -> Result<SourceMerge, Error>`**
  Prepare explicit reconciliation, suggesting incoming data only for absent kept fields.
  Both IDs must exist and differ. Populated fields, including Kind, retain their values.
- **`fn preview_source_merge(&self, plan: &SourceMerge) -> Result<Source, Error>`**
  Preview without mutations, validating the full remapped document and expansion budget.
  Duplicate selected fields, invalid identities or excessive generated text are errors.
- **`fn source_candidates(&self, source: Id) -> Result<Vec<SourceCandidate>, Error>`**
  List identical or similar sources in document order, excluding the requested source.
  The validated document contains at most 1024 sources, so at most 1023 are returned.

## doc_layout

*typographic paragraph flow and page layout* -- crate root [`lib/doc_layout/lib.rs`](../lib/doc_layout/lib.rs)

```text
Bounded paragraph flow and pagination in twips. New native functionality; no legacy counterpart.
Font measurement is supplied by the caller, so pagination is independent of zoom and GUI DPI.
```

### `doc_layout` crate root

- **`struct Metrics`**
  Font metrics in twips (1/20 point). Positive ascent/height and nonnegative descent.
  - `ascent: i32`
  - `descent: i32`
  - `height: i32`

- **`trait Measure`**
  A reusable measurement interface. Widths must be nonnegative, metrics positive and bounded.
  - `fn width(&mut self, text: &str, format: ResolvedFormat) -> i32`
    Measure a fragment at `format.display_size_half_points()`, without GUI zoom.
  - `fn metrics(&mut self, format: ResolvedFormat) -> Metrics`
    Return glyph metrics at `format.display_size_half_points()`, in twips.
    The layout itself accounts for the script baseline displacement.

- **`struct Fragment`**
  A text fragment positioned on a page in twips, ready for the caller's renderer.
  - `leader: doc_core::TabLeader` -- Visual fill for a tab; `text` remains empty and its source is the original tab.
  - `source: Option<(usize, usize)>` -- UTF-8 range in Document::text_projection; None for generated note-number prefixes.
  - `text: String`
  - `format: ResolvedFormat`
  - `x: i32`
  - `baseline: i32`
  - `width: i32`

- **`struct Line`**
  A line with a stable paragraph target for navigation and hit testing.
  - `cell: Option<Id>` -- Owning table cell, or `None` for body and note text.
  - `bar_tabs: Vec<i32>` -- Absolute page x positions of bar tab rules, in twips.
  - `start: usize` -- Start/end in the paragraph's displayed UTF-8 text, excluding an explicit line break.
  - `end: usize`
  - `paragraph: Id` -- Document paragraph ID, or zero for a generated non-editable heading.
  - `x: i32` -- Aligned start in twips, including an empty line's insertion point.
  - `top: i32`
  - `height: i32`
  - `fragments: Vec<Fragment>`

- **`struct Page`**
  One page; an empty document still produces one page.
  - `cells: Vec<CellBox>`
  - `lines: Vec<Line>`
  - `separators: Vec<Separator>` -- Page-bottom rules above footnotes; empty on pages without footnotes.

- **`struct CellBox`**
  A table cell rectangle in page twips, shared by paint, hit testing and resizing.
  - `edges: Vec<CellEdge>` -- Shared edges resolved per adjacent grid segment; offsets are relative to this box.
  - `repeated: bool` -- Display instance of an existing header cell; never a second document cell.
  - `table: Id`
  - `cell: Id`
  - `row: usize`
  - `column: usize`
  - `paragraph: Id`
  - `row_span: usize`
  - `column_span: usize`
  - `last_row_height: u32`
  - `row_end: bool` -- False for a cell fragment ending at a page break instead of its grid row boundary.
  - `rect: [i32;4]`
  - `fill: Option<u32>`
  - `border_width: u16`
  - `border_color: u32`

- **`struct Separator`**
  A horizontal rule separating footnote content from the main page text, in twips.
  - `x: i32`
  - `y: i32`
  - `width: i32`

- **`struct Layout`**
  Laid-out document; page coordinates do not change when the view zoom changes.
  - `settings: PageSettings`
  - `pages: Vec<Page>`

- **`enum Error`**
  Invalid model/measurements or exceeded expansion, fragment or page budget.
  - `Model(doc_core::Error)`
  - `Metrics`
  - `Limit`

**`impl Layout`**

- **`fn paragraph_at(&self, page: usize, y: i32) -> Option<Id>`**
  Hit-test the vertical extent of a line on a page, in page-relative twips.

- **`fn paginate(d: &Document, measure: &mut impl Measure) -> Result<Layout, Error>`**
  Paginate a validated document. Handles formatting runs, whitespace, tabs, line/page breaks,
  paragraph spacing/indents, alignment and heading keep-with-next when it fits on one page.
  Footnotes reserve page-bottom space with continuations; endnotes follow the main text.
  An anchor line and the first line of every note it references must fit on the same page.
  A same-line group that cannot meet that requirement without reordering note bodies fails
  with `Error::Limit`; notes are never silently omitted.
  Rectangular tables flow by rows. No shaping, bidirectional algorithm or Word-identical pagination is claimed.

### `doc_layout/table_fit.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_layout/table_fit.rs`](../lib/doc_layout/table_fit.rs)

- **`enum TableFit`**
  - `Contents`
  - `Page`
  - `Equal`

- **`fn table_fit( d: &Document, id: Id, mode: TableFit, measure: &mut impl Measure, ) -> Result<Vec<u32>, Error>`**
  Compute fixed widths; applying `TableChange::Columns` records the fit as one undo step.
  Contents measures explicit lines and tabs. It wraps text when natural widths exceed the page.


### `doc_view/src/coords.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_view/src/coords.rs`](../lib/doc_view/src/coords.rs)

```text
Shared page coordinate conversion. Rendering, pointer input and thumbnails use one scale.
```

- **`struct PageTransform`**
  An isotropic page transform from twips to an absolute pixel rectangle.

**`impl PageTransform`**

- **`fn new(width_twips: u32, rect: [i32; 4]) -> Option<Self>`**
  Create a transform for a nonempty page and pixel rectangle.
- **`fn scale(&self, twips: i32) -> i32`**
  Scale a twip length to pixels, truncating as in the existing document renderer.
- **`fn unscale(&self, pixels: i32) -> i32`**

- **`fn to_pixels(&self, point: [i32; 2]) -> [i32; 2]`**
  Convert a page-relative twip point to an absolute pixel point.
- **`fn to_page(&self, point: [i32; 2]) -> [i32; 2]`**
  Convert an absolute pixel point to page-relative twips, including points outside it.
- **`fn font_size(&self, half_points: u16) -> i32`**
  Scale a half-point font size to 24.8 pixel units.

- **`fn zoom_width(width_twips: u32, percent: i32) -> i32`**
  Page width in DIP at a zoom percentage, clamped to the supported 10–500 percent range.

- **`fn page_height(width_twips: u32, height_twips: u32, width: i32) -> i32`**

### `doc_view/src/fonts.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_view/src/fonts.rs`](../lib/doc_view/src/fonts.rs)

```text
Document-owned fallback fonts. GUI themes and styles never select a document face.
The SGF1 assets are DejaVu Sans 2.37, byte-identical to jfWord's previous defaults.
```

- **`enum FontError`**
  The text registry could not register the fixed document faces or their italic forms.
  - `Registration` -- The registry is full, or a bundled face is invalid.
  - `Italic` -- A synthetic italic face could not be registered.

- **`struct Fonts`**
  Handles to document fonts registered in one `text::Text` instance.

  Construct once per text engine and retain across document changes. Clones share the
  family cache and registered faces; do not use them with a different text engine.

**`impl Fonts`**

- **`fn new(text: &mut Text) -> Result<Self, FontError>`**
  Register the fixed DejaVu Sans fallback, independently of GUI fonts and theme.
  Failure rolls back every font registered during this call.
- **`fn get(&self, format: ResolvedFormat) -> u32`**
  Resolve the currently supported character attributes to a registered face.
- **`fn families(&self) -> Vec<String>`**
  Bundled families on every platform, plus installed native font families.
  Browser enumeration does not request access to the user's local fonts.
- **`fn prepare(&self, text: &mut Text, document: &Document)`**
  Register requested faces before measuring. Names survive substitutions unchanged.
  The bounded cache is shared by layout, page drawing and thumbnails.
- **`fn substituted(&self, family: FontFamily) -> bool`**
  True when the requested family is unavailable and a bundled substitute is used.
- **`fn ensure(&self, text: &mut Text, family: FontFamily)`**
  Prepare a family for an isolated style-gallery sample using the same resolver as pages.
- **`fn valid(&self, text: &Text) -> bool`**
  Check that every cached face still belongs to the live text engine.

### `doc_view/src/measure.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_view/src/measure.rs`](../lib/doc_view/src/measure.rs)

- **`struct FontMeasure<'a>`**

**`impl<'a> FontMeasure<'a>`**

- **`fn new(text: &'a mut Text, fonts: &'a Fonts) -> Self`**
  Borrow the shared text engine for layout. `paginate` also restores its current font.

- **`fn paginate( document: &Document, text: &mut Text, fonts: &Fonts, ) -> Result<Layout, doc_layout::Error>`**
  Paginate with document fonts and restore the text engine's previous UI font on return.

- **`fn apply_paginated(editor:&mut doc_core::Editor,edit:doc_core::Edit,text:&mut Text,fonts:&Fonts)->Result<doc_core::Id,doc_layout::Error>`**
  Commit only if the new document can be paginated with the actual document fonts.
  Model errors and layout errors preserve the editor's revision and undo history.

- **`fn replace_fragment_paginated(editor:&mut doc_core::Editor,paragraph:doc_core::Id,start:usize,end:usize,fragment:doc_core::DocumentFragment,text:&mut Text,fonts:&Fonts)->Result<(doc_core::Id,usize),doc_layout::Error>`**

- **`fn fit_table(document:&Document,id:doc_core::Id,mode:doc_layout::TableFit,text:&mut Text,fonts:&Fonts)->Result<alloc::vec::Vec<u32>,doc_layout::Error>`**

### `doc_view/src/page.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_view/src/page.rs`](../lib/doc_view/src/page.rs)

```text
Shared page paint adapter and bounded thumbnails. Colors belong to the document.
Extracted unchanged in operation order from jfWord's original preview renderer.
```

- **`fn page_backdrop(ui: &mut ui::Ui, rect: [i32; 4])`**
  Paint the subtle page drop shadow before a page canvas, keeping hit geometry unchanged.

- **`enum PageError`**
  A page index, geometry or thumbnail size cannot be rendered.
  - `Page` -- The requested page is not in the layout.
  - `Geometry` -- The page has invalid physical dimensions.
  - `Limit` -- A thumbnail exceeds the bounded allocation budget.
  - `Fonts` -- The document faces are not registered in the supplied text engine.

- **`struct PageDraw`**
  A retained page drawing usable in `Ui::canvas`, thumbnails and agent render resources.

**`impl PageDraw`**

- **`fn new(layout: Rc<Layout>, index: usize, fonts: Fonts) -> Result<Self, PageError>`**
  Retain one page and the document fonts used during pagination.
- **`fn without_frame(mut self) -> Self`**
  Omit the page outline when a surrounding gallery card paints its own frame.
  Document text, colors, measurement and pagination are unchanged.

```text
Physical page rulers shared by document editors. Geometry uses twips, never text pixels.
```

```rust
/// Space reserved for a document ruler, in device-independent pixels.
RULER_SIZE: i32 = 22
```

  - `page_width: i32` -- Displayed page width in pixels, defining exactly the editor's scale.
  - `dpi: u32` -- Device scale (96 is 100%).
  - `font: u32` -- Registered UI face used for ruler numbers.
  - `palette: RulerPalette` -- View colors, independent of document formatting.
