## doc_io

*conservative DOCX interchange and bounded file I/O* -- crate root [`lib/doc_io/lib.rs`](../lib/doc_io/lib.rs)

```text
Conservative DOCX interchange for the initial document model. New native library code.
Unrepresentable content is rejected before exposing an editable document. No VBA support.
```

### `doc_io` crate root

- **`enum Error`**
  Failure with the unsupported part/element included for a useful UI explanation.
  - `Io`
  - `Limit`
  - `Invalid`
  - `Archive(archive::Error)`
  - `Xml(xml::Error)`
  - `Unsupported(String)`
  - `Model(doc_core::Error)`

- **`fn encode_docx(d: &Document) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error>`**
  Serialize a validated document to a deterministic DOCX package. Sources are native
  bibliography custom XML; citations are CITATION fields with cached display text.
  Footnotes and endnotes use their standard parts and continuous decimal numbering.

- **`fn decode_docx(b: &[u8]) -> Result<Document, Error>`**
  Read the initial editable DOCX profile. Unknown package parts, layout, formatting,
  relationships, fields and source fields are rejected, never silently discarded.
  Full Word interoperability is deliberately not claimed by this early codec.

- **`fn read_file(path: &str, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error>`**
  Bounded whole-file read; refuses oversized files before allocation, including short reads.

- **`fn load_docx(path: &str) -> Result<Document, Error>`**
  Load a DOCX without altering the caller's current editing session on failure.

- **`fn save_docx(path: &str, d: &Document) -> Result<(), Error>`**
  Encode completely before the platform's temporary-file/rename write.

### `doc_io/bibliography.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_io/bibliography.rs`](../lib/doc_io/bibliography.rs)

```text
Shared Word bibliography XML codec: used by DOCX and standalone source lists.
```

- **`fn encode_sources(sources: &[Source]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error>`**
  Encode supported sources as Word Sources.xml, with document-local jf&lt;ID> tags.
  Input IDs must be unique and nonzero. Output is UTF-8, without external resources.

- **`fn decode_sources(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<Source>, Error>`**
  Decode a complete standalone Word source list. External tags are validated but remapped
  to sequential IDs; use Edit::ImportSources to allocate identities in an existing document.
  Unrepresented fields/types abort the whole import; no partial or silent content import.

- **`fn load_sources(path: &str) -> Result<Vec<Source>, Error>`**
  Load a bounded standalone Word bibliography XML file.

- **`fn save_sources(path: &str, sources: &[Source]) -> Result<(), Error>`**
  Export a source list using the platform's temporary-file/rename write.

### `doc_io/bibtex.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_io/bibtex.rs`](../lib/doc_io/bibtex.rs)

```text
Bounded, explicit Unicode BibTeX interchange subset. New native library code.
Syntax reference: Oren Patashnik, BibTeXing, https://texdoc.org/serve/btxdoc/0.
This is a metadata codec, not a TeX interpreter or a BibLaTeX style processor.
```

- **`fn decode_bibtex(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<Source>, Error>`**
  Decode a bounded Unicode BibTeX subset (@book, @article, @online and web @misc).
  Values may be braced, quoted or numeric. Names use Family, Given, single-token
  family names, or one braced corporation. UTF-8 and BOM-marked UTF-16 are accepted.
  Entry keys are validated and remapped to sequential IDs, not retained as metadata.
  Unsupported fields, TeX commands, case protection, macros and suffixes abort the
  entire import. Limits: 4 MiB input, 1024 sources, 64 authors, 4096 bytes per scalar,
  2 MiB retained model. ASCII field whitespace follows BibTeX normalization.

- **`fn encode_bibtex(sources: &[Source]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error>`**
  Encode the representable Source subset as deterministic UTF-8 BibTeX.
  Keys are jf&lt;ID>; IDs must be nonzero, unique and below 0x7fff_ffff. Separate middle
  names, external IDs, language, short titles and other unmapped metadata are errors.
  Export validates the entire list and refuses values whose meaning/spacing would
  change. Unicode is emitted directly; no TeX commands, resource loading or execution.

### `doc_io/clipboard.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_io/clipboard.rs`](../lib/doc_io/clipboard.rs)

```text
Bounded rich clipboard interchange. New native code, no assembly counterpart.
RTF control syntax follows Microsoft's RTF specification; this is a supported profile,
not a general RTF file editor. Internal structured fragments use the DOCX codec instead.
```

- **`fn clipboard_rtf_encode(d: &Document) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error>`**
  Encode body paragraphs as RTF 1 with explicit resolved character formatting, RGB colors,
  paragraph alignment, twip spacing/indents and Unicode. Hard paragraphs and soft breaks
  remain distinct. Fields and note anchors become their visible text; source/note metadata
  and style identities are not transferred. Font families and effective formatting remain.
  Use DOCX for a structured internal copy including the style graph.
  CRLF and lone CR inside text normalize to a single soft break.
  The output is bounded to 16 MiB and color tables to 1024 entries.

- **`fn clipboard_html_encode(d: &Document) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error>`**
  Encode a raw UTF-8 HTML clipboard fragment with inline CSS for resolved character and
  paragraph formatting. No CF_HTML envelope is included: that belongs to the platform
  clipboard layer. Font families use escaped CSS names; fields/anchors become visible text and
  source/note metadata stays in the private DOCX format. The result is bounded to 16 MiB.
  Floating tables carry versioned exact anchors plus CSS float fallback; page units are twips.

- **`fn clipboard_rtf_decode(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<Document, Error>`**
  Decode the bounded RTF clipboard profile into validated, directly formatted paragraphs.
  Accepts group-scoped formatting, Windows single-byte text (874/1250–1258), Unicode/surrogate escapes, colors,
  relative line spacing, twip paragraph metrics, font family names and cached field results.
  Language, style identities and metadata map to model defaults. Unknown ignorable destinations are
  skipped; unsupported visible content (including pictures, lists and objects),
  unsupported encodings or typography and malformed input are rejected atomically.
  Only direct formatting is read: implicit Normal-style/default-character properties in
  metadata are not resolved and fall back to RTF defaults (12pt, automatic color). Other
  named-style references are rejected. This profile is intended for resolved rich selections.
  Rectangular and nested table grids, row/column merges, headers, fills and cell borders are read.
  Limits: 16 MiB input, 2 MiB expanded text, 64 groups, 1024 colors, 256 fonts, 4M tokens.

### `doc_io/html_import.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_io/html_import.rs`](../lib/doc_io/html_import.rs)

- **`fn clipboard_html_decode(bytes:&[u8])->Result<Document,Error>`**
  Decode a UTF-8 HTML clipboard fragment into editable paragraphs and nested tables.
  Handles inline text/CSS, table grids, merged cells, headers, borders and table positions.
  JFLib's versioned data attributes retain exact twip anchors and document page settings.
  Input is bounded to 16 MiB, 2 MiB text, 64 element levels and eight table levels.
  Active content and metadata are ignored; external resources are never requested.

### `doc_io/fragment.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_io/fragment.rs`](../lib/doc_io/fragment.rs)

```text
Versioned, self-contained clipboard transport. Payload is the validated native DOCX profile.
```

- **`fn encode_document_fragment(fragment: &DocumentFragment) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error>`**
  Encode a rich selection for application/x-jfgui-docx-fragment. The version prefix
  prevents future fragment semantics from being interpreted as the current model.

- **`fn decode_document_fragment(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<DocumentFragment, Error>`**
  Decode bounded clipboard data without mutating the receiving document. Unknown versions,
  unsupported DOCX content and invalid source/note references are rejected atomically.

- **`fn clipboard_display_fragment( fragment: &DocumentFragment, context: &Document, ) -> Result<Document, Error>`**
  Materialize field results for external text formats using the originating document.
  In particular a bibliography-only selection and note numbers must use the source
  context, even though the native fragment carries only selected references.

### `doc_io/exchange.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_io/exchange.rs`](../lib/doc_io/exchange.rs)

```text
Format dispatch for standalone bibliography exchange; codecs remain independent of the app.
```

- **`enum BibliographyFormat`**
  Supported standalone source-list formats. Each codec has an explicit editable subset.
  - `WordXml`
  - `Ris`
  - `Bibtex`

**`impl BibliographyFormat`**

- **`fn from_path(path: &str) -> Result<Self, Error>`**
  Select by filename extension, case-insensitively; never guess from malformed content.
- **`fn extension(self) -> &'static str`**
  Default filename extension without a dot.

- **`fn decode_bibliography(bytes: &[u8], format: BibliographyFormat) -> Result<Vec<Source>, Error>`**
  Decode one complete source list. Errors never return a partially decoded list.

- **`fn encode_bibliography( sources: &[Source], format: BibliographyFormat, ) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error>`**
  Encode all records before any file is touched; unrepresentable metadata is an error.

- **`fn load_bibliography(path: &str) -> Result<Vec<Source>, Error>`**
  Load a bounded source file, choosing the codec from its extension.

- **`fn save_bibliography(path: &str, sources: &[Source]) -> Result<(), Error>`**
  Save through the platform's temporary-file/rename writer after complete encoding.

### `doc_io/library.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_io/library.rs`](../lib/doc_io/library.rs)

```text
Native source-library persistence: strict XML, optimistic conflict detection and recovery.
New shared functionality; no user-profile policy or application UI belongs in this layer.
```

- **`fn encode_source_library(library: &SourceLibrary) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error>`**
  Encode the native version-one source library, preserving source IDs and their high-water mark.

- **`fn decode_source_library(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<SourceLibrary, Error>`**
  Decode the complete native source-library format; future versions and unknown data fail closed.

- **`enum LibraryStoreError`**
  Persistent-library failures distinguish stale content, a held lock and codec rejection.
  - `Io` -- A path could not be inspected, read, backed up or atomically replaced.
  - `Busy` -- The cooperative lock could not be acquired (busy or unavailable).
  - `Conflict` -- The file has changed since this store's successful open, reload or save.
  - `Codec(Error)` -- The current file, backup or proposed model is outside the supported native format.

- **`struct LibraryStore`**
  A validated source library and the exact file snapshot used for optimistic concurrency.
  Opening and reloading never write. Saving uses a sibling lock and atomic platform writes.

- **`struct LibraryRecovery`**
  A recovery preview with exact current-file and backup snapshots, safe to keep across a dialog.
  Restoring fails if either reviewed file changes before the caller confirms the operation.

**`impl LibraryRecovery`**

- **`fn library(&self) -> &SourceLibrary`**
  The planned restored model, retaining the greater ID high-water mark of valid current XML.
- **`fn restore(&self) -> Result<LibraryStore, LibraryStoreError>`**
  Restore the reviewed backup under the library lock, preserving replaced bytes in `.damaged`.

**`impl LibraryStore`**

- **`fn open(path: &str) -> Result<Self, LibraryStoreError>`**
  Open an existing valid library, or an empty in-memory library when the path is absent.
  A malformed existing file is an error and is never replaced by an empty model.
- **`fn library(&self) -> &SourceLibrary`**
  The last successfully opened, reloaded or saved library.
- **`fn path(&self) -> &str`**
  The configured native library filename.
- **`fn save(&mut self, library: &SourceLibrary) -> Result<(), LibraryStoreError>`**
  Validate fully, acquire the sibling lock, compare the original bytes, then save atomically.
  An existing valid version is backed up to `.bak` before replacement; backup failure aborts.
- **`fn reload(&mut self) -> Result<(), LibraryStoreError>`**
  Read and validate the current file; on any failure this store keeps its previous state.
- **`fn restore_backup(&mut self) -> Result<(), LibraryStoreError>`**
  Restore the validated `.bak` only if the current file still matches this store's snapshot.
  The replaced bytes are retained in `.damaged`; the good `.bak` is never overwritten.
- **`fn inspect_backup(path: &str) -> Result<SourceLibrary, LibraryStoreError>`**
  Read a backup for review without changing the current file or creating any paths.
- **`fn prepare_recovery(path: &str) -> Result<LibraryRecovery, LibraryStoreError>`**
  Read a valid backup and current bytes for explicit recovery, including malformed current XML.
  This read-only preview can later restore exactly those reviewed snapshots.
- **`fn restore_backup_if(&mut self, expected: &SourceLibrary) -> Result<(), LibraryStoreError>`**
  Restore only the backup model the caller previously inspected; changes return `Conflict`.
- **`fn recover(path: &str) -> Result<Self, LibraryStoreError>`**
  Recover from a malformed current file by restoring a validated `.bak` under the same lock.
  Current bytes are retained in `.damaged` before replacement; neither an unreadable nor an
  oversized current file is overwritten because an exact comparison cannot be established.
  Valid current XML retains its ID high-water mark. A malformed file cannot supply that mark.
- **`fn recover_if_backup( path: &str, expected: &SourceLibrary, ) -> Result<Self, LibraryStoreError>`**
  Recover only the backup model the caller previously inspected, preventing a stale preview.

### `doc_io/preservation.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_io/preservation.rs`](../lib/doc_io/preservation.rs)

```text
Bounded OPC inspection and original-byte preservation for foreign Word documents.
New native library code. Unrepresented content is never exposed as an editable model.
```

- **`enum DocxMode`**
  Whether the entire document is represented by the editable model.
  - `Editable` -- Every supported package component has passed the strict editable codec.
  - `Preserved` -- The original package is retained; the text excerpt is not an editable conversion.

- **`struct DocxReport`**
  Deterministic inspection results for a bounded, structurally checked DOCX package.
  - `mode: DocxMode` -- Editing capability; preservation does not imply rendering or editing compatibility.
  - `main_part: String` -- Main document part resolved through the package relationship.
  - `part_count: usize` -- Number of ZIP parts, including content types and relationships.
  - `diagnostics: Vec<String>` -- Human-readable reasons and feature descriptions, capped at 64 distinct entries.
  - `preview_truncated: bool` -- The reading excerpt reached its independent paragraph or text limit.

- **`struct DocxFile`**
  A DOCX session retaining its exact original archive alongside an optional full model.
  Unknown features remain intact; individually proven text leaves may be edited without
  model conversion. Saving without edits returns the original bytes, including compression,
  ordering, metadata, images, fields and unknown extension parts.

**`impl DocxFile`**

- **`fn open(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<Self, Error>`**
  Inspect a conventional, unencrypted DOCX with bounded ZIP/XML processing.
  Macro-enabled main parts or VBA payloads are rejected. External relationships are
  retained as data only; this library never follows links or executes embedded content.
- **`fn load(path: &str) -> Result<Self, Error>`**
  Read and inspect a file, refusing archives over 32 MiB before allocation.
- **`fn document(&self) -> Option<&Document>`**
  Entire editable document, or `None` when any unsupported content must be preserved.
- **`fn preview(&self) -> &[String]`**
  Main-story plain-text excerpt, excluding separate headers, footers and note parts.
  This deliberately does not claim layout fidelity.
  Field instructions, deleted revisions and unsupported alternate representations
  are omitted; cached visible field text and table-cell paragraphs may be included.
- **`fn report(&self) -> &DocxReport`**
  Original package's capability and compatibility diagnostics.
- **`fn text_runs(&self) -> &[crate::DocxTextRun]`**
  Safe individual text leaves in the preserved main story and related headers, footers,
  footnotes and endnotes. Parts resolve through OPC relationships and content types.
  Signed/protected packages, fields, revisions and content controls are not editable;
  a complex field or tracked revision disables its whole containing story part.
- **`fn replace_text( &mut self, index: usize, expected: &str, replacement: &str, ) -> Result<(), Error>`**
  Replace exactly one eligible text leaf, verifying its current text first.
  Paragraph/run structure and every other package payload remain unchanged.
- **`fn dirty(&self) -> bool`**
  Whether preserved text differs from the last saved checkpoint.
- **`fn revision(&self) -> u64`**
  Monotonic preserved-text revision, including successful undo and redo operations.
  Saving and replacing text with identical text leave the revision unchanged.
- **`fn mark_saved(&mut self)`**
  Establish the saved checkpoint after a successful save, retaining undo history.
- **`fn undo(&mut self) -> bool`**
  Undo the most recent preserved text edit, within the bounded history.
- **`fn redo(&mut self) -> bool`**
  Redo the next preserved text edit.
- **`fn can_undo(&self) -> bool`**
  Whether an earlier preserved text edit is available.
- **`fn can_redo(&self) -> bool`**
  Whether an undone preserved text edit is available.
- **`fn encode(&self, document: Option<&Document>) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error>`**
  Produce original bytes for unchanged content, or serialize a changed fully
  represented model. Passing a model to a preservation-only session is an error.
  None saves preserved text edits if present, otherwise copies the original exactly.
- **`fn save(&self, path: &str, document: Option<&Document>) -> Result<(), Error>`**
  Encode before atomic temporary-file/rename writing; a failed edit cannot overwrite
  a destination. Copying a preserved document retains every original archive byte.

### `doc_io/preserved_text.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_io/preserved_text.rs`](../lib/doc_io/preserved_text.rs)

```text
Provenance-preserving edits of individual Word text leaves, independent of the model.
Original XML syntax, run properties and every unrelated package payload are retained.
```

- **`struct DocxTextRun`**
  An individually editable text leaf in an original Word story part.
  - `index: usize` -- Stable zero-based identifier accepted by DocxFile::replace_text.
  - `part: String` -- Original OPC part path; shared headers are exposed only once.
  - `story: DocxStory` -- The containing story, resolved from the main part's relationship and content type.
  - `note_id: Option<i64>` -- OOXML note identity, not its rendered number. None for the main story and headers/footers.
  - `paragraph: usize` -- One-based XML paragraph number within this part, including table-cell paragraphs.
  - `text: String` -- Current decoded text. Run properties and paragraph structure are kept separately.

### `doc_io/ris.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_io/ris.rs`](../lib/doc_io/ris.rs)

```text
Bounded RIS bibliography interchange. New native codec, no legacy counterpart.
Tag semantics: Clarivate RIS / primary Zotero RIS translator mapping; see public API scope.
```

- **`fn decode_ris(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<Source>, Error>`**
  Decode a complete bounded RIS list: BOOK/JOUR/ELEC, people in AU/A1 order,
  TI/T1 (BOOK: BT), PY/Y1 year only, PB/CY/ET, JOUR JF/JO/T2, ELEC T2,
  VL/IS/SP/EP (not ELEC), UR/DO, SN/ST/N1/LA and Y2 access date.
  UTF-8/BOM and BOM UTF-16, LF/CRLF and multiline values are accepted. Continuations
  strip one six-space indent; other continuation text is preserved with LF.
  ID is validated then remapped sequentially; use Edit::ImportSources for document IDs.
  No corporate/suffix inference, role conversion, abstract/keyword/attachment dropping,
  or publication-date truncation. Unknown or duplicate scalar tags abort the whole list.
  DOI occupies the URL only when a safe doi.org URL and any UR agree. LA is kept verbatim.
  Limits: 4 MiB encoded/decoded input, 4096 tags, 1024 records, 4096 bytes per field,
  64 authors per record and 2 MiB retained model. No input causes filesystem/network access.

- **`fn encode_ris(sources: &[Source]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, Error>`**
  Export deterministic UTF-8 RIS with CRLF and indented continuation lines.
  Only fields represented by decode_ris are accepted; source IDs are local and omitted.
  Corporate authors, separately stored middle names, external GUIDs, incompatible
  type-specific fields and non-year publication dates produce record/tag diagnostics.
  People with commas or outer whitespace in name components cannot be emitted faithfully.
  Validation includes decoding the complete result and model equality (except source IDs),
  so no partial output or silently omitted metadata is ever returned.

### `doc_io/preserved_stories.rs`

Source: [`lib/doc_io/preserved_stories.rs`](../lib/doc_io/preserved_stories.rs)

```text
Related Word stories resolved by OPC identity, never guessed from ZIP file names.
```

- **`enum DocxStory`**
  The Word story containing an individually editable preserved text leaf.
  - `Main` -- Main document, including eligible table-cell paragraphs.
  - `Header` -- A section header part; multiple sections can share the same part.
  - `Footer` -- A section footer part.
  - `Footnote` -- A normal footnote (separator/continuation definitions are excluded).
  - `Endnote` -- A normal endnote (separator/continuation definitions are excluded).

## rt

*the runtime parts apps use: SSE / x87 float semantics, CPU features, locks, crash records* -- crate root [`lib/rt/lib.rs`](../lib/rt/lib.rs)

```text
rt -- the runtime under everything: allocator, memory intrinsics, exact SSE float
semantics, the minimal OS calls the runtime itself needs, panic handling.

  rt::os::{pages_alloc, pages_free, write_stdout, write_stderr, exit, yield_now}   (and on
  Linux the raw syscall helpers plat builds on)
  rt::fx   cvtt / cvt / sqrt / minss / maxss / floor_i / ceil_i / div255
  rt::x87  the extended-precision steps of f64 <-> decimal text (ini.s), fpatan
  rt::sync SpinLock
  rt::cpu  CPU feature bits (CPUID, cached; force_baseline for --simd sse2)
  rt::crash the crash hook, CrashInfo, report (exit 3) -- also for panics

One of the four crates that may contain `unsafe` (rt, simd, plat, plugin).
```

### `rt` crate root

- **`main!(...)`**
  Define the common app entry symbol without requiring the GUI crate in headless tools.

**`mod portable`**

Portable arithmetic, available on native targets for exact backend comparisons.

### `rt/cpu.rs`

Source: [`lib/rt/cpu.rs`](../lib/rt/cpu.rs)

```text
cpu -- CPU feature detection for SIMD dispatch                             [src/core/cpu.s]

  features() -> CPU_* bits     cached; SSE2 is the baseline and has no bit
  force_baseline()             report 0 from now on (plat calls it at process start for
                               `--simd sse2`: baseline kernels only, to compare results)
  cpuid(leaf) -> CpuidResult

CPU_AVX2 and CPU_FMA are only reported when the OS saves the YMM state (CPUID.1:ECX.OSXSAVE
and XGETBV(0) bits 1 and 2).  Thread safe: every caller computes the same cached value.
Lives in rt so that simd (which may not depend on plat) dispatches on the same bits.
```

```rust
CPU_SSE41: u32 = 1
CPU_AVX2: u32 = 2  // AVX2 + OS-enabled YMM state
CPU_FMA: u32 = 4  // FMA3 + OS-enabled YMM state
CPU_POPCNT: u32 = 8
CPU_BMI2: u32 = 16
```

- **`fn cpuid(leaf: u32) -> CpuidResult`**

- **`fn features() -> u32`**
  CPU_\* bits (cached)  [cpu_features]

- **`fn force_baseline()`**
  report no optional features from now on (`--simd sse2`)

### `rt/crash.rs`

Source: [`lib/rt/crash.rs`](../lib/rt/crash.rs)

```text
crash -- crash reports: the hook an app installs, the record the platform fills  [docs/modules/CRASH.md]

  set_hook(Some(f))      f(&CrashInfo) runs once when the process crashes or panics, after
                         the stderr line and before the process ends with exit code 3.  A
                         crash inside it (or a second crashing thread) ends the process at
                         once; a stack overflow skips it.
  report(&info) -> !     the end of every crash (the platform's fault handlers, the panic
                         handler): the hook, then exit(3)
  StackText<N>           a fixed buffer for core::fmt -- crash paths do not allocate (the
                         allocator lock may be held by the crashing thread)

Rust adds one kind, CK_PANIC: the panic message is in CrashInfo::msg.
```

```rust
CK_ACCESS: u32 = 1  // access violation / SIGSEGV
CK_ILLEGAL: u32 = 2  // illegal or privileged instruction
CK_DIVIDE: u32 = 3  // integer division by zero / overflow, SIGFPE
CK_BUS: u32 = 4  // SIGBUS, misalignment, in-page error
CK_STACK: u32 = 5  // stack overflow (no hook: there is no stack left)
CK_OTHER: u32 = 6
CK_PANIC: u32 = 7  // a Rust panic (msg)
FRAMES_MAX: usize = 32
```

- **`struct CrashInfo<'a>`**
  what the crash handler knows (the CRASHINFO record of ASMLIB)
  - `code: u32` -- Windows exception code / Linux signal number (panic: 0)
  - `kind: u32` -- CK_\*
  - `access: u32` -- access violations: 0 read, 1 write, 8 execute, 2 unknown
  - `addr: u64` -- data address of an access violation / bus error (0 = none)
  - `ip: u64` -- instruction pointer
  - `base: u64` -- image base: offset = address - base (Linux 0: the .map lists addresses)
  - `code0: u64` -- the executable code: code0..code1
  - `code1: u64`
  - `regs: [u64; 17]` -- rax rbx rcx rdx rsi rdi rbp rsp r8 .. r15 rip
  - `frames: [u64; FRAMES_MAX]` -- the call chain: return addresses, innermost first (plat)
  - `nframes: u32`
  - `msg: &'a str` -- CK_PANIC: the message

**`impl CrashInfo<'_>`**

- **`const fn empty() -> Self`**

- **`type CrashHook = fn(&CrashInfo)`**

- **`fn set_hook(f: Option<CrashHook>)`**
  install (Some) or remove (None) the crash hook  [plat_crash_hook]

- **`fn busy() -> bool`**
  a crash is being reported (a second one ends the process at once)

- **`fn report(info: &CrashInfo) -> !`**
  run the hook once, then exit(3)

- **`struct StackText<const N: usize>`**
  formatting into a fixed buffer (overflow is cut off)

**`impl<const N: usize> StackText<N>`**

- **`const fn new() -> Self`**
- **`fn bytes(&self) -> &[u8]`**
- **`fn as_str(&self) -> &str`**

### `rt/fx.rs`

Source: [`lib/rt/fx.rs`](../lib/rt/fx.rs)

```text
fx -- scalar SSE float operations with exactly the semantics the assembly uses
(cvttss2si, cvtss2si with round-half-even, sqrtss, minss, maxss), so float results
are bit-identical to ASMLIB.  Rust never fuses a multiply and an add into an FMA.
```

- **`fn cvtt(x: f32) -> i32`**
  cvttss2si: truncate towards zero (0x80000000 for NaN / out of range)

- **`fn cvt(x: f32) -> i32`**
  cvtss2si: round to nearest, ties to even (the default MXCSR mode)

- **`fn sqrt(x: f32) -> f32`**

- **`fn minss(a: f32, b: f32) -> f32`**
  minss a, b: a &lt; b ? a : b (b when unordered)

- **`fn maxss(a: f32, b: f32) -> f32`**
  maxss a, b: a > b ? a : b (b when unordered)

- **`fn floor_i(x: f32) -> i32`**
  floor / ceil the way f_floor / f_ceil do it: truncate, then step if needed

- **`fn ceil_i(x: f32) -> i32`**

- **`fn div255(x: u32) -> u32`**
  round(x / 255) for x in 0..=65025: ((x + 128) \* 257) >> 16 (pmulhuw), equal to
  (x + 128 + ((x + 128) >> 8)) >> 8

- **`fn sqrtd(x: f64) -> f64`**
  sqrtsd

- **`fn maxsd(a: f64, b: f64) -> f64`**
  maxsd a, b: a > b ? a : b (b when unordered)

- **`fn minsd(a: f64, b: f64) -> f64`**
  minsd a, b: a &lt; b ? a : b (b when unordered)

- **`fn cvttd(x: f64) -> i32`**
  cvttsd2si (32-bit): truncate towards zero (0x80000000 for NaN / out of range)

- **`fn cvtd(x: f64) -> i32`**
  cvtsd2si (32-bit): round to nearest even (0x80000000 for NaN / out of range)

- **`fn cvtd64(x: f64) -> i64`**
  cvtsd2si (64-bit): round to nearest even (i64::MIN for NaN / out of range)

- **`fn cvttd64(x: f64) -> i64`**
  cvttsd2si (64-bit): truncate (i64::MIN for NaN / out of range)

```text
sync -- a spin lock for short critical sections (allocator-free, usable in statics)

  lock.lock() -> guard        spins a little, then gives the time slice away

For data that several threads touch briefly (the environment overrides, the pool
queue); long waits belong on a semaphore (plat::Sem).
```

- **`struct SpinLock<T>`**

- **`struct Guard<'a, T>`**

**`impl<T> SpinLock<T>`**

### `rt/x87.rs`

Source: [`lib/rt/x87.rs`](../lib/rt/x87.rs)

```text
x87 -- the extended-precision steps of the decimal <-> double conversions   [src/core/ini.s]

  scale(m, e) -> f64          m * 10^e (e < 0: m / 10^-e), one rounding to double  (f64_parse)
  scale_round(v, k) -> i64    round(v * 10^k) (k < 0: v / 10^-k), to nearest       (f64_format)
  log10_round(v) -> i32       round(log10 v) as fyl2x computes it                  (f64_format)
  pow(x, y) -> f64            x^y as fx_pow computes it (R6-icc)       [src/fx/fx_lut.s]
  exp2 / log2 / cos / sin     fx_exp2 / fx_log2 / fx_cos / fx_sin (r10-fx)  [src/fx/fx_lut.s]
  rz_exp2 / rz_log2           PaintCraft's rz_exp2 / rz_log2 (pn-ui; other rounding)  [dlg_rotzoom.s]

ASMLIB computes these on the x87 unit with 64-bit precision and round to nearest (the
control word is saved and restored in every call), 10^n as fld1 times the double table
1e0..1e22 (1e22 repeated for larger n).  The same instructions run here, so the text a
double is written as and the double a text reads as are bit-identical to the assembly.
```

- **`fn scale(m: u64, e: i32) -> f64`**
  m \* 10^e or m / 10^-e (m &lt; 2^63), rounded once to double  [f64_parse .fp_calc]

- **`fn scale_round(v: f64, k: i32) -> i64`**
  frndint(v \* 10^k) or frndint(v / 10^-k) as a 64-bit integer  [f64_format .ff_scale]

- **`fn log10_round(v: f64) -> i32`**
  fldlg2 / fyl2x / fistp: log10 v rounded to the nearest integer  [f64_format]

- **`fn atan2(y: f64, x: f64) -> f64`**
  fpatan: atan2(y, x) on the x87 unit, rounded once to double (the precision control does
  not apply to fpatan)  [pathdata.s pd_atan2, dash.s dc_atan2]

- **`fn sincos(x: f64) -> (f64, f64)`**
  fsincos: (cos x, sin x) on the x87 unit, each rounded once to double (the precision
  control does not apply to fsincos)  [ui_value.s deg_sincos]

- **`fn pow(x: f64, y: f64) -> f64`**
  fx_pow: x^y -- NaN when x or y is NaN, 1 for y == 0, 0 for x &lt;= 0, x for y == 1, else
  2^(y log2 x) on the x87 unit with 64-bit precision and round to nearest (fyl2x, then
  frndint / f2xm1 / fscale), rounded once to double  [fx_lut.s fx_pow]

- **`fn rz_log2(x: f64) -> f64`**
  fyl2x with y = 1: log2(x) on the x87 unit, rounded once to double (fyl2x is not subject
  to the precision control)  [PaintCraft dlg_rotzoom.s rz_log2]

- **`fn rz_exp2(x: f64) -> f64`**
  2^x as rz_exp2 computes it (|x| &lt; 2^30): i = cvtsd2si x, f = x - i (SSE), then on the x87
  unit f2xm1 f, + 1 (faddp: rounded by the process's precision control, as in the
  assembly), fscale by i, rounded to double  [PaintCraft dlg_rotzoom.s rz_exp2]

### `rt/portable/fx.rs`

Source: [`lib/rt/portable/fx.rs`](../lib/rt/portable/fx.rs)

```text
Scalar IEEE-754 operations matching the native SSE conversion and operand-selection
rules. Integer square root avoids libm imports and gives correctly rounded results.
```

- **`fn nearest(x: f64) -> f64`**
  Round a finite binary64 value to its nearest integer, with ties to even.

- **`fn arithmetic32(a: f32, b: f32, result: f32) -> f32`**
  Select and quiet NaN payloads as scalar SSE arithmetic does, including invalid operations.

- **`fn arithmetic64(a: f64, b: f64, result: f64) -> f64`**
  Binary64 SSE arithmetic NaN payload selection.

- **`fn cvtt(x: f32) -> i32`**
  SSE truncating f32 to i32 conversion, including the indefinite overflow value.

- **`fn cvt(x: f32) -> i32`**
  SSE nearest-even f32 to i32 conversion.

- **`fn cvttd(x: f64) -> i32`**
  SSE truncating f64 to i32 conversion.

- **`fn cvtd(x: f64) -> i32`**
  SSE nearest-even f64 to i32 conversion.

- **`fn cvttd64(x: f64) -> i64`**
  SSE truncating f64 to i64 conversion.

- **`fn cvtd64(x: f64) -> i64`**
  SSE nearest-even f64 to i64 conversion.

- **`fn sqrt(x: f32) -> f32`**
  Correctly rounded binary32 square root; preserves signed zero and quiets NaNs.

- **`fn sqrtd(x: f64) -> f64`**
  Correctly rounded binary64 square root; preserves signed zero and quiets NaNs.

- **`fn minss(a: f32, b: f32) -> f32`**
  SSE minimum: return the second operand for equal or unordered operands.

- **`fn maxss(a: f32, b: f32) -> f32`**
  SSE maximum: return the second operand for equal or unordered operands.

- **`fn minsd(a: f64, b: f64) -> f64`**
  Binary64 SSE minimum.

- **`fn maxsd(a: f64, b: f64) -> f64`**
  Binary64 SSE maximum.

- **`fn floor_i(x: f32) -> i32`**
  Native floor contract: truncate, then decrement if necessary.

- **`fn ceil_i(x: f32) -> i32`**
  Native ceiling contract: truncate, then increment if necessary.

- **`fn div255(x: u32) -> u32`**
  Rounded division by 255 for 0..=65025.

### `rt/portable/math.rs`

Source: [`lib/rt/portable/math.rs`](../lib/rt/portable/math.rs)

```text
Portable replacement for the native x87 entry points. Decimal scaling emulates the
x87 64-bit significand after each multiply/divide. Transcendentals use explicit scalar
binary64 series; native x87 remains unchanged and is the comparison reference.
```

- **`fn scale(m: u64, e: i32) -> f64`**
  Decimal scaling with the same 64-bit significand intermediate rounding as x87.

- **`fn scale_round(v: f64, k: i32) -> i64`**
  Nearest-even integer after decimal scaling, including native indefinite overflow.

- **`fn log10_round(v: f64) -> i32`**
  Nearest integer decimal logarithm; nonpositive inputs produce the SSE indefinite value.

- **`fn log2(x: f64) -> f64`**
  Binary logarithm using a normalized mantissa and a convergent atanh series.

- **`fn exp2(x: f64) -> f64`**
  Platform-independent base-two exponent; finite underflow rounds into subnormals.

- **`fn rz_log2(x: f64) -> f64`**
  Existing rotation/zoom base-two logarithm API.

- **`fn rz_exp2(x: f64) -> f64`**
  Existing rotation/zoom base-two exponent API.

- **`fn pow(x: f64, y: f64) -> f64`**
  Effect power contract: y=0 yields 1, nonpositive bases yield 0, NaNs propagate canonically.

- **`fn atan2(y: f64, x: f64) -> f64`**
  Quadrant-aware arctangent, including signed zeros and infinite operands.

- **`fn sincos(x: f64) -> (f64, f64)`**
  Cosine and sine with shared quadrant reduction; return order matches the x87 API.

- **`fn cos(x: f64) -> f64`**
  Scalar cosine using the common angle reduction.

- **`fn sin(x: f64) -> f64`**
  Scalar sine using the common angle reduction.

## plugin

*the PaintCraft plugin ABI for plugins written in Rust* -- crate root [`lib/plugin/lib.rs`](../lib/plugin/lib.rs)

```text
plugin -- the PaintCraft plugin ABI for plugins written in Rust (no_std cdylib, no imports)
                    [ASMLIB/inc/plugin.inc; docs apps/PaintCraft/docs/PLUGINS.md, plugins/README.md]

A plugin is a library exporting one function, pn_plugin_query(PNAPI*) -> PLUGININFO*, that
PaintCraft (the Rust and the assembly build alike) loads from <root>/Effects, FileTypes or
Shapes.  It is linked against nothing: no rt, no plat, no system library -- the host's
functions come through the PNAPI table.  This crate is all a plugin depends on (+ core):

  abi       the records of plugin.inc / fx.inc / img.inc as #[repr(C)] types (layouts
            asserted): PnApi, PluginInfo, FxDesc, FxProp, FtDesc, ShapeDesc, FxArgs,
            Surface, Job, ImgDec, ImgMeta, ImgOpt; their const constructors
  api       the host table after pn_plugin_query (api()): Block (a PA_ALLOC buffer),
            parallel_for, hash32, log / log!, nthreads; PnAlloc (opt-in global allocator)
  fx        Effect (render / prepare of an effect or adjustment) and its safe view Fx
            (source rows, the destination rows of the ROI, parameters, cancel polling)
  ft        FileType (probe / decode / encode of a file type): Decoder, Image
  mem       memcpy / memmove / memset / memcmp / bcmp (and the MSVC support symbols) for
            core, in assembly so that they never become exports

The unsafe parts (raw host records, the trampolines the host calls) live here; a plugin's
own code stays safe: `#![deny(unsafe_code)]`, with entry!() as its one exception (the
#[unsafe(no_mangle)] export).  Minimal plugin (see plugins/sepiastr):

  #![no_std]
  #![deny(unsafe_code)]
  use plugin::*;
  struct Sepia;
  impl Effect for Sepia {
      type Aux = ();
      fn render(fx: &mut Fx, _: Option<&()>) -> Result<(), i32> { ... }
  }
  static PROPS: [FxProp; 1] = [FxProp::int(0, c"Stärke", 0.0, 100.0, 60.0)];
  static FX: FxDesc = FxDesc::new::<Sepia>(c"Sepia-Stärke", FXF_ADJ, FXC_ADJUST, &PROPS);
  static FX_LIST: [&FxDesc; 1] = [&FX];
  static INFO: PluginInfo = PluginInfo::new(c"Sepia-Stärke (Beispiel)", c"1.0").effects(&FX_LIST);
  fn query(_: &'static PnApi) -> Option<&'static PluginInfo> { Some(&INFO) }
  plugin::entry!(query);
  #[panic_handler]
  fn panic(info: &core::panic::PanicInfo) -> ! { plugin::panic(info) }

Panics: `plugin::panic` logs "panic at FILE:LINE" through PA_LOG and executes ud2,
`plugin::trap` only executes ud2 -- the host's crash guard catches it and disables the plugin
("Absturz in render (ungültiger Befehl ...)"), like any other fault inside plugin code.

Build: python build_rs.py plugin [NAME] (section rust-plugin: cdylib, panic=abort, fat LTO,
linked against nothing, checked like PaintCraft's loader checks it).
```

### `plugin` crate root

*plugin.inc*

```rust
PNAPI_VERSION: u32 = 1  // the PNAPI version this crate is written for
PLUGIN_MAGIC: u32 = 0x4950_4E50  // "PNPI" little endian
PA_SIZE_V1: u32 = 96  // PNAPI table size of version 1
PI_SIZE_V1: u32 = 88  // PLUGININFO size of version 1
```

*error codes (jfgui.inc)*

```rust
ERR_FAIL: i32 = -1
ERR_TRUNC: i32 = -1  // input ends early
ERR_HDR: i32 = -2  // bad header / signature
ERR_NOMEM: i32 = -8  // allocation failed
ERR_UNSUPPORTED: i32 = -9  // valid but unsupported variant
ERR_DIMS: i32 = -12  // width / height out of range
ERR_IO: i32 = -13
ERR_CANCEL: i32 = -14  // cancelled through a cancel flag
ERR_LIMIT: i32 = -15  // resource limit
ERR_FAULT: i32 = -64  // a guarded call faulted (the plugin is disabled)
```

*fx.inc*

```rust
FX_NSLOTS: usize = 32  // parameter slots per effect
FXF_ADJ: u32 = 1  // adjustment (Korrekturen) rather than effect (Effekte)
FXF_PREPARE: u32 = 2  // has a prepare step
FXF_SEED: u32 = 4  // uses a random seed (has an FP_SEED property)
FXF_BANDS: u32 = 8  // render wants full-width row bands
FXF_WHOLE: u32 = 16  // render must get the whole image in one call
FXF_NO_DIALOG: u32 = 32  // applied at once, without the property dialog
FXF_SLOW: u32 = 64  // hint: show progress
FXC_ADJUST: u32 = 0  // categories (FXD_CATEGORY)
FXC_ARTISTIC: u32 = 1
FXC_BLUR: u32 = 2
FXC_COLOR: u32 = 3
FXC_DISTORT: u32 = 4
FXC_NOISE: u32 = 5
FXC_OBJECT: u32 = 6
FXC_PHOTO: u32 = 7
FXC_RENDER: u32 = 8
FXC_STYLIZE: u32 = 9
FP_INT: u8 = 1  // property types: slot i64
FP_DEC: u8 = 2  // f64
FP_BOOL: u8 = 3  // i64 0 / 1
FP_CHOICE: u8 = 4  // i64 index
FP_ANGLE: u8 = 5  // f64 degrees
FP_PAN: u8 = 6  // two slots: f64 x, f64 y (-1..1)
FP_COLOR: u8 = 7  // straight ARGB in the low half
FP_SEED: u8 = 8  // i64 seed
FPF_LOG: u8 = 1  // the slider maps logarithmically
```

*img.inc / jfgui.inc*

```rust
FMT_PARGB: u8 = 1  // premultiplied 0xAARRGGBB
FMT_ARGB: u8 = 3  // straight 0xAARRGGBB (bytes B, G, R, A)
IMG_MAXDIM: u32 = 65535  // largest width / height of a decoded image
IMGD_STRAIGHT: u32 = 1  // decode flags (ImgDec::flags)
IMGD_ICC: u32 = 2
IMGD_EXIF: u32 = 4
IMGD_XMP: u32 = 8
IMGD_NO_ORIENT: u32 = 16
IMGD_PROBE: u32 = 32  // header + metadata only: pixels optional
IMGD_THUMB: u32 = 64
IMM_GRAY: u8 = 1  // colour models (ImgMeta::model)
IMM_RGB: u8 = 2
IMM_PAL: u8 = 3
IMM_CMYK: u8 = 4
IMM_YCC: u8 = 5
IMF_ALPHA: u32 = 1  // ImgMeta::flags
IMF_SRC_PREMUL: u32 = 2
IMF_LOSSLESS: u32 = 4
IMF_ANIMATED: u32 = 8
IMF_HDR: u32 = 16
IFC_ALPHA: u32 = 4  // file type capabilities (FtDesc::caps)
IFC_ANIM: u32 = 16
IFC_PAGES: u32 = 32
IFC_LOSSY: u32 = 128
IFC_LOSSLESS: u32 = 256
IFC_HDR: u32 = 512
```

- **`entry!(...)`**
  export pn_plugin_query(PNAPI\*) -> PLUGININFO\* | 0, calling `query` with the host's table
  (kept for api() when it is a complete version-1 table).  \\`query: fn(&'static PnApi) ->
  Option&lt;&'static PluginInfo>\\`; None refuses loading.  The plugin's one unsafe item.

- **`fn panic(info: &core::panic::PanicInfo) -> !`**
  the panic handler of a plugin: "panic at FILE:LINE" into the plugin log when the host
  table is known, then a fault the host's guard catches (the plugin is disabled).  The
  message itself is left out: formatting it would link core::fmt into every plugin (+6 KB);
  a handler of your own can log it with log!("{}", info.message()).  (Inlined, so that the
  optimizer sees that the message is never read and drops it.)

- **`fn trap() -> !`**
  an illegal instruction (ud2): the host's crash guard disables the plugin

### `plugin/abi.rs`

Source: [`lib/plugin/abi.rs`](../lib/plugin/abi.rs)

```text
abi -- the records of the plugin ABI, version 1, as #[repr(C)] types, and the const
constructors of the descriptors a plugin puts into statics
                     [ASMLIB/inc/plugin.inc, fx.inc (FXARGS, FXDESC, FXPROP), img.inc
                      (IMGDEC, IMGMETA, IMGOPT), thread.inc (JOB), jfgui.inc (SURFACE)]

Every function pointer uses the Win64 ABI (`extern "win64"`) on Linux too.  The layouts are
asserted against the offsets of the include files at the end of this file.  Strings are
NUL-terminated UTF-8 (`c"..."` literals); lists are static slices.  The descriptors hold raw
pointers into the plugin's own image -- the host checks every one of them against the
image's segments before it reads anything.
```

- **`type ItemFn = extern "win64" fn(ctx: *const c_void, index: u64, job: *const Job)`**
  fn(ctx, index, job) of PA_PARFOR

- **`type PrepareFn = extern "win64" fn(*const FxArgs) -> *mut c_void`**
  FXD_PREPARE: fn(FXARGS\*) -> rax aux | 0 (0 also on error: the code in edx)

- **`type RenderFn = extern "win64" fn(*const FxArgs) -> i32`**
  FXD_RENDER: fn(FXARGS\*) -> eax 0 / ERR_CANCEL / ERR_\*

- **`type FreeFn = extern "win64" fn(*mut c_void)`**
  FXD_FREE: fn(aux)

- **`type ProbeFn = extern "win64" fn(*const u8, usize, *mut ImgMeta) -> u32`**
  FT_PROBE: fn(data, len, meta) -> eax 1 recognised / 0

- **`type DecodeFn = extern "win64" fn(*const u8, usize, *mut Surface, *const ImgDec) -> i32`**
  FT_DECODE: fn(data, len, sf, dec) -> eax 0 / ERR_\*

- **`type EncodeFn = extern "win64" fn(*const Surface, *const ImgOpt, *mut *mut u8, *mut usize) -> i32`**
  FT_ENCODE: fn(sf, opt, &buf, &len) -> eax 0 / ERR_\*

- **`struct PnApi`**
  PNAPI -- the host's function table, passed to pn_plugin_query (PA_\*).  Later versions only
  append entries; `size` says how many there are.
  - `size: u32`
  - `version: u32`
  - `alloc: extern "win64" fn(usize) -> *mut u8` -- (size) -> zeroed, 64-byte aligned block | 0; thread safe
  - `free: extern "win64" fn(*mut u8)` -- (ptr) (0 allowed)
  - `realloc: extern "win64" fn(*mut u8, usize) -> *mut u8` -- (ptr, size) -> block | 0
  - `parfor: extern "win64" fn(ItemFn, *const c_void, u64) -> i32` -- (fn, ctx, count) -> 0 / ERR_CANCEL / ERR_FAULT: fn(ctx, index, job) on the workers
  - `hash32: extern "win64" fn(u32, u32, u32, u32) -> u32` -- (x, y, seed, stream) -> deterministic per-pixel random bits (fx_hash32)
  - `log: extern "win64" fn(*const u8)` -- (msg) a UTF-8 line into the plugin log (stderr, "PaintCraft: Plugin &lt;file>: msg")
  - `row: extern "win64" fn(*const Surface, i32) -> *mut u8` -- (sf, y) -> row y of a SURFACE | 0
  - `surfalloc: extern "win64" fn(*mut Surface, i32, i32, *const ImgDec) -> i32` -- (sf, w, h, dec) -> 0 / ERR_\*: a zeroed straight FMT_ARGB surface for decoders
  - `cancelled: extern "win64" fn(*const Job) -> i32` -- (job) -> 1 when the job's cancel flag is set
  - `fmt: *const c_void` -- (buf, cap, fmt, ...) -> length: the host's printf subset (C variadic; core::fmt and
    log_fmt are the Rust way)
  - `nthreads: extern "win64" fn() -> u32` -- () -> worker threads

- **`struct Surface`**
  SURFACE (jfgui.inc SF_\*): pixels as rows of `stride` bytes, 0xAARRGGBB per pixel
  - `px: *mut u8`
  - `w: i32`
  - `h: i32`
  - `stride: i32`
  - `fmt: u8`
  - `flags: u8`
  - `opac: u8`
  - `blend: u8`
  - `aux: u64`

- **`struct Job`**
  JOB (thread.inc): plugins only read `cancel`
  - `func: *const c_void`
  - `ctx: *const c_void`
  - `count: u64`
  - `next: u64`
  - `done: u64`
  - `user: u64`
  - `cancel: AtomicU32` -- nonzero: the job is cancelled (written by the host at any time)
  - `state: u32`
  - `active: u32`
  - `flags: u32`
  - `link: *const c_void`
  - `rsv: u64`

- **`struct FxArgs`**
  FXARGS (fx.inc FXA_\*): one prepare / render call
  - `src: *const Surface` -- source (FMT_ARGB, straight), read-only, the whole image
  - `dst: *const Surface` -- destination: written only inside the ROI
  - `x0: i32`
  - `y0: i32`
  - `x1: i32`
  - `y1: i32`
  - `param: *const u64` -- FX_NSLOTS 8-byte parameter slots (see FxProp)
  - `aux: *const c_void` -- what prepare returned, or 0
  - `job: *const Job` -- poll JOB_CANCEL once per row (0 = none)
  - `sel: *const c_void` -- the selection (TMASK) or 0; not available to plugins of the Rust host

- **`struct ImgDec`**
  IMGDEC (img.inc ID_\*): decode options
  - `flags: u32` -- IMGD_\*
  - `frame: u32`
  - `hint: u32`
  - `rsv: u32`
  - `maxpx: u64`
  - `meta: *mut ImgMeta` -- IMGMETA to fill, or 0
  - `cancel: *const AtomicU32` -- pointer to a u32 that cancels when nonzero, or 0
  - `maxmem: u64`

- **`struct ImgMeta`**
  IMGMETA (img.inc IM_\*): what a probe / decode reports about the image
  - `fmt: u32`
  - `w: u32` -- stored width / height
  - `h: u32`
  - `bits: u8` -- bits per sample of the source
  - `model: u8` -- IMM_\*
  - `orient: u8`
  - `channels: u8` -- samples per pixel incl. alpha (0 = unknown)
  - `flags: u32` -- IMF_\*
  - `frames: u32`
  - `dpix: u32`
  - `dpiy: u32`
  - `cicp: u32`
  - `gamma: u32`
  - `icc: *const u8`
  - `icclen: u64`
  - `exif: *const u8`
  - `exiflen: u64`
  - `xmp: *const u8`
  - `xmplen: u64`
  - `delay: u32`
  - `loops: u32`
  - `quality: u32`
  - `resunit: u32`
  - `chrm: [u16; 8]`
  - `dxgi: u16`
  - `texfl: u8`
  - `rsv: u8`
  - `texn: u32`

- **`struct ImgOpt`**
  IMGOPT (img.inc IO_\*): encoder options
  - `fmt: u32`
  - `quality: u32` -- 1..100 (0 = format default)
  - `flags: u32` -- IOF_\*
  - `bitdepth: u32`
  - `samplebits: u32`
  - `dither: u32`
  - `athresh: u32`
  - `chroma: u32`
  - `effort: u32`
  - `ddsfmt: u32`
  - `mipfilter: u32`
  - `tiffcomp: u32`
  - `meta: *const ImgMeta`
  - `cancel: *const AtomicU32`

- **`struct FxProp`**
  FXPROP (fx.inc FXP_\*): one property of an effect, shown in the generic dialog
  - `typ: u8` -- FP_\*
  - `dec: u8` -- decimals shown (FP_DEC / FP_ANGLE)
  - `slot: u8` -- first slot in the parameter block
  - `flags: u8` -- FPF_\*
  - `rsv: u32`
  - `name: *const u8` -- the label of the dialog
  - `min: f64`
  - `max: f64`
  - `def: f64` -- default (FP_COLOR: the colour in the low 32 bits of the bit pattern)
  - `choices: *const u8` -- FP_CHOICE: the choice list (strings, then an empty one); FP_PAN: the y default as f64
    bits

- **`struct FxDesc`**
  FXDESC (fx.inc FXD_\*): an effect or adjustment
  - `id: u32` -- ignored for plugins (the host assigns one)
  - `flags: u32` -- FXF_\*
  - `name: *const u8` -- the menu name (1..47 bytes, unique; also the settings key)
  - `prepare: Option<PrepareFn>`
  - `render: Option<RenderFn>`
  - `free: Option<FreeFn>`
  - `nprops: u32`
  - `rsv: u32`
  - `props: *const FxProp`
  - `category: u32` -- FXC_\*
  - `rsv2: u32`

- **`struct FtDesc`**
  FTDESC (plugin.inc FT_\*): a file type (decoder and / or encoder)
  - `name: *const u8` -- display name ("QOI")
  - `exts: *const u8` -- 1..8 extensions without dots, ';'-separated ("qoi")
  - `mime: *const u8` -- MIME type or 0
  - `probe: Option<ProbeFn>`
  - `decode: Option<DecodeFn>`
  - `encode: Option<EncodeFn>`
  - `caps: u32` -- IFC_\*
  - `rsv: u32`

- **`struct ShapeDesc`**
  SHAPEDESC (plugin.inc PSD_\*): a shape of the Formen tool
  - `name: *const u8` -- display name (1..63 bytes)
  - `data: *const u8` -- path data in the Geometry syntax of paint.net ShapeKit XAML ("M 0,0 L 100,0 ...")

- **`struct PluginInfo`**
  PLUGININFO (plugin.inc PI_\*): what pn_plugin_query returns
  - `magic: u32`
  - `size: u32` -- size of this record (>= PI_SIZE_V1)
  - `apimin: u32` -- the lowest PNAPI version the plugin needs
  - `flags: u32`
  - `name: *const u8` -- 1..63 bytes
  - `version: *const u8` -- 1..31 bytes
  - `author: *const u8`
  - `url: *const u8`
  - `nfx: u32`
  - `nft: u32`
  - `fx: *const *const FxDesc` -- FXDESC\*[nfx]
  - `ft: *const FtDesc` -- FTDESC[nft]
  - `nshape: u32`
  - `rsv: u32`
  - `shapes: *const ShapeDesc` -- SHAPEDESC[nshape]

**`impl PluginInfo`**

- **`const fn new(name: &'static CStr, version: &'static CStr) -> PluginInfo`**
  a plugin named `name` (1..63 bytes) in version `version` (1..31 bytes), PNAPI 1,
  nothing in it yet
- **`const fn author(mut self, author: &'static CStr) -> PluginInfo`**
- **`const fn url(mut self, url: &'static CStr) -> PluginInfo`**
  origin / URL (1..127 bytes)
- **`const fn api_min(mut self, v: u32) -> PluginInfo`**
  the lowest PNAPI version the plugin needs (default 1)
- **`const fn effects(mut self, list: &'static [&'static FxDesc]) -> PluginInfo`**
  the effects / adjustments (at most 16)
- **`const fn file_types(mut self, list: &'static [FtDesc]) -> PluginInfo`**
  the file types (at most 4)
- **`const fn shapes(mut self, list: &'static [ShapeDesc]) -> PluginInfo`**
  the shapes (at most 32)

**`impl ShapeDesc`**

- **`const fn new(name: &'static CStr, data: &'static CStr) -> ShapeDesc`**

**`impl FtDesc`**

- **`const fn new<T: crate::FileType>( name: &'static CStr, exts: &'static CStr, mime: Option<&'static CStr>, caps: u32, ) -> FtDesc`**
  a file type with the functions of T (only those T says it has); `exts` = "a;b",
  `caps` = IFC_\*

**`impl FxDesc`**

- **`const fn new<E: crate::Effect>(name: &'static CStr, flags: u32, category: u32, props: &'static [FxProp]) -> FxDesc`**
  the effect E named `name` (the menu name, 1..47 bytes), FXF_\* `flags` (FXF_PREPARE is
  added when E has a prepare step), FXC_\* `category`, the properties of its dialog

**`impl FxProp`**

- **`const fn int(slot: u8, name: &'static CStr, min: f64, max: f64, def: f64) -> FxProp`**
  FP_INT: an integer slider min..max (slot: i64)
- **`const fn dec(slot: u8, name: &'static CStr, dec: u8, min: f64, max: f64, def: f64) -> FxProp`**
  FP_DEC: a number with `dec` decimals (slot: f64)
- **`const fn angle(slot: u8, name: &'static CStr, dec: u8, min: f64, max: f64, def: f64) -> FxProp`**
  FP_ANGLE: degrees with `dec` decimals (slot: f64)
- **`const fn boolean(slot: u8, name: &'static CStr, def: bool) -> FxProp`**
  FP_BOOL: a check box (slot: i64 0 / 1)
- **`const fn choice(slot: u8, name: &'static CStr, list: &'static [u8], def: u32) -> FxProp`**
  FP_CHOICE: one of the entries of `list` = b"first\\0second\\0...\\0\\0" (UTF-8; slot: i64
  index); `def` = the index chosen by default
- **`const fn pan(slot: u8, name: &'static CStr, dec: u8, x: f64, y: f64) -> FxProp`**
  FP_PAN: a point -1..1 (slots slot and slot + 1: f64 x, y)
- **`const fn color(slot: u8, name: &'static CStr, argb: u32) -> FxProp`**
  FP_COLOR: a straight ARGB colour (slot: the colour in the low 32 bits)
- **`const fn seed(slot: u8, name: &'static CStr) -> FxProp`**
  FP_SEED: a random seed with a "Neu würfeln" button (slot: i64)
- **`const fn log(mut self) -> FxProp`**
  the slider maps logarithmically (FPF_LOG)

### `plugin/api.rs`

Source: [`lib/plugin/api.rs`](../lib/plugin/api.rs)

```text
api -- the host's PNAPI table after pn_plugin_query, and safe wrappers of its functions
                                                 [ASMLIB/inc/plugin.inc PA_*; PLUGINS.md §2]

  api() -> Option<&Api>          the table (None before pn_plugin_query, or when the host's
                                 table is shorter than version 1)
  Block::new(cap) -> Option<Block>      a zeroed PA_ALLOC buffer (64-byte aligned) with a
                                 length; freed on drop, or handed to the host (into_raw:
                                 the encoder's output)
  parallel_for(count, &f) -> Result     f(index, job) for 0..count on the host's workers
                                 (each item guarded by the host; nesting allowed)
  hash32(x, y, seed, stream), nthreads(), log(msg), log_fmt(format_args!(..)), log!(..)
  PnAlloc                        a #[global_allocator] on PA_ALLOC / PA_FREE / PA_REALLOC for
                                 plugins that use `alloc` (opt-in: declare it in the plugin)

The table pointer is written once (pn_plugin_query, UI thread) and read by every thread
afterwards: an atomic.  Everything else here is stateless.
```

- **`struct Api(PnApi)`**
  the host table, checked to be a complete version-1 table

- **`fn query_entry(api: *const PnApi, query: fn(&'static PnApi) -> Option<&'static PluginInfo>) -> *const PluginInfo`**
  pn_plugin_query's body (entry!): keep the table, ask the plugin

- **`fn api() -> Option<&'static Api>`**
  the host's functions (None before pn_plugin_query or with an incomplete table)

**`impl Api`**

- **`fn raw(&self) -> &PnApi`**
  the raw table (size, version, function pointers)
- **`fn hash32(&self, x: u32, y: u32, seed: u32, stream: u32) -> u32`**
  PA_HASH32: deterministic per-pixel random bits (fx_hash32)
- **`fn nthreads(&self) -> u32`**
  PA_NTHREADS: the host's worker threads
- **`fn log(&self, msg: &str)`**
  PA_LOG: one line (UTF-8, cut at 511 bytes) into the plugin log
- **`fn parallel_for<F: Fn(usize, &Job) + Sync>(&self, count: usize, f: &F) -> Result<(), i32>`**
  PA_PARFOR: f(index, job) for every index 0..count on the host's worker threads and the
  caller -> Ok, Err(ERR_CANCEL) (the job was cancelled) or Err(ERR_FAULT) (an item
  crashed: the plugin is disabled).  `job` is the PNAPI job of the run (poll it in long
  items with Job::is_cancelled).

**`impl Job`**

- **`fn is_cancelled(&self) -> bool`**
  JOB_CANCEL is set: stop early (the host discards the result)

- **`fn parallel_for<F: Fn(usize, &Job) + Sync>(count: usize, f: &F) -> Result<(), i32>`**
  parallel_for through api() (Err(ERR_FAIL) without the host table)

- **`fn hash32(x: u32, y: u32, seed: u32, stream: u32) -> u32`**
  PA_HASH32 through api() (0 without the host table)

- **`fn nthreads() -> u32`**
  PA_NTHREADS through api() (1 without the host table)

- **`fn log(msg: &str)`**
  PA_LOG through api() (nothing without the host table)

- **`fn log_fmt(args: core::fmt::Arguments)`**
  a formatted line into the plugin log: log_fmt(format_args!("{} px", n)) (no allocation)

- **`log!(...)`**
  log!("fmt", args..) -- log_fmt(format_args!(..))

- **`struct Block`**
  a byte buffer in host memory (PA_ALLOC: zeroed, 64-byte aligned): capacity fixed at
  creation, a length that grows with push / extend.  Dropping it frees it (PA_FREE);
  into_raw hands it to the host (an encoder's output, which the host frees).

**`impl Block`**

- **`fn new(cap: usize) -> Option<Block>`**
  a zeroed block of cap bytes (None: no host table / out of memory)
- **`fn capacity(&self) -> usize`**
- **`fn len(&self) -> usize`**
- **`fn is_empty(&self) -> bool`**
- **`fn buf(&mut self) -> &mut [u8]`**
  the whole capacity (zeroed where nothing was written)
- **`fn as_slice(&self) -> &[u8]`**
  the first len bytes
- **`fn set_len(&mut self, n: usize)`**
  set the length (n &lt;= capacity, else a panic)
- **`fn push(&mut self, b: u8)`**
  append one byte (a panic when full)
- **`fn extend(&mut self, s: &[u8])`**
  append bytes (a panic when they do not fit)
- **`fn into_raw(self) -> (*mut u8, usize)`**
  hand the block to the host: (pointer, length); the host frees it

- **`struct PnAlloc`**
  a global allocator on the host's PA_ALLOC / PA_FREE / PA_REALLOC (alignment up to 64; thread
  safe).  Opt-in for plugins that use the alloc crate:
  ```text
  extern crate alloc;
  #[global_allocator]
  static A: plugin::PnAlloc = plugin::PnAlloc;
  ```
  Allocations before pn_plugin_query fail (null).

### `plugin/ft.rs`

Source: [`lib/plugin/ft.rs`](../lib/plugin/ft.rs)

```text
ft -- file types: the FileType trait, the safe views of a decode / encode call and the
trampolines the host calls                 [ASMLIB/inc/plugin.inc FT_*, img.inc; PLUGINS.md §3]

  trait FileType { const PROBE / DECODE / ENCODE; fn probe(data, meta); fn decode(data, d);
                   fn encode(img, opt) -> Block }
  FtDesc::new::<T>(name, exts, mime, caps)     (abi.rs) points at the trampolines
  Decoder: flags() (IMGD_*), meta(), cancelled(), alloc(w, h) (PA_SURFALLOC: a zeroed
           straight FMT_ARGB surface the host owns), row_mut(y), width / height / stride
  Image:   the straight FMT_ARGB surface to encode: width, height, row(y)

Same contract as the built-in codecs (IMGCODEC): any thread, possibly several calls at once,
no globals.  probe: a magic check (fill meta when given).  decode: never read beyond the
data; an error after alloc is fine (the host frees the surface).  encode: the output is a
Block (PA_ALLOC), which the host frees.
```

- **`trait FileType: 'static`**
  a file type: implement it on a unit struct and describe it with FtDesc::new::&lt;T>(..);
  the consts say which functions it has (at least DECODE or ENCODE)
  - `const PROBE: bool = false`
  - `const DECODE: bool = false`
  - `const ENCODE: bool = false`
  - `fn probe(data: &[u8], meta: Option<&mut ImgMeta>) -> bool`
    data starts with this format's signature (fill meta when given: w, h, bits, model,
    channels, flags)
  - `fn decode(data: &[u8], d: &mut Decoder<'_>) -> Result<(), i32>`
    decode data into d (d.alloc(w, h), then the rows) -> Ok / Err(ERR_\*)
  - `fn encode(img: &Image<'_>, opt: &ImgOpt) -> Result<Block, i32>`
    encode img -> the file's bytes in a Block (Block::new(cap), push / extend) / Err(ERR_\*)

- **`struct Decoder<'a>`**
  one decode call: the options and the host's SURFACE record to fill

**`impl<'a> Decoder<'a>`**

- **`fn flags(&self) -> u32`**
  IMGD_\* of the call (IMGD_PROBE: metadata only, pixels optional)
- **`fn meta(&mut self) -> Option<&mut ImgMeta>`**
  the IMGMETA to fill (None: the host wants none)
- **`fn cancelled(&self) -> bool`**
  ID_CANCEL is set: return Err(ERR_CANCEL)
- **`fn alloc(&mut self, w: i32, h: i32) -> Result<(), i32>`**
  PA_SURFALLOC: a zeroed straight FMT_ARGB surface w x h for the result (once; checked
  against the decode limits: ERR_DIMS / ERR_LIMIT / ERR_NOMEM)
- **`fn width(&self) -> i32`**
  the size after alloc (0 before)
- **`fn height(&self) -> i32`**
- **`fn row_mut(&mut self, y: i32) -> &mut [u32]`**
  row y of the result: width pixels 0xAARRGGBB (after alloc, 0 &lt;= y &lt; height, else a
  panic)

- **`struct Image<'a>`**
  the image to encode: straight FMT_ARGB (the host converts)

**`impl<'a> Image<'a>`**

- **`fn width(&self) -> i32`**
- **`fn height(&self) -> i32`**
- **`fn row(&self, y: i32) -> &'a [u32]`**
  row y: width pixels 0xAARRGGBB (0 &lt;= y &lt; height, else a panic)

### `plugin/fx.rs`

Source: [`lib/plugin/fx.rs`](../lib/plugin/fx.rs)

```text
fx -- effects and adjustments: the Effect trait, the safe view of a render / prepare call,
and the trampolines the host calls                  [ASMLIB/inc/fx.inc; PLUGINS.md §3, §4]

  trait Effect { type Aux; const PREPARE; fn render(fx, aux); fn prepare(fx) }
  FxDesc::new::<E>(name, flags, category, props)    (abi.rs) points at the trampolines
  Fx: width / height, the ROI (x0, y0, x1, y1), src_row(y) (the whole source row),
      rows(y) (the ROI parts of the source and destination row y), dst_row(y), param_*(slot),
      cancelled(), job()

Rules of the host (fx.inc): render runs on many worker threads at once, each call for one
ROI, and writes the destination only inside it; the result of a pixel depends only on the
source, the parameters and aux (never on the ROI split); no writable globals; poll the
cancel flag once per row.  Pixels are straight 0xAARRGGBB.  The destination of the Rust
host is backed only inside the ROI -- Fx never hands out anything outside it.
```

- **`trait Effect: 'static`**
  an effect or adjustment: implement it on a unit struct and describe it with
  FxDesc::new::&lt;E>(..)
  - `type Aux: Sync + 'static`
    what prepare computes for render (FXA_AUX); `()` for effects without prepare
  - `const PREPARE: bool = false`
    has a prepare step (called once per run on one worker before any render)
  - `fn render(fx: &mut Fx<'_>, aux: Option<&Self::Aux>) -> Result<(), i32>`
    write the destination inside the ROI -> Ok, Err(ERR_CANCEL) when cancelled, Err(ERR_\*)
  - `fn prepare(fx: &Fx<'_>) -> Result<Option<Self::Aux>, i32>`
    global statistics, tables, ... over the whole source -> Ok(Some(aux)), Ok(None) (no aux)
    or Err(ERR_\*) (only with PREPARE = true)

- **`struct Fx<'a>`**
  one call of render / prepare: the validated FXARGS
  - `x0: i32` -- the ROI: x0 &lt;= x &lt; x1, y0 &lt;= y &lt; y1 inside the image
  - `y0: i32`
  - `x1: i32`
  - `y1: i32`

**`impl<'a> Fx<'a>`**

- **`fn width(&self) -> i32`**
  the image width (source and destination)
- **`fn height(&self) -> i32`**
- **`fn src_row(&self, y: i32) -> &'a [u32]`**
  the whole source row y (0 &lt;= y &lt; height, else a panic)
- **`fn src_px(&self, x: i32, y: i32) -> u32`**
  the source pixel (x, y) (a panic outside the image)
- **`fn dst_row(&mut self, y: i32) -> &mut [u32]`**
  the destination row y inside the ROI: index i is pixel x0 + i (y0 &lt;= y &lt; y1, else a
  panic)
- **`fn rows(&mut self, y: i32) -> (&'a [u32], &mut [u32])`**
  the ROI parts of source and destination row y (index i = pixel x0 + i)
- **`fn param(&self, slot: usize) -> u64`**
  the raw 8 bytes of parameter slot `slot` (0 when the host passed none)
- **`fn param_int(&self, slot: usize) -> i64`**
  FP_INT / FP_BOOL / FP_CHOICE / FP_SEED: the slot as i64
- **`fn param_f64(&self, slot: usize) -> f64`**
  FP_DEC / FP_ANGLE / FP_PAN: the slot as f64
- **`fn param_bool(&self, slot: usize) -> bool`**
  FP_BOOL: slot != 0
- **`fn param_color(&self, slot: usize) -> u32`**
  FP_COLOR: the straight ARGB colour in the low half
- **`fn cancelled(&self) -> bool`**
  the run's cancel flag is set: return Err(ERR_CANCEL) (poll it once per row)
- **`fn job(&self) -> Option<&'a Job>`**
  the run's JOB (None: the host passed none)
